# Headroom — Detailed Capability Audit (every module, every tool)

Companion to **[AUDIT_REPORT.md](AUDIT_REPORT.md)** (the executive/risk audit). This document inventories **every tool in every module**, produced by reading each module's full source. **55 modules · 1,497 documented tool entries.** For each tool: what it does, the rule/formula it implements, where it persists, and its class.

**Persistence/class legend:**
- **Backend** — backed by a server route + DB (real business logic).
- **KV** — user records persisted to the synced key-value store via `useFeatureState` (real persistence, client-side compute).
- **Indicative** — calculator using hardcoded/heuristic rates or sector bands (decision-support, needs periodic update).
- **Simulated** — mimics an integration/automation that isn't wired to a live external system.
- **Preview** — forward-looking concept; not operational (no rails exist yet).

**Method:** one agent per module read the full page file and enumerated the tab-selector array + every rendered component. Static read, not runtime execution — individual formula correctness is not separately unit-verified (only the 47 `lib/` tests exist).

---

## ⚠️ Key honesty findings from the deep dive

These corrected or sharpened assumptions in the summary report — **read before quoting any module as "AI" or "integrated":**

1. **AI CFO Copilot makes ZERO network calls.** Despite the `/api/ai` route existing, `CopilotPage.tsx` is entirely **regex/keyword + deterministic rule math** over the local store. "Ask the Copilot" is a rule engine, not an LLM. Every action is a navigate/clipboard preview — **nothing moves money or files**. *(CFO-Brief's "AI Brief" does call `/api/ai/ask` with a deterministic fallback — so that one is partially real.)*
2. **Connectors are all simulated.** `/api/connectors` only mints an ID; there are **no live bank/POS/CRM/e-commerce integrations**. The whole module is Simulated/Preview.
3. **Documents: OCR & e-Sign are simulated.** Receipt "OCR" parses the **filename only** (not image content); e-Sign statuses are advanced manually (no ASP/Aadhaar-eSign provider); share-links are illustrative tokens. Only the **Vault** (`/api/files`) is real.
4. **WhatsApp sends are `wa.me` deep-links**, not API sends — they open WhatsApp with prefilled text. Only connect/OTP and alert prefs touch the backend. (Legitimate and useful, but not automated server-side messaging.)
5. **Settings "depth" cards persist but aren't all enforced** — preferences (numbering, tax defaults, formats) save to KV but several are **not yet wired** into the invoice/payment/format logic they describe.
6. **Tokens & Frontier are preview/design tools** (e-Rupee, programmable money, autonomous treasury, quantum) — clearly labelled, not operational.
7. **The genuinely backend-integrated core** is: auth/users, invoices, transactions, GST, payroll, credit, collections, lenders, operations/suppliers, treasury, capital, alerts, advisor, files, billing/Razorpay, admin. Everything else is **KV-persisted client-side compute** — fully functional for one business, but not server-orchestrated.

## ━━━ PILLAR 1 · Sell & Get Paid ━━━

## Invoicing & Order-to-Cash (`/invoices`) — 32 tools

GST-compliant invoicing, UPI/WhatsApp collections, and a deep order-to-cash toolbox (quotes, credit control, GST/TDS/TCS computation, e-invoice JSON, AR ageing). Stakeholders: owner, finance, sales. Backend: `/api/invoices` + KV.

Core invoice register (backend-persisted) and global actions:

- **New Invoice** (`NewInvoiceModal`) — customer name/GSTIN/email/WhatsApp, header GST rate (0/5/12/18/28), due date, line items (description, HSN/SAC, qty, unit price, per-line GST) → `POST /api/invoices`; modal preview totals invoice-level (subtotal × header rate), backend recomputes per-line → new invoice with `invoice_number`, `irn`, totals; mirrored into shared store as AR. _Persist: Backend (`/api/invoices`)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Invoice list + row actions** — live `GET /api/invoices` table (number, customer, amount +GST%, due/aging, status badge) with: **Download PDF** (`GET /api/invoices/:id/pdf`, blob), **Send by email** (`POST /api/invoices/:id/send`, draft/sent only with email), **UPI/card link** (opens `UpiQrModal`), **WhatsApp remind** (`POST /api/invoices/:id/remind`, only when aging≠current), **Mark paid** (`PATCH /api/invoices/:id {status}`). Header KPIs: Pending / Overdue / Paid(all-time) sums. _Persist: Backend._ _Class: Backend._
- **UPI QR** (`UpiQrModal`) — invoice id → `POST /api/invoices/:id/upi-link` → server-built UPI deep link + QR image (BIP-21 `upi://pay`, amount = invoice total). _Persist: Backend (generated, not stored)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Auto-Collect** (`collection` tab, `CollectionAutoPanel`) — filters live invoices to overdue (aging≠current, not paid/cancelled) → per-row & "Remind All" call `POST /api/invoices/:id/remind` (WhatsApp + one-tap UPI link); "Sent" state is in-memory only → overdue count badge + total, auto-marks paid on collection via webhook. _Persist: Backend (reminders); session set (sent flags)._ _Class: Backend._

Billing-tools selector (each `useFeatureState` KV unless noted; computations are client-side):

- **Quotation** (`quote`) — customer, valid-until, line items → `computeDoc` per-line GST → numbered `QT-YYYY-NNN`; Convert flips status to "converted" (no auto-invoice, manual re-entry). Note: no GST liability until converted. _Persist: KV `invoice-quotations`._ _Class: KV._
- **Proforma** (`proforma`) — customer, advance %, items → per-line totals + advance payable = total × advance%; numbered `PI-YYYY-NNN`; "Mark converted" flag. Not a tax invoice (no buyer ITC until final invoice). _Persist: KV `invoice-proformas`._ _Class: KV._
- **Recurring / Subscription Billing** (`recurring`) — customer, amount, GST, freq (monthly/quarterly/yearly), next-run → est. MRR (monthly=full, quarterly÷3, yearly÷12, incl GST), due-to-generate count; "Generate now" advances next-run by freq and increments counter (no real invoice posted), pause/resume/delete. _Persist: KV `invoice-recurring`._ _Class: Indicative (cycle simulated; not wired to cron)._
- **Pay Links** (`paylink`, `PaymentLinkBuilder`) — VPA (defaults from `store.firm.upiVpa`), payee name, pick live unpaid invoice or manual amount → client-side `buildUpiLink` (`upi://pay?pa&pn&am&cu=INR&tn`) + hosted card URL `pay.headroom.app/c?…`; copy/open. No funds touch Headroom; mark-paid manual. _Persist: none (links built client-side)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Credit/Debit Note** (`creditnote`, `CreditDebitNoteManager`) — type credit/debit, against-invoice (prefills customer/GST), taxable, GST → gstAmt = round(taxable×rate%), total; numbered `CN/DN-YYYY-NNN`; net-credit/net-debit summary. Note: credit notes reportable in GSTR-1 by 30 Nov post-FY to reverse output tax; debit notes raise liability in issue month. _Persist: KV `invoice-cdnotes`._ _Class: KV._
- **Credit Limit** (`creditlimit`, `CreditLimitManager`) — customer, limit, hold-if-overdue-days → exposure (outstanding + overdue) computed live from unpaid invoices; utilisation% bar; ON HOLD when outstanding>limit or any past-due. _Persist: KV `invoice-credit-limits` (exposure from live backend)._ _Class: KV._
- **Multi-Currency** (`multicurrency`, `MultiCurrencyInvoicing`) — overseas customer, currency (USD/EUR/GBP/AED/SGD/AUD), FCY amount, ₹/unit invoice rate → INR value; numbered `EXP-YYYY-NNN`; enter realisation rate to book FX gain/loss = FCY×(realRate−invRate); net G/(L) summary. Exports under LUT zero-rated (0% IGST). _Persist: KV `invoice-fx`._ _Class: KV._
- **Approval** (`approval`, `ApprovalWorkflow`) — threshold (₹, default 100000); live invoices ≥ threshold listed → "Route for approval" creates pending maker-checker request; checker approve/reject. _Persist: KV `invoice-approval-threshold`, `invoice-approvals`._ _Class: KV._
- **Template Studio** (`template`, `TemplateStudio`) — logo text, primary/accent colours, font, terms, footer → live white invoice preview; reset to default. Claims applied to future PDFs/shares. _Persist: KV `invoice-template-theme`._ _Class: Preview (PDF application not verified here)._
- **Delivery Challan** (`challan`, `DeliveryChallan`) — consignee, purpose (Rule 55: approval/job-work/branch transfer/exhibition/other), vehicle, items → goods value via `computeDoc`; numbered `DC-YYYY-NNN`; "To invoice" sets flag (manual tax-invoice raise). _Persist: KV `invoice-challans`._ _Class: KV._
- **Late-Fee/Interest** (`latefee`, `LateFeeApplier`) — annual rate% (default 18), flat fee, grace days → for live past-due invoices: simple interest = principal × rate% × max(0, daysLate−grace)/365, fee if days>0, payable = principal+interest+fee; "Apply" stores charge (re-invoice manually). _Persist: KV `invoice-late-interest-rate`, `-flat-fee`, `-grace-days`, `-applied`._ _Class: Indicative (computed live; charge not posted to backend)._
- **Ageing Buckets** (`ageing`, `AgeingBuckets`) — live unpaid invoices bucketed by days-past-due: Not due / 1–30 / 31–60 / 61–90 / 90+ → per-customer matrix, bucket totals, total outstanding, overdue total, % overdue. _Persist: none (read-only from backend)._ _Class: Backend (derived)._
- **Bulk (CSV)** (`bulk`, `BulkInvoiceGenerator`) — paste/upload CSV `customer,gstin,description,qty,rate,gst,due_date` → validates (customer/description present, qty>0, rate>0, GST∈0/5/12/18/28), one `POST /api/invoices` per valid row → created count; invalid rows skipped. _Persist: Backend (posts real invoices)._ _Class: Backend._
- **PO Matcher** (`pomatch`, `PoMatcher`) — PO number, PO amount, match-invoice (prefills customer), tolerance% (default 2) → variance = invoice total − PO; status matched / over-billed / under-billed / unmatched within tolerance band. _Persist: KV `invoice-po-match`._ _Class: KV._
- **TDS & Round-off** (`tds`, `TdsRoundOffHelper`) — taxable, GST, TDS section (194C 1%/2%, 194J 10%/2%, 194H 5%, 194I 2%, 194Q 0.1%), "TDS on GST-inclusive" toggle (CBDT Circular 23/2017 — only if GST not shown separately) → round-off = round(total)−total, TDS = round(base × secRate%) on taxable (or rounded total), net payable = rounded − TDS. Full GST still paid to govt; buyer deposits TDS vs PAN (26AS). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (calculator)._
- **Dispute Tracker** (`dispute`, `DisputeTracker`) — pick disputed invoice (prefills customer/amount), reason (price/short-supply/quality/duplicate/tax error/not received/other) → log with status open→in-review→resolved/written-off; open count + value-in-dispute KPIs. _Persist: KV `invoice-disputes`._ _Class: KV._
- **Payment Terms** (`terms`, `PaymentTermsLibrary`) — seeded Net 15/30, 2/10 Net 30, Due-on-receipt; add name, net days, early-pay days/disc%, late rate% → "Due if today" = today+netDays; set default (pre-fills new-invoice due date). _Persist: KV `invoice-payment-terms` (seeded)._ _Class: KV._
- **Milestone Billing** (`milestone`, `MilestoneBilling`) — customer, contract value, GST, milestone stages (name + %, must total 100) → per-stage base = contract×% and +GST; "Bill now" flags stage billed (manual tax invoice). GST due per stage at earlier of invoice/payment. _Persist: KV `invoice-milestones`._ _Class: KV._
- **Advance/Retainer** (`advance`, `AdvanceAdjustment`) — customer (datalist from live invoices), advance received → Receipt Voucher (GST on advance for services); adjust against invoice with remaining-balance guard; received/adjusted/unadjusted-liability KPIs. _Persist: KV `invoice-advances`._ _Class: KV._
- **e-Invoice JSON** (`einvoicejson`, `EInvoiceJsonGenerator`) — your GSTIN + state code, pick live invoice → builds NIC IRP schema 1.1 payload (TranDtls/DocDtls/SellerDtls/BuyerDtls/ItemList/ValDtls); intra-state (buyer state==seller) → CGST+SGST split, inter-state → IGST; PIN/place are placeholders; copy/download .json. e-invoicing mandatory AATO > ₹5 crore. _Persist: KV `invoice-irp-supplier-gstin`, `-state`._ _Class: Backend (data) + Preview (offline upload payload)._
- **Statement of A/c** (`statement`, `StatementOfAccount`) — select customer → running ledger (debit=invoice, credit=paid, balance) from live invoices; billed/received/outstanding KPIs + closing balance. _Persist: none (read-only from backend)._ _Class: Backend (derived)._
- **Partial Payments** (`partial`, `PartialPaymentTracker`) — pick live open invoice, amount (≤ outstanding+0.5 guard), mode (UPI/NEFT-RTGS/Cheque/Cash/Card) → records installment vs GST-inclusive total; per-invoice paid/outstanding/%-paid bars + payment log. _Persist: KV `invoice-partial-payments` (against live invoices)._ _Class: KV._
- **Invoice Margin** (`profit`, `InvoiceMarginAnalyzer`) — enter cost per live invoice → margin = taxable value (subtotal, GST-excluded pass-through) − cost; per-invoice margin% + blended margin% across costed invoices. _Persist: KV `invoice-line-costs`._ _Class: KV._
- **TCS u/s 206C** (`tcs`, `TcsCalculator`) — section (206C(1H) goods >₹50L cumulative, scrap, timber, 206C(1F) motor vehicle >₹10L), sale/receipt, prior receipts (1H only), has-PAN toggle → 1H taxes only receipts beyond ₹50L cumulative, 1F only if sale>₹10L; rate = PAN rate or higher 206CC no-PAN rate; TCS = round(taxable×rate%) collected over and above sale, total to invoice = sale+TCS. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (calculator)._
- **GSTR-1 Summary** (`gstr1`, `Gstr1Summary`) — live invoices auto-classified: B2B (buyer has GSTIN), B2CL (no GSTIN & total >₹2.5L), B2CS (≤₹2.5L) → table-wise count/taxable/tax (GSTR-1 tables 4/5/7) + rate-wise breakup; Export CSV. Place-of-supply/HSN need manual review. _Persist: none (read-only) + CSV export._ _Class: Backend (derived)._
- **Smart Due-Date** (`duedate`, `DueDateSuggester`) — select customer, default days (default 30) → avg days-to-pay from settled invoices (paid_at − created_at); suggested due date = today + (avgDays or fallback). _Persist: KV `inv-duedate-default` (history from backend)._ _Class: Backend (derived)._
- **Duplicate Check** (`duplicate`, `DuplicateDetector`) — match window days (default 14) → groups live invoices by customer (lowercased) + identical rounded GST-inclusive total where consecutive gap ≤ window → flags suspected double-billing. Heuristic; verify before cancelling within IRN window. _Persist: KV `inv-duplicate-window` (data from backend)._ _Class: Backend (derived)._
- **Discount + GST** (`discount`, `DiscountTaxCalculator`) — line items, discount type (% or flat ₹, capped at gross) → discount spread proportionally per line (factor = (gross−disc)/gross), GST charged per-line on post-discount value (Sec 15 CGST Act), total rounded to nearest rupee with round-off ledger entry. Pre-supply discounts only; post-supply needs credit note. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (calculator)._

_Note: tab tokens `all` / `pending` / `paid` are pure UI-state filters over the same backend list and are excluded from the tool count._
## Collections (`/collections`) — 28 tools

Active accounts-receivable command centre: chase overdue invoices, score payers, model recoveries and post-bad-debt provisions — all derived from the live invoice book. Stakeholders: finance, sales. Backend: `/api/collections` + KV (`store.invoices` from the synced app store; durable tool records via `useFeatureState` → `store.featureData` KV namespace, debounced to backend and live-synced across devices).

- **Collections (active AR chase)** — open invoices (`status !== "paid"`) → per-invoice aging via `getAging` (current / 1–30 / 31–60 / 61–90 / 90+ from `differenceInDays(today, dueDate)`), KPI row (total overdue, accounts overdue, avg days overdue, critical 60d+), clickable aging-bucket summary (count/amount/bar), sortable account table, 60d+ critical tip → chase list. Sub-tools: **Reminder modal** (pick template soft/firm/final × channel WhatsApp/email/SMS → opens prefilled `wa.me`/`mailto:`/`sms:` deep link, marks contacted) and **Mark contacted** (in-session `Set`). _Persist: none (contacted set is component state; reminders open the user's own app, server does not deliver)._ _Class: Backend (reads live invoices) + Indicative._
- **Profitability** — all invoices grouped by customer → totalInvoiced, totalPaid, collectionRate (paidCount/invoiceCount), avgDaysToCollect, overdueAmount, blended score → A/B/C tiered table + 4 KPI cards. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **CLV** — invoices per customer + margin% & lifespan-years sliders → AOV, order frequency from invoice-date gaps, ordersPerYear=365/avgFreqDays, CLV = AOV × orders/yr × lifespan × margin → ranked table with Platinum/Gold/Standard tiers (top 20% / top 50%) + top-20% CLV share. _Persist: live store (slider inputs are component state)._ _Class: Indicative (projection)._
- **Risk Score** — invoices per customer → overdueRate, avgDaysLate, recency penalty → riskScore = 40%×overdueRate + 35%×min(avgLate/90,1) + 25%×recency → High ≥65 / Medium ≥35 / Low → table + bucket counts. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Statement** — pick a customer → builds a running-balance ledger (invoice = debit, payment = credit), totals invoiced/received/outstanding/overdue, firm header → copy-to-clipboard plaintext statement of account. _Persist: live store; output via `navigator.clipboard`._ _Class: Backend + Indicative (simplified, no TDS/credit-note/actual payment dates)._
- **Dunning** — open invoices → days overdue mapped to a ladder (D+1 gentle / D+7 / D+15 / D+30 final), shows highest step crossed per invoice, per-step counts, channel toggle → Send opens prefilled WhatsApp/email/SMS deep link with tone-matched dunning message. _Persist: live store; messages open user's own app._ _Class: Backend + Indicative._
- **DSO Trend** — invoices bucketed into last-6-month buckets → monthly DSO ≈ (open AR for month ÷ month sales) × 30, MoM delta, bar chart + worst-payers table (avg days late on open invoices, top 8). _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (DSO approximation)._
- **Promise-to-Pay** — log customer commitment (customer/amount/promised-by date/note) → auto-flags breach when promise date passes while open; mark kept/broken, Chase via WhatsApp; KPIs open/broken/kept/promised-value. _Persist: KV key `collections-promise-to-pay`._ _Class: KV._
- **Agents** — add reps with collection target; route each overdue account to a rep via dropdown → per-rep progress = assigned overdue value ÷ target; unassigned count. _Persist: KV keys `collections-agents` + `collections-agent-assignments`._ _Class: KV._
- **Settlement** — pick open account, choose settle (discount% slider) or write-off (=100%), add reason → computes recoverable/loss, submit for approval; approve/reject lifecycle; summary of pending / approved recovery / bad-debt-or-discount loss. _Persist: KV key `collections-settlements`._ _Class: KV + Indicative (write-off journal = Dr Bad-debt / Cr Debtors described, not posted)._
- **CEI (Collection Effectiveness Index)** — invoices bucketed by due-month over 6 months → CEI = collected ÷ collectible (invoices that fell due that month) × 100, overall + latest, Excellent ≥80 / Healthy ≥60 grade + bar chart. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Bad-Debt (Provision)** — open invoices grouped by aging bucket + editable %-doubtful-by-age policy (default 0/0/10/25/50) → provision = outstanding × rate per bucket, totals + net realisable AR. _Persist: live store (policy % is component state)._ _Class: Indicative (Ind-AS 109 / provision-matrix style; Dr Bad-debt expense / Cr Provision described, not posted)._
- **Payment Plan** — pick open account, set installment count / interval days / first-due date → splits balance into equal installments (rounding remainder on final one so it sums exactly), schedule table, copy-to-clipboard plan. _Persist: live store; output via clipboard._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Late Interest** — open overdue invoices + annual-rate slider (default 18%) → simple interest = principal × rate × days÷365 per invoice, totals principal/interest/accounts. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (MSME delayed-payment interest; statutory is 3× RBI rate compound — rate is user-set)._
- **Forecast** — open invoices weighted by aging-keyed recovery probability (current 0.95 → 90d+ 0.25), bucketed into next-6-week buckets by due week (overdue → this week) → expected = Σ(amount × prob), face value, risk-adjusted shortfall + bar chart. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (projection)._
- **Worklist** — open invoices → priority = 40%×value + 35%×age(cap 90d) + 25%×recovery-risk, each scaled 0–100 → ranked chase list, top-5 highlighted, focus value. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Early-Pay (Discount)** — open AR + discount%/pay-within/net-days inputs → per-invoice discounted amount & customer saving, implied annual cost = (d÷(100−d))×(365÷days saved); cash-if-all-take-it. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Legal Notice** — pick overdue account + grace days → auto-fills a formal demand-notice draft (firm name, customer, invoice ref, amount, due date, days overdue, pay-within) → copy-to-clipboard. _Persist: live store; output via clipboard._ _Class: Indicative (boilerplate, "not legal advice")._
- **KPI Board** — whole invoice book → DSO (open AR ÷ avg daily credit sales over 90d, with invoice-share fallback), CEI (collected ÷ (collected+overdue) single-period proxy), ADD (DSO − Best-Possible DSO), Best-Possible DSO (current AR only) + open/overdue/collected values and explainer. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (proxy formulas)._
- **Disputes** — log a contested/short-paid invoice (pick invoice, disputed amount capped at invoice total, reason code) → quarantines disputed amount, computes still-chaseable clean balance; open/resolved toggle; KPIs open disputes / disputed value / clean value. _Persist: KV key `col-disputes`._ _Class: KV._
- **Concentration** — open invoices grouped by customer + alert-threshold slider → each customer's share of open AR, top-3 share, Herfindahl HHI (Σ share², <1500 diversified / >2500 concentrated), red alert for customers exceeding threshold + share-bar table. _Persist: live store (threshold is component state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Defaulters** — open past-due invoices grouped by customer → overdue value, overdue-invoice count, worst days overdue → leaderboard ranked by overdue value (medals top 3) + share-of-total. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **Behavior** — pick a customer → timeline of every invoice with days-late (unpaid: due→today; paid: invoice/due date proxy), avg days late, on-time rate. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (paid-late is a proxy; store has no explicit payment date)._
- **A/B Templates** — edit two reminder variants (subject+body), log which variant sent to a customer, toggle paid → per-variant sent/paid/rate and current winner. _Persist: KV keys `col-ab-variants` + `col-ab-results`._ _Class: KV._
- **Letter Series** — pick overdue invoice → stage auto-selected by days overdue (reminder D+1 / second D+30 / final D+60), suggested-stage hint, generates escalation letter text → copy or print (opens print window). _Persist: live store; output via clipboard/print._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Interest Invoice** — pick overdue invoice + annual rate (default 18%) → interest = principal × rate × days÷365, generate debit-note record; list of saved notes + total billable. _Persist: KV key `col-interest-invoices`._ _Class: KV + Indicative (MSME interest; confirm contract/statutory rate)._
- **NACH Mandates** — register an e-NACH/auto-debit mandate per customer (UMRN, debit cap, frequency monthly/quarterly/as-presented, next-debit date) → pending→active/rejected/cancelled lifecycle; active count + covered value/cycle. _Persist: KV key `col-nach-mandates`._ _Class: KV (control sheet only — not the NPCI register; no real debit fired)._
- **Ageing by Rep** — assign each customer a sales-rep owner → rolls open AR up by rep across aging buckets (current/1–30/31–60/61–90/90+) with grand total; unassigned roll up together. _Persist: KV key `col-customer-owners` (owner map); live store for invoices._ _Class: Backend + KV._
## Receivables (`/receivables`) — 23 tools

Track outstanding invoices and chase overdue payments while scoring debtors, provisioning losses, and forecasting collections. Stakeholders: finance, sales. Backend: KV-synced (reads live invoices).

- **Overview** — live `store.invoices` (split into pending/paid) → aging buckets via `agingBucket(daysOverdue)` (current ≤0, 30d ≤30, 60d ≤60, 90d >60) with per-bucket count+amount, total outstanding, list/kanban views, per-invoice chase (mailto reminder via `chaseMessage`)/mark-paid/delete, paid list, and a Customer Payment DNA leaderboard (score = pay-rate × 60% + speed component `max(0,40−avgDaysLate×2)`, banded Excellent/Good/Fair/Poor) → outstanding overview + per-customer reliability. _Persist: live store (invoices via addInvoice/updateInvoice/deleteInvoice)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Customer Risk Scoring** (`risk-score`) — per-customer invoice groups → exposure (open sum), payRate (paid/total), avgDaysLate (paid invoices), worstOverdue (open max) → score = payRate×0.45 + lateScore `max(0,30−avgDaysLate×0.6)` + overdueScore `max(0,25−worstOverdue×0.4)`, banded Low/Medium/High/Severe → ranked risk table + total/at-risk exposure. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Factoring / Discounting Estimator** (`factoring`) — selected open invoices (face) + advance rate %, discount % p.a., service fee %, tenor days → advanced = face×adv; discountCost = advanced×rate×(days/365); serviceFee = face×fee; netProceeds = face−totalCost; effectiveCostPct = totalCost/face; annualisedPct = (totalCost/advanced)×(365/days) → proceeds waterfall + effective/annualised cost. _Persist: none (local component state)._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Cash Application** (`cash-app`) — live `store.transactions` (revenue inflows, positive, unapplied) matched against open invoices via normalised name/invoice-number + amount: exact (name/num hit AND |Δ|≤1), likely (name hit OR amount within 2%), none → suggested/unmatched lists; Apply records invoiceId→txnId link. _Persist: KV `receivables-cash-applied` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Concentration Risk** (`concentration`) — open invoices grouped by customer + alert threshold % → per-customer share, top1, top3, HHI = Σ(share%²) (>2500 highly concentrated) → breach alert + AR-by-customer bars. _Persist: none (threshold local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **AR Confirmation Mailer** (`ar-confirm`) — open balances grouped by customer + per-customer contact + as-of date + firm name → builds auditor-style positive-confirmation letter with invoice breakdown → opens mailto/WhatsApp draft, logs send timestamp. _Persist: KV `receivables-ar-contacts` + `receivables-ar-confirm-sent` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **DSO Trend** (`dso-trend`) — invoices over trailing 6 months → per-month: AR open at month-end ÷ that month's billed credit sales × days-in-month = DSO → current/MoM-delta/6-mo-avg + bar chart. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **AR Turnover** (`ar-turnover`) — invoices + window days → netSales (billed in window), avgAR proxy = (openAR + min(openAR,billed))/2, turnover = netSales/avgAR, annualised ×(365/days), implied DSO = 365/annualised, graded Strong/Healthy/Slow/Weak → turnover ratio + DSO. _Persist: none (window local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **ECL Provisioning** (`ecl-matrix`) — open invoices bucketed by aging + editable loss % per bucket → ecl = gross×rate/100 per bucket; net carrying = gross−ecl; blended = eclTotal/grossTotal (simplified Ind-AS 109/IFRS 9 provision matrix) → provision matrix table. _Persist: KV `rec-ecl-rates` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Credit Utilization** (`credit-util`) — open exposure per customer + editable per-customer credit limit → util = exposure/limit×100, headroom = limit−exposure, status ok/near(≥80%)/over(≥100%)/unset → utilization bars + over/near/unset counts. _Persist: KV `rec-credit-limits` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Collection Forecast** (`cash-timeline`) — open invoices + per-customer avg pay-lag (learned from paid history) → expected pay date = due + lag, bucketed into 8 weeks (or overdue), expected8w sum → weekly cash-landing bar chart. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Overdue Heatmap** (`overdue-heatmap`) — open invoices → per-customer × aging-bucket amount matrix (top 12 by total), cell intensity scaled by amount/maxCell, hue by bucket → customer × bucket heatmap. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Dunning Funnel** (`dunning-funnel`) — open invoices placed by days-past-due into stages not-due/reminder(1–15)/follow-up(16–30)/escalation(31–60)/final-demand(60d+) → per-stage count+amount, actionable sum, per-stage "Chase" toast (queue prompt) → staged funnel bars. _Persist: live store (read-only); chase is a toast prompt only._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Promise-to-Pay** (`promise-to-pay`) — select open invoice + promised date/amount/note → logs promise; row state derived: kept (invoice paid), broken (date passed unpaid), upcoming (dueIn days); promisedAmt = sum of open promised → tracked-promises list + kept/broken/promised totals. _Persist: KV `rec-promise-to-pay` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Dispute Tracker** (`disputes`) — select open invoice + disputed amount + reason → disputed = min(amount, invoice), chaseable = invoice−disputed; toggle open/resolved → quarantined vs still-chaseable totals + dispute list. _Persist: KV `rec-disputes` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Early-Pay Discount** (`early-discount`) — open invoices + discount %, window days, net days → implied APR = (disc/(1−disc))×(365/(net−window))×100; per-invoice dso (since invoice date), eligible if dso ≤ window, discountAmt = amount×disc, netIfTaken; offered/cost-if-all-taken sums → APR + per-invoice offers. _Persist: none (params local state)._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Credit-Hold List** (`credit-hold`) — open invoices per customer (exposure, worst overdue) + overdue-days threshold + shared credit limits → flags tooOverdue (worst ≥ threshold) or overLimit (exposure > limit); hold unless cleared; override toggle → stop-shipping candidate list + held exposure. _Persist: KV `rec-credit-hold-cleared` (write) + reads `rec-credit-limits` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Write-Off Policy** (`write-off`) — open invoices + doubtful-after days policy → candidates where daysOverdue ≥ threshold; tick to approve write-off; candidate/approved/pending totals → uncollectible candidate list (local intent-to-write-off audit trail). _Persist: KV `rec-writeoff-policy-days` + `rec-writeoff-approved` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Payment Timeline** (`pay-timeline`) — invoices for selected customer → per-invoice lateness = max(0, days past due) (paid settlement approximated at today, no paidDate in model), avgLate (paid), onTime count → days-to-pay history bars + stats. _Persist: live store (read-only); selection local state._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Days Beyond Terms** (`days-beyond-terms`) — open invoices per customer → value-weighted DBT = Σ(dbt×amount)/Σamount, worst, count; portfolio weighted DBT across all → per-customer DBT ranking. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Reliability Ranking** (`reliability-rank`) — all invoices per customer → onTimeRate = onTime/paid, overduePenalty = min(0.4, openOverdue×0.1), score = max(0, (onTimeRate−penalty)×100) → ranked reliability leaderboard. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Overdue Interest** (`interest-accrual`) — open invoices + interest % p.a. + grace days → per overdue invoice: overdue = max(0, daysPastDue−grace), interest = amount×rate×(overdue/365) simple interest; total accrued + overdue principal → claimable-interest list (MSMED-Act framing). _Persist: KV `rec-interest-rate-pa` + `rec-interest-grace` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Collection Target** (`collection-target`) — invoices + monthly target → collected = paid invoices dated this month, dueThisMonth = open invoices due this month, pct = collected/target, gap = max(0, target−collected) → progress bar + gap/due cards + check-progress toast. _Persist: KV `rec-collection-target` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
## Payments & UPI (`/payments`) — 32 tools

Collect over UPI/cards, build links, track autopay & NACH mandates, reconcile settlements/UTRs/reserves, model MDR/gateway cost, and watch success rate — India-first money movement. Stakeholders: owner, finance, sales. Backend: partial (Razorpay webhook/billing); rest KV (per-feature `useFeatureState` keys) or pure compute.

- **Overview** — reads `store.transactions` (this-month inbound), `pay-mandates`, `pay-refunds` → derives collected-this-month, active mandate count + monthly-capped value, pending refunds & value, a hardcoded "~0.9%" blended MDR card; jump buttons to other tabs → 4 KPI cards + quick-action grid. _Persist: reads pay-mandates, pay-refunds (live store); collected proxied from store.transactions._ _Class: Indicative (blended MDR is a literal; collected is a revenue proxy, not real settlements)._

- **UPI QR / Intent** — inputs payee VPA (pa, regex-validated `name@bank`), payee name (pn, defaults firm name), amount (am, blank = open), ref/order id (tr, "Auto" fills `HDM`+timestamp), note (tn) → builds standard `upi://pay?` URI (cu=INR, am to 2dp) → renders scannable QR, copyable link, "Open in UPI app" anchor, share-text. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend (open NPCI UPI spec, no gateway)._ External call: image GET to `api.qrserver.com/v1/create-qr-code`.

- **Payment Links** — inputs title, base amount, GST % (default firm.gstRate/18), include-GST & allow-partial toggles, expiry days (→ expiry date), payee VPA → computes GST (rounded), total, expiry date; builds a `upi://pay` link (am=total) and a multi-line share-request text → preview card + copy + WhatsApp share. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend (UPI intent) + Preview._ External call: `wa.me/?text=` share.

- **AutoPay Mandates** — inputs customer, cap ₹, frequency (monthly/quarterly/yearly/as-presented), rail (upi-autopay/enach/card-si), next debit date → adds active mandate row; cycle button toggles active→paused→revoked; computes active count, monthly-equivalent value (freq factor), days-to-debit with overdue/≤3d styling → KPI cards + sortable table. _Persist: `pay-mandates`._ _Class: KV._

- **MDR / Surcharge** — inputs amount, method (upi/rupay-debit/debit/credit/amex/netbanking/wallet), pass-on toggle → applies indicative rate table (UPI 0%, RuPay 0%, debit 0.9%, credit 2%, amex 3%, netbanking 1%, wallet 1.5%) + 18% GST on fee → MDR fee, GST, total cost, you-net or customer-pays, plus a "steer to UPI saves X" banner. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (rates are market estimates, not contracted)._

- **Settlement Recon** — inputs settlement date, gross sales, MDR %, payout received → expected payout = gross − fee − 18% GST on fee; variance = received − expected, matched if |var|<₹1 → KPI cards (batches, unmatched, net variance) + table with red-highlighted mismatches. _Persist: `pay-settlements`._ _Class: KV._

- **Refund Tracker** — inputs customer, order ref, amount, reason → logs pending refund (dated); mark refunded/reject/remove; computes pending vs processed sums, ageing (>5d pending highlighted) → KPI cards + table. _Persist: `pay-refunds`._ _Class: KV._

- **Split Payment** — inputs total captured + N parts (name, percent|fixed, value) defaulting to 10% commission / 90% seller; add/remove parts → per-party share (percent of total or min(fixed,total)), allocated sum, unallocated remainder (green if ~0) → breakdown card. _Persist: none (component useState)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Success Rate** — inputs method, outcome (success/failed), amount, decline reason (from fixed list, only when failed) → records attempt (timestamped); computes overall success rate, attempts, revenue captured vs at-risk, per-method success bars, top-decline ranking → KPI cards + bar charts. _Persist: `pay-attempts`._ _Class: KV._

- **Collect Request** — inputs customer name, 10-digit mobile (normalized to 91-prefixed, validated), amount, due-in days (→ due date), reference, your VPA → composes a WhatsApp message with embedded `upi://pay` link → preview + "Send on WhatsApp" + copy. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend (UPI intent) + Preview._ External call: `wa.me/<phone>?text=`.

- **Method Mix** — reads `pay-attempts` (successful) else falls back to inferring a mix from `store.transactions` bucketed by amount band (≤₹2k UPI / ≤₹50k Cards-UPI / NEFT) → per-method count, value, % share → stacked bar + legend + total. _Persist: reads pay-attempts (live store); else transaction-band heuristic._ _Class: Indicative (inference path) / KV (attempts path)._

- **AutoPay Calc** — inputs charge/cycle, frequency (weekly/monthly/quarterly/yearly), cap headroom % → recommended cap = ceil(charge×(1+buffer)/10)×10, annual value, debits/year, PIN-less (≤₹15k) vs step-up flag, next 4 debit dates with 24h pre-debit notice dates. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (NPCI ₹15k rule applied)._

- **Bulk Payout** — inputs beneficiary, account no, IFSC (regex-validated), amount → adds payee row; detects duplicate account numbers; computes total + dup count → KPI cards + table + CSV export (`beneficiary_name,account_number,ifsc,amount,narration`) via Blob download. _Persist: `pay-bulk-payees`._ _Class: KV (+ client CSV export, no transfer executed)._

- **Reminder Plan** — inputs customer, amount, due date, channel (whatsapp/sms/email), cadence (once/3-day/weekly) → schedules reminder; mark paid/reopen; computes open count, overdue (due<today), outstanding sum, days-to-due styling → KPI cards + table. _Persist: `pay-reminders`._ _Class: KV (planning only; sending is manual via Collect Request)._

- **QR Batch** — inputs payee VPA, label prefix (default "Table"), count (1–50 clamped), optional fixed amount → generates per-label `upi://pay` URIs with label as tr reference → grid of scannable QRs + per-link copy + copy-all. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend (UPI intent)._ External call: per-QR GET to `api.qrserver.com`.

- **Dispute Tracker** — inputs customer, order ref, disputed amount, evidence window days (→ deadline) → logs chargeback at "received"; stage transitions received→evidence-sent→won/lost; computes open count, amount-at-risk, win rate, days-left-to-deadline (lapsed if <0) → KPI cards + table. _Persist: `pay-disputes`._ _Class: KV._

- **EMI on Invoice** — inputs principal, tenure months, annual interest % (0 = no-cost), first-instalment date → reducing-balance EMI (or equal split at 0%), total payable/interest, dated amortization schedule (principal/interest/balance) + shareable plan text. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (standard EMI math)._

- **Convenience Fee** — inputs target net ₹, fee %, flat fee, add-18%-GST toggle → gross-up solve `gross = (net + flat×gstMult)/(1 − pct×gstMult)` so net is preserved after fee+GST; outputs customer-pays, fee base, fee incl. GST, effective add-on %; warns if fee % too high (denom≤0). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Settle Forecast** — inputs capture date, gross, instrument (upi T+1/0%, card T+2/1.8%, netbanking T+1/1%) → projects settlement date = capture + profile days, payout = gross − MDR − 18% GST; groups by settle date, sums upcoming payouts → KPI cards + settlement-calendar table. _Persist: `pay-forecast-sales`._ _Class: KV + Indicative (T+1/T+2 cycles & rates are estimates)._

- **Tip & Rounding** — inputs bill, tip mode (percent/fixed/none) + value (with 5/10/15/18% presets), round-up step (none/₹1/₹5/₹10) → tip, subtotal, round-up delta (ceil to step), customer-pays → itemized breakdown card. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **UTR Recon** — inputs UTR/RRN (regex 12–22 alnum), amount, side (expected-book / received-bank), note → matches expected vs received entries sharing a UTR into matched/missing/unexpected/mismatch (|diff|<₹1) → KPI cards + status-coded table. _Persist: `pay-utr`._ _Class: KV._

- **NACH Register** — inputs UMRN, customer, max debit ₹, frequency (monthly/quarterly/half-yearly/yearly/adhoc), mode (e-NACH/physical), sponsor & customer bank, valid-from/until → registers mandate as "pending"; approve/reject/cancel; computes active count, monthly-equiv value, expiring ≤30d → KPI cards + table. _Persist: `pay-nach`._ _Class: KV (register of record; distinct from live AutoPay tracker)._

- **Gateway Compare** — inputs monthly volume, txns/mo + editable gateway rows (name, MDR %, flat ₹, success %, settle days; seeded Razorpay/Cashfree/PhonePe) → per-gateway total cost (MDR + flat×n + 18% GST), realised GMV (×success%), effective cost % of realised GMV; tags cheapest & best-value → sortable table. _Persist: none (seeded useState)._ _Class: Indicative (seed rates illustrative)._

- **Dunning Ladder** — inputs ARPU/MRR, active subs, monthly fail % + editable retry steps (T+day, channel upi-autopay/whatsapp/sms/email/call, action; seeded 5-step ladder) → at-risk MRR = subs×fail%×MRR; waterfall applies per-channel recovery heuristics (autopay .35, call .4, wa .25, sms .15, email .1) sequentially → KPI cards + ladder waterfall + churn-risk remainder. _Persist: `pay-dunning`._ _Class: Simulated (recovery % are planning heuristics)._

- **Virtual Accounts** — input customer/cost-centre → deterministically derives a virtual account no (`firmSlug+custSlug+seq`), a collect VPA (`firm.cust@yesbank`), fixed IFSC `YESB0CMSNOC`; collision-checks; copy details/remove → table. _Persist: `pay-vaccounts`._ _Class: Simulated (synthetic credentials; "in production map to real CMS VA range")._

- **Payee Verify** — inputs expected payee, type (vpa name-check / bank penny-drop), identifier (VPA or account no), IFSC (bank only), name-at-bank → normalizes names (strips Pvt/Ltd/LLP/punctuation) and fuzzy-matches (equal or substring) → result verified / name-mismatch; logs check → KPI cards + table. _Persist: `pay-verify`._ _Class: Simulated (no real penny-drop/VPA API; name-at-bank is user-entered, match is local fuzzy)._

- **Instant Settle** — inputs settlement amount, instant-settle fee %, days brought forward, borrowing APR % → fee+18% GST, carry value = amt×APR×days/365, net benefit, breakeven APR (fee ÷ (amt×days/365)); worth-it verdict banner. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (decision model)._

- **Duplicate Guard** — inputs customer, amount, order ref, date, tolerance window days → flags likely double-charges (identical non-empty ref, OR same customer + same amount within window); groups them, computes suspected-dupe count, duplicate exposure (extras beyond first per group), flagged value → KPI cards + per-group "refund" callouts + table. _Persist: `pay-dupe-entries`._ _Class: KV (heuristic match)._

- **Fee Tier Model** — inputs tier rows (volume ceiling ₹, rate % 0–5, dedup ceilings) + expected monthly volume → marginal slab pricing (each band charged only on volume inside it; overflow at top-band rate), per-band fee breakdown, total fee, blended MDR % → tier chips + breakdown. _Persist: `pay-feetiers`._ _Class: Indicative (slab math)._

- **Payment Allocation** — inputs open invoices (number, outstanding; dedup) + amount received + rule (oldest-first / pro-rata) → allocates pool oldest-first (min(pool,due) sequential) or pro-rata (min(due, recv×due/total)), computes applied + balance per invoice and any unapplied advance → invoice list + advance line. _Persist: `pay-alloc-invoices`._ _Class: Indicative (allocation math)._

- **Rolling Reserve** — inputs month, gross processed ₹, reserve % (0–100), hold months → held = gross×pct%, release month = month + hold; flags released when releaseMonth ≤ current month; computes currently-locked-up, releasing-this-month, months-tracked → KPI cards + held/released table. _Persist: `pay-reserves`._ _Class: KV + Indicative._

- **Method Downtime** — inputs method (UPI/Cards/Netbanking/Wallet/EMI), start datetime, minutes, failed txns, value lost → logs outage; computes total downtime minutes, total value lost, flakiest method (most cumulative minutes) → KPI cards + outage table. _Persist: `pay-downtime`._ _Class: KV._
## Sales & CRM (`/sales`) — 32 tools

A WhatsApp-enquiry-to-collected-cash CRM for India's SMB sales teams: lead capture, Kanban pipeline, GST-correct quoting, commissions, targets and customer analytics. Stakeholders: sales, owner. Backend: KV-synced (many tools read live invoices/transactions from the shared store).

- **Overview** — reads deals (KV) + leads (KV) + live `store.invoices` → open pipeline, weighted forecast (Σ value × `STAGE_PROB`), closed-won, overdue follow-up count, outstanding AR; quick-jump buttons + AR nudge to Customer 360 → KPI dashboard. _Persist: reads `sales-deals` + `sales-leads` + live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Pipeline Board (Kanban)** — title, customer, rep, value, source, expected-close inputs → adds Deal at `enquiry`, ‹›-moves through `enquiry→quoted→negotiation→won→lost`, columns total per stage, open pipeline + weighted forecast (value × stage probability) → 5-column board. _Persist: `sales-deals`._ _Class: KV._
- **Deal Tracker** — reads deals → flat list sorted by value desc, inline stage `<select>`, expected-close aging (days overdue if past date and not won/lost) → sortable table. _Persist: `sales-deals`._ _Class: KV._
- **Quote → Order** — buyer, GSTIN (15-char regex validated), order discount %, line items (name/qty/rate/GST slab 0/5/12/18/28) → per-line gross→after-discount→GST→total, subtotal/taxable/GST/grand totals, generates `upi://pay` collect link on accept (firm name from store) → quotation + sales-order confirmation. _Persist: none (local component state)._ _Class: Simulated (order/UPI link is in-memory, not stored or charged)._
- **Commissions** — flat-rate %, rep, deal value, margin %, scheme (flat vs tiered) → commission = flat% or tiered (4% <₹2.5L · 6% ₹2.5L–₹10L · 8% >₹10L); totals revenue closed, total commission, effective payout % → table + KPIs. _Persist: `sales-commissions`._ _Class: KV._
- **Customer 360** — select customer (derived from live invoices + incoming transactions) → lifetime value (collected + incoming txns), total billed, outstanding, overdue, per-customer invoice list with status → profile dashboard. _Persist: none (reads live store)._ _Class: Backend (live invoices + transactions)._
- **Sales Forecast** — reads deals + editable per-stage probability overrides (default 10/35/60/100/0%) → weighted = Σ value × prob, closed-won, open pipeline, per-stage bars → forecast view. _Persist: reads `sales-deals` (overrides are local state)._ _Class: KV._
- **Leads & Follow-ups** — name, phone, source, next-follow-up date, note → captures lead at `new` status, inline status select (new/contacted/qualified/dropped), overdue follow-up count, tel:/wa.me action links → lead table. _Persist: `sales-leads`._ _Class: KV._
- **Win / Loss** — deal, value, outcome (won/lost), reason (preset win/loss reason lists) → win rate = won ÷ total, won/lost counts, value won, top loss-reason tally → KPIs + table. _Persist: `sales-winloss`._ _Class: KV._
- **Target vs Actual** — rep, monthly target ₹, achieved ₹ → per-rep attainment % with colour bands (≥100 green / ≥70 yellow / else red), team target/achieved/attainment → progress bars. _Persist: `sales-targets`._ _Class: KV._
- **Rep Leaderboard** — reads deals → groups by rep, sums won value/count and open value/count (excludes lost), sorts by won value desc, medals top 3 → ranked table. _Persist: reads `sales-deals`._ _Class: KV._
- **Discount Approval** — list price, cost, discount asked %, commission %, floor margin %, approval threshold % → net price, commission, net margin ₹/%, flags below-floor (block) and needs-approval (route to owner) → margin calculator + verdicts. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Territory Planner** — territory, rep, pincodes, accounts, potential ₹ → totals territories/accounts/potential, avg accounts per rep → table + KPIs. _Persist: `sales-territories`._ _Class: KV._
- **Activity Log** — contact, type (call/whatsapp/visit/email/meeting), outcome (connected/no-answer/follow-up/closed), duration, note → prepends timestamped entry; totals activities, calls, minutes, connect rate (connected+closed ÷ total) → log table. _Persist: `sales-activities`._ _Class: KV._
- **Quote Expiry** — quote ref, customer, value, valid-until (default +15d) → sorts by validity, classifies live/expiring(≤3d)/expired, live quote value, expiring-soon + expired counts → tracker with status badges. _Persist: `sales-quote-expiry`._ _Class: KV._
- **Cross-Sell** — select a product (mined from live invoice descriptions) → co-occurrence: products appearing in the same customer's basket as the selected product, ranked by count (top 8) → "also bought" suggestions. _Persist: none (reads live invoices)._ _Class: Backend (live invoices)._
- **Churn Risk** — reads live invoices per customer → risk score: inactivity (days since last due date: >90→50, >60→30, >30→15) + overdue dues (+30) + one-time-buyer (+20), capped 100, level high≥60/med≥30/low; revenue at risk = billed of med+high → ranked list. _Persist: none (reads live invoices)._ _Class: Backend (live invoices)._
- **NPS & Feedback** — customer, score 0–10, comment → NPS = %promoters(9–10) − %detractors(0–6); promoter/passive/detractor counts and per-entry bucket → KPIs + feedback table. _Persist: `sales-nps`._ _Class: KV._
- **Sales Playbook** — seeded 10-step deal checklist + custom steps → toggle done, progress %, reset-for-new-deal → onboarding/deal checklist. _Persist: `sales-playbook`._ _Class: KV._
- **Renewals** — customer, product/contract, value, renewal date (default +30d), status (active/renewed/lapsed) → sorts by date, due-≤14-days alert, active value, due-soon count, renewed value → renewal tracker. _Persist: `sales-renewals`._ _Class: KV._
- **Referrals** — referrer, new customer, deal value, reward %, status (pending/closed/paid) → reward = value × reward%, closed value, rewards due (closed) vs paid → referral table. _Persist: `sales-referrals`._ _Class: KV._
- **Source ROI** — reads deals + editable per-source monthly spend → per-source leads, won count, conversion %, won value, cost/lead, ROI (won value ÷ spend); blended ROI/spend/won totals → channel ROI table. _Persist: `sales-source-spend` (reads `sales-deals`)._ _Class: KV._
- **Rep Scorecard** — reads deals → per-rep totals, won/lost, win rate (won ÷ closed), won value, open value, avg won deal, sorted by won value → scorecard table. _Persist: reads `sales-deals`._ _Class: KV._
- **RFM Segments** — reads live invoices per customer → R (recency: ≤30d=3/≤90d=2/else 1), F (frequency: ≥5=3/≥2=2/else 1), M (monetary vs max total: ≥50%=3/≥20%=2/else 1); score→segment Champion≥8/Loyal≥6/At risk≥4/Hibernating → segmented table + tally. _Persist: none (reads live invoices)._ _Class: Backend (live invoices)._
- **Incentive Sim** — expected revenue, quota, flat rate %, base rate % (to quota), accelerator % (above) → flat cost = rev×flat; accelerator cost = belowQuota×base + aboveQuota×accel; attainment %, picks cheaper plan, cost as % of revenue → scheme comparison. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Account Plan** — account, annual goal ₹, owner, objective, next step, status (active/won/stalled) → total goal value, won goal value → key-account plan cards. _Persist: `sales-account-plans`._ _Class: KV._
- **Rate Card** — SKU, list price, GST slab; editable per-row Tier-1/Tier-2 discount % (default 5/10) → Tier net = list×(1−tier%)×(1+GST%) → GST-aware price-list table. _Persist: `sales-rate-card`._ _Class: KV._
- **Pipeline Velocity** — reads deals + editable avg cycle days → velocity/day = (open deals × win rate × avg deal size) ÷ cycle days; win rate, avg deal, monthly projection (×30) → velocity dashboard. _Persist: reads `sales-deals`._ _Class: KV._
- **Conversion Funnel** — reads deals → counts deals that "reached or passed" each flow stage (enquiry→quoted→negotiation→won), % of enquiries (of top), step drop-off % per stage, lost count → funnel bars. _Persist: reads `sales-deals`._ _Class: KV._
- **Revenue / Customer** — reads live invoices per customer → invoices count, avg invoice, collected (paid), total billed, % of total revenue, sorted by billed desc → concentration-risk table. _Persist: none (reads live invoices)._ _Class: Backend (live invoices)._
- **Quote Acceptance** — reads deals → "quoted" = reached ≥quoted stage (incl. lost); acceptance = won ÷ decided (won+lost); count + value-weighted acceptance (won value ÷ quoted value), still-open count → acceptance dashboard. _Persist: reads `sales-deals`._ _Class: KV._
- **Seasonal Pattern** — reads live invoices → buckets amount + count by calendar month, peak month, average of active months, above-average highlighting → revenue-by-month bars. _Persist: none (reads live invoices)._ _Class: Backend (live invoices)._
## E-commerce & ONDC (`/marketplace`) — 32 tools

Reconcile marketplace payouts (Amazon, Flipkart, Meesho, ONDC, D2C/Shopify), surface true per-SKU margin, and stay GST-compliant in one ledger. Stakeholders: sales, ops, owner. Backend: KV-synced (all persistence via `useFeatureState` localStorage-backed keys; no server API).

- **Overview** — reads KV state from settlement, SKU & RTO tabs → recomputes net payout (`Σ orderValue−fees−refunds−tcs`), total RTO loss, count of SKUs below break-even (`skuNet<0`), channel count → 4 KPI cards + 10 jump-to-tool cards + TCS/Sec-52 advisory. _Persist: reads `mkt-settlement-lines`, `mkt-sku-rows`, `mkt-rto-rows`._ _Class: KV._
- **Settlement Recon** — paste Amazon/Flipkart CSV (`orderId,orderValue,fees,refunds,tcs`) + optional expected bank credit → header auto-detect & parse, per-line & total net payout (`gross−fees−refunds−tcs`), variance vs expected (matched if `|Δ|<1`, else shortfall/excess) → KPI cards + reconciled/mismatch banner + line table. _Persist: `mkt-settlement-lines`._ _Class: KV._
- **Fee Calculator (Commission)** — channel + selling price, referral %, closing fee, shipping fee, ad %, GST-on-fees % → `referral=price×ref%`, `feeBase=referral+closing+shipping+ad`, `feeGst=feeBase×gst%` (ITC-claimable), `totalFee`, `netRealisation=price−totalFee`, effective fee % (red if >30%) → per-order economics breakdown. _Persist: none (local useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Return / RTO Loss** — SKU, channel, type (rto/return), order value, COGS, forward/reverse freight, event count → `rtoLossOf = (fwdFreight+revFreight)×count` (RTO & return both modeled as freight bleed; goods recovered) → total loss, units lost, avg loss/unit, table. _Persist: `mkt-rto-rows`._ _Class: KV._
- **Sales Consolidator** — per-channel orders, gross sales, fees, returns (re-add overwrites channel) → `net=gross−fees−returns`, totals, per-channel net mix % → KPI cards + table. _Persist: `mkt-channel-sales`._ _Class: KV._
- **Payout Calendar** — channel, cycle days, last payout date, pending amount (re-add overwrites) → `next=lastPayout+cycleDays`, `daysAway=ceil((next−today)/day)`, sorted soonest-first, total locked → due/overdue/Nd badges + table. _Persist: `mkt-payout-cfg`._ _Class: KV._
- **TCS u/s 52** — operator, net taxable sales, month → `tcsCollected = sales×1%` (0.5% CGST + 0.5% SGST per Sec 52, GSTR-8), per-row Matched/Unmatched toggle; totals + reconciled/claimable sum (only reconciled rows) → cards + table. _Persist: `mkt-tcs52`._ _Class: KV._
- **Listing P&L / SKU** — SKU, channel, price, COGS, fees %, ad/unit, shipping, return rate %, units sold → `skuNet = price−cogs−fees(price×feesPct)−ad−shipping−returnProvision(price×returnPct%×0.5)`, margin %, total profit (`net×units`); negative rows highlighted → table. _Persist: `mkt-sku-rows`._ _Class: KV._
- **Channel Compare** — reads `mkt-channel-sales` (no input) → per-channel `net`, margin % (`net/gross`), fee load (`fees/gross`), AOV (`gross/orders`), sorted by margin desc → best/weakest banners + horizontal margin bars. _Persist: reads `mkt-channel-sales`._ _Class: KV._
- **ONDC Readiness** — toggle 8 checklist steps (GST, PAN/bank, catalog+HSN, FSSAI, seller-app, logistics, returns policy, floor-price) → completion % bar; 100% shows go-live banner. _Persist: `mkt-ondc-checklist`._ _Class: KV._
- **Ad ROAS** — ad spend, sales from ads, gross margin % → `ROAS=sales/spend`, `ACoS=spend/sales%`, `breakEvenACoS=grossMargin%`, `profitAfterAd=sales×gm%−spend`, profitable if `ACoS≤breakEvenACoS` → KPI cards + profitable/loss banner. _Persist: none (local useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Target-Margin Price** — COGS, referral %, fixed fee, shipping, target net margin %, output GST % → back-solve `basePrice=(cogs+ship+fixed)/(1−ref−margin)`, buyer MRP `=basePrice×(1+gst)`, referral amt, profit/unit; infeasible if `1−ref−margin≤0` → result cards. _Persist: none (local useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Inventory Sync** — SKU + warehouse + per-channel listed qty (Amazon/Flipkart/Meesho/ONDC; re-add overwrites by SKU) → `listed=Σchannels`, oversell flag if `listed>warehouse` → total WH, total listed, oversell-risk count + in-sync/oversold table. _Persist: `mkt-inventory-sync`._ _Class: KV._
- **FBA Fee Estimator** — actual weight, L/W/H cm, pick&pack, storage ₹/cu.ft/mo, months stored, units → `volWeight=L×W×H/5000`, `billed=max(actual,vol)`, `slabs=ceil((billed−0.5)/0.5)`, `weightHandling=40+slabs×20`, `storage=(L×W×H/28316.8)×rate×months`, `perUnit`, total `×units` → fee breakdown (explicitly "indicative"). _Persist: none (local useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **PPC Allocator** — total monthly budget + campaign name/weight rows → per-campaign `share=weight/Σweight`, `alloc=budget×share` → weighted bars + % split. _Persist: `mkt-ppc-rows` (budget itself is local useState, not persisted)._ _Class: KV._
- **Repricing Sim** — COGS, fees %, current/new price, min margin %, units@current, elasticity → `netUnit(p)=p−p×fees−cogs`, `floorPrice=cogs/(1−fees−minMargin)`, breach if `new<floor`, demand `newUnits=u0×(1−pricePct×elasticity)`, `profitΔ=newProfit−curProfit` → floor banner + projection cards. _Persist: none (local useState)._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Rating Tracker** — SKU, channel, rating (0–5), # reviews → review-weighted avg `Σ(rating×reviews)/Σreviews`, total reviews, count below 4.0★ (buy-box risk); sub-4.0 rows flagged → cards + healthy/at-risk table. _Persist: `mkt-review-rows`._ _Class: KV._
- **GSTR-8 Recon** — operator, month, TCS per books, TCS in GSTR-8 → `diff=gstr8−books`, matched if `|diff|<1`, else under/over-reported; totals + mismatch count → cards + status table. _Persist: `mkt-gstr8-rows`._ _Class: KV._
- **Refund Cost** — SKU, type (refund/returnless/replacement), order value, refund amt, lost-goods/replacement COGS, count → `refundCostOf`: returnless=`refund+replCogs`, replacement=`replCogs+refund`, refund=`refund`; ×count → total cost, events, returnless subtotal + table. _Persist: `mkt-refund-rows`._ _Class: KV._
- **Listing Quality** — toggle 8 checks (title, 7+ images, 5 bullets, A+ content, video, HSN/GST, backend search terms, buy-box price band) → score % bar; <100% shows "fix before scaling ads" nudge. _Persist: `mkt-listing-quality`._ _Class: KV._
- **COD vs Prepaid** — total orders, COD share %, AOV, gross margin %, COD RTO %, prepaid RTO %, RTO cost/order → split orders, per-segment `rtos=cnt×rtoRate`, `net=delivered×grossMargin−rtos×rtoCost`, net/order each; total net + prepaid-vs-COD gap insight. _Persist: none (local useState)._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Buy-Box Win Rate** — SKU, channel, listing views, buy-box wins (capped at views), your price, lowest price → per-row `winRate=wins/views`, `gap=yourPrice−lowest`; blended rate `Σwins/Σviews` (color-banded 80/50) → cards + win%/gap table. _Persist: `mkt-buybox-rows`._ _Class: KV._
- **Reserve Release** — channel, held amount, held-on date, release-in days → `release=heldOn+releaseDays`, `daysAway`, sorted soonest; total held + releasing-within-7-days sum → released/Nd badges + table. _Persist: `mkt-reserve-rows`._ _Class: KV._
- **Chargeback Ledger** — order ID, channel, reason (preset list), amount, raised-on; per-row status select (open/won/lost) → open exposure `Σopen.amount`, lost written-off sum, `winRate=won/settled%` → cards + ledger table. _Persist: `mkt-chargeback-rows`._ _Class: KV._
- **Bundle COGS** — component rows (name, unit COGS, qty) + bundle price, fees %, shipping → `totalCogs=Σcogs×qty`, `fees=price×feesPct`, `netMargin=price−totalCogs−fees−shipping`, margin % → component list + margin breakdown. _Persist: none (components are local useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Break-even Price** — COGS, fees %, shipping, ad/unit, return %, target margin % → `breakEven=(cogs+ship+ad)/(1−fees−return×0.5)`, `targetPrice=(cogs+ship+ad)/(1−fees−return×0.5−target)`; invalid if `denomTgt≤0` → break-even + target-margin price cards. _Persist: none (local useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Festival Planner** — event name/date, projected units, AOV, discount % → `projGmv=units×AOV×(1−disc)`; 7-task template auto-dated as `eventDate+dueOffset` with days-away/late badges, toggle + reset; readiness % → GMV/readiness cards + dated checklist. _Persist: `mkt-festival-name`, `mkt-festival-date`, `mkt-festival-tasks` (units/AOV/discount are local useState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Cash-Cycle Cost (Holding)** — channel, monthly GMV, payout-lag days, borrowing APR % (re-add overwrites) → `dailyGmv=GMV/30`, `lockedCapital=dailyGmv×payoutDays`, `annualCost=locked×APR%`; totals → capital-locked + annual-cost cards + table. _Persist: `mkt-holding-rows` (APR is local useState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Coupon ROI (Promo)** — promo name, discount spend, baseline units, promo units, margin/unit → `incrUnits=max(0,promo−baseline)`, `incrMargin=incrUnits×margin`, `netGain=incrMargin−spend`, `roi=netGain/spend` → spend/net cards + per-promo table. _Persist: `mkt-promo-roi`._ _Class: KV._
- **Conversion Tracker** — listing/ASIN, sessions, orders → per-row `cvr=orders/sessions`, blended `Σorders/Σsessions`; flag rows `cvr<0.6×blended` → blended card + flagged CVR table. _Persist: `mkt-conversion-rows`._ _Class: KV._
- **Return Rate / SKU** — SKU, delivered, returned → per-row `rate=returned/delivered`, sorted worst-first; portfolio `ΣreturnedΣdelivered` (color-banded 20%/10%) → rate card + table. _Persist: `mkt-return-rate-rows`._ _Class: KV._
- **Fee Reconciliation** — order/settlement ID, expected fee, charged fee, tolerance % → `diff=charged−expected`, `overcharged` if `diff>expected×tol%` (or `diff>0` when expected=0); net variance + disputable-overcharge sum → cards + overcharged/OK table. _Persist: `mkt-fee-recon-rows`._ _Class: KV._
## WhatsApp (`/whatsapp`) — 22 tools

Turn WhatsApp into the SMB's billing/collections/marketing channel: a connect-and-alerts hub plus 21 message-composer tools that build customer-ready text and fire it via `wa.me` deep-links. Stakeholders: owner, sales, finance. Backend: `/api/whatsapp` (send-otp, verify-otp, register DELETE) + a 9 AM morning-brief digest cron driven by KV prefs; webhook-style chat commands (CASH/FORECAST/etc.) are documented in the UI but no inbound webhook handler is exercised from this page. NOTE: connect/OTP/disconnect and alert prefs are the only **real backend / KV-persisted** surface; every "Send" action across the 21 composer tools is a **wa.me deep-link** (opens WhatsApp with text pre-filled — nothing is sent server-side, no delivery, no read receipts); the chat mockup and digest are **preview-only**.

Tabs (23 buttons): Overview + 22 tool tabs. The list below is the 22 tools (Overview = connect hub; 21 composers).

- **Overview / Connect hub** — inputs: 10-digit phone → `POST /api/whatsapp/send-otp` (e164 `+91…`), 6-digit OTP → `POST /api/whatsapp/verify-otp`; Disconnect → `DELETE /api/whatsapp/register`. Alert-preference toggles (low_cash, overdue, gst_due, credit_offer, payroll, weekly) gated by `canEdit()`; chat-command copy buttons; an animated 3-step chat mockup (DigestBubble → FORECAST reply → forecast response, hard-coded). Output: connected state, OTP toasts, prefs that feed the server morning brief. _Persist: live backend (OTP/register) + KV store (`whatsappPreferences`, merged over `DEFAULT_WA_PREFS`)._ _Class: Backend (connect/alerts) + Preview (chat mockup, digest, stats 94%/<30s/80%)._

- **Invoice & Pay** (#177) — inputs: pick an open invoice (from `store.invoices`), optional customer WA number, optional pay-link URL → builds a branded invoice message (customer, #, amount, due, description, pay-link, "reply PAID") → preview + Send. _Persist: reads KV `invoices`; tool keeps no own state._ _Class: Indicative (wa.me deep-link; pay-link is just pasted text, no real payment rail)._

- **Reminder Bot** (#178) — inputs: none (auto-derives overdue invoices: `status==="overdue"` or pending+past-due) → dunning ladder D+1 gentle / D+7 firm / D+15 final-notice by computed days overdue, sorted, with totals → per-row "Remind" send. _Persist: reads KV `invoices`._ _Class: Indicative (wa.me deep-link per row; "auto-staged" is computed, not auto-sent)._

- **Sales Capture** (#179) — inputs: customer (default "Walk-in"), item/note, amount → books a dated sale row; today/all-time totals; per-row delete; "Send recap to my WhatsApp" of latest 10. _Persist: live store via `useFeatureState("wa-sales-capture")`._ _Class: KV (data) + Indicative (recap send is wa.me)._

- **Statement** (#180) — inputs: pick a customer (derived from invoices), optional WA number → builds a statement-of-account ledger (per-invoice lines, outstanding vs settled, "reply PAY/QUERY") → preview + Send. _Persist: reads KV `invoices`._ _Class: Indicative (wa.me deep-link)._

- **Approvals** (#181) — inputs: type (payment/invoice/expense), reference, amount, requested-by → queues an approval item; pending count/value; per-row Send request (wa.me, "reply YES/NO REF") and manual Approve/Reject buttons that set local status. _Persist: live store via `useFeatureState("wa-approvals")`._ _Class: KV (queue + status) + Indicative (send is wa.me; YES/NO is recorded manually, no inbound parsing)._

- **Broadcast** (#182) — inputs: segment (all / outstanding / fully-paid, customers rolled up from invoices), message body with `{name}` merge → per-recipient personalised message; sent/remaining counters; per-row Send marks sent locally. _Persist: reads KV `invoices`; `sent` map is component state only (not persisted)._ _Class: Indicative (one-at-a-time wa.me deep-links, explicitly "no bulk-send backend")._

- **Order Status** (#183) — inputs: customer, order ref, stage (confirmed/packed/shipped/delivered), optional ETA, optional WA number → emoji status-update message → preview + Send. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (wa.me deep-link)._

- **Pay Receipt** (Payment-Confirmation #184) — inputs: optional open invoice (auto-fills amount/customer/#) or manual amount, mode (UPI/Bank/Cash/Cheque/Card), optional ref/UTR, optional WA number → branded "payment received" receipt → preview + Send. _Persist: reads KV `invoices`; does NOT mark the invoice paid._ _Class: Indicative (wa.me deep-link; receipt only, no ledger update despite header comment)._

- **Festive Offer** (#185) — inputs: festival (6 presets w/ greeting), offer text, promo code (upper-cased), optional valid-till → festive greeting + promo message → preview + Send + Copy. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (wa.me deep-link) + Preview/Copy._

- **Price List** (Catalog #186) — inputs: add items (name, price, optional unit) to a saved list; optional WA number → builds a full price-list message (incl. firm GSTIN if set) → table + preview + Share; per-row delete. _Persist: live store via `useFeatureState("wa-price-list")`._ _Class: KV (catalog) + Indicative (share is wa.me)._

- **Review Ask** (#187) — inputs: customer, optional review URL, optional WA number → 5-star feedback-request message (link or "reply 1–5") → preview + Send. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (wa.me deep-link)._

- **Chat Link / QR** (#188) — inputs: your WA number (10+ digits), pre-filled greeting → builds a `wa.me` click-to-chat link + renders a printable QR via external `api.qrserver.com` image endpoint; Copy link / Test open / Open QR full size. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (link generator) + relies on external QR service (live third-party image fetch, not Headroom backend)._

- **GST Invoice** (#189) — inputs: pick an invoice, inter-state checkbox, optional WA number → treats amount as GST-inclusive, backs out taxable value and CGST/SGST (or IGST) split from `firm.gstRate` (default 18) → tax-breakup invoice message + summary cards → preview + Send. _Persist: reads KV `invoices` + `firm`._ _Class: Indicative (wa.me deep-link; client-side tax math, warns if no GSTIN)._

- **COD Confirm** (#190) — inputs: customer, order ref, COD amount, optional address/ETA, optional WA number → "keep exact cash ready" COD-confirmation message ("reply CONFIRM/CHANGE") → preview + Send. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (wa.me deep-link)._

- **Service Reminder** (AMC #191) — inputs: add reminders (customer, service, due date, optional amount) → computes days-to-due, flags due ±30d, sorts; per-row Remind (wa.me, "reply RENEW") + delete. _Persist: live store via `useFeatureState("wa-service-reminders")`._ _Class: KV (tracked dates) + Indicative (remind is wa.me; no scheduled auto-send)._

- **Loyalty Points** (#192) — inputs: customer name, points delta, optional default WA number; Add / Redeem clamps at 0, upserts per-customer balance → totals; per-row Send balance (wa.me) + delete. _Persist: live store via `useFeatureState("wa-loyalty-members")`._ _Class: KV (balances) + Indicative (send is wa.me)._

- **Quick Replies** (#193) — inputs: title + body snippet with `{firm}` merge (seeded with 3 defaults: hours/payment/thanks), optional default WA number → saved snippet library cards; per-snippet Send (wa.me) / Copy / delete. _Persist: live store via `useFeatureState("wa-quick-replies", DEFAULT_SNIPPETS)`._ _Class: KV (library) + Indicative (send is wa.me)._

- **Product Launch** (#194) — inputs: product, optional tagline/price/launch-offer, optional WA number → launch announcement message ("reply INTERESTED") → preview + Send + Copy. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (wa.me deep-link) + Copy._

- **Win-Back** (#195) — inputs: lapsed-after days (default 60), comeback offer → finds customers whose newest invoice is older than cutoff (from invoice history), shows last-seen + sent/remaining; per-row Win back marks sent locally. _Persist: reads KV `invoices`; `sent` map is component state only._ _Class: Indicative (wa.me deep-links; segment is computed)._

- **Appointment** (#196) — inputs: customer, purpose, date, optional time/location/WA number → appointment reminder ("reply CONFIRM/RESCHEDULE") → preview + Send. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (wa.me deep-link)._

- **Referral Ask** (#197) — inputs: customer, referral reward (default "₹100 off for both of you"), optional referral link, optional WA number → referral-request message → preview + Send. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (wa.me deep-link)._

### Persistence & class summary
- **Backend (live API):** connect flow only — send-otp, verify-otp, register DELETE; alert prefs feed the server morning brief.
- **KV (`useFeatureState`/store, synced across devices):** alert prefs (`whatsappPreferences`); Sales Capture, Approvals, Price List, Service Reminders, Loyalty Members, Quick Replies. Tools reading (not writing) KV `invoices`/`firm`: Invoice & Pay, Reminder Bot, Statement, Broadcast, Pay Receipt, GST Invoice, Win-Back.
- **Indicative (wa.me deep-link, no real send/delivery):** all 21 composer "Send/Remind/Share/Announce/Win back" actions — they open WhatsApp with pre-filled text only.
- **Preview-only:** Overview chat mockup, morning-digest bubble, and the 94% / <30s / 80% stat tiles.
- **External dependency:** Chat Link / QR renders via third-party `api.qrserver.com` (not Headroom infra). Pay-links and review/referral links are user-pasted strings — no payment or tracking rail is wired.
- **Note:** `component-state-only` send-tracking (`sent` maps in Broadcast & Win-Back) resets on reload — not persisted.

## ━━━ PILLAR 2 · Tax & Compliance ━━━

## GST & Indirect Tax (`/gst`) — 52 tools

A single GST/indirect-tax workbench for an Indian SMB: compute liability, file-prep returns (GSTR-1/3B/9/9C), reconcile ITC against GSTR-2B, run dozens of section-accurate calculators (RCM, Rule 42/43, Sec 17(5), Rule 89(5), cess, advances, cross-charge, etc.), and track compliance deadlines/exposures. Stakeholders: owner, finance, CA. Backend: `/api/gst` (calendar, returns, liability, verify) + KV-synced via `store.featureData` (the `useFeatureState` hook persists to localStorage, debounce-pushes to backend, and live-syncs across devices) and the live `store` (firm/invoices/transactions).

Source: `/Users/prathmeshwalimbe/Downloads/new/src/features/gst/GstPage.tsx` (4393 lines). Persistence hook: `/Users/prathmeshwalimbe/Downloads/new/src/hooks/useFeatureState.ts`. Reconcile engine: `/Users/prathmeshwalimbe/Downloads/new/src/lib/gstReconcile.ts`. Ledger engine: `gstLedger` in `/Users/prathmeshwalimbe/Downloads/new/src/lib/finance.ts`.

### Tab selector (order as rendered, 52 entries)
`calculator · ledger · gstr1 · returns · match · calendar · eway · rcm · hsn · verify · itc · gstr9 · lut · refund · composition · qrmp · tdsgst · einvoice · notice · gstr3b-prep · itc-recon · liability-forecast · place-supply · multi-gstin · rate-impact · blocked-credit · itc-reversal · vendor-score · drc03 · gst-advances · zero-rated · health-score · interest-fee · threshold · gstr1-3b · rule180 · einv30 · inverted · cdn-register · cess · gstr9c · jobwork · isd · branch-transfer · pmt09 · cross-charge · free-samples · pure-agent · audit-ready`

(Note: the array defines 52 tabs; `returns` shows a live count in its label. All 52 are documented below.)

---

### Tools

- **GSTR-3B Calculator** (`calculator`) — Server-side liability compute: picks month/year → `GET /api/gst/liability?month&year` returns `output_tax`, `input_tax_credit`, `net_liability`, plus a Table 3.1-style breakdown (taxable turnover, output CGST/SGST, ITC CGST/SGST). "Save as GSTR-3B Draft" does `POST /api/gst/returns {return_type:"GSTR-3B"}` (de-dupes by period) and routes to Returns. Implements the GSTR-3B net-liability identity (output − ITC). _Persist: backend `/api/gst/liability` + `/api/gst/returns`._ _Class: Backend._

- **GST Ledger** (`ledger`) — 12-month electronic-credit-ledger simulation via `gstLedger(store, rate, 12)`: per month shows taxable sales, output tax, input credit, net, ITC carry-forward, cash payable; surplus months (input>output) accumulate ITC c/f that offsets later cash — mirrors the portal's credit-ledger carry-forward. Summary strip totals output/input/cash/carry. Rate from `firm.gstRate ?? 18`. _Persist: live store (derived from `store` transactions/invoices)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **GSTR-1 — Outward Supplies** (`gstr1`) — Builds B2C/B2B outward summary from `store.invoices` filtered to selected month: per invoice taxable = amount, CGST = SGST = round(taxable × (rate/2))/100, total = taxable+CGST+SGST. KPIs (invoice count, taxable turnover, output CGST+SGST, gross). CSV export (`GSTR1-<Mon>-<Yr>.csv`). Splits the firm rate into equal CGST/SGST halves; IGST/HSN B2B-B2C splits flagged as needing GSP. _Persist: live store (`store.invoices`)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Returns** (`returns`) — Read-only register of computed returns from `GET /api/gst/returns` (loaded on mount): period, type, output tax, ITC, net liability, status (filed/draft chip), GSTN ARN. Populated by the Calculator's save action. _Persist: backend `/api/gst/returns`._ _Class: Backend._

- **GSTR-2B Reconciliation / 2B Match** (`match`) — Upload GSTR-2B JSON (`parse2BJson`) + purchase register xlsx/csv (`parseRegisterRows`), then `reconcile(reg, 2B)` matches by GSTIN+invoice. Summary: ITC at risk (not in 2B), mismatched tax, available-unclaimed (only in 2B), matched ITC. Line table with status meta (missing_in_2b / mismatch / missing_in_books / matched), capped at 200 rows on screen; full CSV export (`gstr2b-reconciliation.csv`). Implements Rule 36(4) 2B-match gating. _Persist: none (in-memory uploads; recon state held in component)._ _Class: Backend (client-side compute on user-uploaded portal files)._

- **Calendar** (`calendar`) — Due-date list from `GET /api/gst/calendar`: each row label + due date + penalty note; computes days-left, color-codes urgent (≤7d) / soon (≤30d) / overdue ("Nd late"). _Persist: backend `/api/gst/calendar`._ _Class: Backend._

- **E-Way Bill Estimator** (`eway`) — Inputs invoice value, distance, ODC flag, exempt/cancelled flag. Rule: required iff value > ₹50,000 and not exempt; validity = max(1, ceil(distance / (ODC ? 20 : 200) km/day)); expiry = today + validity days (date-fns). Shows required Yes/No, validity, expiry, formula. Implements e-way-bill ₹50,000 threshold + 200 km/day (20 km ODC) validity rule. _Persist: none (transient useState)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **RCM Register** (`rcm`) — Reverse-charge tracker. Quick-fill chips for 10 notified RCM categories (legal/advocate 18%, GTA 5%, sponsorship, security, rent-a-cab/motor-vehicle 5%, OIDAR import 18%, director services, unregistered-dealer, insurance agent, works-contract 12%). Add entry → RCM tax = round(amount × rate/100); ITC claimable = same amount (same-month credit for business use). Register table + CSV (`RCM_Register.csv`), totals for tax and ITC. Implements RCM (Sec 9(3)/9(4)) self-liability + matching ITC. _Persist: none (rcmEntries on useState; not useFeatureState)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **HSN / SAC Code Lookup** (`hsn`) — Static 47-row HSN/SAC table (goods + services) with standard rates (0/3/5/12/18/28); search by code or description; rate chips colour-coded; labels HSN vs SAC. Reference only — flags concessional/exempt/state notifications as needing portal verification. _Persist: none (search term on useState; data is a const)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Verify GSTIN** (`verify`) — Validates a 15-char GSTIN via `GET /api/gst/verify?gstin=`; returns valid flag, state, state code, PAN, source. When `source === "format"` it explicitly discloses it only checked structure + check digit offline (no live trade-name/active status), never fabricating data. Keeps a recent-verifications history (last 10) with re-verify; "why verify" ITC-risk guidance. Implements GSTIN structure/check-digit validation. _Persist: none (result + history on useState)._ _Class: Backend (offline format check; live status deferred)._

- **ITC Optimizer** (`itc`) — From negative (purchase) transactions, groups by month, estimates ITC = round(amount × 0.18/1.18) (assumes 18% inclusive). Ages each month into Current / 1–3 mo / >3 mo / Lapsed(>2yr) buckets; flags "urgent" (>3 mo) and lapsed ITC. Implements Sec 16(4) 2-year ITC-claim time limit. Includes a Sec 17(5) blocked vs eligible quick-reference. _Persist: live store (`store.transactions`)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **GSTR-9 Annual Return Builder** (`gstr9`) — Aggregates current-FY (Apr–Mar) transactions: outward taxable (positive txns), output tax @18%, inward purchase, ITC = round(inward × 0.18/1.18), net payable / excess credit. Part II Table 4 + Part III Table 6/7 cards, net-position banner, monthly breakdown, CSV export (`GSTR9-FY..`). Notes Dec-31 due date and ₹5cr → 9C audit trigger. Implements GSTR-9 table structure (4A/4B/9, 6B/6C/7). _Persist: live store (`store.transactions`)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **LUT Tracker** (`lut`) — Letter of Undertaking (Form GST RFD-11) register: ref no, FY, filed date, export type (goods/services/both); auto-derives active/expired by FY-end (Mar 31).
