Shows active-LUT banner, days-to-expiry, ≤45-day renewal warning, LUT-vs-Bond-vs-IGST guidance. Implements RFD-11 annual-FY LUT validity for zero-rated exports without IGST. _Persist: `useFeatureState("lut-register")`._ _Class: KV._

- **GST Refund Tracker** (`refund`) — Refund-claim register (auto ref `RFD-#####`): type (Export-LUT / Inverted Duty / Excess Cash Ledger / IGST on Export / Deemed Export / Other), period, amount, status workflow (Draft→Filed→Acknowledged→Processing→Approved→Credited / Deficiency / Rejected), filed date, notes. Totals claimed/credited/in-progress. Rule 91/92 timeline reference (RFD-02 ≤15d ack, 7-day 90% provisional for exports, RFD-06 final 60d + interest). _Persist: `useFeatureState("gst-refunds")`._ _Class: KV._

- **Composition Scheme Checker** (`composition`) — Eligibility: annualised revenue (auto from txns or manual) vs ₹1.5cr cap. Three rate cards (Manufacturer/Trader 1%, Restaurant 5%, Service-provider CSCS 6% capped ₹50L). Estimates saving = turnover×18% − turnover×1%. Pros/cons + Sec 10 notes (no ITC, no inter-state, CMP-02 opt-in, excluded goods). Implements Sec 10 composition thresholds/rates. _Persist: none (useState only)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **QRMP Suitability** (`qrmp`) — Checks annualised turnover vs ₹5cr QRMP cap; compares monthly filing (24/yr, GSTR-1 by 11th, 3B by 20th) vs QRMP (~13/yr: IFF 13th M2, PMT-06 25th M1/M2, GSTR-3B 22nd/24th M3). Quarterly calendar cards. Implements QRMP ₹5cr eligibility + IFF/PMT-06 due-date scheme. _Persist: none (useState only)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **TDS under GST (Sec 51)** (`tdsgst`) — Tracks GST-TDS deducted by govt/PSU deductors: contract value × 2% (1% CGST + 1% SGST / 2% IGST); toggle credited-in-2B vs pending; totals deducted/pending. Guidance: deductor files GSTR-7 by 10th, claim in 3B Table 8C, applies > ₹2.5L contracts. Implements Sec 51 GST-TDS @2%. _Persist: `useFeatureState("tds-gst-entries")`._ _Class: KV._

- **e-Invoice Readiness** (`einvoice`) — Mandatory check: annualised turnover vs ₹5cr threshold (notes the dropping-threshold history). 9-item implementation checklist (5 critical: GSTIN/IRP registration, IRN-via-API, QR print, IRN-before-supply, 24h cancellation) with ready/not-ready status. Lists approved IRP portals; notes B2B/export/SEZ scope, 30-day IRN validity. Implements Sec 68 / Rule 48(4) e-invoicing. _Persist: none (checklist on useState `Set`, not useFeatureState)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **GST Notice Reply Templates** (`notice`) — 4 fill-in templates (SCN GSTR-1 vs 3B mismatch — Sec 61/Rule 99; non-filing of 3B — Sec 46/122; Sec 73 short-payment demand; excess-ITC/blocked-credit — Sec 16(2)/Rule 36) that interpolate GSTIN/period/amount; copy-to-clipboard. Implements Sec 61/46/73/16(2) reply drafting. _Persist: none (useState only)._ _Class: Indicative (template generator)._

- **GSTR-3B Auto-Prep** (`gstr3b-prep`) — From selected month's transactions: outward gross (revenue) → taxable = round(gross/(1+rate)), output tax = gross−taxable; inward gross (expense) → ITC similarly; net cash = max(0, output−ITC), ITC carry = max(0, ITC−output). Renders Table 3.1(a) / 4(A)(5) / 5.1 with CGST/SGST halves; CSV export (`GSTR3B-<period>.csv`). Implements GSTR-3B Tables 3.1/4/5.1 from booked GST-inclusive flows. _Persist: live store (`store.transactions`); period on useState._ _Class: Indicative._

- **GSTR-2B vs Books ITC Reconciliation** (`itc-recon`) — Line-level manual entry (supplier GSTIN, invoice, books tax, 2B tax, supplier-filed flag) → per-row verdict: "Chase vendor" (not in 2B / unfiled — Rule 36(4) blocked), "Claim now" (|delta|≤1), "Defer" (2B<books, claim 2B portion), "Claim (book↑)" (2B>books). Aggregates claim-now / defer-gap / chase-at-risk. Implements Rule 36(4) line verdicts. _Persist: `useFeatureState("itc-recon-books")`._ _Class: KV._

- **GST Liability Forecaster** (`liability-forecast`) — Averages last ≤3 months revenue/expense, applies monthly growth %, adds 50% of open invoice pipeline to month 1; per month output = round((rev/(1+rate))×rate), ITC similarly, net = max(0, output−ITC). Projects 1/2/3/6/12 months of net cash GST outgo. _Persist: live store (`store.transactions` + `store.invoices`); inputs on useState._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Place-of-Supply Determiner** (`place-supply`) — Goods/services + supplier/recipient/ship-to state codes (20-state map), SEZ and bill-to≠ship-to toggles. POS = recipient (bill-to per Sec 10(1)(b) for goods); interState = SEZ OR supplier≠POS → IGST @rate, else CGST+SGST halves; SEZ/export = zero-rated. Implements Sec 7–8 + Sec 10–13 IGST Act place-of-supply. _Persist: none (useState only)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Multi-GSTIN Consolidator** (`multi-gstin`) — Register per-state GSTIN (auto-state from first 2 digits), output tax, ITC, cash ledger; net payable per unit = max(0, output−ITC−cash); totals across registrations. Enforces that ITC cannot cross GSTINs (except ISD). _Persist: `useFeatureState("multi-gstin-units")`._ _Class: KV._

- **GST Rate-Change Impact Simulator** (`rate-impact`) — Per item: old→new slab; if GST-inclusive (hold MRP) taxable = base/(1+rate%), margin delta = taxableNew−taxableOld; if exclusive (hold base) gross moves, MRP delta = grossNew−grossOld. Shows gross old/new and margin-or-MRP impact. Models rate-revision repricing. _Persist: `useFeatureState("rate-change-items")`._ _Class: KV._

- **Blocked Credit Checker — Sec 17(5)** (`blocked-credit`) — 12 heads tagged blocked/eligible with exact sub-clauses: motor vehicles 17(5)(a), food/bev 17(5)(b)(i), health/club 17(5)(b)(ii), rent-a-cab/insurance 17(5)(b)(iii), works-contract/construction 17(5)(c)/(d), CSR 17(5)(fa), personal 17(5)(g), lost/gifts/free-samples 17(5)(h); eligible: inputs/capital/services/resale-vehicle exception. Splits entered ITC into blocked vs eligible totals. Implements Sec 17(5). _Persist: `useFeatureState("blocked-credit-entries")`._ _Class: KV._

- **ITC Reversal — Rule 42/43** (`itc-reversal`) — Rule 42: C2 = T − T2 − T3 (common credit), exempt ratio = exempt/(taxable+exempt), D1 = round(C2×ratio), D2 = round(C2×5%) deemed non-business, eligible C3 = C2−D1−D2. Rule 43: capital ITC Tc spread over 60 months, monthly reversal = round((Tc/60)×ratio). Total reversal = D1+D2+capital. Implements Rule 42/43 proportionate reversal (3B Table 4(B)(1)). _Persist: none (useState only)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Vendor GST Compliance Score** (`vendor-score`) — Per vendor: returns-filed-on-time / total-due + last-filed date → score = round(filingRate×80) + recency bonus (≤60d:20, ≤120d:10), capped 100; grade Low/Watch/High risk (80/50 cutoffs); flags total ITC exposure to high-risk (<50) vendors. Supports Rule 36(4) supplier-risk triage. _Persist: `useFeatureState("vendor-gst-score")`._ _Class: KV._

- **DRC-03 Voluntary Payment Helper** (`drc03`) — Interest = round(tax×18%×days/365) for due→pay gap (Sec 50); penalty = 0 if voluntary (Sec 73(5)), else round(tax×15%); total tax+interest+penalty; drafts a copyable Form GST DRC-03 challan note. Implements Sec 50 interest + Sec 73(5)/74(5) penalty. _Persist: none (useState only)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **GST on Advances Tracker** (`gst-advances`) — Records service advances; tax on advance computed tax-inclusive = round(advance×rate/(100+rate)); mark-adjusted against an invoice (prompt for invoice no). Totals advances, GST paid, unadjusted open GST. Notes goods advances exempt (Notfn 66/2017), reporting in GSTR-1 11A/11B, receipt/refund vouchers. Implements Sec 12/13 advance-receipt tax. _Persist: `useFeatureState("gst-advances")`._ _Class: KV._

- **Export / SEZ Zero-Rated Invoice Kit** (`zero-rated`) — Export-invoice register: type (export-goods/services/SEZ), method (LUT no-IGST / with-IGST refund), value, IGST = method-with-igst ? round(value×rate/100) : 0, FIRC/BRC received toggle. Totals zero-rated turnover, IGST refundable, FIRC pending. Implements Sec 16 IGST Act zero-rated supply (LUT vs IGST-refund) + FIRC tracking. _Persist: `useFeatureState("zero-rated-invoices")`._ _Class: KV._

- **GST Health Score & Filing Streak** (`health-score`) — Logs GSTR-1/3B filings (period, type, on-time). Score/100 = filing punctuality (50; default 30 if none) + GSTIN-configured (15) + registered (10) + recent-activity-in-75d (25); band Excellent/Good/Needs-work/At-risk. Computes consecutive on-time GSTR-3B streak. Actionable nudges (late-fee/interest, GSTIN, streak). _Persist: `useFeatureState("gst-filing-log")` + reads `store.firm`._ _Class: KV._

- **Interest & Late-Fee Calculator** (`interest-fee`) — Days late = due→file gap; interest = round(tax×18%×days/365) (Sec 50, simple); late fee = min(perDay×days, cap) where perDay = ₹50 normal / ₹20 nil, cap ₹10,000 / ₹1,000; split CGST+SGST. Total = interest + late fee. Implements Sec 50 interest + Sec 47 late fee. _Persist: none (useState only)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Registration & Threshold Advisor** (`threshold`) — Trailing-12-month inflow turnover (or manual) vs thresholds: registration goods ₹40L (₹20L special) / services ₹20L (₹10L special); composition cap goods ₹1.5cr / services ₹50L; e-invoice ₹5cr; GSTR-9C ₹5cr. Progress bar to reg threshold, must-register flag, milestone table (crossed/within). Implements registration/composition/e-invoice/9C threshold rules. _Persist: live store (`store.transactions`); inputs on useState._ _Class: Indicative._

- **GSTR-1 vs GSTR-3B Reconciliation** (`gstr1-3b`) — GSTR-1 taxable derived from `store.invoices` sum, tax = round(taxable×rate)/100; enter filed 3B taxable + tax; computes deltas and match flags (|Δ|<1). Match → reconcile banner; mismatch → ASMT-10 scrutiny warning. Implements GSTR-1/3B (Table 3.1) liability cross-check. _Persist: live store (`store.invoices`); 3B inputs on useState._ _Class: Indicative._

- **180-Day ITC Reversal Tracker (Rule 37)** (`rule180`) — Per purchase invoice: ITC = round(amount×rate/100), days-since invoice date, reverse-by = invoiceDate+180d; flags overdue (>180d unpaid) and due-soon (151–180d); totals ITC-to-reverse and due-soon; mark-paid toggle. Implements Rule 37 / Sec 16(2) second proviso 180-day payment rule. _Persist: `useFeatureState("gst-rule180-bills")`._ _Class: KV._

- **e-Invoice 30-Day Reporting Tracker** (`einv30`) — Per invoice: days-left = 30 − days-since invoice date, report-by = invoiceDate+30d; flags locked (deadline passed → IRP rejects IRN) and urgent (≤5d); counts locked + urgent; mark-reported toggle. Implements the 30-day IRP/IRN reporting deadline. _Persist: `useFeatureState("gst-einv30-invoices")`._ _Class: KV._

- **Inverted-Duty Refund Calculator (Rule 89(5))** (`inverted`) — Max refund = max(0, round((inverted-turnover × Net ITC ÷ adjusted-total-turnover) − output-tax-payable)); Net ITC = input-goods ITC only; flags ineligible portion (services + capital goods) = total ITC − Net ITC as non-refundable. Implements the Rule 89(5) inverted-duty formula. _Persist: none (useState only)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Credit / Debit Note GST Register** (`cdn-register`) — Records Sec 34 notes (credit reduces output liability; debit increases) linked to original invoice; tax = round(taxable×rate/100); totals credit-note GST, debit-note GST, net adjustment = debit−credit. Implements Sec 34 CDN reporting in GSTR-1. _Persist: `useFeatureState("gst-cdn-register")`._ _Class: KV._

- **Compensation Cess Calculator** (`cess`) — 8 sin/luxury categories (aerated drinks 28%+12%, small petrol car 28%+1%, small diesel 28%+3%, mid car 28%+17%, SUV 28%+22%, cigarettes 28%+36%, pan masala 28%+60%, coal 5%+₹400/tonne specific). GST = round(base×gst%), cess = round(base×cessPct%) (ad-valorem; specific-cess items show ₹0 with a note), total = base+GST+cess. Implements GST compensation cess on top of GST. _Persist: none (useState only)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **GSTR-9C Reconciliation Helper** (`gstr9c`) — Enter books-vs-return figures for turnover (Table 5/7), ITC (Table 12), tax paid (Table 9); diffs flagged when |Δ|>1 with per-head consequence notes (demand Sec 73/74, ITC reversal+interest, DRC-03 shortfall). Overall reconciled/gap banner. Implements GSTR-9C books↔annual-return reconciliation (>₹5cr). _Persist: none (useState only)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Job-Work ITC-04 Tracker** (`jobwork`) — Delivery-challan register for goods sent to job workers: limit 365d (inputs) / 1095d (capital); days-out; flags overdue past window (deemed supply) and at-risk value; mark-received toggle; counts open/overdue + deemed-supply value at risk. Implements Sec 143 job-work 1yr/3yr return windows + ITC-04. _Persist: `useFeatureState("gst-jobwork-itc04")`._ _Class: KV._

- **ISD Credit Distributor (Rule 39)** (`isd`) — Allocates common input-service ITC across branch GSTINs pro-rata to branch turnover: share = branchTO/totalTO, amount = round(credit×share); rounding residual assigned to last branch so distributed = credit exactly. Implements Rule 39 ISD distribution (GSTR-6). _Persist: `useFeatureState("gst-isd-branches")`; credit amount on useState._ _Class: KV._

- **Branch-Transfer Stock Invoicer** (`branch-transfer`) — Cross-GSTIN (same PAN) stock-transfer valuation: interState = both states set and differ → IGST @rate, else CGST/SGST halves; total = base+tax; notes Rule 28 deemed-value (full-ITC branch) and >₹50k e-way bill. Implements Schedule I distinct-persons supply + Rule 28. _Persist: none (useState only)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **PMT-09 Fund Transfer** (`pmt09`) — Stages cash-ledger reshuffles between major heads (IGST/CGST/SGST/Cess) + minor head (Tax/Interest/Penalty/Fee/Other); validates source≠dest and positive amount; computes net change per head from staged transfers. Models Form PMT-09 electronic-cash-ledger transfers (credit ledger untouched). _Persist: `useFeatureState("gst-pmt09-transfers")`._ _Class: KV._

- **Cross-Charge Calculator** (`cross-charge`) — Allocates HO common cost across branches by turnover share, applies optional notional markup; per branch interState (vs HO state) → IGST else CGST/SGST halves; taxable = portion×(1+markup%), tax = round(taxable×rate%); total GST across branches. Implements Schedule I distinct-persons cross-charge per Circular 199/2023 (salary needs no markup). _Persist: `useFeatureState("gst-crosscharge-branches")` + `useFeatureState("gst-crosscharge-cost")`; markup/rate/HO/branch-form on useState._ _Class: KV._

- **Free Samples / Gifts ITC Reversal** (`free-samples`) — Per giveaway: value = cost/unit × qty; reversal required unless type = Buy-one-get-one (single composite supply, Circular 92/2018); ITC reversal = round(value×rate%); totals giveaway cost + ITC-to-reverse. Implements Sec 17(5)(h) reversal (3B Table 4(B)(1)). _Persist: `useFeatureState("gst-freesample-items")`._ _Class: KV._

- **Pure-Agent Expense Tagger (Rule 33)** (`pure-agent`) — 5 Rule-33 condition checkboxes (authorised, separately indicated at actual, additional to own services, no title, no own use); if all met reimbursement excluded from taxable value (taxable = fee), else included (taxable = fee+reimbursement); GST = round(taxable×rate%); shows pass-through vs taxed reimbursement + invoice total. Implements Rule 33 pure-agent value exclusion. _Persist: none (useState only)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **GST Audit-Readiness Checklist** (`audit-ready`) — 14-item checklist grouped by area (Returns/ITC/RCM/Documents/Annual/Records): 1↔3B & 3B↔books recon, 2B match, Sec 17(5) blocked, Rule 42/43 & 17(5)(h) reversals, Rule 37 180-day, RCM self-invoices, e-invoice IRN, e-way bills, HSN/rate, GSTR-9/9C, ledger tally, vendor-GSTIN verification, notice responses. Progress % bar. Frames Sec 65 audit / Sec 61 scrutiny readiness. _Persist: `useFeatureState("gst-audit-checklist")`._ _Class: KV._

---

### Classification summary
- **Backend (`/api/gst`):** Calculator, Returns, Calendar, Verify GSTIN (offline format check), 2B Match (client compute on user-supplied portal files).
- **KV (`useFeatureState`, synced):** LUT, Refund, TDS/TCS-GST, 2B-vs-Books, Multi-GSTIN, Rate-Change, Blocked-Credit, Vendor-Score, GST-Advances, Zero-Rated, Health-Score, Rule180, e-Inv-30, CDN Register, Job-Work, ISD, PMT-09, Cross-Charge, Free-Samples, Audit-Readiness (20 trackers).
- **Indicative (derived from live store or pure useState calculators; no filing/GSP integration):** Ledger, GSTR-1, E-Way, RCM, HSN, ITC Optimizer, GSTR-9, Composition, QRMP, e-Invoice Readiness, Notice Templates, GSTR-3B Auto-Prep, Liability Forecaster, Place-of-Supply, ITC Reversal (42/43), DRC-03, Interest & Late-Fee, Threshold Advisor, GSTR-1-vs-3B, Inverted-Duty, Cess, GSTR-9C, Branch-Transfer, Pure-Agent.
- No Simulated/fabricated-data tools: Verify GSTIN explicitly refuses to invent trade-name/status; Calculator/Returns note v2 GSP (Masters India) integration for actual e-filing.
## Income Tax & TDS (`/tax`) — 38 tools

"Tax Autopilot" — a single-page Income-Tax/TDS workbench that turns the firm's live P&L into advance-tax, TDS, capital-gains, regime, presumptive, corporate and compliance estimates plus durable trackers (TCS/TDS-return/26AS/197/notices/MSME). Stakeholders: owner, finance, CA. Backend: mixed — calculator tools are client-side `useState` (nothing persisted); record-keeping trackers use `useFeatureState` (KV-synced to `store.featureData`, debounced to backend, propagated across devices); the Overview reads the live `store.transactions` and writes to the live Forecast store via `addObligation`.

### Tab-selector array (37 buttons, in render order)

The header renders one tab array (`TaxPage.tsx:151-155`). Tokens: `overview`, `regime`, `advtax`, `44ad`, `cg`, `audit`, `tcs`, `mat`, `angel`, `tds-return`, `form26as`, `tds-finder`, `ldc-197`, `depreciation`, `loss-setoff`, `itr-prefill`, `form15ca`, `sec80`, `eq-levy`, `advtax-calendar`, `tax-notice`, `hra`, `house-prop`, `44ae`, `gratuity`, `relief-89`, `donation-80g`, `cg-exempt`, `interest-234`, `115ba`, `partner-remun`, `80jjaa`, `esop-tax`, `buyback`, `194n`, `43bh`, `presumptive-cmp`, `surcharge`. (`overview` is the default tab and not a separate calculator; it renders the dashboard described below. That yields 37 tab buttons and 38 distinct tools counting Overview's 5 sub-widgets as one composite.) These are render-routing tokens, not UI filter tokens, so all are documented below.

### Overview dashboard (`taxTab === "overview"`) — composite live tool

The default tab. Reads live `store.transactions` and `firm`. YTD window = `startOfYear(today)` to today (`amount > 0` = revenue, `amount < 0` = expense). Five sub-widgets:

- **Summary cards (4)** — inputs: live txns + `firm.gstRate`/`gstRegistered`. Compute: YTD Net Profit (rev − exp); Annual Tax Estimate = `ytdProfit × 0.25` (flat 25% effective rate, not slab-based); GST This Month = last-month revenue × `gstRate%` (default 18%) if GST-registered; Next Deadline = days to nearest auto-computed deadline (red ≤10d, yellow ≤30d). _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **YTD P&L Snapshot bar** — Revenue / Expenses / Profit as % of revenue bars. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (reads synced txns)._
- **TDS Deposit quick card** — TDS estimate = current-month expense txns (`category === "expense"`) × 2% flat; labels "7th of next month." Heuristic 2%, not section-accurate. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Upcoming Deadlines timeline** — `computeTaxCalendar(today)` (`TaxPage.tsx:27-64`) generates up to 10 sorted deadlines: Advance Tax (15 Jun 15% / 15 Sep 45% / 15 Dec 75% / 15 Mar 100%), GSTR-3B (20th, next 4 months), TDS Deposit (7th, next 3 months), ITR Filing (31 Jul). Each row shows estimated amount (advance-tax instalment via `installmentDue` map = annualTaxEst × 15/45/75/100%; GSTR-3B = gstLiability; TDS = tdsEst) and an "Add to Forecast" button → `addObligation({type:"tax"})` then navigates to `/forecast`. _Persist: live store; obligations persist to the live Forecast/store._ _Class: Backend._
- **GST Filing CTA + empty-state** — navigates to `/gst` or `/transactions`. _Persist: none._ _Class: Preview._

### Calculator & tracker tools

- **Regime Optimizer (`regime`)** — `RegimeOptimizer` (`TaxPage.tsx:1045`). Inputs: gross income, 80C (cap ₹1.5L), 80D (₹25k / ₹50k senior), 80CCD(1B) NPS (cap ₹50k), 24(b) home-loan interest (cap ₹2L), HRA exemption, senior-citizen flag. New regime: taxable = gross − ₹75,000 std deduction; slabs 0/5/10/15/20/30% at 3/7/10/12/15L (`NEW_BANDS`); 87A rebate = full slab tax if taxable ≤ ₹7L; +4% cess. Old regime: gross − ₹50k − Chapter VI-A/24(b) deductions; slabs 0/5/20/30% at 2.5/5/10L (`OLD_BANDS`); 87A rebate = min(slab, ₹12,500) if taxable ≤ ₹5L; +4% cess. Output: cheaper regime + savings + breakdown table. Surcharge not modelled. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Advance Tax Estimator (`advtax`)** — `AdvanceTaxEstimator` (`TaxPage.tsx:1177`). Default liability auto-derived from txns: annualised (rev − cost) × 25%. Editable liability + 4 editable "paid" cells. Sec 211 schedule 15/45/75/100% (15 Jun/Sep/Dec/Mar). 234C = shortfall × 1% × months (3/3/3/1). 234B = if total paid < 90% liability, shortfall × 1% × 1 month (indicative). Sec 208: not mandatory if liability ≤ ₹10,000. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Presumptive 44AD/44ADA (`44ad`)** — inline IIFE (`TaxPage.tsx:164`). Inputs: scheme toggle, turnover/gross receipts, digital-receipts flag. Presumptive income = turnover × (44AD: 6% digital / 8% else; 44ADA: 50%). Less ₹75,000 std deduction; new-regime slabs (0/5/10/15/20/30%); +4% cess. Eligibility caps: 44AD ≤ ₹3 cr, 44ADA ≤ ₹75 lakh (else 44AB audit warning). Shows advance-tax instalments (15/45/75/100%). _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Capital Gains (`cg`)** — inline IIFE (`TaxPage.tsx:270`). Inputs: asset (equity/debt/property), buy, sell, holding months. LTCG thresholds: equity 12mo, property 24mo, debt 36mo. Post-Budget-2024 rates: equity STCG 20% / LTCG 12.5% above ₹1.25L exemption; debt STCG 30% / LTCG 20% (note says slab/20%); property STCG 30% / LTCG 12.5% (no indexation). +4% cess. Loss → STCL/LTCL set-off + 8-year carry-forward note. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Tax Audit 44AB (`audit`)** — inline IIFE (`TaxPage.tsx:548`). Inputs: business/professional toggle, manual turnover (auto-fills from all positive txns), digital-receipts % slider. Thresholds: business ₹1 cr (₹10 cr if digital ≥95%, Finance Act 2020), professional ₹50 lakh. Output: audit required/not + headroom + progress bar + reference table. Penalty note: 0.5% of turnover, max ₹1.5L. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **TCS Tracker (`tcs`)** — inline IIFE (`TaxPage.tsx:648`). Sec 206C rate table (scrap 1%, timber 2.5%, tendu 5%, minerals/liquor 1%, motor vehicle >₹10L 1%, sale of goods >₹50L 206C(1H) 0.1%). Add buyer/goods/amount/date; TCS = amount × rate; deposited toggle; pending = total − deposited. Form 27EQ quarterly, deposit by 7th. _Persist: `tcs-entries` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **MAT / AMT Check (`mat`)** — `MatChecker` (`TaxPage.tsx:814`). Inputs: book profit, additions, deductions, normal taxable income, company/LLP toggle. Adj book profit = bp + add − ded. MAT (Sec 115JB) = 15% × adj book profit (company); AMT (Sec 115JC) = 18.5% (LLP/individual). Normal tax = income × 25% (115BAA simplified). MAT applies if MAT > normal; MAT credit (Sec 115JAA) carried 15 years. Lists common add-backs/deductions. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Angel Tax (`angel`)** — `AngelTaxChecker` (`TaxPage.tsx:911`). Sec 56(2)(viib). Inputs: issue price, FMV, shares, investor type (resident/foreign), DPIIT flag, Cat-I/II AIF flag. Excess premium = max(0, issue×qty − fmv×qty); tax = 30% if not exempt; surcharge 12% >₹1cr / 7% >₹10L (note: thresholds in code are 1cr/10L not 50L/1cr). Exempt if DPIIT-registered, AIF Cat-I/II, or foreign investor. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **TDS Return 24Q/26Q/27Q (`tds-return`)** — `TdsReturnGenerator` (`TaxPage.tsx:1308`). Uses `TDS_SECTIONS` master (192/194A/194C/194C2/194H/194I-L/194I-P/194J-P/194J-T/194Q/194O/206C1H with rate, no-PAN rate, threshold, form). Add deductee/PAN/section/amount/date; rate auto-picks no-PAN (20% min, Sec 206AA) if PAN ≠ 10 chars; TDS = amount × rate. Groups into quarterly statements (Q1 Apr-Jun etc.) by form. Deposited toggle. Late fee note ₹200/day Sec 234E. _Persist: `tds-return-rows` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **26AS / AIS Recon (`form26as`)** — `Form26ASRecon` (`TaxPage.tsx:1435`). Add deductor/section/books-TDS/26AS-TDS rows. Verdict: matched (|diff|<1), short in 26AS (chase deductor), or extra in 26AS (claim). Claimable = min(books, 26AS); at-risk = max(0, books − 26AS). _Persist: `form26as-rows` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **TDS Section Finder (`tds-finder`)** — `TdsSectionFinder` (`TaxPage.tsx:1518`). Pick payment nature + amount + PAN flag → returns section, applicable rate (normal or no-PAN), threshold, TDS = amount × rate (only if amount ≥ threshold). Full `TDS_SECTIONS` reference table. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Lower-Deduction 197 (`ldc-197`)** — `LowerDeductionTracker` (`TaxPage.tsx:1604`). Add vendor/cert-no/section/cert-rate/valid-till/payment. Applied TDS uses cert rate while valid, falls back to section normal rate once expired (valid-till < today). Total saved = Σ payment × (normal − effective)%. _Persist: `ldc-197-rows` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Depreciation Schedule (`depreciation`)** — `DepreciationSchedule` (`TaxPage.tsx:1694`). IT Act block-of-assets WDV. `DEP_BLOCKS`: buildings 5%/10%, furniture 10%, P&M 15%, motor vehicles 15%, computers/software 40%, books 40%, intangibles 25%. Dep = full rate on (opening WDV + additions ≥180 days) + half rate on additions <180 days; closing WDV = open + additions − dep. _Persist: `depreciation-assets` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Loss Set-off & C/F (`loss-setoff`)** — `LossSetoffPlanner` (`TaxPage.tsx:1782`). Heads with rules: business (8yr, any head except salary), speculative (4yr, only speculative), STCL (8yr, STCG/LTCG), LTCL (8yr, only LTCG), house property (8yr, ≤₹2L inter-head), horses (4yr, same activity). Expiry AY = incurred AY + cfYears. _Persist: `loss-setoff-rows` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **ITR Pre-Fill Pack (`itr-prefill`)** — `ItrPrefillPack` (`TaxPage.tsx:1871`). Reads live txns: revenue, expenses, net profit. Entity toggle → form (individual/HUF ITR-3/4, firm ITR-5, company ITR-6). Assembles Sch BP / Part B-TI line items for CA transcription. _Persist: none (useState); reads live store._ _Class: Backend (reads synced txns) / Preview._
- **Form 15CA/15CB (`form15ca`)** — `Form15CAHelper` (`TaxPage.tsx:1945`). Rule 37BB part determination: not taxable → Part D (no 15CB); taxable & ≤ ₹5L/FY → Part A; taxable & > ₹5L → Part C (CA's 15CB required). TDS u/s 195 = amount × DTAA/withholding rate. Lists all four parts. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Sec 80 Maximiser (`sec80`)** — `Sec80Maximiser` (`TaxPage.tsx:2027`). Entity-level: 80G = donation × 50%/100%; 80JJAA = additional wages × 30%; 35AD = 100% capex. Tax saved = total deductions × 25% (assumed corporate rate). Note: forfeited under 115BAA/BAB (except 80JJAA). _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Equalisation Levy / 194O (`eq-levy`)** — `EqualisationLevyTracker` (`TaxPage.tsx:2117`). Types: EL online-ads 6% (Sec 165), EL e-commerce 2% (Sec 165A, withdrawn 1-Aug-2024), 194O e-commerce participant 1%. Levy = amount × rate; running total. Disallowance u/s 40(a) note. _Persist: `eq-levy-rows` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Adv. Tax vs TDS Calendar (`advtax-calendar`)** — `AdvTaxCashCalendar` (`TaxPage.tsx:2188`). Auto liability from txns (annualised profit × 25%) editable; less TDS credit; net advance tax = liability − TDS (Sec 209). Schedule rows 15/45/75/100% with per-date instalment and cumulative. _Persist: none (useState); reads live store._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Tax Notice / Demand Responder (`tax-notice`)** — `TaxNoticeResponder` (`TaxPage.tsx:2270`). Notice types with recommended action: 143(1) intimation, 139(9) defective, 143(2) scrutiny, 148 reassessment, 245 adjustment, 156 demand. Add ref/AY/type/demand/due-date; status cycles open→responded→closed; open demand & overdue counters. _Persist: `tax-notice-rows` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **HRA Exemption 10(13A) (`hra`)** — `HraExemptionCalc` (`TaxPage.tsx:2364`). Rule 2A least-of-three: actual HRA; 50% (metro) / 40% (non-metro) of Basic+DA; rent − 10% of Basic+DA. Exempt = least; taxable = HRA − exempt. Old regime only. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **House Property 24(b) (`house-prop`)** — `HousePropertyCalc` (`TaxPage.tsx:2442`). Let-out/self-occupied toggle. NAV = rent − municipal tax (0 if self-occupied); less 30% std deduction (24a); less interest (24b: capped ₹2L self-occupied, full if let-out). Loss set-off vs other heads capped ₹2L (Sec 71(3A)); balance carried 8 years. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Presumptive 44AE Transport (`44ae`)** — `Presumptive44AE` (`TaxPage.tsx:2525`). Heavy (GVW >12t): ₹1,000 × tons × months; other: ₹7,500 × months. Eligible only if ≤10 goods carriages (else 44AB audit warning). _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Gratuity / Leave Encashment (`gratuity`)** — `GratuityLeaveExemption` (`TaxPage.tsx:2597`). Gratuity Sec 10(10): least of received, formula (covered 15/26 × salary × years / not-covered ½-month × completed years), ₹20L cap. Leave Sec 10(10AA): least of received, leave value (avg/30 × earned days), 10 months' avg salary, ₹25L cap (CBDT Notif 31/2023). Non-government employees. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Arrears Relief 89(1) (`relief-89`)** — `Relief89Calc` (`TaxPage.tsx:2709`). Relief = (extra tax on arrears in current year) − (extra tax had arrears been taxed in earlier year), floored at 0. Uses new-regime slabs (`NEW_BANDS`) + 4% cess for both years (indicative; actual Form 10E uses each year's slabs). Form 10E note. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Donations 80G (`donation-80g`)** — `Donation80GCalc` (`TaxPage.tsx:2792`). Categories: 100%/50% no-limit, 100%/50% qualifying-limit (10% of AGTI). Cash > ₹2,000 disallowed. Non-qualifying = Σ allowed × pct; qualifying capped at 10% AGTI then apportioned × pct; total deduction = sum. Old regime only; Form 10BE note. _Persist: `tax-80g-donations` + `tax-80g-agti` (useFeatureState — both rows AND the AGTI input persist)._ _Class: KV._
- **CG Exemption 54/54EC/54F (`cg-exempt`)** — `CapitalGainExemptionPlanner` (`TaxPage.tsx:2886`). Sec 54: exemption = min(gain, reinvested house). Sec 54EC: min(gain, invested, ₹50L bond cap), NHAI/REC, 6-month/5-yr lock. Sec 54F: proportionate = gain × min(invested, net consideration)/net consideration. Tax saved = exemption × 12.5% (LTCG). ₹10cr investment cap note. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Interest 234A/B/C (`interest-234`)** — `Interest234Calc` (`TaxPage.tsx:2977`). 234A = unpaid × 1% × months late (late ITR). 234B = if paid < 90% & tax > ₹10k, unpaid × 1% × 4 months (assumed). 234C = per-instalment shortfall (proportional paid assumption) × 1% × 3/3/3/1. All 1%/month simple; shows total interest, tax+interest payable, effective interest %. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Corporate Rate 115BAA/BAB (`115ba`)** — `CorporateRate115BA` (`TaxPage.tsx:3067`). Inputs: taxable income, incentives foregone, new-manufacturer flag. Regular: 25% + surcharge (7% >₹1cr / 12% >₹10cr) + 4% cess (MAT 15% note). 115BAA: 22% on (income + incentives added back) + 10% flat surcharge + 4% cess (eff. 25.17%), no MAT. 115BAB: 15% (eff. 17.16%) for eligible new manufacturers. Picks lowest. Irrevocable note. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Partner Remuneration 40(b) (`partner-remun`)** — `PartnerRemuneration40b` (`TaxPage.tsx:3156`). Revised FY25 (Finance No.2 Act 2024) slabs: on first ₹6L of book profit = higher of ₹3L or 90% (₹3L if profit ≤0); balance >₹6L = 60%. Allowable = tier1 + tier2; disallowed = paid − allowable. Interest-to-partner 12% cap note; Sec 28(v) note. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **New-Employee 80JJAA (`80jjaa`)** — `NewEmployee80JJAA` (`TaxPage.tsx:3220`). Eligible if emoluments ≤ ₹25,000/month AND days ≥ 240 (≥150 apparel/footwear/leather). Additional wages = n × wage × 12; year deduction = 30%; 3-year benefit = ×3. Form 10DA note. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **ESOP Tax (`esop-tax`)** — `EsopTaxPlanner` (`TaxPage.tsx:3290`). Stage 1 perquisite = (FMV-at-exercise − strike) × shares × slab% × 1.04 cess. Stage 2 CG = (sale − FMV) × shares; LT threshold listed 12mo / unlisted 24mo. Listed: STCG 20% / LTCG 12.5% above ₹1.25L; unlisted: STCG slab / LTCG 12.5%; ×1.04 cess. Eligible-startup 5-year deferral note. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Share Buyback 115QA (`buyback`)** — `BuybackTax115QA` (`TaxPage.tsx:3393`). Old (≤30-Sep-2024): company 20% × (buyback − issue) × 1.12 surcharge × 1.04 cess; proceeds exempt to shareholder Sec 10(34A). New (≥1-Oct-2024): full consideration = deemed dividend × shareholder slab × 1.04; issue cost = capital loss (c/f 8yr); TDS u/s 194 (10% resident) note. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Cash Withdrawal 194N (`194n`)** — `CashWithdrawal194N` (`TaxPage.tsx:3479`). Filer threshold ₹1cr (₹3cr if co-op society payer) flat 2% on excess. Non-filer (no ITR 3 yrs): 2% on ₹20L–threshold band, 5% above threshold. Net received = amount − TDS; creditable in 26AS note. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **MSME 43B(h) Disallowance (`43bh`)** — `Msme43BhChecker` (`TaxPage.tsx:3561`). Add MSME vendor/amount/days-outstanding/agreement flag. Disallowed if days > 45 (written agreement) or > 15 (none). Total disallowed, est. extra tax (~30% notional). Applies to Micro/Small only; clear before 31 Mar to restore. _Persist: `tax-43bh-entries` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Presumptive vs Books (`presumptive-cmp`)** — `PresumptiveVsBooks` (`TaxPage.tsx:3660`). Inputs: 44AD/44ADA toggle, turnover, actual expenses, digital flag. Presumptive income = turnover × 6%/8%/50%; books income = turnover − expenses. Both taxed via local new-regime slab fn (+4% cess); cheaper path + saving shown. 5-year opt-out lock note. _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Surcharge & Marginal Relief (`surcharge`)** — `SurchargeMarginalRelief` (`TaxPage.tsx:3753`). New/old regime toggle. Surcharge bands: 10% >₹50L, 15% >₹1cr, 25% >₹2cr (new caps at 25%), 37% >₹5cr (old only). Marginal relief = excess (tax+surcharge over threshold) − income above threshold, when positive. +4% cess on (tax + surcharge after relief). _Persist: none (useState)._ _Class: Indicative._

### Shared infrastructure (not standalone tools)

- `TDS_SECTIONS` master (`TaxPage.tsx:1291`) — feeds TDS Return, 26AS Recon, Section Finder, 197 Tracker.
- `slabTax` / `NEW_BANDS` / `OLD_BANDS` (`TaxPage.tsx:1026-1043`) — reused by Regime Optimizer and Arrears Relief.
- `INP` constant + per-component `inp` — shared input styling.

### Accuracy notes for auditors

- **Overview Annual Tax Estimate and TDS card are flat-rate heuristics** (25% effective, 2% TDS), not slab/section computations — explicitly "Indicative."
- **Angel Tax surcharge thresholds in code** (>₹1cr 12%, >₹10L 7%) differ from the standard individual 50L/1cr trigger; verify before relying.
- **234B/234C and Interest 234 tool** use proportional/assumed-month approximations (each labels itself indicative).
- **Equalisation Levy 2% (e-commerce)** is retained though withdrawn w.e.f. 1-Aug-2024 — kept for prior-period entries (stated in-tool).
- KV-persisted trackers (TCS, TDS Return, 26AS, 197, Depreciation, Loss Set-off, Eq-Levy, Tax Notice, 80G donations+AGTI, 43B(h)) survive reload and sync across devices; all other calculators reset on navigation.
# Compliance & Secretarial (`/compliance`) — 30 tools

One-stop statutory cockpit that derives every Indian SMB filing date, license, register and penalty from the firm profile + transactions, with per-record trackers persisted to the synced store. Stakeholders: CA, owner, ops. Backend: KV-synced via `useFeatureState` (writes to `store.featureData[key]` → localStorage + debounced backend push + cross-device live-sync; read-only roles gated).

Tabs are an array of 26 IDs (`overview` … `gst-turnover-recon`); the `overview` tab additionally renders 5 inline tools (Contract Expiry, MSME Checker, Labour-Law Checklist, ROC Filing Calendar, Insurance Calendar) → **30 distinct tools** below.

## Overview tab — derived calendar + 5 inline tools

- **Compliance Calendar (Next 6 Months + KPI strip)** — firm profile (`gstRegistered`, payroll-tagged txns), `computeFinancialSnapshot`, `store.obligations` → rolls 6 months of monthly statutory dates (TDS deposit 7th, GSTR-1 11th, GSTR-3B+payment 20th with net payable, PF/ESI 15th if payroll, advance-tax instalments 15th of Mar/Jun/Sep/Dec) + fixed annual ROC dates (MGT-7/7A 60d-of-AGM, AOC-4/AOC-4-XBRL 30d-of-AGM, LLP Form-11 30-Jun, LLP Form-8 30-Oct, DIR-3 KYC 31-Mar, MSME Form-1 30-Nov) + user obligations → sorted event list with day-countdown/overdue chips, cash-due-30d KPI, GST-payable KPI, est. annual tax KPI, and warnings (not-GST-registered banner, overdue-obligations banner). _Persist: none (computed; reads `store.obligations`)._ _Class: Backend (reads synced store)._
- **Cost of Filing Late (GST penalty slider)** — `lateDays` slider 1–90 × `snap.gstThisMonth.netPayable` → `gstLatePenalty` returns late fee ₹50/day + interest @18% p.a. + total. _Persist: none (transient slider)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Advance Tax (FY) panel** — `snap.advanceTax` instalments → label, cumulative %, instalment amount, paid-window styling; note: 25% corporate rate on est. profit, §234C 1%/month. _Persist: none (computed)._ _Class: Backend (derived)._
- **Contract Expiry Tracker** — add Contract {name, counterparty, kind (Vendor/Customer/Employment/Lease/Service/NDA/Loan/Insurance/Other), expiry date, value, notes} → `differenceInDays(expiry)` buckets expired/urgent ≤30d/warning ≤90d, "expiring soon" badge count, sorted by expiry. _Persist: `contracts` (live store)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._
- **MSME / Udyam Registration Checker** — Plant&Machinery + annual turnover + Udyam no. + registered toggle → category rules (Micro ≤₹1cr P&M & ≤₹5cr T.O; Small ≤₹10cr & ≤₹50cr; Medium ≤₹50cr & ≤₹250cr; else Not-MSME) → category badge, threshold table with "← You", 8-item benefits list (PSL, CGTMSE collateral-free ≤₹10L, ECLGS subvention, 45-day MSMED protection, stamp duty, ISO/barcode subsidy, GeM preference, 3× bank-rate delayed-payment interest). _Persist: none (transient useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Labour Law Compliance Checklist** — employee count (seeded 15 if payroll txns exist) → filters 11 acts by threshold (EPF≥20, ESI≥10, Bonus≥20, Gratuity≥10, MinWages≥1, PT≥1, Maternity≥10, Shops≥1, LWF≥1, Factories≥10, ContractLabour≥20, POSH≥10) → applicable list with risk badge (high/medium/low), frequency, description, done/total counter; checked-state is component-local (`Set`). _Persist: none (local checkbox Set; count transient)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **ROC / MCA Filing Calendar** — fixed FY-derived table → 8 filings (MGT-7/7A 60d-of-AGM, AOC-4 30d, ADT-1 15d, DIR-3 KYC 30-Sep, LLP Form-11 30-May, LLP Form-8 30-Oct, INC-20A 180d-of-incorp, MSME-1 Apr&Oct) with parsed-date urgency (overdue/Due Soon/Upcoming/Scheduled), entity, form, note. _Persist: none (static computed)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Insurance Calendar** — add policy {name, type (Fire&Burglary/Shopkeeper/Marine/GroupHealth/GPA/D&O/Prof Indemnity/Vehicle/WC/Cyber/KeyMan/Other), insurer, premium, sum insured, renewal date, notes} → sorts by renewal, expired/urgent ≤30d status, "renewing soon" count, KPIs (count, total annual premium, renewing ≤30d). _Persist: `insurance-policies` (live store)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._

## ROC / MCA & Companies-Act tabs

- **ROC Filing Auto-Prep (AOC-4 / MGT-7)** — AGM date + entity type (private/public/small/OPC) + revenue (Σ positive txns) + snapshot (profit, cash) → form selection (small/OPC → MGT-7A, turnover ≥₹100cr → AOC-4 XBRL), due dates computed off AGM (AOC-4 +30d / OPC 180d-from-FY-end, annual return +60d, ADT-1 +15d), auto-filled filing data + ₹100/day-no-cap fee notes. _Persist: none (transient AGM/entity)._ _Class: Indicative (auto-filled figures derived from synced books)._
- **DIR-3 KYC & DPT-3 Tracker** — DIN-holder list {name, DIN, mode KYC-WEB vs e-form, done} + DPT-3 toggle + outstanding-deposit amount → KYC due 30-Sep (rolls to next yr if past, ₹5,000 reactivation), DPT-3 due 30-Jun (₹5,000 + ₹500/day); pending counter, deposit/exempted-loan reporting note. _Persist: `compliance-din-holders`, `compliance-dpt3-applies` (live store; exemptAmt transient)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._
- **Board / AGM Meeting Manager** — log meeting {kind Board/AGM/EGM/Committee, date, agenda, resolutions, minutes textarea, done} → Companies-Act 2013 statutory timelines table (≥4 board mtgs/yr gap ≤120d, 7-day board notice, AGM 9mo first/6mo subsequent gap ≤15mo, 21-clear-day AGM notice, minutes 30d, MGT-14 30d) with "action needed" flag when board count <4. _Persist: `compliance-meetings` (live store)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._
- **Statutory Registers** — seeded 7-register checklist {Members MGT-1, Directors&KMP Sec170, Charges CHG-7, RPT MBP-4, Share Transfers SH-6, Loans&Investments MBP-2, Deposits DPT-2} → toggle maintained + stamp updated-date, done/total counter. _Persist: `compliance-registers` (live store)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._
- **Shop & Establishment / Trade License Renewals** — generic `LicenseTracker` (seedAuthority "State Labour Dept") → add license {name, authority, number, expiry} → sorted by expiry, color bands (≤30d/≤60d/expired). _Persist: `compliance-shop-licenses` (live store)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._
- **FSSAI / Industry License Tracker** — same `LicenseTracker` (seedAuthority "FSSAI") → add/track licenses by expiry with countdown bands. _Persist: `compliance-industry-licenses` (live store)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._
- **Event-Based ROC Filing Tracker** — pick form (PAS-3, MGT-14, DIR-12, CHG-1, CHG-4, INC-22, SH-7, ADT-3) + event date → adds event-window (mostly 30d) → computes due date, filed/pending toggle, overdue counter, ₹100/day-no-cap + CHG-1 condonation note. _Persist: `comp-event-roc` (live store)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._
- **Director Disqualification Checker (Sec 164)** — add director {name, DIN} + tick 9 trigger flags (164(1)(a)–(h) unsound/insolvent/convicted/court-order/unpaid-calls/RPT-conviction/no-DIN-KYC; 164(2)(a)/(b) 3-yr no-filing / deposit-dividend default) → per-director disqualified-if-any-trigger badge with count. _Persist: `comp-dir-disqual` (live store)._ _Class: Indicative (self-assessment)._
- **Significant Beneficial Owner (SBO / BEN-2) Register** — add individual {name, PAN, direct %, indirect %} → SBO rule: indirect ≥10% OR (indirect >0 & total ≥10%) → SBO flag, BEN-2 due/filed toggle, pending counter; rejects holding >100%; BEN-1→BEN-2-30d note. _Persist: `comp-sbo-register` (live store)._ _Class: Indicative (fact-specific determination)._
- **Secretarial Standards Checklist (SS-1 / SS-2)** — 12 ICSI checklist items grouped SS-1 (board: 7d notice, quorum, 4 mtgs/120d, no 12-mo absence, minutes 30d, draft circ 15d) + SS-2 (general: 21-clear-day notice, explanatory statement, quorum 5/15/30, MGT-11 proxy 48h, attendance register, separate minute book 30d) → toggle done, % progress bar; §118(10)/MR-3 note. _Persist: `comp-secretarial-std` (live store, keyed map)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._

## Labour, tax & licensing tabs

- **Labour-Law Compliance Calendar** — fixed monthly/half-yearly recurrences → next due dates for PF/EPFO ECR (15th), ESI (15th), Professional Tax (~21st state), TDS-on-salary §192 (7th), Labour Welfare Fund (half-yearly Jun/Dec), with frequency + countdown. _Persist: none (static computed)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Multi-Act Penalty Estimator** — act dropdown (ROC, late TDS deposit, late TDS return 234E, EPF/PF, ESI, GST) + amount + days delayed → per-act formula: ROC ₹100/day; TDS-deposit 1.5%/part-month §201(1A); TDS-return ₹200/day capped at TDS §234E; PF 12% §7Q + 5–25% §14B by delay slab; ESI 12% p.a.; GST ₹50/day + 18% p.a. → penalty + days + part-months. _Persist: none (transient)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Compliance Health Score & Risk Heatmap** — seeded 7 weighted areas (GST 20%, TDS 15%, ROC 15%, PF/ESI/PT 15%, IncomeTax 15%, Licenses 10%, POSH 10%) cycled good/warning/bad → weighted score /100 (good=1, warning=0.5, bad=0), grade (Strong/Moderate/At-risk/Critical), areas-at-risk count, tappable heatmap tiles. _Persist: `compliance-health-areas` (live store)._ _Class: Backend (KV) self-rated._
- **Professional Tax — Multi-State Registration Tracker** — add state (8 PT states with max ₹2,500/yr + frequency info; lists 8 no-PT states) → per state track EC/RC obtained toggles + EC/RC numbers + PT-this-period amount → states tracked, registrations pending, total PT payable/period; rejects duplicate state. _Persist: `comp-pt-states` (live store)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._
- **POSH & Statutory Policy Tracker** — seeded 6-policy checklist {POSH policy ≥10 emp, IC constituted, annual report 31-Jan, training annual, code-of-conduct, complaints register} toggle done + IC-validity check (woman presiding + ≥50% women + external NGO member) → valid/incomplete verdict. _Persist: `compliance-posh-policies` (live store; IC checkboxes transient)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._

## Sector-licensing & disclosure tabs

- **Trademark / IP Renewal Tracker** — add asset {name, kind TM/Patent/Copyright/Design, reg no, class, reg date} → term rules (TM 10yr, Patent 20yr, Design 10yr, Copyright life+60 none) → computes renewal date + countdown, "renewing ≤6mo" count, lapsed badge. _Persist: `comp-ip-assets` (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Import-Export (IEC) Compliance Tracker** — IEC number + last-DGFT-update date + per-port AD-Code list {port, AD bank, registered toggle} → annual IEC-update window Apr–Jun (deadline 30-Jun, deactivation if missed), 3-item checklist (IEC obtained, updated this FY, AD-code at every port). _Persist: `comp-iec-number`, `comp-iec-updated`, `comp-iec-adcodes` (live store)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._
- **Environmental / Pollution Consent (CTE / CTO) Tracker** — add consent {kind CTE/CTO, category Red/Orange/Green/White, number, issued, valid-until} → category validity rules (Red 5yr, Orange 10yr, Green 15yr, White exempt) → sorted by validity, expired/≤90d bands, category color. _Persist: `comp-pollution-consents` (live store)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._
- **Fire / Safety NOC Tracker** — add record {premise, type Provisional/Final NOC/Fire Safety Cert, number, valid-until, mock-drill note} → sorted by validity, "renewing ≤60d" count, expired/≤60d bands, editable mock-drill field. _Persist: `comp-fire-noc` (live store)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._
- **CSR Applicability & Spend Tracker** — net worth + turnover (seeded Σ positive txns) + net profit (seeded snapshot) + CSR spent → Sec 135 applicability (net worth ≥₹500cr OR turnover ≥₹1,000cr OR net profit ≥₹5cr) → 2% obligation (of net profit proxy), spent, shortfall, progress bar, CSR-2/Schedule-VII unspent-transfer note. _Persist: none (transient; seeded from synced store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Related-Party Transaction (RPT) Register** — log {party, relation (8 options), nature, amount, basis Arm's-length/Not, board-approval toggle, date} → Sec 188/MBP-4 register, total value, awaiting-approval count, arm's-length badge, AOC-2/shareholder-approval note. _Persist: `comp-rpt-register` (live store)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._

## Master calendar & reconciliation tabs

- **Contract / Agreement Template Library** — pick template (NDA, Employment, MSA, Vendor/Supply) + counterparty → interpolates firm name + counterparty + today into 4 starter contract bodies → copy-to-clipboard. _Persist: none (transient; firm name from synced store)._ _Class: Preview (boilerplate generator, copy-only)._
- **Annual Compliance Master Calendar** — FY-scoped consolidation of all streams (GST/Income Tax/ROC-MCA/Labour/TDS) gated by `gstRegistered` + payroll → 17 rows: monthly TDS/GSTR-1/GSTR-3B/PF-ESI + advance-tax Q1–Q4 + tax audit 3CA/3CB-3CD + ITR 31-Oct + DPT-3/DIR-3-KYC/AOC-4/MGT-7 + GSTR-9/9C 31-Dec + MSME-1 H1/H2 → stream filter chips, countdown, copy-all to clipboard (TSV). _Persist: none (computed; reads synced firm profile/txns)._ _Class: Backend (derived) / Indicative dates._
- **MSME Form-1 — Delayed Payments to MSE Suppliers** — add due {supplier, Udyam no, invoice date, amount, paid toggle} → ages each, overdue beyond 45d, interest ≈ 3× bank rate (19.5% p.a. simple) → reportable amount, est. interest exposure, total tracked KPIs, current half-year period label, §43B(h) deduction note. _Persist: `comp-msme-form1` (live store)._ _Class: Indicative (simplified interest)._
- **GST vs Books Turnover Reconciliation (GSTR-9C prep)** — books turnover (Σ positive txns) + GSTR-1/3B turnover + adjustments (less exempt, less non-GST income, add unbilled) → adjusted books vs GST diff, % diff, status (reconciled <₹1 / minor ≤1% / material review / info) → line-by-line schedule + diff banner; GSTR-9C >₹5cr note. _Persist: `comp-gst-recon-gstr`, `comp-gst-recon-unbilled`, `comp-gst-recon-exempt`, `comp-gst-recon-nongst` (live store; books from synced store)._ _Class: Backend (KV) / Indicative recon._
# Payroll Module — Audit Appendix

## Payroll & Workforce (`/payroll`) — 45 tools

Runs monthly Indian SMB payroll end-to-end — employee master, run/disburse, statutory compliance (PF/ESI/PT/LWF/gratuity/bonus/TDS), salary structuring, separation, MIS, and a suite of HR planning calculators. Stakeholders: owner, finance, CA. Backend: `/api/payroll` (employees, runs, run, disburse) + `/api/ewa` + KV-synced `featureData` (via `useFeatureState`) for tracker/list tools.

The tab-selector array (line 226) defines **45 tabs**. `employees` and `runs` are the two backend-master tabs; the remaining 43 are statutory/HR tools. Plus there is one always-mounted modal (Add Employee) and the run/disburse actions in the page header. Each rendered tool is documented below; UI-state filter tokens (tab IDs themselves) are excluded.

---

### Core master & run (backend)

- **Add Employee (modal)** — captures name, email, PAN, gross_salary, joining_date, bank_account, IFSC → POSTs to `/api/payroll/employees`; live preview estimates monthly TDS from annual gross under the new regime (5% on 3–6L, 10% on 6–9L, 15% on 9–12L, 20% on 12–15L, 30% above 15L; no std deduction in preview) → persisted employee row. _Persist: Backend `/api/payroll/employees`._ _Class: Backend._

- **Employees** — lists every employee with gross, configured monthly TDS, derived net (gross − tds_monthly), and status badge; summary cards show active count, gross monthly, TDS monthly. Read-only table off the master. _Persist: Backend `/api/payroll/employees`._ _Class: Backend._

- **Payroll runs** — lists historical `/api/payroll/runs` with gross/TDS/net totals, draft/disbursed status, expandable per-employee breakdown. The header "Run … Payroll" button POSTs `/api/payroll/run` {run_month, run_year} (server computes the run); "Disburse" POSTs `/api/payroll/runs/:id/disburse`. _Persist: Backend `/api/payroll/runs` + `/run` + `/disburse`._ _Class: Backend._

- **EWA (Earned Wage Access)** — GETs `/api/ewa` (day-of-month, per-employee earned-to-date and max_advance = up to 50% of wages earned so far this month); "Request Advance" POSTs `/api/ewa/request` {employee_id, amount}, deducted from next salary. _Persist: Backend `/api/ewa` + `/api/ewa/request`._ _Class: Backend._ Carries a `PreviewBadge` (capability `ewaPayout`), so the payout rail is a preview.

---

### Compliance & document tools

- **Salary Slips** — per-employee/month slip: splits gross into Basic 50% / HRA 20% / Special 20% / Transport 10%; deducts PF = 12% of basic, Professional Tax = ₹200 if gross > ₹15,000 else 0, and the employee's configured monthly TDS → net = gross − (PF + PT + TDS); rendered as a printable card, exported to PDF via `exportElementAsPdf`. _Persist: none (derived from master; month/year are transient UI state)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Form 16** — per-employee TDS certificate for a chosen FY: annualGross = gross×12, less ₹75,000 standard deduction → net taxable; new-regime slabs (0 ≤3L; 5% 3–7L; 10% 7–10L; 15% 10–12L; 20% 12–15L; 30% >15L), +4% health & education cess → annual TDS and monthly TDS. Part A (employer/employee/PAN/placeholder TAN "MUMB00000A") + Part B (income detail); downloads CSV. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._ (TAN/TDS-deposit figures are illustrative.)

- **PF ECR** — EPFO Electronic Challan-cum-Return for active employees: pfWages = min(gross, ₹15,000); EE EPF = 12% of pfWages; EPS = min(8.33% of pfWages, ₹1,250); ER EPF diff = 12% − EPS; UANs are placeholders (`10000000000NN`). Totals footer; downloads fixed-width-style `.txt` ECR. _Persist: none (placeholder UANs, must be replaced before filing)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **ESI / Bonus (labor)** — three sub-panels in one tab:
  - **ESI Contributions** — for employees with gross ≤ ₹21,000: EE 0.75% + ER 3.25% of gross → monthly totals. _Class: Indicative._
  - **Payment of Bonus Act** — for employees gross ≤ ₹21,000: calc wage = min(gross, ₹7,000 ceiling); min bonus = calcWage×12×8.33%, max = calcWage×12×20% (annual). _Class: Indicative._
  - **Gratuity Provision** — 15/26 × monthly gross × years (per-year provision + 5-year illustration); notes ≥5 yr vesting and ₹20L cap. _Class: Indicative._
  - _Persist: none._

- **F&F Settlement (fnf)** — separation dues for one employee: per-day = gross ÷ 26; salary due = perDay × calendar days in last month; notice pay = perDay × notice days (zero if employer waives); leave encashment = perDay × leave-balance days; gratuity = (15/26) × gross × floor(years) only when service ≥5 yr (years derived from joining_date); gross settlement minus outstanding advance = net payable; downloads CSV. _Persist: none (inputs transient)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Variance** — month-over-month payroll variance from `store.transactions` tagged category `payroll` (last 12 months): MoM change, %, trend badge (Spike >10% / Up / Down <−5% / Flat); avg/highest/lowest cards; >15% spike warning. _Persist: live store (`store.transactions`)._ _Class: Backend._ (Sources real ledger transactions, not statutory math.)

- **Prof. Tax (pt)** — state-wise PT slabs for 8 states (MH, KA, WB, TN, AP, TS, GJ, MP) with their actual graduated slab tables (e.g. MH ₹175 for 7.5–10k, ₹200 ≥10k; TN ₹208 ≥21k; KA ₹150/₹175/₹200 bands); computes per-employee monthly + annual PT; downloads CSV. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Flexi Benefits (flexi)** — tax-efficient salary structuring (annual): basic = % of gross (slider 30–70), HRA = % of basic, LTA, food coupons (capped ₹2,200/mo ⇒ ₹26,400), employer NPS 80CCD(2) = % of basic, special allowance = balancing figure; HRA assumed 80% exempt (metro simplification), LTA/food/NPS fully tax-free; tax saving = total tax-free × 30% slab. _Persist: none (all sliders transient)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **LWF (lwf)** — Labour Welfare Fund per-employee/employer contributions for 12 states (e.g. MH ₹6+₹12 Jun&Dec, WB ₹3+₹6 monthly, Delhi/Rajasthan N/A); annualised by frequency (Monthly ×12, Jun&Dec ×2, Annual ×1) × headcount. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Offer Letter (offer)** — generates a plain-text offer letter from candidate/designation/dept/joining/probation/location/reporting + gross; compensation split basic 50% / HRA 20% / special (balance) / annual CTC = gross×12; copy-to-clipboard. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **ESOP Pool (esop)** — stock-option pool & vesting tracker: pool size, FMV, strike (all KV); per grant vested = floor(granted × min(monthsElapsed, vestingYears×12) ÷ (vestingYears×12)), zero before cliff; notional = vested × max(0, FMV − strike); pool utilisation %, over-allocation warning. _Persist: KV — `esop-pool-size`, `esop-fmv`, `esop-strike`, `esop-grants`._ _Class: KV._

- **CTC Optimizer (ctc)** — old-regime tax-efficient annual CTC split: basic = % of CTC, HRA = 50%/40% of basic (metro toggle), employer NPS 80CCD(2) = min(pct,14)% of basic, employer EPF = 12% of min(basic, ₹1.8L), LTA = min(10% basic, ₹60k), food ₹26,400, special = balance; HRA exemption u/s 10(13A) = min(HRA, rent − 10% basic, 50/40% basic); tax-free total = HRA exempt + LTA + food + NPS. _Persist: none (inputs transient)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Attendance (attendance)** — per-employee/month attendance register: payable days, present, LOP, comp-off, leave-encash days; per-day = gross ÷ payable days; LOP deduction = perDay × LOP; encash add-back = perDay × encash days; net = gross − LOP + encash; downloads CSV. _Persist: KV — `payroll-attendance`._ _Class: KV._

- **Gratuity (gratuity)** — Payment of Gratuity Act 1972 accrual: basic+DA proxy = 50% of gross; service years from joining_date with >6-months-rounds-up rule; accrued = min(₹20L cap, (15/26) × basic × eligible years); per-year accrual cost; vested flag at ≥5 yr; growth-assumption slider; downloads CSV. _Persist: none (rows derived from master; only the slider is local state)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Reimbursements (reimburse)** — expense-claim workflow: submit (employee, date, category, amount, description), approve/reject status, pending/approved totals to merge into payroll; remove claims. _Persist: KV — `payroll-reimbursements`._ _Class: KV._

- **TDS u/s 192 (tds192)** — per-employee salary-TDS projection with regime toggle: annualGross = gross×12; new regime std deduction ₹75k (no other), old regime ₹50k + 80C (≤1.5L) + 80D (≤50k) + 80CCD(1B) (≤50k) + home-loan 24(b) (≤2L) + HRA exemption; slab tax via shared `computeSlabTax` (NEW or OLD bands); 87A rebate (new: full ≤7L; old: ≤₹12,500 up to 5L); +4% cess → annual TDS; even-monthly and balance-over-remaining-months figures. _Persist: none (inputs transient)._ _Class: Indicative._ (Surcharge >₹50L not modelled.)

- **Bonus Accrual (bonus)** — Payment of Bonus Act 1965: eligible if gross ≤ ₹21,000; calc wage = min(gross, ₹7,000); annual min = calcWage×12×8.33%, max = ×20%, declared = ×clamp(pct,8.33,20)%; declared-rate slider; per-employee register + totals; downloads CSV. _Persist: none (only slider local)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Contractor Payouts (contractor)** — vendor/gig TDS register (separate from salary): rate = 1% (194C) / 10% (194J), or 20% if no valid PAN (regex-validated); TDS applied only when gross ≥ ₹30,000 threshold; net = gross − TDS; totals; downloads CSV labelled "26Q feed". _Persist: KV — `payroll-contractor-payouts`._ _Class: KV._

- **Salary Benchmark (benchmark)** — illustrative market annual-CTC bands (p25/p50/p75) for 9 SMB roles × city cost-of-living factor (Bengaluru 1.15 … Tier-2/3 0.80); positions an employee's CTC vs adjusted band, gap-to-median and suggested monthly correction. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._ (Static illustrative medians — explicitly "not a live market feed.")

- **Appraisal Planner (appraisal)** — increment-cycle planner: total hike budget = % of current annual payroll; per-employee editable hike %, hike amount, new annual; allocated vs remaining/over-budget; "distribute evenly"; downloads CSV. Hikes do NOT auto-apply to live salaries. _Persist: KV — `payroll-appraisal-hikes` (budget % slider is local)._ _Class: KV._

- **Payroll Journal (journal)** — month-end GL voucher for active employees: salary expense (Dr) = gross + employer PF (12% of min(gross,15k)) + employer ESI (3.25% if gross ≤21k); credits = net payable, PF payable (EE+ER), ESI payable, PT (₹200/₹175/0 by gross), TDS; checks Dr=Cr balance; downloads CSV. _Persist: none (derived from master)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Headcount Cost (headcount)** — 12-month fully-loaded forecast: loaded = (base gross + planned-hire CTC active that month) × 1.18 (employer PF+ESI+gratuity+bonus proxy); add planned hires with start month; monthly projection + 12-month total; downloads CSV. _Persist: KV — `payroll-planned-hires`._ _Class: KV._

- **Statutory Liability (liability)** — balance-sheet provisions: per-employee min statutory bonus (8.33% of min(gross,7k)×12, eligible if gross ≤21k) + leave-encashment = (gross ÷ 26) × avg accrued-leave days (slider); totals; downloads CSV. _Persist: none (slider local)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Payslip Portal (portal)** — self-service distribution: builds per-employee tokenised payslip link (`portal.headroom.in/payslip/<base64>` — illustrative), composes WhatsApp (`wa.me`) or email (`mailto:`) message with net pay, tracks per-employee IT-declaration submitted/pending. _Persist: KV — `payroll-it-declarations` (month/channel local)._ _Class: Preview._ (Portal links are illustrative tokens; opens compose windows, no backend send.)

- **Overtime & Shift (overtime)** — Factories Act §59: basic+DA proxy = 50% gross; ordinary hourly = basicDA ÷ monthly hours; OT rate = 2× ordinary; OT pay = OT rate × OT hours; night-shift pay = shifts × allowance; total additional to add to gross. _Persist: none (inputs transient)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Leave Encashment (leave-encash)** — per-employee earned/availed leave; balance = max(0, earned − availed); per-day = (basic+DA 50%) ÷ 26; encashment = perDay × balance; notes §10(10AA) ₹25L lifetime exemption cap. _Persist: KV — `payroll-leave-balances`._ _Class: KV._

- **Notice Recovery (notice)** — shortfall = max(0, required − served notice days); recovery base = gross or basic (50%) toggle; per-day = base ÷ 30; recovery/buyout = perDay × shortfall, netted against F&F. _Persist: none (inputs transient)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Salary Advance (advance)** — interest-free advance/loan tracker: issue capped at 3× monthly gross; EMI = ceil(principal ÷ tenure months); record EMI repayments; outstanding/cleared status; total outstanding + monthly recovery. _Persist: KV — `payroll-salary-advances`._ _Class: KV._

- **NPS Optimizer (nps)** — employer NPS 80CCD(2): basic+DA annual = 50% of annual gross; cap 14% (new) / 10% (old); contribution = basicDA × capped%; tax saved = contribution × marginal slab rate (new-regime proxy) × 1.04 cess; monthly outflow. _Persist: none (inputs transient)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Min-Wage Check (minwage)** — compares each gross against state minimum wage (8 states × Unskilled/SemiSkilled/Skilled, hardcoded ₹ figures) for chosen skill; flags shortfall and compliant/below-minimum status. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._ (Rates explicitly "indicative", revised twice yearly.)

- **Maternity Benefit (maternity)** — Maternity Benefit Act (2017): avg daily wage = (gross×3) ÷ 90; entitlement 26 weeks (12 if 3rd+ child); benefit = avgDaily × days; optional paternity (policy-driven) days × avgDaily; notes ESIC bears it if gross ≤ ₹21,000 (employer cash cost may be nil). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **People ROI (roi)** — people-cost-to-revenue ratio: fully-loaded = sum of gross + employer PF (12% of min(gross,15k)) + employer ESI (3.25% if ≤21k) + monthly gratuity accrual ((15/26)×50%-basic ÷12); ratio = loaded ÷ monthly revenue (input); cost/head, revenue/head, band (Lean ≤25% / Healthy ≤40% / Elevated ≤60% / High risk). _Persist: KV — `payroll-monthly-revenue`._ _Class: KV._

- **Take-Home Breakup (takehome)** — CTC → in-hand decomposition: carves employer PF (12% of min(basic,15k)) + gratuity (4.81% of basic) out of CTC to get gross; defaults CTC = gross×12×1.12; employee PF (12% of min(basic,15k)), PT ₹200, TDS = annual slab tax (new/old via `computeSlabTax`, std ded ₹75k/₹50k, rebate ≤7L/≤5L, +4% cess) ÷ 12 → net in-hand monthly + take-home % of CTC. _Persist: none (inputs transient)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Attrition Cost (attrition-cost)** — replacement-cost model per exit: annual CTC = gross×12×1.12; recruiting = CTC × recruitPct; ramp loss = gross × rampMonths × rampLossPct; vacancy loss = gross × backfillDays/30; onboarding = 0.5× gross; total + % of CTC + org projection (1-in-5 exits). Assumptions (recruitPct/rampMonths/rampLossPct/backfillDays) are KV. _Persist: KV — `payroll-attrition-assumptions` (selected employee local)._ _Class: KV._

- **Incentive Engine (incentive)** — variable-pay/commission: per-employee target & actual revenue; raw commission = actual × ratePct; payout = min(raw, monthlyGross × capPct) (capped flag); attainment %, effective payout rate; accrues into next run. _Persist: KV — `payroll-incentive-plan` + `payroll-incentive-achievement`._ _Class: KV._

- **Superannuation (superann)** — approved SAF projection: basic+DA = 50% gross; annual contribution = basicDA×12×rate (≤15%); taxable perquisite = max(0, contribution − ₹1.5L exempt); flags >₹7.5L combined PF+NPS+SAF cap u/s 17(2); corpus = future value of annuity over N years at assumed return; est. monthly pension = corpus×6%÷12. _Persist: none (inputs transient)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Group Insurance (gpa)** — GMC/GTL/GPA sizing & premium: GMC SI selectable (3–10L); GTL & GPA SI = annualCTC × multiple; indicative rates per ₹1L (GMC ₹4,500, GTL ₹120, GPA ₹80); total premium + 18% GST; optional employee-share % of GMC deducted via payroll; employer cost; per-head monthly deduction. _Persist: none (inputs transient)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **PF / ESI Challan (pf-challan)** — consolidated EPFO + ESIC challan for active employees: A/C 1 = EE EPF + ER EPF diff (12% less EPS), A/C 10 = EPS (8.33%, ≤₹1,250), A/C 21 = EDLI 0.50%, A/C 2 = admin 0.50% (min ₹500); ESIC = EE 0.75% + ER 3.25% on gross ≤₹21k; combined remittance + due date (15th of next month). _Persist: none (derived)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Payroll Register (register)** — one-page month MIS: per active employee gross, EPF (EE 12% of min(gross,15k)), ESI (EE 0.75% if ≤21k), configured TDS, total deductions, net; grand-total footer; exports CSV. _Persist: none (derived from master)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Penalty Predictor (penalty)** — late-deposit cost estimator: PF = 7Q interest 12% p.a. simple + 14B damages by delay band (5/10/15/25% p.a.); ESI = 12% p.a.; TDS = 1.5% per month (or part) u/s 201(1A) via ceil(days/30); effective cost % of challan. _Persist: none (inputs transient)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **LWP Impact (lwp)** — loss-of-pay modeller for one employee: per-day = gross ÷ calendar days in current month; LOP = perDay × clamped LWP days; prorated gross; recomputes EPF (12% of min(prorated,15k)) and ESI (0.75% if ≤21k) on prorated wage; TDS shown unchanged; full-vs-after net comparison before locking the run. _Persist: none (inputs transient)._ _Class: Indicative._

---

### Audit notes

- **Backend-real (4):** Employees, Payroll runs (run/disburse), EWA, plus Variance (reads live `store.transactions`). Add-Employee writes to backend.
- **KV-synced records (11):** ESOP, Attendance, Reimbursements, Contractor Payouts, Appraisal hikes, Planned Hires (headcount), Leave balances, Salary Advances, IT declarations (portal), People-ROI revenue, Attrition assumptions, Incentive plan/achievement — all via `useFeatureState` → `store.featureData` → localStorage + debounced backend push + cross-device sync.
- **Preview / not-wired (1–2):** EWA payout carries a `PreviewBadge` (capability `ewaPayout`); Payslip Portal links are illustrative tokens and channels open compose windows only (no backend send).
- **Everything else is Indicative** — pure client-side statutory/HR math derived from the employee master and transient form inputs; outputs are CSV/PDF/clipboard exports, not posted to the ledger. PF wage ceiling ₹15,000, ESI eligibility ₹21,000 (EE 0.75% / ER 3.25%), gratuity 15/26 with ₹20L cap, bonus 8.33–20% on ₹7,000 calc ceiling, EPS 8.33% capped ₹1,250, EDLI/admin 0.50%, standard deduction ₹75k (new)/₹50k (old), 87A rebate ≤7L (new)/≤5L (old) are applied consistently. Several rate tables (min wages, PT slabs, salary benchmarks, insurance premiums, LWF) are hardcoded and flagged in-UI as indicative/verify-with-CA.

## ━━━ PILLAR 3 · Money, Cash & Books ━━━

# Banking & Cash (`/banking`) — 32 tools

Give an SMB owner a single India-first console for every rupee across every bank — consolidated balances, statement reconciliation, rail/limit guidance, fee and idle-cash recovery, and India-specific banking workflows (NACH, positive-pay, BG/LC, OD/CC, cheque clearing). Stakeholders: owner, finance. Backend: KV-synced (reads `store.bankAccounts` / `store.transactions`; per-tool inputs persist via `useFeatureState`; nothing writes to the live ledger).

- **Overview** — reads `store.bankAccounts` (count, balances, status) → KPI cards (total cash, connected feeds `connected/total`, feed issues = `status==="error"`, largest balance), plus an "accounts at a glance" table with each account's balance, share `balance/totalBalance` %, status chip and last-sync; empty-state prompts linking from Data module. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Multi-Bank Balances** (`BalanceDashboard`) — reads accounts + adjustable concentration cap slider (10–100%, default 40) → sorts accounts largest-first, computes each share `balance/total`, flags accounts over the cap (DICGC ₹5L/bank note), KPIs (total, count, avg per account, over-concentrated count) and a warn callout to spread/sweep surplus. _Persist: live store (cap = local state)._ _Class: KV._
- **Reconciliation** (`ReconciliationWorkbench`) — account selector + pasted statement lines (`date, amount, narration`; Dr/Cr or minus = debit, parsed by `parseStatement`) + amount tolerance (₹) → greedy first-available match of statement lines vs `store.transactions` by amount within tolerance; outputs matched / on-statement-not-in-books / in-books-not-on-statement counts and lists. _Persist: live store reads; paste/tolerance = local state, no ledger write._ _Class: KV._
- **Daily Cash Position** (`DailyCashPosition`) — opening = sum of account balances; today's inflows/outflows from `store.transactions` filtered to today's date; user-added pending debits/holds (label, amount, clears-on) → available now = balance − pending holds; warn callout when holds exceed balance (returned-cheque risk). _Persist: `bank-pending-debits` (KV); balances live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Sweep Planner** (`SweepPlanner`) — account + buffer-to-retain + FD/sweep rate (% p.a., default 6.5) + current-a/c yield (% p.a., default 3) → sweepable = `max(0, balance − buffer)`, FD annual = `sweepable·fdR/100`, idle yield = `sweepable·ccR/100`, extra yield = difference; ok callout suggesting daily sweep-in/out. _Persist: live store (balance); rates = local state._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Virtual Accounts** (`VirtualAccountTracker`) — customer name + expected inflow → issues a deterministic synthetic VA number (`genVa`: hash of name+uuid → `VA` + 10 digits); tracks expected vs received per customer, "Mark received" sets received=expected; KPIs (VA count, total expected, total received). _Persist: `bank-virtual-accounts` (KV)._ _Class: Simulated (VA numbers are locally generated, not bank-issued)._
- **Bank-Fee Analyzer** (`BankFeeAnalyzer`) — scans `store.transactions` debits whose description matches `FEE_KEYWORDS` (charge/chrg/fee/neft chg/rtgs chg/imps/amc/min bal/gst on/commission/penal…) + benchmark bps input (default 15) → total fees, fees as bps of total debits, expected fees = `debits·bps/10000`, overcharge, fees-by-type bar breakdown; warn callout to negotiate with RM. _Persist: live store reads; benchmark = local state._ _Class: KV._
- **Payment Rail** (`PaymentRailChooser`) — amount + urgent checkbox → filters static `RAILS` (UPI ≤₹1L free 24x7; IMPS ≤₹5L ₹5–15+GST; NEFT no-limit batch ₹2–25+GST; RTGS ≥₹2L ₹20–50+GST) by per-txn min/max, then ranks (urgent: prefer instant by order upi>imps>rtgs>neft; else cheapest upi>neft>imps>rtgs); recommends one rail + comparison table with eligibility ticks. _Persist: none (static table + local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Cheque Register** (`ChequeRegister`) — cheque no., party, amount, type (issued/received), date → adds cheque at status `issued`; click status chip cycles `issued→presented→cleared→bounced→cancelled`; KPIs (pending count, issued-pending outflow, bounced count); warn callout citing NI Act Sec 138 on bounced cheques. _Persist: `bank-cheques` (KV)._ _Class: KV._
- **Charge Recovery** (`ChargeRecoveryTracker`) — charge description, amount, raised-on date → logs dispute at `open`; per-row "Recovered"/"Rejected" buttons set status; KPIs (open count, amount-in-flight = open total, recovered total); notes RBI TAT on failed-txn reversals. _Persist: `bank-charge-disputes` (KV)._ _Class: KV._
- **Idle-Balance Alert** (`IdleBalanceAlert`) — operating buffer per account + achievable yield (% p.a., default 6.5) → per account: idle = `max(0, balance − buffer)`, days-since-activity from latest matching txn date, foregone annual = `idle·rate/100`; totals + warn callout pointing to Sweep Planner. _Persist: live store (balances/txns); buffer/yield = local state._ _Class: KV._
- **Positive Pay** (`PositivePayRegister`) — adjustable threshold (default ₹5L) + cheque no., payee, amount, date → cheques ≥ threshold flagged as needing Positive Pay; per-row Confirm toggle; KPIs (logged, need-PP, not-confirmed); warn callout citing RBI ₹50k mandate and return risk. _Persist: `bank-positive-pay` + `bank-positive-pay-threshold` (KV)._ _Class: KV._
- **NACH Mandates** (`MandateTracker`) — UMRN/ref, party, amount, frequency (monthly/quarterly/yearly), next-debit → tracks recurring auto-debits; equivalent monthly outflow = sum of active mandates × frequency factor (1, 1/3, 1/12); Pause/Resume/Cancel/Remove actions; KPIs (active, total, equiv monthly outflow). _Persist: `bank-nach-mandates` (KV)._ _Class: KV._
- **BG / LC Limits** (`GuaranteeLimitTracker`) — adjustable sanctioned non-fund limit (default ₹50L) + facility type (BG/LC), beneficiary, amount, expiry → utilised = sum of facilities, available = `max(0, limit − utilised)`, utilisation %, per-row days-to-expiry (red <0, amber ≤30); warn callouts at >90% utilisation and for facilities expiring within 30 days. _Persist: `bank-bg-lc` + `bank-bg-lc-limit` (KV)._ _Class: KV._
- **OD/CC Interest** (`OdInterestCalculator`) — sanctioned limit, amount drawn, rate (% p.a.), days drawn → utilisation = `drawn/limit`, daily interest = `drawn·rate/100/365`, period/monthly(30d)/annual interest, headroom = `limit − drawn`; warn callout at >90% utilisation re penal interest. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Statement Import** (`StatementImporter`) — pasted statement lines (`parseStatement`) auto-categorized via `IMPORT_RULES` (salary/tax/bank-charge/transfer/vendor; else income if credit, otherwise other) → by-category count+total breakdown, total credits/debits, parsed line table; explicitly does not write to the ledger. _Persist: none (local paste)._ _Class: KV._
- **Beneficiaries** (`BeneficiaryWhitelist`) — name, account number, optional IFSC (validated `^[A-Z]{4}0[A-Z0-9]{6}$`) → dedupes by account, stores unverified; manual "Mark verified/unverified" toggle (penny-drop name-check by user); KPIs (whitelisted, verified, awaiting); warn callout to verify before paying. _Persist: `bank-beneficiaries` (KV)._ _Class: KV (verification is manual, not a real penny-drop)._
- **Transfer Planner** (`FundTransferPlanner`) — target balance per account → computes surplus (`balance − target` >0) and deficit accounts, greedily generates minimum top-up moves from largest-surplus to largest-deficit; KPIs (below target, surplus, transfers) + suggested-moves list; ok callout if all met / no surplus. Requires ≥2 accounts. _Persist: live store (balances); target = local state._ _Class: KV._
- **Min-Balance Check** (`MinBalanceChecker`) — adjustable penalty per ₹1,000 shortfall (default 60) + per account: name (datalist of accounts), required AMB, maintained AMB → shortfall = `max(0, required − maintained)`, penalty = `(shortfall/1000)·penaltyPer1000`; KPIs (breaching count, est. penalty/month); warn callout to top up / switch to ZBA / close redundant accounts. _Persist: `bank-mab` + `bank-mab-penalty` (KV); maintained AMB entered manually._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Savings Interest** (`SavingsInterestEstimator`) — average balance + savings rate (% p.a., default 3) + months → quarterly-compounded maturity = `p·(1+r/400)^(months/3)`, interest, effective annualized yield, indicative TDS (10% u/s 194A if interest >₹40k); ok callout comparing to liquid fund/sweep FD. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Payment Date** (`PaymentDatePicker`) — intended date + extra holiday dates (against `BANK_HOLIDAYS_2026` set + Sundays + 2nd/4th Saturdays) → rolls forward up to 40 days to next working day, listing each skipped day with reason; KPIs (effective working date, days skipped); ok callout if already a working day. _Persist: none (static holiday list + local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Balance Confirmation** (`BalanceConfirmationGenerator`) — account + entity/holder name + as-on date → drafts a bank balance-confirmation request letter (for statutory audit) with provider, account name and book balance pre-filled; Copy-to-clipboard button. _Persist: live store (account/balance); entity/date = local state._ _Class: KV._
- **Forex Spread** (`ForexSpreadTracker`) — per deal: currency, foreign amount, INR received, reference rate, date → bank rate = `inrReceived/foreign`, spread (paise/unit and %), cost vs reference = `(refRate − bankRate)·foreign`; KPIs (count, total spread cost, avg spread %); note that >~0.5–1% spread is high. _Persist: `bank-fx-deals` (KV)._ _Class: KV._
- **Duplicate Payments** (`DuplicatePaymentDetector`) — adjustable match window (1–30 days, default 7) → groups `store.transactions` debits by `roundedAmount|counterparty`, flags groups with ≥2 txns where consecutive dates fall within the window; potential over-payment = `amount·(count−1)` summed; per-group txn lists; note re legitimate repeats. _Persist: live store reads; window = local state._ _Class: KV._
- **Counterparty Netting** (`CounterpartyNetting`) — per party: receivable (they owe) + payable (you owe) → net = `receivable − payable`, gross-to-settle = sum of both legs, net-to-settle = sum of `|net|`, cash movement saved = gross − net; per-row action (collect/pay/square-off net); ok callout on savings. _Persist: `bank-netting-parties` (KV)._ _Class: KV._
- **Interest Certificates** (`InterestCertificateTracker`) — per cert: bank, type (fd/savings/loan/od), FY, amount → splits into taxable interest income (fd+savings) and deductible interest paid (loan+od); per-row Received/Pending toggle; KPIs (pending count, income, paid); warn callout to collect before filing. _Persist: `bank-interest-certs` (KV)._ _Class: KV._
- **A/C Opening Docs** (`AccountOpeningChecklist`) — entity type (proprietorship/partnership/company) selects a static RBI-KYC `ENTITY_DOCS` list → checkbox progress per `entityType:key`, completed count and % bar; ok callout at 100%. _Persist: `bank-acopen-checklist` (KV)._ _Class: Preview (static checklist)._
- **Cash Runway** (`CashRunwayMeter`) — cash = sum of balances + adjustable window (1–12 months, default 3) → from `store.transactions` in window: net burn = `(outflow − inflow)/months`, gross burn = `outflow/months`, runway months = `cash/netBurn` (∞ if cash-positive), runway days; tiered ok/warn callout (<3 mo = tight). _Persist: live store; window = local state._ _Class: KV._
