- **Deposit Slip** (`CashDepositSlip`) — account + depositor + date + note counts per `DENOMS` (500/200/100/50/20/10) → per-denom value = `denom·count`, total notes and total amount; Copy-slip builds a text pay-in slip to clipboard. _Persist: live store (account); counts = local state._ _Class: KV._
- **Clearing Tracker** (`ClearingTracker`) — instrument (local-cheque CTS T+1 / outstation ~T+3 / NEFT-inward same-day / cash@CDM same-day), ref, amount, deposit date → expected credit via `addWorkingDays` (skips Sat/Sun) per `CLEAR_TAT`; cleared if past; KPIs (still clearing, funds in float = uncleared total, cleared); per-row "Nd to go"/"Clears today"/"Cleared"; note holidays ignored. _Persist: `bank-clearing-tat` (KV)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **OD Renewal** (`OdRenewalReminder`) — per facility: bank, type (cc/od), sanctioned limit, renewal date + adjustable lead-time slider (15–90 days, default 45) → days-to-renewal, due if `days≤leadDays`, overdue if `days<0`; KPIs (facilities, total limit, due-within-window); warn callout to collate financials/stock statements 3–6 weeks ahead. _Persist: `bank-od-renewals` (KV); leadDays = local state._ _Class: KV._
- **Spend by Bank** (`SpendByBank`) — groups all `store.transactions` by resolved account name (or "Unassigned") → per bank: debit/credit/count/net; KPIs (total outflow, accounts with activity, busiest account), outflow-distribution bars (`debit/totalDebit`) and a full per-bank table. _Persist: live store reads only._ _Class: KV._
## Working Capital (`/working-capital`) — 24 tools

One-sentence purpose: turn the SMB's live ledger (DSO/DIO/DPO, AR/inventory/payables/cash) into cash-conversion-cycle diagnostics, bank-finance sizing (drawing power, MPBF/Tandon), and discount-vs-borrow / factoring-vs-OD funding decisions. Stakeholders: owner, finance. Backend: KV-synced (`store` snapshot drives every tool; a handful of input parameters persist via `useFeatureState` → `store.featureData`, localStorage + debounced backend sync; all other inputs are transient `useState`).

All tools derive from `computeFinancialSnapshot(store)` (DSO, DIO, DPO, CCC, AR, inventory, payables, cash, overdue AR, monthlyExpense, monthlyRevenue, currentRatio, quickRatio, netWorkingCapital, workingCapitalGap, obligationsDue90) — i.e. read live from the synced store, not re-persisted.

- **Overview (Working-Capital Snapshot + Financing + Simulators)** — store snapshot + `agingBuckets(invoices)`, `financingOptions(gap, AR)`, `earlyPayAnnualizedReturn(disc,days)`, `paymentTermsSuggestions(store)` → KPI strip (CCC=DSO+DIO−DPO, DSO/DIO/DPO), funding-gap callout (gap ≈ months×opex), cycle bar visual, AR-ageing chart, early-pay APR table (discount/(1−discount)×365/days; worth if >18%), financing-options table ranked by effective annual cost, cash-release simulator (15d DSO / 10d DIO / 10d DPO each freeing days/30 × monthlyExpense), terms-negotiator (per-party cash impact), WC ratios (current/quick/NWC/cycle-funding). _Persist: live store (derived; no writes)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **CCC Dashboard** — `peerKey` select (5 sector medians) + snapshot legs → CCC=DSO+DIO−DPO; deterministic 6-month back-projection (factor 1.18→1.0, DPO inverse), vs-peer delta, cash impact = (your CCC − peer CCC) × daily opex; per-leg you-vs-peer table. _Persist: none (peerKey transient)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Inventory Days Optimizer** — `targetDio`, `carryPct` (default 22) → daily COGS = inventoryValue/DIO; targetInventory = dailyCOGS×target; cashReleased = inventory − target; annualCarrySaving = released×carry; redeploy rows (14% OD / 18% discounting / carry). _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Payables Trade-off (Payables-Stretch vs Early-Pay Discount)** — `spend`, `discPct`, `discDays`, `netDays`, `stretchTo`, `borrowPct` → earlyApr = earlyPayAnnualizedReturn (discount-vs-borrow); discountSaved = spend×disc; stretch cashHeld = spend/30×extraDays, stretchValue; verdict: take discount if APR > cost of capital, else stretch (with MSME 43B(h) caution). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **OD/CC Utilisation & Drawing-Power Tracker** — `limit`, `drawn`, `stockMargin` (25), `debtorMargin` (40) → drawing power = inventory×(1−sMargin) + AR×(1−dMargin); effectiveLimit = min(sanctioned, DP); utilisation%, headroom, overdrawn flag, DP-shortfall warning, drawing-power build-up table. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **WC Gap & Funding (Funding-Need Quantifier)** — `marginPct` (25) → currentAssets = inventory+AR; WC gap = max(0, CA − payables); ownMargin = gap×margin; permissibleBankFinance = gap − ownMargin (Tandon II); funding-mix bar; cross-check vs cycle gap; `financingOptions` table on bank-finance need. _Persist: none (marginPct transient)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Trade-Cycle Optimizer** — `tDso`, `tDio`, `tDpo` sliders → targetCCC = DSO+DIO−DPO; daysSaved = current CCC − target; cashFreed = daysSaved × daily opex; verdict band. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Discount vs Borrow (Dynamic Discounting)** — `invoiceAmt`, `discPct`, `daysEarly`, `borrowPct` → apr = earlyPayAnnualizedReturn; discountSaved = amt×disc; borrowCostToPayEarly = amt×borrow×early/365; netBenefit; take if APR > borrow rate. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Seasonal WC Planner** — `peakLift` (25) over fixed 12-month Indian demand index → scaled = idx + (idx−1)×lift; monthly WC = baseWC×scaled; peak/trough/extra-line-at-peak; year bar chart vs base line. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Net WC Trend** — snapshot → deterministic 6-month back-projection of NWC = AR+inventory−payables (factor 1.2→1.0, AP inverse); MoM change, NWC % of annual revenue, cash tied up; multi-line component chart. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Cash Locked in WC** — snapshot → gross = AR+inventory; netLocked = gross − payables; releasable = all overdue AR + 20% inventory + 15% payables (term extension); component bars with levers. _Persist: live store (derived)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **AR Acceleration (Receivables Acceleration Planner)** — `discPct`, `uptakePct` (40) + `agingBuckets` → per-bucket recovery rates [0.95,0.85,0.7,0.5,0.3], pullForward = amount×rate; discountCost = AR×uptake×disc; netCashPulled; daysShortened = net ÷ daily opex; bucket action table. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Liquidity Ratios Tracker** — snapshot (cash, AR, inventory, payables, obligationsDue90) → currentRatio = CA/CL, quickRatio = (cash+AR)/CL, cashRatio = cash/CL (CL = payables + dues≤90d); target bars (1.5/1.0/0.5x); component table. _Persist: live store (derived)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **MPBF (Tandon)** — `otherCa`, `otherCl` (both KV-persisted) → CA = inventory+AR+otherCa; WC gap = CA − (payables+otherCl); Method I MPBF = gap − 25%×gap (CR≈1.17); Method II MPBF = CA − OCL − 25%×CA (CR≈1.33); implied NWC + current ratios; Method-I-vs-II table. _Persist: KV — `wc-mpbf-other-ca`, `wc-mpbf-other-cl`._ _Class: KV._
- **Stock Statement (Stock & Book-Debt Generator)** — `stockMargin` (25), `debtorMargin` (40), `excludeOver90` (yes), `creditors` (all KV) → paidStock = inventory − creditors; eligibleStock = paidStock×(1−sMargin); eligibleDebtors (excl >90d via overdue) ×(1−dMargin); drawingPower = sum; copy-to-clipboard for bank submission. _Persist: KV — `wc-stockstmt-stock-margin`, `wc-stockstmt-debtor-margin`, `wc-stockstmt-excl90`, `wc-stockstmt-creditors`._ _Class: KV._
- **Debtor Financing (Bill-Discounting Eligibility)** — `advancePct` (80, KV), `discountRatePct` (13, KV), `tenorDays`, `processingPct` → eligible = AR − overdue; advance = eligible×advance; interestCost = advance×rate×tenor/365; processingCost; effectiveAnnualCost = total/advance×365/tenor; days-of-runway. _Persist: KV — `wc-debtorfin-advance`, `wc-debtorfin-rate` (tenor/processing transient)._ _Class: KV._
- **Creditor Stretch (Creditor-Stretch Impact)** — `extraDays`, `msmeSharePct` (30) → dailyPurchases = payables/DPO; cashFreed = dailyPurchases×extraDays; newDPO, newCCC; MSME 43B(h) risk if newDPO>45 (msmeAtRisk = payables×share); annualSaving = freed×14%. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **WC Turnover** — snapshot → annualRevenue = monthlyRevenue×12; NWC = AR+inventory−payables; turnover = revenue/NWC; WC per ₹ sales; efficiency band (≥6/≥4/≥2.5/<2.5); leaner-by-10-days scenario. _Persist: live store (derived)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Factoring vs OD** — `need`, `tenorDays`, `odRatePct` (14), `factorRatePct` (12), `factorFeePct` (1), `recourse` → odCost = amt×odRate×tenor/365; factorCost = amt×factorRate×tenor/365 + amt×fee; effective annualised each; cheaper-of verdict + recourse note. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Operating Cycle Breakdown** — snapshot → operatingCycle = DSO+DIO; supplierFunded = min(DPO, cycle); selfFunded = max(0, cycle−DPO); supplierCover%; selfFundedCash = daily opex × selfFunded; segment bars. _Persist: live store (derived)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Current-Ratio Planner** — `targetRatio` (1.5) → ratio = CA/CL (CA = cash+AR+inventory, CL = payables+dues≤90d); Lever A extraAssets = target×CL − CA; Lever B paydown = CL − CA/target; meets-target headroom. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Terms Gap (Supplier vs Customer)** — snapshot → termsGap = DSO − DPO; gapCash = daily opex × |gap|; favourable if ≤0 (free float) else cash you bridge; DSO/DPO rows. _Persist: live store (derived)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **WC Efficiency Score** — snapshot → composite 0–100 of four 0–25 sub-scores: CCC (0d→25, 90d→0), receivables quality (0%→25, 40% overdue→0), supplier-credit cover (DPO ÷ operating cycle ×25), current ratio (1.5x→25, 0.5x→0); grade Excellent/Healthy/Average/Strained; driver bars. _Persist: live store (derived)._ _Class: Indicative._
## Cash-Flow Forecast (`/forecast`) — 24 tools

Cash-flow planning workspace: a probabilistic 90-day projection plus 23 specialised forecasting/planning tools (run-rate, AR/AP timing, seasonality, capex, GST, runway) for an Indian SMB. Stakeholders: owner, finance, CA. Backend: deterministic client-side `forecastEngine` (Monte-Carlo, unit-tested) + `finance.ts` aggregates; durable records via the synced `store.featureData` KV bag (localStorage + backend sync).

- **Probabilistic Forecast (main tab / `runForecast` + `generateForecast`)** — store (transactions, bank balances, active scenarios) + slow-month slider (0–100% revenue) + burn-inflation slider (80–150%) → Monte-Carlo engine produces P10/P50/P90 paths with scenarios + revenue/burn factors baked into both bands; derives risk strip (breach probability, expected time-to-pressure, Cash-Flow-at-Risk 95%, likely runway P50/P10), a 45-day-early pressure alert, and an obligation-marker overlay. "Generate" persists the BASE (no-scenario) forecast for the dashboard; the page chart recomputes live. _Persist: `store.forecast` (KV, base only) + `slowPct`/`burnFactor` live UI state._ _Class: Backend._
- **Scenarios manager** — name, type (new_hire / contract_won / loan_draw / custom), amount, start date → stored scenario list; active scenarios fold into the Monte-Carlo forecast and the Best/Base/Worst tool. Toggle active / delete. _Persist: `store.scenarios` (live store)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Cash Obligations manager** — name, amount, due date → obligation list rendered as red reference lines on the main chart and scheduled into 13-week / AR-AP tools. Auto-adds a `GSTR-3B` tax obligation (last-month revenue × firm GST rate, due 20th) on forecast generation if GST-registered. _Persist: `store.obligations` (live store)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Ask AI (forecast insight)** — current balance, monthly burn, P10 runway, active scenario → POST `/api/ai/ask` with a cash-advisor system prompt → 3–4 sentence explanation + 2 suggestions; falls back to a "check ANTHROPIC_API_KEY" message on error. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend (LLM)._
- **Remind me (obligations)** — upcoming obligations → schedules on-device notifications 1 day before each due date via `scheduleReminders` (native only); cancels prior IDs first. _Persist: OS notification store (device)._ _Class: Backend (native)._
- **Notify me (overdue invoices)** — invoices marked overdue or past due date → schedules immediate on-device reminders per invoice (native only). _Persist: OS notification store (device)._ _Class: Backend (native)._
- **13-Week Rolling Cash Forecast (#76)** — bank opening balance + trailing-90-day variable run-rate (weekly base in/out, transfers excluded) + open invoices (collected the week their due-date falls in, overdue→week 0) + dated obligations + active-loan EMIs (every ~4 weeks from next payment date) → 13 weekly rows of inflow/outflow/net/closing, totals, lowest weekly close, and a safety threshold = firm `safetyThresholdDays` × daily burn; rows breaching the buffer are flagged red. _Persist: none (live recompute)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Receivables-Driven Inflow Projection (#77)** — open invoices: each invoice's expected collection date (due-date + slip; healthy 7d, overdue 7 + 0.3×daysOverdue capped 45d) × pay-probability (current 92%; overdue logistic decay 0.85 − daysOverdue/250, floor 20%) → expected-value rows bucketed by month, gross vs probability-weighted chart, gross/expected/at-risk totals. Empty state if no open invoices. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Seasonality Detector (#78)** — transaction history (transfers excluded) grouped by calendar month → per-month revenue/expense indexed to the average of active months (=100), peak/trough revenue month, seasonality strength from coefficient-of-variation (Strong >0.35 / Moderate >0.15 / Weak). Needs ≥3 distinct active months. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Best / Base / Worst Projection (#79)** — active scenarios + optimism slider (5–40%) + pessimism slider (5–50%) → re-runs `runForecast` three times (best: +optimism revenue / leaner burn; base: 1×/1×; worst: −pessimism revenue, floor 0.4× / +pessimism burn), plots P50 of each over 90 days, with ending-cash + runway per line. _Persist: none (live UI sliders)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Cash Buffer / Minimum-Balance Alert (#80)** — base `runForecast` result + user-set floor (defaults to engine `thresholdCash` ≈ 14-day burn, rounded ₹1L) → first day P10 and P50 paths dip below the floor, worst-case low, breach risk %; green/red verdict and floor-overlay chart. _Persist: `forecast-min-balance-floor` (KV)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Revenue Forecast (#81)** — trailing-3-month average revenue run-rate × monthly growth (seeded from firm CMGR via `cmgr`, clamped −10..20, overridable −10..20%) over a 3–24 month horizon → projected monthly revenue line (actual vs projected) and horizon total. _Persist: `fc-rev-growth-pct` (KV); horizon live UI._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Expense Forecast (#82)** — trailing-6-month average outflow per category (expense / payroll / tax / loan; transfers & inflows excluded) × single annual-inflation dial (0–20%) compounded across a 3–24 month horizon → per-category base table + projected monthly total expense, horizon total. _Persist: none (inflation & horizon live UI)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Headcount-Cost Forecast (#83)** — current payroll run-rate (trailing-3-month payroll average) + planned-role roster (title, monthly cost, count, start month) → 12-month people-cost ramp (each role adds from its start month), fully-loaded month-12 cost, planned-hire count. _Persist: `fc-planned-roles` (KV); add-form fields live UI._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Burn-Rate & Zero-Cash Date (#84)** — trailing-6-month average net (negated → burn) × burn-change dial (−50..+50%) against current bank cash → months of runway, straight-line zero-cash date, cash-depletion chart (≤18 months); red alert if runway <6 months. _Persist: none (burn-adj live UI)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Monthly Cash-Bridge Waterfall (#85)** — direct-method `monthlyCashFlow` ledger for a chosen month (slider over last 6) → waterfall: opening → receipts → suppliers → payroll → taxes → financing → closing, plus opening/operating-net/financing-net/closing stat cards. Empty state if no cash-flow data. _Persist: none (month-index live UI)._ _Class: Backend._
- **AR / AP Timing Forecast (#86)** — open invoices (by due-week) vs obligations + active-loan EMIs (every ~4 weeks) over 13 weeks → weekly receivables/payables/net, totals, count of "tight" weeks (AP > AR); paired bar chart + table with negative-net weeks flagged red. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Fixed vs Variable Cost Projection (#87)** — trailing-6-month outflows split into fixed (payroll / loan / tax / `isRecurring`) vs variable, variable scaled by an activity dial (40–160%) → 12-month stacked fixed/variable chart, total/month, fixed-share % with high-operating-leverage flag (>70%). _Persist: none (activity live UI)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Break-Even Cash Date (#88)** — trailing average revenue/expense + monthly revenue-growth assumption (−5..15%) → 24-month monthly-net + cumulative-cash projection, pinpointing the month net first turns positive; "already positive" / "24+ months" edge cases with a red warning if never reached. _Persist: none (growth live UI)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Rolling 12-Month P&L Forecast (#89)** — trailing-3-month revenue/expense run-rates grown by separate revenue (CMGR-seeded, overridable −10..20%) and expense (−5..10%) dials → 12-month revenue/expense/net/cumulative table + chart, 12-mo totals and net margin. _Persist: none (both growth dials live UI)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Capex / Funding Plan (#90)** — capex items (name, amount, month 1–12, loan-funded %) + trailing-6-month net run-rate against current cash → 12-month cash projection net of self-funded outlays, lowest projected cash, cash-safest month, below-zero breach warning. _Persist: `fc-capex-items` (KV); add-form fields live UI._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Owner Draw / Dividend Planner (#91)** — trailing-6-month net run-rate × draw-% dial (0–100%, never drawn from a loss) against current cash, keeping a `safetyThresholdDays`×daily-burn reserve floor → safe monthly/annual draw, 12-month buffer projection, red/green verdict on whether the draw erodes the floor. _Persist: `fc-owner-draw-pct` (KV)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Credit-Sale Aging Forecast (#92)** — open invoices bucketed by days overdue (Current / 1–30 / 31–60 / 61–90 / 90+) × bucket collection-probability curve (92% → 80 → 60 → 42 → 25%) → gross vs expected per bucket, total/overdue outstanding, expected-to-collect. Empty state if no open credit sales. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Forecast Accuracy Tracker (#93)** — walk-forward back-test: predict each month's revenue as its trailing-3-month average vs actual → MAPE, bias (over/under-forecast), 0–100 accuracy score with A–D grade, predicted-vs-actual chart + per-month error table. Needs ≥2 testable months. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend._
- **Product / Service Mix Forecast (#94)** — product lines (name, units/month, unit price, MoM unit growth −20..30%) → bottom-up 12-month revenue projection (units compounded by growth × price), month-1 + 12-mo revenue, top-line concentration % with >60% red flag. _Persist: `fc-products` (KV); add-form fields live UI._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Weekly Cash Calendar** — opening-cash input + recurring expense run-rate (÷4.3 → weekly out) vs invoices due each week over 8 weeks → running balance, lowest weekly balance, weeks-in-deficit, 8-week net swing; balance chart + per-week breakdown. _Persist: `fc-weekly-opening-cash` (KV)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Vendor Payment-Timing Forecast** — scheduled vendor payments (vendor, amount, due-in-days 0–180) → 6-month outflow profile bucketed by due month, total committed, heaviest payment month; monthly outflow bar chart. _Persist: `fc-vendor-payments` (KV); add-form fields live UI._ _Class: Indicative._
- **GST Payment Forecast** — trailing-6-month average revenue × firm GST rate (default 18%) × revenue-growth dial (−10..20%) → 6-month projected monthly GST outflow (due 20th), next-month GST, 6-month total. Gated: "not GST registered" empty state unless `firm.gstRegistered`. _Persist: `fc-gst-rev-growth` (KV)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Runway with Pipeline** — trailing-6-month burn (avgExp − avgRev) against current cash + pipeline deals (name, value, win %) → risk-weighted inflow (value × win%) layered in to extend base runway; cash, burn, base runway vs with-pipeline runway, weighted uplift. _Persist: `fc-pipeline-deals` (KV); add-form fields live UI._ _Class: Indicative._
# Audit Appendix — Wealth & Treasury Module

## Wealth & Treasury (`/treasury`) — 34 tools

Turns idle current-account cash into risk-graded, tax-efficient yield via sweeps, FD/RD ladders, T-bills/G-Secs, goal buckets, allocators and tax/return calculators. Stakeholders: owner, finance. Backend: `/api/treasury` + KV-synced (durable records persist through `useFeatureState` → `store.featureData[<key>]` in the "app" KV namespace, written to localStorage and pushed/polled to the backend; pure calculators hold transient `useState` only).

### Tab-selector array (`src/features/treasury/TreasuryPage.tsx`, lines 48–83) — 34 entries, in order

`overview` · `sweep` · `ladder` · `compare` · `allocate` · `yield` · `tbill` · `goal` · `split` · `posttax` · `maturity` · `sip` · `debteq` · `emergency` · `sweepfd` · `corpfd` · `smallsave` · `swod` · `income` · `capgain` · `rebalance` · `xirr` · `waterfall` · `gold` · `reit` · `mtm` · `dicgc` · `policy` · `accrued` · `almatch` · `incfcast` · `liqtier` · `ylq` · `oppcost`

(The `Tab` union type at lines 20–26 lists the same 34 ids. Every id maps to exactly one rendered component at lines 92–125 — 34 tools total. UI-state filter tokens such as `182`/`360`/`balanced`/`rd`/`fd`/`91`/`364` are mode toggles inside individual tools, not separate tools, and are excluded per spec.)

### Tools

- **Overview** — at-a-glance idle-cash dashboard: inputs → reads `store.bankAccounts` for total cash; estimates operating buffer = `max(₹5L, 35% of total)`; investable surplus = `total − buffer`; yield forgone/yr = `investable × (7% − 3%)`; plus a static horizon→instrument→yield deployment guide (0–7d liquid, 1–6m short-debt/FD, 6m–3y corp-FD/G-Sec/T-bill) → 4 KPI cards + guidance grid. _Persist: none (derived from live `store.bankAccounts`)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Idle-Cash Sweep Planner** — sizes a runway buffer then sweeps overflow into a liquid/overnight fund: inputs current cash, weekly opex, liquid-fund yield %, buffer weeks (2–16 slider), sweep % (0–100 slider) → buffer = `opex × weeks` (or 35% of cash if no opex), overflow = `cash − buffer`, sweep = `overflow × sweep%`, extra yield = `sweep × yield%` → 4 KPI cards + sweep recommendation (notes liquid funds redeem T+1, ₹50k/25% instant). _Persist: none (seeded from `totalBalance`)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **FD / RD Ladder Builder** — splits a lump sum (FD) or monthly instalment (RD) across staggered maturities: inputs amount, rate %, longest tenure (months), rungs (2–8 slider), FD/RD mode toggle → per rung step = `maxTenure/rungs`; FD maturity = `perRung × (1+r/4)^(4·years)` (quarterly compounding); RD FV = annuity-due `perRung × ((1+i)^m − 1)/i × (1+i)` with `i = r/12` (monthly instalments, approx) → totals (invested/maturity/interest) + per-rung table with maturity dates. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Liquid Fund vs FD Comparator** — after-tax yield face-off: inputs amount, horizon (months), slab %, FD rate %, liquid rate % → FD gross = `P·(1+fd/4)^(4·yrs) − P` taxed at slab; liquid gross = `P·(1+liq)^yrs − P` taxed at slab (post-Apr-2023, no indexation, tax deferred to redemption) → two net-gain cards with BETTER flag + delta and timing note. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Surplus-Cash Allocator** — risk-graded tier split: inputs surplus ₹, tier toggle (conservative/balanced/growth) → applies a hardcoded weight×assumed-yield mix per tier (e.g. balanced = 40% liquid / 35% short-debt / 15% AAA corp-FD / 10% G-Sec/SGB); blended yield = `Σ(weight×yield)/100`, annual return = `surplus × blended` → blended-yield + annual-return cards + per-bucket allocation bars. _Persist: none (seeded from 50% of `totalBalance`)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Treasury Yield Calculator** — compound-interest engine: inputs principal, nominal rate %, years, compounding frequency (annual/half-yearly/quarterly/monthly) → FV = `P·(1+r/n)^(n·t)`, interest = `FV − P`, effective yield = `(FV/P)^(1/t) − 1` → maturity/interest/effective-yield cards. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **T-Bill / G-Sec Return Estimator** — zero-coupon sovereign discount instrument: inputs face value, discount/cut-off yield %, tenor toggle (91/182/364-day) → price = `F/(1 + dr·days/365)`, gain = `F − price`, annualised yield = `(gain/price)·(365/days)·100` → purchase price / redemption / gain / annualised-yield cards (notes RBI Retail Direct, gain taxed as interest). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Goal-Based Savings Planner** — builds yield-bearing buckets toward dated targets (GST, advance-tax, capex, bonus): inputs goal name, target ₹, already-saved ₹, deadline date, yield % → months left = `days/30`, gap = `target − saved`, required monthly = growth annuity `gap·i/((1+i)^m − 1)` with `i = rate/12/100` → per-goal progress bar + funded % + monthly contribution; add/remove goals. _Persist: `trez-goals` (KV-synced)._ _Class: KV._

- **Owner Personal-vs-Business Split** — separates founder drawings from retained treasury: inputs monthly net profit, business yield %, personal yield %, drawings % (slider), of-drawings-save % (slider) → draw = `profit × draw%`, retained = `profit − draw`, personal save = `draw × save%`, personal spend = remainder; biz annual yield = `retained×12×bizYield`, personal annual yield = `personalSave×12×personalYield` → 4 split cards + annual-yield summary. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Post-Tax Return Calculator** — net return incl. Sec 194A TDS: inputs amount, rate %, slab %, instrument toggle (Bank FD / Debt fund / T-Bill·G-Sec), senior-citizen checkbox → 1-yr gross = `P × rate`, tax = `gross × slab`, net = `gross − tax`, post-tax yield = `net/P`; TDS = `10% of gross` only for FD when gross > ₹40k (₹50k senior) → gross/tax/net/post-tax-yield cards + TDS warning vs no-TDS note. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Maturity Calendar** — single tracker for every FD/RD/T-bill/G-Sec/fund lock-in: inputs label, instrument type (dropdown), invested ₹, maturity value ₹, maturity date → sorts by date, totals invested/maturity, sums "maturing in 90 days"; per-row days-to-maturity badge (Matured/Today/Nd, ≤30d highlighted); add/remove holdings. _Persist: `trez-holdings` (KV-synced)._ _Class: KV._

- **SIP / Recurring-Investment Planner** — monthly drip with annual step-up: inputs monthly amount, years, expected return %, step-up % (0–25 slider) → month-by-month loop FV `(fv+contrib)·(1+r/12)`, contribution stepped up each completed year by step-up %, tracks invested vs future → invested/corpus/gain cards. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Debt-Fund vs Equity-Fund Allocator** — horizon-capped split: inputs amount, horizon years, debt return %, equity return %, risk appetite % (slider) → equity cap by horizon (`<1y→0`, `1–3y→40`, `3–5y→70`, `5y+→90`), equity% = `min(cap, risk)`, debt% = remainder; blended = `(debt%·debtRet + equity%·eqRet)/100`, projected = `A·(1+blended)^yrs` → debt/equity/blended/projected cards + cap warning when horizon limits below appetite. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Emergency-Fund Target Calculator** — sizes a months-of-cover contingency reserve: inputs monthly fixed opex, already-reserved ₹, build-over months, months-of-cover (3–12 slider) → target = `opex × months`, gap = `target − have`, monthly top-up = `gap/buildMonths`, funded % → target/reserved/gap/top-up cards + funding progress bar. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Sweep-In FD Auto-Threshold Config** — stores flexi/sweep-FD rules: inputs account (dropdown from `store.bankAccounts`), sweep trigger ₹, keep-liquid floor ₹, break-chunk ₹ → validates floor>0, chunk>0, sweep-back < trigger; shows total cash across banks; renders saved rules as plain-English sweep/break sentences; add/remove rules (intent only — actual sweep set up at the bank). _Persist: `trez-sweepfd-rules` (KV-synced)._ _Class: KV._

- **Corporate-FD Comparator** — ranks issuer deposits by maturity value: inputs amount, issuer, rating (dropdown AAA…A+/Sovereign-ish), rate %, tenure months; seeded with SBI/Bajaj/Shriram rows → maturity = `A·(1+rate/4)^(4·years)` (quarterly compounding) per row, sorted desc, BEST flag on top; rating-colored badges; add/remove (warns corp deposits are NOT DICGC-covered). _Persist: `trez-corpfd` (KV-synced, seeded with 3 rows)._ _Class: KV._

- **NSC / KVP / SGB Calculator** — small-savings maturity: inputs amount, instrument toggle (NSC 7.7%/5y, KVP 7.5%/~9.5y, SGB 2.5%/8y), and (SGB only) assumed gold gain % → NSC/KVP maturity = `A·(1+rate)^years`; SGB = `A + coupon(A·rate·years) + priceGain(A·(1+gold)^years − A)` → maturity/total-gain/effective-gain cards + SGB coupon-vs-appreciation breakdown (coupon slab-taxed, price gain LTCG-exempt to maturity). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Sweep-FD vs Overdraft Comparator** — cheapest way to cover a short cash gap: inputs shortfall ₹, days short, OD/CC rate %, FD rate forgone %, break penalty % → OD cost = `S·odRate·days/365`; break cost = forgone interest `S·fdRate·days/365` + penalty `S·penalty·days/365` → two cost cards with CHEAPER flag + delta. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Dividend / Interest Income Tracker** — logs interest/dividend/coupon credits for Form 26AS reconciliation: inputs source, type (dropdown), amount ₹, TDS deducted ₹, date → totals income / TDS / net received, breakdown by type, date-sorted ledger; add/remove entries (notes equity dividend >₹5k attracts 10% TDS u/s 194). _Persist: `trez-income` (KV-synced)._ _Class: KV._

- **Capital-Gains-on-Redemption Estimator** — STCG/LTCG hit before redeeming: inputs asset toggle (equity/debt), buy/cost ₹, sell/proceeds ₹, held months, (debt) slab % → gain = `proceeds − cost`; equity ≥12m → LTCG 12.5% above ₹1.25L exemption; equity <12m → STCG 20%; debt (post-Apr-2023) → slab on full gain → gross/treatment/tax/net cards + LTCG-exemption note and "wait to 12m" STCG warning. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Asset-Allocation Rebalancer** — drift-to-target buy/sell actions: inputs sleeve name, current value ₹, target %; seeded with cash/debt/equity rows → current% = `r.current/total`, target value = `total·target%`, delta = `targetValue − current` (+buy/−sell), action suppressed unless `|delta| ≥ 1% of total`; warns when targets ≠ 100%; per-sleeve table + total; add/remove. _Persist: `trez-rebalance` (KV-synced, seeded with 3 rows)._ _Class: KV._

- **Portfolio XIRR Calculator** — true annualised return on irregular cashflows: inputs date, amount ₹, note; "Invested" (−) / "Inflow·value" (+) buttons → Newton-Raphson XIRR on NPV `Σ amount/(1+rate)^years` (years from first flow, 100 iterations, guards on derivative/divergence), requires ≥1 neg + ≥1 pos flow → invested/inflows/XIRR cards + date-sorted flow table; add/remove flows. _Persist: `trez-xirr` (KV-synced)._ _Class: KV._

- **Surplus-Deployment Waterfall** — priority cascade of one surplus across claims: inputs surplus ₹, weekly opex, buffer weeks, GST due, advance tax, emergency top-up, high-cost debt → top-down `take(need)` consumes remaining cash in 6 tiers (buffer `opex×weeks` → GST → advance-tax → emergency → prepay high-cost debt → deploy-to-yield remainder); nothing reaches the yield tier until prior claims are fully met → 6 tier cards with amount + % bars. _Persist: none (seeded from `totalBalance`)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Gold / SGB Allocation Planner** — sizes a gold sleeve via SGB: inputs investable portfolio ₹, horizon years, gold CAGR %, SGB coupon %, gold allocation % (0–25 slider) → goldAlloc = `P×gold%`; SGB price growth = `goldAlloc·((1+cagr)^yrs − 1)`, coupon income = `goldAlloc·coupon·yrs`, total = sum → allocation/coupon/appreciation/total-gain cards + SGB-vs-physical edge note (coupon slab-taxed, price gain LTCG-free at 8y maturity). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **REIT / InvIT Income Estimator** — post-tax distribution yield: inputs amount ₹, distribution yield %, slab %, interest-portion % (slider), dividend-portion % (slider) → gross = `P × distYield`; interest part + dividend part taxed at slab, return-of-capital = remainder (tax-deferred, cuts cost base); net = `gross − tax`, post-tax yield = `net/P` → gross/taxable/RoC/post-tax-yield cards + RoC explainer. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Mark-to-Market Tracker** — live unrealised P&L across the book: inputs holding name, units, avg cost/unit ₹, current price ₹ (editable inline per row) → per row invested = `units×cost`, value = `units×price`, pnl = `value − invested`, pct; book totals invested/value/P&L/return → KPI cards + per-holding table with editable price; add/remove (notes unrealised gains untaxed until redeemed; FDs hold at face). _Persist: `trez-mtm` (KV-synced)._ _Class: KV._

- **Counterparty / Bank Exposure (DICGC)** — flags deposits over the ₹5L per-bank insurance cover: inputs bank, total deposit ₹ → per bank insured = `min(amount, ₹5,00,000)`, over = `max(0, amount − 5L)`, coverage bar `min(100, 5L/amount)`; book totals deposits/insured/uninsured; OVER COVER badge + red banner suggesting spread across banks or move to T-bills/G-Secs; add/remove. _Persist: `trez-dicgc` (KV-synced)._ _Class: KV._

- **Treasury Policy & Limits** — board-ready investment-policy generator: inputs buffer weeks, min credit rating (dropdown), max-per-issuer %, max-per-bank %, dual-approval-above ₹, equity allowed toggle, permitted-instruments multi-select chips → renders a 6-clause policy statement from the settings and a "Copy policy text" button (writes plain-text policy to clipboard via `navigator.clipboard`). _Persist: `trez-policy` (KV-synced object: bufferWeeks, minRating, maxIssuerPct, maxSingleBankPct, allowEquity, dualApprovalAbove, allowedInstruments)._ _Class: KV._

- **Accrued-Interest Calculator** — interest earned-but-not-credited for mid-tenure valuation / month-end accrual entries: inputs principal/face ₹, coupon/rate %, last-paid/start date, as-of date, day-count basis toggle (Actual/365 FD-bond, Actual/360 money-mkt) → days = `asOf − start`, accrued = `P·r·days/basis` (simple), interest/day = `P·r/basis` → days-accrued / per-day / accrued-interest cards. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Asset-Liability (Cash-Flow) Matcher** — lines maturing inflows against known outflows by time bucket: inputs type (inflow/outflow), name, amount ₹, date → buckets cashflows into 0–30 / 31–90 / 91–180 / 180+ days, per bucket assets − liabs = gap (negative = will need to break a deposit or borrow); 4 bucket-gap cards + date-sorted item table; add/remove (gaps are within-bucket only, no carry-forward). _Persist: `trez-almatch` (KV-synced)._ _Class: KV._

- **Interest-Income Forecast** — multi-year projection of recurring treasury income: inputs deployed corpus ₹, blended yield %, slab %, corpus growth %/yr (slider), horizon years (1–10 slider) → year loop: gross = `corpus·yield`, tax = `gross·slab`, net = `gross − tax`; next-year corpus = `corpus·(1+growth) + net` (net reinvested) → total gross/tax/net cards + per-year table (opening corpus, gross, tax, net). _Persist: none (seeded from 50% of `totalBalance`)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Liquidity-Tier Allocator** — buckets cash by when it's needed (not risk): inputs total cash ₹, Tier-1 (0–7d) % slider, Tier-2 (1–6m) % slider; Tier-3 (6m+) = remainder → tiers carry hardcoded indicative yields (T1 6.8%, T2 7.4%, T3 7.9%) and instrument lists; blended = `Σ(pct×yield)/100`, annual income = `cash×blended` → blended-yield + annual-income cards + 3 tier allocation bars. _Persist: none (seeded from `totalBalance`)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Yield vs Liquidity Tradeoff** — scores instruments on a yield/liquidity blend: input single preference slider (0=pure liquidity … 100=pure yield) → 7 hardcoded options (savings, sweep FD, liquid fund, 91-day T-bill, short-debt fund, 1y FD, AAA corp-FD) each with indicative yield and 0–100 liquidity score; score = `w·(yield/maxYield·100) + (1−w)·liquidity`, sorted desc → best-fit banner + ranked score bars. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Idle-Cash Opportunity Cost** — cost of leaving cash at a low rate: inputs idle cash ₹, current rate %, achievable rate %, idle period months (1–36 slider) → earnNow = `A·(1+cr)^t − A`, earnBetter = `A·(1+br)^t − A`, forgone = difference, cost/day = `forgone/(months×30.4)`, spread = `br − cr` → earning-now / could-earn / opportunity-cost / cost-per-day cards + red forgone-yield banner. _Persist: none (seeded from `totalBalance`)._ _Class: Indicative._

---

### Classification summary

- **KV (durable records synced via `useFeatureState` → `store.featureData`):** 11 tools — Goal Planner (`trez-goals`), Maturity Calendar (`trez-holdings`), Sweep-FD Config (`trez-sweepfd-rules`), Corporate-FD Comparator (`trez-corpfd`), Income Tracker (`trez-income`), Rebalancer (`trez-rebalance`), XIRR (`trez-xirr`), Mark-to-Market (`trez-mtm`), Bank Exposure/DICGC (`trez-dicgc`), Treasury Policy (`trez-policy`), Asset-Liability Matcher (`trez-almatch`).
- **Indicative (transient `useState` calculators, no persistence):** 23 tools — Overview, Sweep Planner, Ladder Builder, Liquid-vs-FD, Surplus Allocator, Yield Calculator, T-Bill Estimator, Owner Split, Post-Tax Calculator, SIP Planner, Debt-vs-Equity, Emergency Fund, NSC/KVP/SGB, Sweep-vs-OD, Cap-Gains Estimator, Surplus Waterfall, Gold/SGB Planner, REIT/InvIT Estimator, Accrued Interest, Interest-Income Forecast, Liquidity-Tier Allocator, Yield-vs-Liquidity, Idle-Cash Opportunity Cost.
- **Backend / Simulated / Preview:** none — no tool issues a real `/api/treasury` write or executes an actual sweep/order; persisted records ride the generic KV `featureData` sync, and all yields/projections are illustrative (every tool carries a "not advice / not guaranteed" disclaimer).
- **Live-store reads:** Overview, Sweep Planner, Surplus Allocator, Sweep-FD Config, Surplus Waterfall, Interest-Income Forecast, Liquidity-Tier Allocator and Idle-Cash Opportunity Cost seed/derive from `store.bankAccounts` / `totalBalance`.
# Spend Management (`/spend`) — 18 tools

Surfaces where the money goes and where it's leaking — duplicate vendors, silently-growing subscriptions, category mix vs the tenant's own norm, plus budgeting, policy and approval workflows. Stakeholders: finance, ops. Backend: KV-synced (reads `store.transactions`; user-built records persisted via `useFeatureState` → `store.featureData`, written to localStorage + debounced backend push + cross-device sync).

## Page-level (top, always rendered)

- **Summary cards (4)** — `store.transactions` (negative-amount = expenses) → this-month total spend, count of duplicate-vendor categories, count of growing subscriptions, count of categories >5% over benchmark → four KPI tiles. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **3-Month Spend Trend** — expenses grouped by calendar-month windows (current + prior 2) → summed monthly totals → 3-bar chart (latest bar highlighted red). _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **Duplicate Vendor Detection** — expenses grouped by category; categories holding >1 distinct counterparty flagged → top 5 by total category spend, names the highest-spend vendor as consolidation target, shows total spend per category → consolidation-opportunity list. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **Silently Growing Subscriptions** — vendors present in BOTH current month and 3-months-ago window → growth% = (m0−m3)/m3; keep >15% growth & m0>0, top 6 by growth → rising-vendor list (was→now, +%). _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **Category vs Your 12-Month Norm (benchmark)** — per-category current-month % of total spend vs the tenant's OWN trailing-12-month median %-mix (median of monthly category shares via `percentiles`); falls back to a static Indian-SMB REFERENCE table only when no history → delta (over >+5% / under <−5%), bar with benchmark marker, source tag (your median vs ref). _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (own-norm); Indicative (static reference fallback)._

## Single-scroll sections (each a self-contained component)

- **#94 Spend Categorisation & Top Vendors** — all-history expenses → spend by category (% bars) and top 8 vendors by amount, with top-5 vendor concentration share (>60% = "concentrated" flag) → mix + vendor table. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **#95 Corporate Card / Petty-Cash Manager** — manual rows (holder, card/petty kind, limit/float, spent, note) → totals (limit, spent), per-account utilisation %, count over sanctioned limit → reconciliation list. _Persist: `spend-card-entries` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._
- **#96 Subscription / SaaS Spend Tracker** — manual subs (name, amount, monthly/quarterly/annual cycle, renewal date) → normalise each to monthly run-rate, annualise (×12), flag renewals due within 30 days → KPIs + upcoming-renewal alerts + list. _Persist: `spend-subscriptions` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._
- **#97 Budget vs Actual by Cost Center** — manual cost centers (name, monthly budget, matched spend category) vs this calendar-month actuals pulled live from transactions by category → variance, used%, over-budget flag + count, progress bars. _Persist: `spend-cost-centers` (featureData); actuals from live store._ _Class: KV + Backend._
- **#98 Duplicate / Anomaly Payment Detector** — live expenses → (a) duplicate suspects: same payee + same rounded amount with ≥1 pair within 5 calendar days; (b) outliers: payments > mean + 2.5σ of all spend (needs ≥4 txns) → two lists with counts. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (heuristic)._
- **Spend-by-Category Trend (12 months)** — expenses bucketed into 12 monthly bins per category → 12-bar sparkline each, total per category, rising/easing tag (last-6-mo sum vs prior-6-mo sum) → per-category trend. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **Recurring-Spend Detector** — vendors billed in ≥3 of the last 6 months → avg monthly spend, annualised (×12), months-seen, last-seen; top 10 → auto-detected subscriptions + total annual commitment. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **Budget-vs-Spend Gauge** — manual single business-wide monthly budget vs live month-to-date spend → used%, run-rate projection (MTD/dayOfMonth × daysInMonth), remaining, over / projected-over flags → envelope gauge. _Persist: `spd-monthly-budget` (featureData); spend from live store._ _Class: KV + Backend._
- **Spend Forecast (next 3 months)** — 6-month spend history → avg of non-zero months + simple slope (last-2-mo avg vs prior-2-mo avg) projected forward 3 months (floored at 0) → actual+forecast bar chart, avg monthly, projected next-3-month total. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (directional projection)._
- **Savings-Opportunity Finder** — live expenses → (a) categories with ≥3 vendors → ~5% consolidation saving; (b) recurring vendors whose 2nd-half-of-6-months spend >1.25× 1st-half → ~50% of the increase as saving; top 8 by saving → estimated annual savings list. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (heuristic saving estimates)._
- **Expense-Policy Threshold Checker** — manual per-category caps vs this month's live transactions exceeding their cap → breach list (payee, date, cap, amount), top 12 by amount + breach count. _Persist: `spd-policy-rules` (featureData); txns from live store._ _Class: KV + Backend._
- **T&E (Travel) Spend Tracker** — live expenses matched against travel/flight/hotel/cab/fuel keyword list (counterparty + description) → 6-month monthly travel-spend bars, top 6 travel vendors, total + txn count. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (keyword heuristic)._
- **Spend Approval Queue** — manual pre-spend requests (vendor/purpose, amount, requester, note) with pending/approved/rejected status → pending-value total, status filter, approve/reject actions with full status trail. _Persist: `spd-approval-queue` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._
- **Spend Variance vs Last Month** — live expenses for current vs previous calendar month, by category → per-category delta (₹ and %), ranked by largest rupee swing; totals (this/last month, change) → variance list. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **Discretionary vs Committed Spend** — this month's live spend split: committed = payroll/loan/tax, discretionary = ops/transfers → amounts + % of spend, split bar → two-way breakdown. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **Top-Growing Expense Categories** — last-3-months vs prior-3-months spend per category → keep positive deltas, top 6 by rupee increase → ranked rising-cost-head list (₹ delta, % , window-over-window). _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **GST / ITC-Eligible Spend Split** — this month's live spend split (ops = ITC-eligible, others = not) + user-set assumed GST rate (clamped 0–28%) → embedded input GST = eligible × rate/(100+rate), eligible %, eligible/ineligible/claimable tiles → ITC planning estimate. _Persist: `spd-itc-rate` (featureData); spend from live store._ _Class: KV + Indicative (tax estimate, not a filing)._
- **Approval Turnaround Tracker** — reads the same `spd-approval-queue`; user stamps decision time per decided request → per-request turnaround (days = decidedAt − created), avg decision time, pending count, oldest pending → SLA view. _Persist: `spd-approval-queue` (read) + `spd-approval-decided` (featureData, decision timestamps)._ _Class: KV._

### Notes
- A page-level CTA ("Connect more accounts") routes to `/connectors` — navigation only, not a tool.
- The page is single-scroll sections (no tabs). Pure UI affordances excluded: status filter chips on the Approval Queue, chart legends, and bar/marker styling.
## Budgeting & FP&A (`/budgets`) — 17 tools

Plan, allocate, phase, reforecast and stress-test budgets against live transaction actuals. Stakeholders: owner, finance. Backend: KV-synced (per-tool `useFeatureState` keys + the shared `budgets` store collection persisted via `addBudget`/`updateBudget`/`deleteBudget`).

- **Budget Manager (core grid)** — create/edit/delete budget category (label, category, monthly limit ₹, colour) via `AddBudgetModal`; each budget validated (`amount > 0`) → spend vs limit computed from current-month negative-amount transactions matched by `category`, with `pct` (capped 100), `overspend`, `warn` (≥80%), `remaining`, and a month-over-month delta vs prior month → per-card progress bars + summary cards (Total Budget, Spent This Month, alert counts: `N over, N near`) + overall spend/limit bar. _Persist: `store.budgets` (CRUD via AppContext)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Zero-Based / Rolling Budget Builder** (`#zero-based`) — per-period (YYYY-MM) line items (category, label, justified ₹); add/remove lines, "Roll forward prior month" copies prior month's lines with new ids → each line's justified amount compared to live per-category actuals (outflows in period window `-01`..`-31`); totals = Period Budget, Actual (live), Variance (budget − actual) → cards + line table. _Persist: KV `zbb-periods`._ _Class: KV (computation reads live transactions)._
- **Department Budget Allocation & Approval** (`#dept-alloc`) — set top-down budget pool ₹, allocate to departments (name, amount ₹, category) with status workflow draft→submitted→approved/rejected (+reopen) → unallocated/over-allocated = pool − sum(allocated); each row shows live current-month per-category spend with over-flag → pool/allocated/unallocated cards + table. _Persist: KV `dept-budget-pool` + `dept-allocations`._ _Class: KV (live spend overlay)._
- **Capex Budget & Approval Tracker** (`#capex`) — capital items (asset/project, planned ₹, target date) with status planned→approved→completed (+reopen) and editable actual `spent` → variance = planned − spent, overrun flagged when spent > planned; totals = Planned, Approved (non-planned), Actual Spent, Overruns count → cards + table. _Persist: KV `capex-items`._ _Class: KV._
- **Annual Budget Builder** (`#annual-builder`) — annual ₹ per line (category, mode even|seasonal) → spreads across 12 months: even = annual/12, seasonal = India festival-weighted curve `SEASONAL[12]` normalised; monthly totals, grand total, monthly average, peak month → stat cards + 12-month spread table with totals row. _Persist: KV `bud-annual-lines`._ _Class: KV (deterministic spread)._
- **Budget-vs-Actual Variance Report** (`#variance-report`) — pick month → each `store.budgets` line vs live per-category actual outflows in that month; variance = monthlyLimit − actual, var% = variance/limit, sorted worst-variance first, over flagged when variance < 0; totals = Budgeted, Actual, Net Variance, Lines Over → cards + table. _Persist: live store (budgets + transactions); `period` is local UI state, not persisted._ _Class: Backend (live read, no own KV)._
- **Flexible Budget Recalc** (`#flexible-budget`) — cost lines (fixed ₹ + variable ₹/unit), planned vs actual volume → static budget = Σ(fixed + var×plannedVol), flexed = Σ(fixed + var×actualVol), volume effect = flexed − static → cards + per-line static/flexed table. _Persist: KV `bud-flex-lines` + `bud-flex-planned-vol` (actual volume is local state)._ _Class: KV._
- **Cash Budget Planner** (`#cash-budget`) — opening cash ₹ + per-month planned inflow/outflow rows (one per month, sorted) → running closing = opening + Σ net; net = inflow − outflow per month; surfaces projected closing + lowest balance (red if negative) → cards + month table. _Persist: KV `bud-cash-opening` + `bud-cash-rows`._ _Class: KV._
- **Headcount / Manpower Budget** (`#headcount-budget`) — roles (dept, headcount, monthly CTC ₹, start month) → fully-loaded cost = base × (1 + `PF_ESI_LOAD` 0.13 employer PF+ESI); totals = headcount, monthly base, monthly loaded, annual loaded (×12) → cards + role table. _Persist: KV `bud-headcount`._ _Class: KV (statutory-load assumption is indicative ~13%)._
- **Project Budget Tracker** (`#project-budget`) — projects (name, client, budget ₹) with editable `spent` → used% = spent/budget, remaining, overrun flagged when spent > budget; totals = Total Budget, Total Spent, Overruns count → cards + table. _Persist: KV `bud-projects`._ _Class: KV._
- **Budget Utilization Gauges** (`#utilization-gauge`) — for each `store.budgets`, live current-month per-category spend → consumption% (conic-gradient ring), pace vs expected (`dayOfMonth/daysInMonth`); flags >100% over, >expected+10% pacing-ahead, else on-pace. _Persist: live store (budgets + transactions); no own KV._ _Class: Backend (live read)._
- **Forecast-vs-Budget Reforecast** (`#reforecast`) — per budget: annual budget = monthlyLimit×12; YTD actual = live calendar-year per-category outflow; run-rate = YTD/monthsElapsed; projected rest = runRate × monthsRemaining × (1 + adjust%); reforecast = YTD + projected; variance = annualBudget − reforecast → cards + table with user trend-adjustment input. _Persist: KV `bud-reforecast-adjust` (reads live budgets + transactions)._ _Class: KV (run-rate projection — Indicative)._
- **Marketing Budget Allocator** (`#marketing-roi`) — channels (prior spend ₹, revenue ₹) + marketing pool ₹ → ROAS = revenue/spend, weight = roas/Σroas (fallback equal-split), recommended = pool × weight; sorted by ROAS desc; blended ROAS = Σrev/Σspend, top channel → cards + table. _Persist: KV `bud-mktg-budget` + `bud-mktg-channels`._ _Class: KV (ROI-weighted allocation — Indicative recommendation)._
- **Cost-Cutting Simulator** (`#cost-cutting`) — last-3-months live per-category outflow → monthly average; per category set cuttability easy/medium/hard → modeled saving = monthlyAvg × `CUT_PCT` (0.30/0.15/0.05); totals = monthly spend, monthly saving, annualised saving (×12) → cards + table with % of total. _Persist: KV `bud-cut-levels` (levels only; spend read live)._ _Class: KV (savings %s are modeling assumptions — Simulated)._
- **Budget Reallocation Tool** (`#reallocation`) — requires ≥2 budgets; queue from→to transfer moves (amount ₹, distinct budgets) → net delta per budget, revised limit = monthlyLimit + delta; total-before vs total-after must reconcile (green when equal, pool conserved); shows queued moves list → cards + revised-limit table. _Persist: KV `bud-realloc-moves` (reads live budgets; does NOT mutate store budgets)._ _Class: KV (preview/simulation — does not write back to budgets)._
- **Quarterly Phasing Planner** (`#quarterly-phasing`) — annual ₹ per line auto-phased even/4 across Q1–Q4, each quarter editable → phased = Σ quarters, flags lines where phased ≠ annual; quarter totals + grand total → Q1–Q4 cards + editable table with totals row. _Persist: KV `bud-phasing-lines`._ _Class: KV._
- **Contingency Reserve Planner** (`#contingency`) — last-6-months live monthly outflow totals → mean, std, coefficient of variation; suggested buffer% = clamp(cv×100, 5–30), manual override wins; reserve = mean × usePct, annual = ×12 → cards + 6-month bar chart. _Persist: KV `bud-contingency-pct` (stats read live)._ _Class: KV (volatility-sized buffer — Indicative)._
## Transactions & Bookkeeping (`/transactions`) — 27 tools

A unified ledger plus a Tally-grade bookkeeping suite: search/categorise/reconcile booked transactions, then layer journals, ledgers, GST/TDS, period locks, and budgeting on top. Stakeholders: finance, CA. Backend: `/api/transactions` (live `store.transactions` via `useApp` / `addTransaction` / `updateTransaction`) + KV (`featureData` namespace via `useFeatureState`, localStorage-mirrored and backend-synced) + `/api/ai/scan-receipt` (Claude vision).

Persistence legend: **Backend** = booked `store.transactions` / `updateTransaction`. **KV** = `useFeatureState` synced bag. **Indicative** = derived estimate (tax/accrual). **Simulated** = `useState` only, lost on unmount. **Preview** = computes a proposed change without writing.

Tools below cover (a) the header action buttons, (b) the primary `view === "transactions"` table view, (c) the five legacy view buttons, and (d) the 20-button "Tools" tab strip. UI filter pills/tokens (revenue/expense/payroll/loan/tax/transfer, search, date/account filters) are excluded per spec.

### Header actions & main ledger view

- **Scan Receipt** — camera/photo (`capturePhoto`) → `POST /api/ai/scan-receipt` (Claude vision extracts vendor/amount/date/category/description) → signs amount (revenue +, else −) and drops a prefilled transaction into the ledger via `addTransaction` for review; toasts on no-amount/failure. Gated by `canEdit()`. _Persist: Backend (`store.transactions`)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Export CSV** — current filtered+sorted transaction list → builds quoted CSV (Date, Description, Category, Counterparty, Amount, Account-name) → browser download. Gated by `canExport()`. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend (read)._
- **Reconcile (modal)** — opens `ReconcileModal` (separate component) over the page. _Persist: per that component._ _Class: Backend._
- **Transactions Table** — search + category/account/date filters → sortable (date/description/amount, asc/desc) paginated table (50/page); inline per-row category edit writes via `updateTransaction`; unusual-spend flag (outflow > 2.5× that category's average abs outflow); recurring badge from `t.isRecurring`. _Persist: Backend (inline category edits)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Financial Intelligence Bar** — over all txns → 4 derived KPI cards: spend velocity (7-day vs 30-day daily-avg % change), largest expense this month, potential-duplicate count (same amount + same counterparty within 3 days), category spend spike (>20% MoM rise vs last month). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Bulk Categorise / Create Rule** — floating bar on multi-select → set category on all selected via `updateTransaction`; if exactly one counterparty selected, "Create rule" saves a counterparty→category map to `localStorage` (`hr_cat_rules`) and re-categorises all matching txns. _Persist: Backend (txns) + localStorage (rule map)._ _Class: Backend._

### Legacy view buttons

- **PDC Register** — form (party, cheque no, bank, amount, due date, receive/issue) → records post-dated cheques; pending receivable/payable totals; overdue rows (due < today) highlighted; status transitions pending→cleared/bounced; CSV export. _Persist: KV (`pdc-register`)._ _Class: KV._
- **Bounce Tracker** — form (party, cheque, bank, amount, bounce date, reason, notes) → auto-sets a +30-day re-present date; tracks status open→represented→recovered→legal; totals bounced/recovered/open; NI Act §138 guidance note. _Persist: KV (`bounce-cases`)._ _Class: KV._
- **UPI Dashboard** — manual UPI entry form (VPA, name, amount, received/paid, UTR, date, note) merged with auto-detected book txns whose notes/description contain "upi"; received/paid totals, top-5 counterparties bar chart. _Persist: Simulated (manual entries `useState`); auto-detected rows are Backend-derived._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Bank Recon (Statement)** — opening balance + manually-entered statement rows (date, desc, debit, credit) → computes statement closing vs Headroom book balance (sum of all txn amounts + opening), difference, unmatched count; per-row match toggle. _Persist: Simulated (`useState` rows)._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Recurring Templates** — template form (desc, amount, income/expense, category, monthly/quarterly/annual, next date, counterparty) → monthly-equivalent (amount ÷ 1/3/12); monthly recurring income/expense/net KPIs; projects upcoming occurrences over next 90 days (guarded loop); active/paused toggle. Planning only — not booked to ledger. _Persist: KV (`recurring-templates`)._ _Class: KV (occurrences = Indicative)._

### Tools tab strip (20)

- **Auto-Categorise (Rules Engine)** — build rules (field payee/description, op contains/equals, text, min/max ₹ on abs amount, target category) → first-match-wins evaluation over the ledger; shows which txns *would* re-classify (capped 100). Does not write — apply happens on the main table / Bulk Tag. _Persist: KV (`txn-cat-rules`) for the rules._ _Class: Preview._
- **Recon Workbench** — manually-added bank lines (date, signed amount, narration) → auto-matches one book txn per line by same amount (±0.5) within ±3 days; counts matched/unmatched(bank)/book-without-line; manual clear/reopen of unmatched lines; lists book txns with no bank line (deposits-in-transit / outstanding cheques). _Persist: Simulated (lines & cleared set `useState`)._ _Class: Simulated (matching is Backend-derived)._
- **Split Txn** — pick a booked txn → allocate its absolute amount across N legs (category, memo, amount); "split equally" helper; live allocated/remaining balance check. Computation/preview only; does not post the split. _Persist: Simulated (legs `useState`)._ _Class: Preview._
- **Bulk Tag** — filter ledger by search + from-category → multi-select rows (cap 300 shown) → re-categorise all selected to a target category via `updateTransaction`. _Persist: Backend (`store.transactions`)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Transfer Detect** — finds offsetting debit/credit pairs (same magnitude, opposite sign, ±2 days, *different* bank accounts) → "Tag (all) as transfer" sets both legs' category to `transfer` via `updateTransaction` so self-transfers aren't double-counted in revenue/spend; shows gross value moved and already-tagged count. _Persist: Backend (txn category writes)._ _Class: Backend (detection = Indicative)._
- **Cost Centers** — create projects/cost-centres; assign each txn to a project (txnId→projectId map) → per-project P&L = assigned inflows (revenue) − assigned outflows (cost) → net. _Persist: KV (`txn-cost-centers` list + `txn-cost-center-map` assignments)._ _Class: KV._
- **Cash / Accrual** — basis toggle (cash | accrual) + window (3/6/12M) → cash basis = revenue received & expenses paid in window; accrual adds unpaid invoiced revenue (`store.invoices` where status≠paid) as a revenue adjustment; expense-side accrual not modelled. P&L statement (cash vs accrual columns). _Persist: none (UI state only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Journal Entry** — double-entry voucher composer: legs (account, debit | credit, mutually exclusive), narration, date → enforces balanced (Dr=Cr, non-zero) and every leg named → posts a durable JV record (auto voucher no `JV-####`); does NOT touch the cash/bank ledger. _Persist: KV (`txn-manual-journals`)._ _Class: KV._
- **Trial Balance** — re-foots live over all booked txns: each category's net lands as a debit (net outflow) or credit (net inflow), plus a "Cash & Bank (control)" account absorbing total net movement so columns tie out; shows total Dr/Cr and balanced flag. Single-entry-derived. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Day Book** — pick a day (date picker + prev/next arrows) → opening balance (sum of all txns before the day), that day's receipts/payments ordered by id with a running balance, closing balance. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend (read/derived)._
- **Party Ledger** — pick a counterparty → chronological statement of account (debit = outflows, credit = inflows) with running balance; total received/paid/net. For ledger confirmations. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend (read/derived)._
- **Opening Balance** — per-bank-account opening balance entry + "as-of" date → current balance = opening + movement (sum of txns dated on/after as-of); totals across accounts. For Tally migration. _Persist: KV (`txn-opening-balances` + `txn-opening-asof`)._ _Class: KV._
- **Write-Off** — record bad-debt / short-receipt / discount / rounding / other write-offs (date, party w/ datalist of counterparties, amount, reason, notes) → total written off, bad-debt subtotal, by-reason breakdown. Register only — not posted to ledger. _Persist: KV (`txn-write-offs`)._ _Class: KV._
- **Period Lock** — set a "locked up to (inclusive)" date → flags txns on/before it as locked (correct via reversing journal, not edit) vs open; counts/value locked vs open entries; soft lock only (no enforcement), framed against MCA audit-trail expectation. _Persist: KV (`txn-period-lock-upto`)._ _Class: KV._
- **Chart of Accounts** — maintain CoA grouped under 5 heads (asset/liability/equity/income/expense), seeded from txn categories (codes 1000–5100); add/remove accounts; live usage (txn count + abs value) mapped from categories onto account names. _Persist: KV (`txn-chart-of-accounts`)._ _Class: KV._
- **GST Ledger** — month + slab (5/12/18/28%) → back-computes tax embedded in revenue (output GST) and expense (input GST/ITC) lines treating amounts as tax-inclusive (factor = rate/(100+rate)) → net GST payable or ITC carry-forward. GSTR-3B-style estimate. _Persist: KV (`txn-gst-ledger-rate`)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **TDS Ledger** — section (194C/J/H/I/Q with preset rates) + threshold ₹ → per-counterparty gross of expense+payroll outflows; where gross ≥ threshold, estimated TDS = gross × rate; parties-above-threshold count, total estimated TDS, gross deductible spend. _Persist: KV (`txn-tds-ledger-section` + `txn-tds-ledger-threshold`)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Cash / Bank Split** — classifies each txn's account as cash-type (name/provider contains "cash"/"petty"/"wallet", or no account) vs bank → cash vs bank net (in − out) cards, total net, cash share of outflow %. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend (read/derived)._
- **Counterparty 360** — per-counterparty profile built live from txns: received (in), paid (out), net, txn count, last-seen date; sortable by most-paid / most-received / net exposure / activity. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend (read/derived)._
- **Budget vs Actual** — per spend category (expense/payroll/tax/loan) monthly budget entry + month picker → actual outflow for month, utilisation % bar (amber >80%, red on overrun), per-row over-budget delta; total budget/actual/remaining-or-over. _Persist: KV (`txn-budget-actual`)._ _Class: KV._
## Financial Statements (`/statements`) — 22 tools

Generates a full suite of accounting-standard-compliant statements and management reports derived live from the firm's bank transactions, loans, payroll, fixed-asset register and confirmed investments. Stakeholders: finance, CA, owner. Backend: every figure is computed in-browser from the live `store` via the finance lib (`incomeStatement`, `balanceSheet`, `cashFlowStatement`, `monthlyCashFlow`, `monthlyAggregates`) and the depreciation lib (`bookValue`, `depreciationBetween`, `accumulatedDepreciation`, `totalGrossCost/NetBookValue/AccumulatedDepreciation`); no statement is persisted — only tool-specific assumption inputs persist via `useFeatureState` into `store.featureData` (the synced "app" KV namespace).

Tabs are driven by the `TABS` array (22 entries). A period preset selector (`month`/`quarter`/`fy`/`ttm`, Indian FY 1 Apr–31 Mar) drives the Income Statement and Cash Flow tabs; all other date-bounded tabs use the same `range` or recompute their own FY/YoY bounds. PDF/Excel export (via `exportPdf`/`exportExcel`) is shown on every tab except Fixed Assets, plus tab-local PDF buttons on Schedule III, Depreciation Schedule and MIS Pack.

- **Income Statement** — inputs: revenue/expense/payroll/COGS transactions, fixed-asset register (depreciation), active loans (interest), period preset → computation: P&L from `incomeStatement` (Revenue → COGS → Gross Profit/margin → payroll + other opex → EBITDA/margin → depreciation → EBIT → finance cost → PBT → income tax estimated at 25% of PBT → Net Profit/margin); side margin cards (Gross ≥30%, EBITDA ≥15%, Net ≥8% health colouring) → output: vertical P&L with % of revenue + PDF/Excel. _Persist: live store (depreciation est. ₹0 until assets added)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Balance Sheet** — inputs: cash/AR/inventory/payables/GST/debt balances + paid-in capital, as-of `today` → computation: `balanceSheet` (current assets, net fixed assets [est. if no register], total assets; current + non-current liabilities; equity with retained earnings as the **balancing figure**); shows a Balanced / Out-of-balance check (Assets = Liabilities + Equity) → output: two-column Assets vs Liabilities & Equity + PDF/Excel. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (retained earnings is a derived balancing plug)._
- **Cash Flow** — inputs: actual bank cash movements over the period + `monthlyCashFlow(12)` → computation: `cashFlowStatement` direct method (receipts − supplier/employee/tax payments = operating; investing with capex shown as "not tracked yet"; financing = loan proceeds − repayments + equity; net change; opening/closing cash) plus a rolling 12-month month-by-month table anchored to today's bank position → output: direct-method statement, opening/net/closing cards, monthly grid + PDF/Excel (adds monthly sheet). _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (capex not tracked → investing = 0)._
- **Fixed Assets** (`FixedAssetRegister`) — inputs: user-entered assets (name, category, cost, purchase date, method WDV/SLM, useful life, salvage, optional disposal date), gated by `canEdit()` → computation: per-asset `accumulatedDepreciation`/`bookValue` at today + register totals (`totalGrossCost`/`AccumulatedDepreciation`/`NetBookValue`); WDV rate from `wdvAnnualRate` (Schedule II); add/edit/delete via `addFixedAsset`/`updateFixedAsset`/`deleteFixedAsset` → output: gross-block/accum-dep/NBV summary + register table; feeds real depreciation into P&L and net fixed assets into the balance sheet. _Persist: live store (`store.fixedAssets`)._ _Class: Backend._
- **AS-3 Cash Flow** (`As3CashFlowStatement`) — inputs: period range, direct/indirect method toggle, `cashFlowStatement` + `incomeStatement` → computation: AS-3 / Ind AS 7 format with sections A/B/C; indirect method reconciles PBT + depreciation add-back + finance cost = operating profit before WC, then a single **derived working-capital-&-other plug** (`operating − (netProfit+dep+interest+tax)`) − tax to tie back to the direct operating figure; reconciliation-check card (closing − opening − net < ₹1) → output: statutory cash-flow statement + operating-cash and reconciliation cards. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (indirect WC line is a reconciling plug)._
- **Schedule III** (`ScheduleThreeBalanceSheet`) — inputs: `balanceSheet` at today → computation: Companies Act 2013 Schedule III Part I vertical format — I. Equity & Liabilities (Shareholders' funds: share capital + reserves; Non-current: long-term borrowings; Current: short-term borrowings, trade payables, other CL, GST provisions) and II. Assets (Non-current PPE; Current: inventories, receivables, cash) with Note references (1–8) mapping to the Notes tab; statutory balance check → output: two-section statutory balance sheet + dedicated PDF export. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **Notes to Accounts** (`NotesToAccounts`) — inputs: `balanceSheet` (as-of) + `incomeStatement` (period) → computation: auto-drafts Note A significant accounting policies (5 static policy statements) plus 10 numbered disclosure notes (Share Capital, Reserves & Surplus, Borrowings, Trade Payables, PPE, Inventories, Trade Receivables, Cash, Revenue, Cost/Employee Benefits) reconciling to Schedule III note numbers → output: policy list + two-column note cards; flags that contingent liabilities, related-party, MSME §43B(h), CSR and OCI must be added manually. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (auto-drafted disclosure; statutory items absent)._
- **Comparative** (`ComparativeStatements`) — inputs: `incomeStatement` for current FY-to-date vs full prior comparable FY, view toggle (comparative/common-size) → computation: per-line YoY (`(cur−prior)/|prior|`, with 0-base guard) for 11 P&L lines, or common-size each line as % of revenue (one decimal) for both years → output: comparative table (prior, current, change, YoY%) or common-size table (₹ + %rev for each year). _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (comparative/common-size analysis)._
- **Segment Report** (`SegmentReporting`) — inputs: live transactions in window (excl. transfers) → computation: AS-17 / Ind AS 108 proxy — revenue grouped by `counterparty` (revenue category), top 5 segments + folded "Other segments"; total direct cost (expense + payroll) **allocated pro-rata to segment revenue share**; per-segment result and result margin; totals row → output: segment KPI cards + per-segment table (revenue, % of total, allocated cost, result, margin). _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (segments inferred from top counterparties; common costs pro-rata — refine with CA)._
- **Ratio Pack** (`RatioPack`) — inputs: `balanceSheet` (today) + `incomeStatement` (FY-to-date) → computation: Schedule III mandatory + key ratios — Current, Quick, Debt–Equity, DSCR (EBITDA ÷ (interest+ST debt)), Interest Coverage, Inventory/Debtors/Creditors/Net-Capital Turnover, Net Profit, ROCE, ROE; each with formula, benchmark and pass/neutral health flag; turnover ratios use **period-end balances as a proxy for averages** → output: 12 ratio cards. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (period-end balances proxy averages; cash-centric not ledger-based)._
- **Fund Flow** (`FundFlowStatement`) — inputs: `balanceSheet` at today vs same date one year prior + `incomeStatement` (FY) → computation: lender-format sources & applications — sources = funds from operations (net profit + depreciation), fresh capital, long-term borrowings raised, fixed-asset sales (derived from NBV/dep deltas); applications = capex additions, long-term repayments; closing **balancing figure = change in working capital** → output: two-column sources/applications + WC-movement banner. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (non-current deltas derived from YoY balance-sheet reconstruction)._
- **Working Capital** (`WorkingCapitalStatement`) — inputs: `balanceSheet` today vs one year prior → computation: line-by-line change in current assets (cash, receivables, inventory) and current liabilities (payables, GST, ST debt, other CL); rule: a rise in a current asset increases WC, a rise in a current liability decreases it; net WC and increase/decrease total → output: schedule of changes in working capital table. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (prior-period figures reconstructed from balance-sheet logic dated a year back)._
- **Changes in Equity** (`ChangesInEquity`) — inputs: `balanceSheet` today vs one year prior + `incomeStatement` (FY) → computation: SOCIE (Ind AS Division II, Schedule III) — opening capital/reserves from prior BS; movements = profit for period → reserves, capital issued (Δ paid-in capital), dividends/other adjustments as a **balancing reserve-move plug** (`Δretained − netProfit`); closing must tie to current BS equity (tolerance < ₹2 check) → output: capital/reserves/total-equity movement table + tie-out banner. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (dividends/other line is a balancing plug; OCI absent)._
- **Depreciation Sch.** (`DepreciationSchedule`) — inputs: `store.fixedAssets`, FY bounds → computation: Companies Act Schedule II per-asset opening/closing WDV (`bookValue` at FY start/end), depreciation for year (`depreciationBetween`, telescopes exactly), accumulated dep, method (WDV/SLM), useful life, disposal flag; register-level totals (gross cost, accum dep, dep-for-year, NBV) → output: KPI cards + per-asset schedule with totals + dedicated PDF; empty state prompts adding assets. _Persist: live store (`store.fixedAssets`)._ _Class: Backend._
- **EPS & Net Worth** (`EpsNetWorth`) — inputs: `balanceSheet` (as-of) + `incomeStatement` (period) + user assumptions face value (default ₹10) and potential dilutive shares → computation: shares = paid-up capital ÷ face value (proxy); Basic EPS = net profit ÷ shares; Diluted EPS = net profit ÷ (shares+potential); AS-20 / Ind AS 33; Net Worth (Companies Act §2(57)) = paid-up capital + free reserves − accumulated losses; book value per share → output: 4 KPI cards + EPS build-up and Net Worth schedules. _Persist: `stm-eps-face-value`, `stm-eps-potential-shares` (featureData KV)._ _Class: Backend (computation) + KV (assumptions); share count is a proxy (no share register)._
- **Cost Sheet** (`CostSheet`) — inputs: `incomeStatement` (period) → computation: classical cost build-up — prime cost (COGS) + direct labour (payroll) = works/factory cost + depreciation = cost of production + admin/selling overheads (other opex) + finance cost = cost of sales; profit = revenue − cost of sales; each line as % of revenue; margin cards (gross ≥30%, prime ≤60%, overhead ≤25%) → output: cost build-up statement + margin cards. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (payroll classified as direct labour, opex as overheads — working classification for a cash-centric system)._
- **MIS Pack** (`MisPack`) — inputs: `incomeStatement` + `balanceSheet` + `cashFlowStatement` + `monthlyCashFlow(6)` → computation: board-ready snapshot — 8 headline KPIs (revenue, net profit, net/EBITDA margin, cash, operating CF, receivables, current ratio with health tones), P&L summary, balance-sheet summary (incl. net working capital), 6-month cash trend → output: KPI grid + summaries + cash-trend table + dedicated MIS PDF (KPIs, P&L, BS). _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (assembles same live figures as other tabs)._
- **Indirect Cash Flow** (`IndirectCashFlow`) — inputs: `balanceSheet` today vs one year prior + `incomeStatement` + `cashFlowStatement` (FY) → computation: full indirect method building each working-capital movement from YoY balance-sheet deltas (Δreceivables/inventory as use of cash, Δpayables/GST/other CL as source), operating profit before WC = PBT + dep + interest, then − tax = derived operating cash; compares derived vs direct-method operating cash with the difference shown as accrual-timing → output: operating-section build-up + comparison cards. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (decomposed WC, not a single plug; difference is accrual timing)._
- **3-Statement Forecast** (`ProjectedStatements`) — inputs: `incomeStatement` (period run-rate) + `balanceSheet` (today) + user assumptions revenue growth %, cost growth %, horizon months (1–36) → computation: annualises the period window to a full-year base, applies growth across horizon fraction to produce projected P&L (revenue, COGS, payroll, opex, EBITDA, dep, interest, PBT, tax est. 25%, net profit); projected position assumes profit + depreciation accrete to cash with no new capex/financing/dividends (projected cash/equity/assets) → output: KPI cards + projected P&L and projected position schedules. _Persist: `stm-proj-rev-growth`, `stm-proj-cost-growth`, `stm-proj-horizon-months` (featureData KV)._ _Class: Preview (planning model, not a statutory statement) + KV (assumptions)._
- **Break-even** (`BreakEvenAnalysis`) — inputs: `incomeStatement` (period) + user fixed-opex-share % slider (default 70%) → computation: splits costs (COGS fully variable; payroll, depreciation, finance cost fixed; other opex split by slider); contribution = revenue − variable; CM ratio; break-even revenue = fixed ÷ CM ratio; margin of safety (₹ and %); degree of operating leverage (contribution ÷ EBIT) → output: KPI cards (break-even revenue, CM%, MoS%, DOL) + cost-behaviour and break-even/safety schedules. _Persist: `stm-be-fixed-opex-pct` (featureData KV)._ _Class: Backend (computation) + KV (fixed/variable split assumption)._
- **Budget Variance** (`BudgetVariance`) — inputs: user-entered budget targets (revenue, COGS, payroll, other opex) + actuals from `incomeStatement` → computation: per-line variance (actual − budget), variance % (vs budget base), direction-aware favourable/adverse flag (revenue favourable if above, costs favourable if below); EBITDA variance = net effect across all four lines → output: empty-state prompt until a budget is entered, then budget-vs-actual table with status badges and EBITDA total row. _Persist: `stm-budget-targets` (featureData KV)._ _Class: Backend (variance computation) + KV (budget targets)._
- **Monthly Trend** (`MonthlyTrendPL`) — inputs: `monthlyAggregates(transactions, 12, today)` → computation: per-month revenue, expense, cash-basis net (revenue − total expense), margin %; stats (12-month revenue/net, avg monthly revenue, profitable-month count, peak month); revenue bar relative to max → output: KPI cards + month-by-month trend table with revenue trend bars and totals row. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (simplified cash-basis P&L trend — differs from statutory net profit which charges dep/interest/tax)._
# Debt Management (`/debt`) — 25 tools

Single consolidated cockpit for every business loan — amortisation, prepayment/refinance maths, covenant & coverage monitoring, and capital-structure planning. Stakeholders: owner, finance. Backend: KV-synced — every tool reads the live `store.activeLoans` (id, lender, outstanding, rate, monthlyEmi, principal) and several read `computeFinancialSnapshot(store)` (revenue/net/interest/DSCR/ICR); user inputs and a few settings persist via `useFeatureState` KV keys. All maths is computed client-side from `@/lib/finance` (`emi`, `amortizationSchedule`, `totalInterest`, `prepaymentImpact`, `irr`) plus a local `remainingMonths()` solver; nothing is sent to a server. There are **22 tab tools** in the tab array; the **Overview** tab additionally renders **3** distinct embedded analysis tools (counted below).

> Note: the EMI-mode/structure/strategy radio toggles inside individual tools (e.g. _reduce tenure vs reduce EMI_, _avalanche vs snowball_, _step-up vs step-down_, _interest-only vs balloon_, _EMI vs bullet_, _buy vs lease_, _reset EMI vs hold EMI_) are mode switches **within** a single tool, not separate tools — mentioned once here and not enumerated per option.

## Overview tab (`overview`)
