- **Debt KPI Strip** — reads `store.activeLoans` + snapshot → shows Total Outstanding, Monthly Debt Service (with pure-interest split), Weighted Avg Rate, and DSCR (green/red vs the 1.25x lender bar) → 4 KPI cards. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Active Loans Table** — per loan: outstanding, rate, EMI, % paid off (`(principal−outstanding)/principal`), and Est. Interest Left (`totalInterest` over `remainingMonths`); click selects a loan to analyse → sortable-style table feeding the embedded analysers. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Amortisation Chart** — selected loan's `amortizationSchedule(outstanding, rate, remainingMonths)` → stacked Principal/Interest area chart + next-12-month schedule table (opening/EMI/interest/principal/closing). _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: KV._
- **Prepayment Simulator (embedded)** — lump-sum slider (10k → outstanding) on the selected loan → `prepaymentImpact()` reducing-balance, same-EMI → interest saved, months cut, new payoff term. _Persist: none (local slider)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Refinance Check (embedded)** — new-rate slider (8% → current) for the same remaining term → `totalInterest` at old vs new rate, new EMI (`emi`), lifetime saving; CTA to run a lender auction (`/lenders`). _Persist: none (local slider)._ _Class: Indicative._

## Tab tools

- **Loan Amortisation & Prepayment Simulator** (`amortise`, #86) — principal/rate/tenure/prepay-amount/prepay-month inputs (one-click prefill from any active loan) + tenure-vs-EMI prepay mode → from-scratch reducing-balance schedule, re-cast with a one-time prepayment (reduce tenure keeps EMI; reduce EMI keeps tenure) → monthly EMI, total interest, interest saved, months cut / new term, 24-month schedule with prepay column. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **DSCR / Interest-Coverage Tracker** (`dscr`, #87) — snapshot-derived NOI (monthly net + interest add-back, annualised) → DSCR (NOI ÷ debt service), ICR (EBIT-proxy ÷ interest), Debt/NOI leverage, annual NOI; user adds covenant rows (name/metric/operator/threshold) and each is flagged OK/Breach against live metrics. _Persist: live store (metrics) + KV `debt-covenants` (covenant list)._ _Class: KV._
- **Debt Consolidation / Refinance Comparator** (`refinance`, #88) — refinance amount (auto = total outstanding) + user-added lender offers (rate/tenure/processing%/other fees) → per offer: EMI, lifetime interest, fees, all-in cost, and effective APR via `irr()` of net-disbursal-vs-EMI cashflows; ranks offers, marks BEST, compares to current weighted blended cost. _Persist: live store + KV `debt-refi-offers`._ _Class: KV._
- **Moratorium & Restructuring Re-cast Modeller** (`moratorium`, #89) — pick loan or manual P/R/N + moratorium months, tenure extension, restructured rate, capitalise-interest toggle → accrued interest in holiday (`P·((1+r)^m−1)`), re-cast principal, new EMI, extra lifetime interest, total months to close → before/after comparison. _Persist: live store (loan read); none for inputs._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Debt Schedule / Repayment Ladder** (`schedule`, #90) — merges all loans into one forward calendar over a 12–60mo horizon → combined EMI, interest, principal, outstanding and active-loan count per month; detects roll-off months (freed cash); step/area chart + month-by-month table. _Persist: live store + KV `debt-ladder-horizon`._ _Class: KV._
- **Interest-Cost Optimizer (avalanche vs snowball)** (`optimizer`, #91) — monthly surplus budget + strategy → simulates paying all minimum EMIs then routing surplus to highest-rate (avalanche) or smallest-balance (snowball) loan first, rolling to next as each closes → total interest, interest saved vs EMI-only, debt-free month, ranked attack order with per-loan close month, and which strategy wins. _Persist: live store + KV `debt-optimizer-budget`._ _Class: KV._
- **Weighted-Average Cost of Debt (WACD)** (`wacd`, #92) — all loans → blended rate `Σ(rate×outstanding)/Σoutstanding`, monthly/annual interest bill, costliest loan; per-loan weight, contribution-to-WACD (bps), monthly interest and its share, flagging above-blend loans; bar chart vs blend. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Foreclosure / Pre-closure Charges Calculator** (`foreclosure`, #93) — pick loan + penalty% (of outstanding) + optional 18% GST toggle + other charges → interest saved by closing now (`totalInterest` over remaining), penalty + GST + other = total cost, net benefit, and break-even penalty% → pays-off / cheaper-to-continue verdict. _Persist: live store + KV `debt-foreclosure-penalty`._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Balloon / Bullet Repayment Planner** (`balloon`, #94) — amount/rate/tenure + interest-only (100% bullet) or partial-amortisation (balloon %) mode → monthly payment (interest on full balance + EMI on amortising slice), balloon due at maturity, interest over term, extra interest vs fully-amortising loan, 12-month schedule + refinancing-risk warning. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Step-Up / Step-Down EMI Planner** (`stepemi`, #95) — pick loan or manual P/R/N + direction (up/down) + annual step% → solves base EMI so PV of all stepped payments = principal (keeps tenure), schedules per-year EMI, total interest vs flat EMI; EMI-by-year table + delta-vs-flat. _Persist: live store (loan read); none for inputs._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Interest-Subsidy / Subvention Estimator** (`subsidy`, #96) — pick loan or manual P/R/N + scheme (interest subvention / CLCSS capital subsidy / MUDRA) + subvention% or eligible capex + CLCSS% → subvention benefit (`grossInterest·min(1,sub/rate)`), effective post-subsidy rate, CLCSS capital subsidy (capped ₹15L on ₹1Cr eligible), total benefit. _Persist: live store (loan read) + KV `debt-subvention-pct`._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Loan Against FD / Securities Estimator** (`lasf`, #97) — asset type (FD ≤90% LTV / shares-MF ≤50% LTV) + asset value + yield + draw% + tenure → sanction limit, drawn amount & EMI, loan interest (FD rate +2% or ~10.5% LAS), yield kept on pledged asset, and net carry cost (interest − yield). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Gold-Loan Estimator** (`gold`, #98) — net grams + purity (24/22/18K) + 24K ₹/gram + LTV (≤75% RBI cap) + loan rate + tenure + EMI-vs-bullet structure → pledged gold value (`grams·purityFactor·rate`), eligible loan, monthly EMI or interest-only, total interest (+ bullet principal); auction/top-up risk warning. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Equipment Finance vs Lease Comparator** (`equip`, #99) — asset cost + loan rate + down% + term + residual% + monthly lease rental → buy path (down + EMI×N − residual kept = net cost of ownership) vs operating lease (rental×N = net cost), side-by-side cards with CHEAPER badge + verdict. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Interest-Rate Reset (Repo / MCLR) Impact** (`reset`, #100) — bps slider (−200…+200) + reset-EMI (hold tenure) vs hold-EMI (reset tenure) mode → per loan: new rate, new EMI or new term, EMI/term delta, extra lifetime interest; combined EMI change, total extra interest, months added / loans repriced. _Persist: live store + KV `debt-reset-bps`._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Group Exposure / Loan-Stacking Tracker** (`stacking`, #101) — all loans + snapshot revenue → FOIR (total EMI ÷ monthly income, ~50% cap), debt ÷ annual revenue, active-lender count (stacking flag at 4+), top-lender concentration; per-lender exposure table + over-leverage verdict. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Working-Capital Demand Loan / OD-CC Calculator** (`wcdl`, #102) — sanctioned limit + avg utilisation% + rate + commitment% (on idle) + renewal fee → avg drawn/idle balance, monthly & annual interest on drawn only, commitment fee, all-in annual cost, effective rate on drawn, and savings vs a fully-drawn term loan. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Interest-Coverage (ICR) Trend & Rate-Shock** (`icr`, #103) — snapshot OCF (net + debt service add-back) + shock slider (0…+500bps) → ICR (OCF ÷ interest, aim ≥3x), pre-debt cash flow, annual interest, shocked ICR at +Nbps with extra interest/yr; ICR-vs-bps area-curve + breach warning (<2x). _Persist: live store + KV `debt-icr-shock-bps`._ _Class: KV._
- **Debt-Maturity Profile** (`maturity`, #104) — buckets each loan's `remainingMonths` into 0–12m/1–2y/2–3y/3–5y/5y+ windows → outstanding, loan count and share% per bucket; bar chart highlighting any >40% window as a refinancing "wall" + laddering verdict. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Prepayment-Penalty vs Interest-Saved** (`prepaypenalty`, #105) — lump-sum + penalty% → per loan: applied amount (capped at outstanding), interest saved via `prepaymentImpact`, penalty (`applied·pen%`), net benefit; ranks loans by net payback, flags worth-it, recommends best target. _Persist: live store + KV `debt-prepay-penalty-pct`._ _Class: KV._
- **Debt-to-Equity Target Planner** (`gearing`, #106) — net worth/equity input + target D/E slider (0.5–3x) → current D/E (total debt ÷ equity), debt allowed at target, repay-needed (if over-geared) or borrowing headroom, or equity-to-inject alternative; covenant-cap warning. _Persist: live store + KV `debt-de-equity`._ _Class: KV._

---

Count: **25 tools** — 22 tab tools (#86–#106, plus Overview) and 3 embedded Overview analysers (KPI strip, Prepayment Simulator, Refinance Check); all client-side off live KV `store.activeLoans`.

## ━━━ PILLAR 4 · Funding & Investors ━━━

## Credit & Embedded Lending (`/credit`) — 30 tools

Embedded-lending and credit-intelligence workspace: real backend underwriting + offer acceptance plus a deep suite of indicative loan-sizing, eligibility, cost-comparison and score-planning calculators for Indian SMEs. Stakeholders: owner, finance. Backend: `/api/credit` (apply / accept / payment) + KV-synced (`store.featureData` via `useFeatureState` → localStorage + backend, plus `creditApplications`/`creditOffers`/`activeLoans` in the synced AppContext store).

### Tab-selector array (30 tabs, in order)

`overview` · `apply` · `loans` · `notyet` · `wc` · `equip` · `cc` · `fd` · `wcscore` · `captable` · `valuation` · `aapull` · `matcher` · `comscore` · `discount` · `docpack` · `foir` · `emicalc` · `flatred` · `dscr` · `drawing` · `gstelig` · `lap` · `prepay` · `odterm` · `scoreplan` · `offercmp` · `invadv` · `nbfcbank` · `scheme`

> Shared helpers: `emi(P, annualRate%, months)` = standard reducing-balance EMI; `totalInterest()` = EMI×n − P. A proactive amber CTA fires when runway is 1–44 days. The page header carries a `PreviewBadge` for the `creditDisbursement` capability (real disbursement is gated/preview).

---

### Core lending flow (backend-backed)

- **Overview** (`overview`) — what it does: renders the application/offer dashboard. Inputs → live `creditApplications`, `creditOffers`, `activeLoans`, `transactions`, `bankAccounts` from the synced store → outputs three KPIs (best UW score `Math.max` of application scores, max approved amount, active-loan count); the **Pre-Qualified Offers** grid (real server offers for the approved application, recomputing EMI/total interest/total repayment client-side via `emi()`, "Best offer" = largest amount, with an RBI-Digital-Lending APR/cooling-off note); a **"Right time to borrow?"** verdict (≥65 = borrow now; else wait-30-days if this-month revenue > 1.05× last-month, else points-to-50 message); an **EMI capacity gauge** (sum of active-loan EMIs as % of monthly burn, safe/caution/high-risk bands at 25%/40%, and headroom `(burn×0.3 − emiLoad)/1000` ₹K/mo); and a static **score-factors weighting** table (9 signals: revenue consistency 25, avg revenue 20, business age 15, debt-service 15, customer concentration 10, overdraft 8, engagement 7). _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Backend (offers/score) + Indicative (timing/capacity computed client-side)._

- **Apply** (`apply`) — what it does: submit a pre-qualification. Inputs → loan amount (₹, min 100000), term (6/12/18/24/36 mo), purpose (7 options) → `POST /api/credit/apply {requested_amount, term_months, purpose}` returns `{application, offers[], underwriting:{score, approved_amount}}`; persists a `creditApplication` (status approved if offers exist, else rejected) and each real `creditOffer` keyed by its server offer id (rate normalised `apr_equivalent×1000/10`). Honest error handling: 429 → "one application every 90 days", 409 → "in progress", else underwriting-unreachable toast (never fabricates approval). Also shows a static **hardship-protection** list (revenue drop >30% → 60-day pause, etc.). _Persist: `creditApplications` + `creditOffers` (live store) + backend._ _Class: Backend._

- **Active Loans** (`loans`) — what it does: track disbursed loans. Inputs → `activeLoans` store list → per loan shows outstanding/principal, % repaid progress bar, next-payment date+amount; **Record Payment** posts `POST /api/credit/loans/:id/payment {amount}` then decrements outstanding and rolls next-payment date +1 month; **Early repayment calculator** (slider 1→termMonths) recomputes EMI on outstanding and shows interest saved = `totalInterest(outstanding,rate,termMonths) − totalInterest(outstanding,rate,customMonths)`. Loan creation happens via the KFS-modal **Accept** flow → `POST /api/credit/accept/:offerId` → adds an `ActiveLoan`, marks application `funded`. _Persist: `activeLoans` (live store) + backend payment._ _Class: Backend._

- **Not yet** (`notyet`) — what it does: explains the score gap for declined/low-score users. Inputs → 6-month monthly revenue (computes CoV = std/mean), business age (months since first transaction), per-account overdraft proxy (days cumulative balance < 0), top-counterparty concentration (top payer ÷ 6-month revenue), avg monthly revenue → outputs a checklist of 5 improvements each with a point value (CoV>0.25 +8, age tiers +10/+5, concentration>40% +6, overdrafts +5, revenue<₹3L +5), projected score = `min(100, bestScore + gapTotal)`, and an indicative limit `meanRev×3`. _Persist: none (live store read; checklist derived, not saved)._ _Class: Indicative (computed from live transaction data)._

- **KFS modal** (rendered, not a tab) — what it does: RBI Digital Lending 2022 Key Facts Statement before accepting an offer; shows lender, amount, APR, term, EMI, total interest/repayment, ₹999 processing fee, prepayment/cooling-off/grievance terms; "Accept Loan" triggers the backend accept flow. _Persist: triggers backend `accept` + `activeLoans`._ _Class: Backend._

---

### Working-capital, treasury & cap-table tools

- **WC Sizing** (`wc`, `WCSizingTab`) — what it does: Tandon Committee 2nd-method working-capital limit. Inputs → annual net sales (auto-seeded from sum of positive transactions), debtor days / inventory days / creditor days sliders → daily sales = rev/365; AR req = dailySales×debtorDays, inventory req = dailySales×invDays×0.6 (COGS≈60%), AP credit = dailySales×0.6×creditorDays, net WC = AR+inv−AP → output MPBF = `0.75 × net WC requirement` (indicative limit). _Persist: none (form-local; annual-rev pre-seeded from store)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Finance vs Lease** (`equip`, `EquipmentFinanceLease`) — what it does: after-tax total-cost-of-ownership compare for buying (term finance) vs operating lease of an asset. Inputs → asset cost, down-payment %, loan rate/tenure, monthly lease rent/tenure, residual buyout %, corporate tax % → finance: EMI on (cost−DP), gross = DP+EMI×n, tax saving = 15% WDV depreciation × tax × years; lease: gross = rent×months + residual, tax saving = full rent deductible × tax × years → outputs net-of-tax cost for each and the cheaper "winner" with the delta. _Persist: none (form-local)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **CC Utilization** (`cc`, `CcUtilizationTab`) — what it does: track business credit cards and utilisation. Inputs → per-card name/bank/limit/balance/due-date/min-due (min-due auto = 5% of balance) → outputs totals (limit, balance, overall utilisation %, total min due), >70% utilisation warning, per-card util bar (green/orange/red at 30%/70%) and overdue-flagging; balance is editable inline. _Persist: KV — `useFeatureState("credit-cards")`._ _Class: KV._

- **FD / RD** (`fd`, `FdRdTab`) — what it does: fixed/recurring-deposit maturity tracker. Inputs → kind (FD/RD), bank, principal/total, monthly instalment (RD), rate, tenure+unit, start date, TDS-applicable flag → FD interest = quarterly compounding `P((1+r/4)^(months/3)−1)`; RD interest via the standard RD maturity formula; TDS = 10% of interest if flagged; computes maturity date, net interest, days-left, maturing-in-30-days, maturity value → portfolio KPIs + sorted cards with elapsed-time progress bars. _Persist: KV — `useFeatureState("fd-rd")`._ _Class: KV._

- **WC Health Score** (`wcscore`, `WcHealthScore`) — what it does: weighted working-capital health grade. Inputs → current assets/liabilities, inventory, receivables, payables, annual revenue (auto-estimated from store) and COGS (default rev×0.6) → computes current ratio, quick ratio, DSO, DPO, DIO, cash-conversion-cycle (DSO+DIO−DPO), working capital → scores 5 ratios on good/bad thresholds (weights 25/20/20/15/20, DSO & DIO inverted) → weighted /100 score with Excellent/Good/Fair/Poor grade + per-component bars. _Persist: none (form-local; revenue seeded from store)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Cap Table** (`captable`, `CapTableTab`) — what it does: equity ownership register + dilution simulator. Inputs → shareholders (name, share class of 6, shares held, amount invested; seeded with founders/ESOP/angel) → outputs total shares, total capital, founder ownership %, sorted ownership bars; **Dilution simulator** inputs new investment + pre-money → post-money = pre+invest, new investor % = invest/post, price/share = pre÷existing shares, new shares = invest/price, and a before/after dilution table per holder. _Persist: KV — `useFeatureState("cap-table")` (holders); simulator inputs form-local._ _Class: KV (register) + Simulated (dilution)._

- **Valuation** (`valuation`, `ValuationTab`) — what it does: three-method enterprise-value estimator + blend. Inputs → DCF (annual FCF, growth %, WACC %, terminal growth %, projection years) → year-by-year projected FCF discounted to PV + discounted terminal value `FCFₙ(1+g)/(WACC−g)` (guarded: WACC must exceed terminal growth); revenue-multiple (annual revenue auto-annualised from store × multiple); EBITDA-multiple (EBITDA × multiple) → outputs each EV plus blended = equal-weight average of methods with a value. _Persist: none (form-local; revenue auto-seeded)._ _Class: Indicative._

---

### Underwriting & eligibility analysers

- **AA Underwriting** (`aapull`, `AaUnderwritingPull`) — what it does: models the credit profile a lender derives from an Account Aggregator bank-statement feed (no live AA fetch). Inputs → avg monthly inflow + avg balance (seeded from live transactions/bank balances), existing EMI outflow, banking vintage, inflow CoV, top-payer concentration, bounces (6mo), overdraft days (6mo) → 7 weighted sub-scores (inflow 25, stability 20, buffer 15, bounces 15, OD days 10, vintage 10, concentration 5) → /100 score + risk band (Prime/Near-prime/Sub-prime/Decline-likely); indicative limit = 50% FOIR on surplus (inflow−EMI), capitalised at ~16% over 36 mo, then risk-adjusted ×1/0.7/0.4/0 by band. _Persist: none (form-local; inflow/balance seeded from store)._ _Class: Indicative (estimator — explicitly no AA backend call)._

- **Eligibility Matcher** (`matcher`, `LoanEligibilityMatcher`) — what it does: matches profile against 6 hardcoded Indian SME products. Inputs → annual turnover, vintage (mo), bureau score, collateral-available flag → each product checks 4 gates (min turnover/vintage/score, collateral need); match % = passed/4, indicative ticket = `min(maxTicket, 25% of turnover)` shown only if fully eligible → sorted by odds then rate. Products: Unsecured Term Loan, WC OD/CC, CGTMSE, Invoice/Bill Discounting, LAP, Merchant Cash Advance. _Persist: none (form-local)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Commercial Score** (`comscore`, `CommercialScoreTracker`) — what it does: logs business bureau scores over time. Inputs → bureau (CIBIL MSME Rank 1–10 / CRIF Highmark / Experian), score, as-of date, note → validates CMR 1–10, tracks latest reading, change-vs-prior (inverts "improving" direction for rank where lower is better), risk/quality verdict bands, full reverse-chronological table with delete. _Persist: KV — `useFeatureState("commercial-credit-score")`._ _Class: KV._

- **Invoice Discounting** (`discount`, `InvoiceDiscountingConnector`) — what it does: TReDS-style receivable listing + financing-bid estimator. Inputs → buyer/drawee, invoice no, amount, due date (→ tenure days), discount rate % p.a. → discount charge = `face × rate × tenure/365`, advance ≈ 90% of face, net now = advance−charge, effective annualised cost; listed↔funded status toggle → portfolio totals. _Persist: KV — `useFeatureState("invoice-discounting")`._ _Class: KV (listings) + Indicative (bid math)._

- **Loan Doc Pack** (`docpack`, `LoanDocumentPack`) — what it does: auto-assembles the lender document checklist. Inputs → loan type (unsecured/WC/LAP/CGTMSE) + entity (proprietorship/partnership-LLP/Pvt Ltd) → filters ~16 documents by applicability rules into KYC/Financial/Collateral groups, ticking persists, shows %-ready progress and a "ready to submit" banner at 100%. _Persist: KV — `useFeatureState("loan-doc-pack")` (checked map); selectors form-local._ _Class: KV._

---

### Loan-math calculators

- **FOIR / Capacity** (`foir`, `FoirCalculator`) — what it does: Fixed-Obligation-to-Income-Ratio repayment-capacity test. Inputs → net monthly income (seeded = (revenue−expenses)/months from store), existing EMIs (seeded from active loans), other fixed obligations, FOIR ceiling slider 30–70%, proposed new rate/tenure → current FOIR = obligations/income, EMI headroom = `income×cap − obligations`, max new principal reverse-solved from the EMI formula at the proposed rate/tenure; verdict bands at cap and 0.8×cap. _Persist: none (form-local; income/EMI seeded)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **EMI & Schedule** (`emicalc`, `EmiAmortizationTab`) — what it does: reducing-balance EMI with full amortization table. Inputs → principal, rate % p.a., tenure (1–360 mo) → EMI via `emi()`, total interest, total repayment, interest/principal ratio, and month-by-month principal/interest/balance split (final EMI clears rounding residue). _Persist: none (form-local)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Flat vs Reducing** (`flatred`, `FlatVsReducingTab`) — what it does: exposes the true cost of a flat-rate quote. Inputs → principal, quoted rate %, tenure → flat interest = `P×rate×years`, flat EMI = (P+flatInterest)/months; binary-searches the reducing-balance rate that yields the same EMI (the effective rate); compares against a genuine reducing loan at the same nominal rate and shows the extra cost. _Persist: none (form-local)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **DSCR** (`dscr`, `DscrCalculator`) — what it does: Debt-Service-Coverage-Ratio covenant check. Inputs → annual NOI (seeded ≈ annualised revenue−expenses from store), existing annual debt service (seeded = active-loan EMI×12), new loan amount/rate/tenure → current DSCR = NOI/existingDebt, post-loan DSCR = NOI/(existing+new annual debt), max new loan keeping DSCR≥1.25 (reverse EMI solve); verdict bands at 1.5/1.25/1.0 + debt-service build-up table. _Persist: none (form-local; NOI/debt seeded)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Drawing Power** (`drawing`, `DrawingPowerTab`) — what it does: OD/CC drawing-power from stock & debtor statement. Inputs → closing stock, stock margin %, creditors-against-stock, eligible debtors, debtor margin %, optional sanctioned limit + currently utilised → paid stock = stock−creditors, DP = `paidStock×(1−stockMargin) + debtors×(1−debtorMargin)`, effective limit = min(sanction, DP), available/utilisation %, over-drawn warning. _Persist: none (form-local)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **GST Eligibility** (`gstelig`, `GstEligibilityTab`) — what it does: GST-turnover loan-limit estimator. Inputs → annual GST turnover, net margin %, sales multiple ×, consecutive GSTR filings → turnover method = monthly sales × multiple; profit-coverage method = 50% of annual profit serviceable, capitalised 36 mo @18%; filing factor (≥12mo=1, ≥6=0.7, ≥3=0.4, else 0.15) → indicative limit = `min(turnover, profit) × filingFactor`. _Persist: none (form-local)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **LAP / LTV** (`lap`, `LapLtvTab`) — what it does: Loan-Against-Property eligibility. Inputs → property market value, existing loan on property, property type (residential 70 / commercial 60 / industrial 50% base LTV), occupancy (self 0 / rented −5 / vacant −10 adj), rate, tenure → applicable LTV (min 30%), gross eligible = value×LTV, net = gross−existing, EMI + total interest on net via `emi()`. _Persist: none (form-local)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Prepayment Optimizer** (`prepay`, `PrepaymentOptimizer`) — what it does: compares the two prepayment outcomes. Inputs → outstanding/rate/months-left (quick-fillable from an active loan), lump-sum, prepay/foreclosure fee % → Option A (keep EMI, shorten tenure): new tenure via `−ln(1−P·r/EMI)/ln(1+r)`, months saved, interest saved; Option B (keep tenure, lower EMI): new EMI on reduced principal, EMI reduction, interest saved; each saving nets the fee, highlights the larger. _Persist: none (form-local; pre-fill reads active loans)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **OD vs Term Loan** (`odterm`, `OdVsTermLoanTab`) — what it does: true-cost compare for fluctuating WC needs. Inputs → amount/limit, period (mo), OD rate, TL rate, avg OD utilisation %, OD setup fee %, TL processing fee % → OD cost = `avgUtilised × odRate × months/12 + setupFee` (interest only on used balance); TL cost = total reducing-balance interest + processing fee → cheaper winner + delta. _Persist: none (form-local)._ _Class: Indicative._

---

### Planning & comparison tools

- **Score Planner** (`scoreplan`, `ScoreImprovementPlanner`) — what it does: commit to score-improving actions and project the gain. Inputs → base score (max of real application scores) + 6 toggleable actions with point values (consistency +8, buffer +5, diversify +6, GST filing +7, reduce EMI load +6, age tier +5) and effort tags → projected = `min(100, base + gained)`, points-left, progress bar to the 50 approval line. _Persist: KV — `useFeatureState("cr-scoreplan-done")` (durable committed-actions map)._ _Class: KV (plan) + Indicative (projection anchored to real score)._

- **Compare 3 Offers** (`offercmp`, `ThreeOfferCompare`) — what it does: side-by-side effective-APR compare of 3 competing offers. Inputs per offer → lender, amount, rate, months, fee %, insurance % → EMI + total interest via helpers; fee/insurance as ₹ of amount; true total cost = interest+fee+insurance; effective APR = `(totalCost/amount)/(months/12)`; flags the lowest true-total-cost "Best". _Persist: KV — `useFeatureState("cr-offercmp")` (3 offer rows)._ _Class: KV (inputs) + Indicative (math)._

- **Invoice Advance** (`invadv`, `InvoiceAdvanceCalculator`) — what it does: cash-today-vs-cost on a single unpaid invoice. Inputs → invoice face value, advance % (50–95), processing fee %, financing rate % p.a., expected days to payment → advance = face×advance%, reserve = face−advance, finance cost = `advance×rate×days/365`, process fee = face×fee%, net upfront = advance−costs, final net once buyer pays = netUpfront+reserve, effective annual cost = `(totalCost/advance)/(days/365)`. _Persist: none (form-local)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **NBFC vs Bank** (`nbfcbank`, `NbfcVsBankCompare`) — what it does: prices rate vs speed across a bank and an NBFC. Inputs → amount, tenure, cost-of-waiting (₹/day), and per-channel rate/fee/disbursal-days → for each: EMI, total reducing-balance interest, fee ₹, delay cost = days×₹/day, true total = interest+fee+delayCost → cheaper winner + delta (only meaningful once cost-of-delay > 0). _Persist: none (form-local)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Scheme Finder** (`scheme`, `SchemeFinder`) — what it does: filters 7 Indian MSME credit/subsidy schemes by profile. Inputs → amount needed, purpose, women-owned / new-business / collateral-free flags → matches schemes where need≤max, purpose included, and women/new/collateral filters satisfied → sorted ascending by limit, each with max, eligibility text and tags. Schemes: MUDRA Shishu/Kishor/Tarun, CGTMSE, Stand-Up India, PMEGP, PSB Loans in 59 Minutes. _Persist: none (form-local; scheme catalog hardcoded)._ _Class: Indicative._

---

### Persistence & classification summary

- **Backend (`/api/credit`):** Apply, Active Loans (record payment), KFS-modal Accept flow, and the Overview offer/score cards (server-returned offers and underwriting score).
- **KV-synced (`useFeatureState` → `store.featureData`):** CC Utilization (`credit-cards`), FD/RD (`fd-rd`), Cap Table holders (`cap-table`), Commercial Score (`commercial-credit-score`), Invoice Discounting (`invoice-discounting`), Loan Doc Pack (`loan-doc-pack`), Score Planner (`cr-scoreplan-done`), Compare 3 Offers (`cr-offercmp`).
- **Indicative / Simulated (form-local, no persistence):** WC Sizing, Finance vs Lease, WC Health Score, Valuation, AA Underwriting (explicitly no live AA call), Eligibility Matcher, FOIR, EMI & Schedule, Flat vs Reducing, DSCR, Drawing Power, GST Eligibility, LAP/LTV, Prepayment Optimizer, OD vs Term Loan, Invoice Advance, NBFC vs Bank, Scheme Finder, plus the Not-yet gap analyser and the Cap Table dilution simulator. Several seed their inputs from live store data (transactions, bank balances, active loans) but do not save their own state.
## Capital (`/capital`) — 5 tools

Founder-facing fundraising suite: run live equity/revenue-share raises and model runway, instruments, grants, and capital deployment. Stakeholders: owner, investor. Backend: `/api/capital` + KV.

Tabs (`capTab` array, line 75): `raises` (Raises), `runway` (Runway Planner), `safe` (SAFE / Note Modeller), `grants` (Grant / Subsidy Finder), `use-of-funds` (Use of Funds).

- **Raises** — track select (rev_share $10K–$500K / reg_cf up to $5M / reg_a_plus up to $75M) + target amount (₹), validated >0 → `addCapitalRaise` creates draft; Publish flips status to `active` (investor portal live); + Investor (active only) records investor email + amount, computes `equityPct = (amount / targetAmount) * 10` (flat indicative formula), sets investment status `pending`, increments `raisedAmount` via `updateCapitalRaise` → stats (active raises, total raised, total investors), per-raise progress bar (% funded, investor count) and investor list with amount/equity/status → output. _Persist: live store via AppContext (`addCapitalRaise`/`updateCapitalRaise`/`addCapitalInvestment`, `capitalRaises`/`capitalInvestments`)._ _Class: Backend._

- **Runway-Extension Planner** (#105) — cash on hand, monthly burn, target runway (months, default 18), cost-cut lever (%, clamped 0–100), capital-raise lever (₹); cash pre-filled from sum of all transaction amounts, burn pre-filled from last-90-day avg monthly outflow → `adjustedBurn = burn*(1-cut/100)`, `baseRunway = cash/burn`, `newRunway = (cash+raise)/adjustedBurn`, `hitsTarget`, and gap solvers: `cashNeeded = max(0, target*adjustedBurn - cash)` and `cutNeeded = (1 - (cash/target)/burn)*100` → 4 metric cards (current runway, adjusted burn, projected runway, vs target ±mo) + green/orange verdict banner listing the extra-raise or extra-cut needed to close the gap. _Persist: none (transient `useState`; reads live `store.transactions`)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **SAFE / Convertible-Note Modeller** (#106) — instrument toggle (SAFE/Note), investment, valuation cap, discount % (clamped 0–100), priced-round pre-money, pre-round FD shares (default 10M), plus note-only interest p.a. % (default 8) and term-to-conversion yrs (default 1.5) → notes accrue `principalPlusInterest = inv*(1+(intR/100)*yrs)` (SAFE = inv); `roundPPS = round/shares`, `discountPPS = roundPPS*(1-disc/100)`, `capPPS = cap/shares`, converts at lower of the two; `sharesIssued = amount/conversionPPS`, `effValuation = conversionPPS*shares`, `ownershipPct = sharesIssued/(shares+sharesIssued)*100`, `usedCap` flag → 4 cards (amount converting, conversion price/sh, shares issued, ownership) + narrative banner stating cap-vs-discount path and effective pre-money. _Persist: none (transient `useState`)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Grant / Subsidy Finder** (#107) — MSME profile inputs: category (micro/small/medium), years in operation, and 7 toggles (Udyam, manufacturing, women-led, SC/ST, exporter, upgrading tech, DPIIT startup) → filters a hardcoded 8-scheme catalog (CGTMSE, PMEGP, CLCSS, Startup India Seed Fund, Stand-Up India, MSE-CDP/Export Promotion, State Capital-Investment Subsidy, State Power-Tariff/SGST Reimbursement) by per-scheme `test(profile)` predicates → matched-count header + cards (Central/State badge, benefit, "why you qualify") with empty-state Udyam nudge. _Persist: none (transient `useState`; static `GRANT_SCHEMES` array)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Use-of-Funds Tracker** (#108) — total capital raised (₹) + add-allocation form (category, committed ₹, deployed ₹); validates category non-empty, committed >0, deployed ≤ committed → `totalCommitted`/`totalDeployed` sums, `uncommitted = raised - committed`, `remaining = committed - deployed`, per-line deployed % → 4 cards (committed, deployed, remaining, uncommitted color-flips red if negative) + over-committed warning banner + allocation table with progress bars and per-row delete. _Persist: KV — `useFeatureState("capital-use-of-funds", [])` for lines and `useFeatureState("capital-total-raised", "")` for the raised total._ _Class: KV._
## Lenders (`/lenders`) — 18 tools

Role-gated lender/credit cockpit: AA-verified borrower marketplace plus a borrower-side toolkit for shortlisting lenders, comparing offers, tracking covenants/borrowing-base/disbursement, and running the live debt book. Stakeholders: owner, finance, investor (route guard: only `investor` + `super_admin`; others redirected to `/dashboard`). Backend: `/api/lenders` (queue, bid) + KV via `useFeatureState`; analytical tools derive from the live synced store (`useApp`).

Most tools share `useLenderFinancials()` — a memoized snapshot over `store.transactions/invoices/inventory/bankAccounts/activeLoans` computing revenue, expenses, EBITDA, interest, cash, debt service, debt outstanding, AR (open/overdue), stock at cost, and ratios (DSCR, current ratio, leverage = debt/EBITDA, interest coverage). Ratios sentinel to `99` ("n/a") when no debt/denominator exists.

- **Marketplace (Co-lending Auction)** — fetches AA-verified borrower applications `GET /api/lenders/queue` → shows 3 KPIs (in-queue count, AA-verified count, total loan volume) and per-app cards (business, city, industry, monthly revenue, color-coded credit score, requested date, loan amount); each app opens **BidModal** → lender enters interest rate (8–36% p.a.) + processing fee (0–5%), previews estimated monthly interest income (`loan_amount × rate/100 / 12`), posts `POST /api/lenders/bid {application_id, interest_rate, processing_fee}` → bid placed, local Set marks app as bid (in-session). _Persist: live backend (queue + bid); bid-placed flag in-memory only._ _Class: Backend._
- **Covenant Dashboard** — user-defined covenants (metric ∈ DSCR/current ratio/leverage/interest cover, op min `≥` or max `≤`, threshold) tested live against the financial snapshot → per-row actual, breach test, headroom % ((actual−threshold)/threshold for min; inverse for max), status (breach / at-risk if headroom <10% / ok); KPI counts tracked/at-risk/in-breach; add/delete covenants. _Persist: KV `lender-covenants`._ _Class: KV + Indicative (ratios from live store)._
- **Borrowing-Base Certificate Generator** — inputs AR advance %, stock advance %, exclude-overdue toggle, sanctioned limit, currently-drawn → eligible AR (open less overdue) × AR rate + stock-at-cost × stock rate = gross drawing power; capped to limit; less drawn = available headroom or excess-overdrawn (with margin-call warning); renders a dated borrowing-base certificate. _Persist: none (component state; AR/stock from live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Lender MIS Pack** — period (month) + cadence selectors → assembles a 3-section reporting pack: period-scoped P&L (revenue/opex/net profit from transactions in selected month), live covenant ratios, receivables & liquidity (AR, overdue, inventory, cash, debt outstanding); "Copy MIS pack" copies plain-text to clipboard. _Persist: KV `lender-mis-cadence` (cadence only); period/figures live/in-memory._ _Class: KV + Indicative._
- **Lender Directory & Shortlist** — seeded shortlist of lenders (name, type Bank/NBFC/Fintech, indicative rate, max ticket, turnaround days, 1–5 relationship stars) → blended fit score 0–100 = rate (50%, normalized cheapest-best) + TAT (30%, normalized fastest-best) + relationship (20%), sorted, top flagged with star; editable relationship rating; add/delete lenders. _Persist: KV `lnd-shortlist`._ _Class: KV + Indicative (scoring)._
- **Loan-Offer Comparison** — competing offers (lender, amount, rate, tenure months, fee %) → per-offer reducing-balance EMI, total interest, processing fee, total cost, and effective annual cost (= total cost ÷ principal annualized over tenure); lowest effective-cost offer flagged/starred with summary banner; add/delete offers. _Persist: none (component state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Application-Status Tracker** — tracked applications (lender, amount, stage ∈ submitted/docs/underwriting/sanctioned/disbursed/declined, applied-on) → KPI counts (total, in-progress, sanctioned value) and per-app funnel progress bar by stage order; stage dropdown advances/declines; add/delete. _Persist: KV `lnd-app-tracker`._ _Class: KV._
- **Disbursement / Drawdown Planner** — sanctioned limit + tranches (date, amount, note) sorted by date → running cumulative utilization and undrawn balance per tranche; KPIs sanctioned/total-drawn/undrawn; over-sanction warning when draws exceed limit; add/delete tranches. _Persist: KV `lnd-disbursement` (tranches); sanction in component state._ _Class: KV._
- **Rate-Negotiation Prep** — current rate (defaults to outstanding-weighted average of live `activeLoans`) → six live "leverage points" (DSCR ≥1.5×, leverage <2.5×, current ratio ≥1.5×, interest cover ≥3×, overdue AR <10%, cash > annual debt service), each worth fixed basis points (50/50/25/25/25/25) → arguing room (bps), target rate (`max(8, current − bps/100)`), estimated annual saving on outstanding; "Copy negotiation brief" to clipboard. _Persist: none (live store + component state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Repayment Track-Record** — per live active loan computes repaid principal (principal − outstanding), % repaid, months elapsed (inferred from amortized principal ÷ EMI, capped at term), months left, next EMI date → KPI totals (borrowed/repaid/outstanding) and per-loan progress cards. _Persist: none (read-only from live `activeLoans`)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Syndication / Multi-Lender Split** — total facility + participants (lender, share %, rate) → per-participant amount (facility × share%), share-weighted blended rate, total share allocated; KPIs participants/share-allocated/blended-rate; warning if shares ≠ 100% (under/over-allocated); add/delete participants. _Persist: KV `lnd-syndication` (participants); facility in component state._ _Class: KV + Indicative._
- **Security / Collateral Register** — secured exposure + collateral items (asset, type, value, haircut %) → per-item realisable value (value × (1−haircut)), gross security value, total realisable cover, cover ratio (realisable ÷ exposure), shortfall; KPIs gross/realisable/cover-ratio; top-up-demand warning on shortfall; add/delete items. _Persist: KV `lnd-collateral-register` (items); exposure in component state._ _Class: KV + Indicative._
- **Refinance-Opportunity Scanner** — benchmark market rate vs live `activeLoans` → per-loan rate gap (rate − benchmark) and first-year estimated annual saving (outstanding × gap%) for loans >0.5% above benchmark, sorted by saving; flags REFINANCE vs AT-MARKET; KPIs scanned/candidates/total-saving. _Persist: none (read-only from live store; benchmark in component state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Lender-Relationship CRM** — seeded lender contacts (lender, contact/RM, last-contacted, next action, next date) → flags overdue (next date < today) / due-today, sorted by next date; KPIs relationships/due-today/overdue; "Log touch" stamps today, add/delete relationships. _Persist: KV `lnd-relationship-crm`._ _Class: KV._
- **Drawdown-vs-Sanction Utilization Trend** — monthly data points (month, sanctioned, drawn) → utilization % per month, sorted; KPIs latest/average/peak utilization; bar chart (amber >75%, red >90%) + table; add/update (re-adding a month overwrites it), delete. _Persist: KV `lnd-utilization-trend`._ _Class: KV._
- **Lender Concentration Risk** — groups live `activeLoans` outstanding by lender → per-lender share, sorted; Herfindahl-style HHI (Σ share²) → concentration level (high ≥0.50, moderate 0.25–0.50, low diversified); KPIs lenders/largest-share/concentration; share table with bars and total; high-concentration warning naming the top lender. _Persist: none (read-only from live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Interest-Paid Summary** — per live active loan estimates annual interest (outstanding × rate) and monthly interest, sorted by annual interest → KPIs total annual/monthly interest, blended cost of debt (total annual ÷ total outstanding); per-loan table with totals row; rates ≥16% flagged red. _Persist: none (read-only from live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Sanction vs Drawn / Repaid** — per live active loan compares sanctioned principal vs current outstanding → repaid (principal − outstanding) and repaid %; KPIs total sanctioned/outstanding/repaid; per-loan progress bars with totals row. _Persist: none (read-only from live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
# Valuation & Cap Table (`/valuation`) — 23 tools

One-sentence purpose: Turn the firm's live financials into a defensible valuation range (multiples + DCF) and a full suite of fund-raising / cap-table / equity calculators so a founder can model dilution, term-sheet mechanics, ESOP economics and investor returns. Stakeholders: owner, investor. Backend: KV-synced (live financial snapshot from `store` via `computeFinancialSnapshot`; only the Dilution Waterfall persists records via `useFeatureState` → `store.featureData` KV).

Section quick-nav lists 20 anchor entries; the page also renders three always-on top-of-page tools (headline KPI strip, assumptions sliders + method chart, DCF table, dilution panel) before the navigable sections — 23 distinct tools total below.

## Always-on top tools (not in the anchor nav)

- **Indicative Valuation Range (headline + assumptions + method chart)** — inputs: live `annualRevenue` (3-mo avg revenue × 12), `baseFcf` (max(monthlyNet,0)×12), industry default multiple (`INDUSTRY_MULTIPLES`, fallback 3x), user sliders revenue-multiple (0.5–15x), DCF annual growth (0–100%), discount/risk rate (10–40%) → computation: `multipleVal = annualRevenue × multiple`; `dcf.enterpriseValue` via `dcfValuation` (5-yr explicit FCF growth then discounted, plus 4% perpetual-growth terminal value, terminal cap at discount−1%); blended midpoint = (multipleVal + DCF EV)/2; football-field range low = min(×, DCF)×0.85, high = max(×, DCF)×1.15. Warns + uses 10%-of-revenue FCF proxy if not yet FCF-positive → output: KPI cards (annual revenue, indicative valuation + range, implied multiple, raised-so-far from confirmed `capitalInvestments`), horizontal bar chart by method, football-field range bar. _Persist: none (live store snapshot; slider state local useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **5-Year DCF Table** — inputs: same DCF assumptions (baseFcf proxy, growth, discount) → computation: per-year projected FCF, discount factor (pv/fcf), present value; terminal value at 4% perpetual growth discounted back; enterprise value = Σ PV + terminal PV (`dcfValuation`) → output: year-by-year table with terminal-value and enterprise-value rows. _Persist: none (live)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Dilution at this Valuation** — inputs: blended-midpoint pre-money, amount-to-raise slider (₹5L–₹5Cr, seeded from active/draft `capitalRaise.targetAmount`) → computation: `dilution(preMoney, raise)` → postMoney = pre + raise, investorPct = raise/post, founderRetainedPct = 100 − investorPct; flags >25% investor stake as aggressive → output: pre/post-money + investor-stake cards, founder-retained note, nav buttons to `/capital` and `/credit`. _Persist: none (live + local slider)._ _Class: Indicative._

## Navigable section tools

- **Comparable-Company Multiples** (#109) — inputs: sector select (`SECTOR_MULTIPLES` rev & EBITDA low/high bands per industry), annual revenue, annual EBITDA (auto-filled from run-rate), basis toggle revenue/EBITDA → computation: low/mid/high = input × sector multiple band (mid = average of low & high) → output: low/mid/high value cards + bar chart; 20–35% private-company liquidity-discount caveat. _Persist: none (local useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Berkus / Scorecard (Pre-Revenue)** (#110) — inputs (Berkus): five risk-reduction factor sliders (idea/prototype/team/relationships/rollout, ₹0–₹5L each); inputs (Scorecard): regional-median pre-money + seven weighted factor sliders (0–200% vs median) → computation: Berkus pre-money = Σ factor values (₹25L cap); Scorecard = median × Σ(weight × score/100) → output: Berkus or Scorecard pre-money valuation card. _Persist: none (local useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **DCF Sensitivity / Tornado** (#111) — inputs: live DCF base (FCF, growth, discount), assumption-swing slider (±5–50%) → computation: re-runs `dcfValuation` flexing each driver (FCF growth rate, discount rate inverse, base-year FCF) by ±swing, computes high−low range per driver, sorts by impact → output: base EV + sorted tornado bars (downside red / upside green). _Persist: none (live + local slider)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **409A-style FMV for ESOP** (#112) — inputs: equity value (from blended midpoint), fully-diluted shares, liquidation-preference stack, DLOM slider (0–50%) → computation: commonEquity = max(0, equity − pref); adjustedCommon = commonEquity × (1−DLOM); FMV/share = adjustedCommon/shares; raw/share = equity/shares → output: common-equity, after-DLOM, FMV/share (409A), undiscounted/share cards; appraiser/strike-price disclaimer. _Persist: none (local useState; equity seeded from live midpoint)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **VC Method — Exit-Value Backsolve** (#vc-method) — inputs: round size (seeded from raise), exit EV/Revenue multiple, investor target return (x), years-to-exit (2–10), revenue CAGR to exit (0–120%) → computation: exitRevenue = baseRev × (1+CAGR)^years; exitValue = exitRevenue × exitMultiple; post-money today = exitValue/targetReturn; investorOwnership = raise/postMoneyToday; pre-money = post − raise; implied IRR = (exitValue/postToday)^(1/years)−1 → output: exit value, post-money/pre-money today, investor stake cards + implied annual IRR. _Persist: none (local; revenue from live snapshot)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **First-Chicago Method** (#first-chicago) — inputs: best/base/worst-case valuations (seeded ×2.5 / ×1 / ×0.3 of midpoint), best & base probability sliders (worst = remainder) → computation: expected = Σ(value × probability)/100 → output: per-scenario contribution rows + expected (probability-weighted) valuation. _Persist: none (local; seeded from live midpoint)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Runway → Next-Round Planner** (#runway-planner) — inputs: cash-in-bank (live `snap.cash`), net monthly burn (live, from negative monthlyNet else ₹5L), time-to-close-raise slider (2–9 mo), safety-buffer slider (0–6 mo) → computation: runwayMonths = cash/burn; startInMonths = max(0, runway − closeTime − buffer); cashAtClose = cash − burn×(start+close); status ok/soon/urgent → output: current-runway, start-raising-in, cash-at-close cards + colored status banner. _Persist: none (live + local sliders)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Dilution Waterfall** (#dilution-waterfall) — inputs: editable list of rounds (name, pre-money, raise; seeded Seed + Series A from live midpoint), add/remove rounds → computation: walks rounds, per round post = pre+raise, newInvestorPct = raise/post, founderPct compounds × (1−newInvestorPct); final founder ownership after all rounds → output: editable table (post-money, new stake, founder-after per round) + final founder-ownership card; warns below 50%. _Persist: `featureData["val-dilution-waterfall"]` (KV-synced via `useFeatureState`)._ _Class: Backend (KV)._
- **Option-Pool Shuffle** (#pool-shuffle) — inputs: pre-money, round size, target-pool % (0–25% of post-money), pre/post-money timing toggle → computation: investorPct = raise/post; pre-money pool dilutes founders only (poolFromFounders = poolPct); post-money pool shared (poolPct × (1−investorPct)); founderPct = 100 − investor − poolFromFounders; investorEff adjusted in post case → output: founder/investor/option-pool stake cards + who-pays-for-pool note. _Persist: none (local; seeded from live midpoint & raise)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Convertible-Note Cap / Discount Converter** (#note-converter) — inputs: note principal, valuation cap, next-round pre-money, shares-before-round, discount %, interest %/yr, months → computation: accrued interest = inv × rate × months/12; roundPrice = pre/shares; discountPrice = roundPrice × (1−disc); capPrice = cap/shares; conversion price = lower (better for noteholder); shares issued = (principal+interest)/finalPrice; post-conversion ownership → output: principal+interest, round/conversion price-per-share, shares-to-noteholder cards; flags whether cap or discount won. _Persist: none (local; cap/pre seeded from live midpoint)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **ESOP Grant Value Calculator** (#esop-grant) — inputs: options granted, total fully-diluted shares, strike price/share, equity value (live midpoint), exit-growth multiplier (1–20x) → computation: pricePerShare = equity/total; grossNow = options × price; exerciseCost = options × strike; netNow = max(0, gross − cost); ownership% = options/total; exitPrice = price × exitX; netAtExit = max(0, options × exitPrice − cost) → output: ownership, gross-value-today, net-of-exercise, net-at-exit cards + perquisite-tax note. _Persist: none (local; equity from live midpoint)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Founder Vesting Schedule** (#vesting-schedule) — inputs: founder shares under vesting, vest length (mo), cliff (mo), months-elapsed slider → computation: vestedNow = 0 before cliff else min(total, total×elapsed/totalMonths); vestedPct; unvested; milestone schedule (cliff + yearly checkpoints + full-vest) → output: vested-now / vested-% / unvested cards, progress bar with cliff marker, milestone table; pre-cliff forfeiture warning. _Persist: none (local useState)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Rule of 40 Score** (#rule-of-40) — inputs: revenue growth % (live growth) + profit/EBITDA margin % (live net/revenue margin) → computation: score = growth + margin; passes if ≥40; impliedMultiple = clamp(2 + score×0.18, 0.5–14); impliedVal = annualRevenue × multiple → output: pass/below score banner (green/yellow/red bands) + implied EV/Revenue and implied valuation cards. _Persist: none (live snapshot + local inputs)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Liquidation-Preference Stack** (#liq-pref-stack) — inputs: exit/sale value (live midpoint), preferred capital invested, investor ownership %, preference multiple slider (1–3x), participating/non-participating toggle → computation: pref = invested × multiple; non-participating = min(exit, max(pref, exit×ownership)); participating = min(exit, pref + (exit−pref)×ownership); founderProceeds = exit − investor; investor return-x; breakeven exit = pref/ownership → output: preference-amount, investor-proceeds, founder/common, investor-return-x cards + proceeds split bar + regime explanation. _Persist: none (local; exit seeded from live midpoint)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Secondary-Sale Capital-Gains Tax** (#secondary-tax) — inputs: shares sold, cost/share, sale price/share (seeded from midpoint), holding-period slider (1–60 mo), high-income surcharge select (0/10/15/25%) → computation: gain = max(0, proceeds − costBasis); long-term if >24 mo; baseRate = 12.5% LTCG (unlisted, post-Jul-2024) else 30% STCG; effectiveRate = baseRate × (1+surcharge) × 1.04 cess; tax = gain × rate; net = proceeds − tax → output: gross-gain, effective-rate, tax-payable, net-in-hand cards + CA-confirm disclaimer. _Persist: none (local; sale price from live midpoint)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Down-Round & Anti-Dilution Impact** (#down-round) — inputs: last-round post-money (live ×1.2), prior investor ownership %, new-round pre-money (live ×0.6), new round size, protection toggle none/weighted-average/full-ratchet → computation: isDown if newPre < prevPost; drop %; newInvestorPct = raise/newPost; naive old-investor % = prevOwn × (1−newInvestorPct); weighted-average pulls ~halfway back to prior %; full-ratchet ≈ prior × 0.95 reset; founderShare = 100 − protected − newInvestor → output: up/down-round banner + new-investor / old-investor (naive & after-clause) / founder-left stake cards + clawback note. _Persist: none (local; seeded from live midpoint)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Investor MOIC / IRR** (#investor-moic) — inputs: amount invested (live raise), post-money this round (live mid+raise), exit/sale value (live ×6), years-to-exit slider (1–12), future-dilution slider (0–70%) → computation: entryPct = inv/post; exitPct = entry × (1−futureDilution); exitProceeds = exit × exitPct; MOIC = proceeds/inv; IRR = MOIC^(1/years)−1; simplistic payback = years/moic → output: entry-stake, stake-at-exit, MOIC, gross-IRR cards (tone by 3x / 25% thresholds) + exit-proceeds + recovery note. _Persist: none (local; seeded from live values)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **EV → Equity Value Bridge** (#ev-ebitda-bridge) — inputs: enterprise value (live DCF EV), total debt, cash & equivalents (live cash), minority interest → computation: netDebt = debt − cash; equityValue = EV − netDebt − minority → output: stepped bridge rows (+EV, −debt, +cash, −minority) + equity-value-to-shareholders card with net-debt/cash position. _Persist: none (live EV + cash, local debt/minority)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **ARR-Multiple Valuation** (#arr-multiple) — inputs: annual recurring revenue (live run-rate), YoY growth (live), net-revenue-retention slider (70–150%), gross-margin slider (30–95%) → computation: multiple = max(0.5, 6x anchor + growthAdj ((g−30)/30×2) + nrrAdj ((nrr−100)/10×0.6) + marginAdj ((gm−70)/10×0.4)); valuation = ARR × multiple; conservative/base/premium tiers (×0.75 / ×1 / ×1.25) → output: quality-adjusted valuation card + tier bar chart; >10x caveat. _Persist: none (live ARR/growth + local sliders)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Discounted-Payback Period** (#discounted-payback) — inputs: up-front investment (live FCF ×2), discount-rate slider (1–40%, live default), horizon slider (3–15 yrs) → computation: per-year cf = baseFcf × (1+growth)^(y−1); pv = cf/(1+rate)^y; cumulative discounted & undiscounted; payback = interpolated year where cumulative crosses investment (Infinity if never) → output: discounted-payback vs simple-payback cards + per-year cash-flow / PV / cumulative-PV table (crossing year highlighted). _Persist: none (live FCF/growth + local inputs)._ _Class: Indicative._

---

Notes:
- Every tool is calculator-style and renders client-side. Inputs auto-seed from the live financial snapshot (`computeFinancialSnapshot(store)`) and the active/draft capital raise, but slider/field state is transient `useState` and does NOT persist — except the Dilution Waterfall, the only tool that writes durable records to the KV-synced `store.featureData` (key `val-dilution-waterfall`).
- Core engine helpers live in `src/lib/finance.ts`: `dcfValuation` (5-yr DCF + Gordon-growth terminal value, terminal growth capped at discount−1%), `dilution`, `computeFinancialSnapshot`.
- No backend valuation API exists; all outputs are clearly labeled indicative / sanity-check, with disclaimers on 409A (needs independent appraiser) and secondary-sale tax (confirm with CA).
## Term Sheet (`/term-sheet`) — 18 tools

Model a fundraise and the deal mechanics around it (dilution, liquidation, control, vesting, conversion) and produce a ready-to-share, non-binding term sheet. Stakeholders: owner (founder), investor. Backend: KV-synced (only the readiness checklist persists via `store.featureData`; all other tools are stateless in-component `useState`).

- **Generator** — company, investor, investment, round type (priced / SAFE / convertible / rev-share), pre-money, valuation cap, discount %, interest % p.a., maturity months, new option-pool %, rev-share repayment multiple, liq-pref dropdown (1x non-participating / 1x participating / 1.5x non-participating / None), board-seat + pro-rata toggles → `termSheetMath()` in `@/lib/finance` computes post-money, investor stake %, founder % after, repayment total (rev-share/convertible), conversion note, price-per-share note → live computed-economics cards plus a printable (`window.print()`) non-binding term-sheet document keyed by round type, dated today, valid 30 days. Company prefilled from `store.firm`. _Persist: none (form state only; firm name read from store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Offer Comparator** — 2-3 editable offers (investor, investment, pre-money, liq-pref ×, participating, new pool %, board seats, pro-rata) → per offer: post-money = pre+investment, investor % = inv/post, founder % = 100 − investor − pool (pool created pre-money so it dilutes founders not the investor); "best" heuristics highlight highest pre-money, highest founder %, lowest liq-pref × (non-participating), fewest board seats → side-by-side comparison table with winning cell per row marked ✓. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Liquidation Pref** — exit value, investor capital, investor ownership %, preference multiple (×), participation cap (×, 0 = uncapped), participating toggle → non-participating takes GREATER of preference or as-converted equity (no double-dip); participating takes pref + pro-rata of remainder, optionally capped at capX×investment; investor payout, return multiple, % of exit, common (founders/ESOP) payout → 4 metric cards + 2-bar investor-vs-common waterfall. Single class assumed. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **ESOP Top-up Impact** — founder %, prior-investor %, current pool %, target pool % (post top-up), post-money valuation → pre-money pool top-up = target − current; founders + prior investors scaled down (existing pool keeps shares) to absorb the increment; founder dilution and founder value diluted (post-money × dilution) → cards + before/after/change stakeholder table; validates inputs total ~100%. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Clause Explainer** — static 7-clause library (liquidation pref, anti-dilution, drag/tag-along, ROFR, board composition, protective provisions, pro-rata, founder/reverse vesting) each tagged founder-friendly/neutral/watch → accordion expands to what-it-is / why-it-matters / how-to-negotiate. No numeric inputs/computation. _Persist: none._ _Class: Preview (static reference content)._
- **Anti-Dilution** — original price/share, investor preferred shares, pre-round shares outstanding (fully diluted), new round price/share, new money raised → compares No protection vs broad-based weighted-average (CP2 = CP1 × (A+B)/(A+C)) vs full-ratchet (conv price = new price); each yields adjusted conversion price, as-converted shares = origShares×origPrice/CP, bonus shares → table; warns when not a down round (no trigger). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Pro-Rata Rights** — current ownership %, new round size, new round pre-money → pro-rata cheque = roundSize × ownership; ownership if maintained vs if skipped (diluted = ownership×pre/post); dilution lost → 4 cards + post-money line. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **SAFE vs Priced** — amount to raise, SAFE post-money cap, SAFE discount %, priced-round pre-money, next-round pre (SAFE converts) → SAFE ownership = max(raise/cap, raise/(discountPrice+raise)) reporting which basis (cap vs discount) binds; priced ownership = raise/(pre+raise); founder dilution each path → 4 cards + plain-language verdict (which dilutes less, with India FEMA/CCPS caveat). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Vesting Schedule** — total grant shares, vesting period (years), cliff (months), months served → nothing vests before cliff, then linear monthly accrual = totalShares × min(m,totalMonths)/totalMonths; vested now, vested %, unvested, monthly accrual → cards + progress bar + sparse milestone table (cliff, each year, now, fully-vested); cliff warning. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Term-Sheet Checklist** — 11-item default readiness checklist (valuation, 1x non-part liq-pref, weighted-average anti-dilution, board majority, pool sizing, narrowed vetoes, founder vesting, drag threshold, FEMA/CCPS, Section 56(2)(viib) angel-tax, lawyer review); toggle/reset items → done count, % progress bar, all-done banner. _Persist: `featureData["ts-readiness-checklist"]` via `useFeatureState` (localStorage + debounced backend push + cross-device sync)._ _Class: Backend (KV-synced)._
- **Multi-Class Waterfall** — exit value, common ownership % (founders+ESOP), 1-4 editable preferred classes (name, invested, pref ×, participating, seniority) → preferences paid in seniority order (lowest first) capped by remaining exit; class as-converted ownership approximated pro-rata to invested capital over (100−common)%; non-participating class takes greater of pref or as-converted, participating takes pref + share of residual; common = exit − total preferred → seniority-sorted payout table (with basis + % of exit) + common residual row. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Board Composition** — founder seats, investor seats, independent seats, "independents lean founder" toggle → total, seats for majority = floor(total/2)+1, founder bloc, investor bloc; determines founder-controls / investor-controls / deadlock → 4 cards + colored seat-composition bar + verdict banner (with even-board deadlock note). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Tranche Planner** — pre-money, 1-5 editable tranches (label, amount, milestone trigger) → total raise, post-money = pre + total, dilution if fully drawn = total/post; cumulative dilution as each tranche lands (all priced at one pre-money) → cards + per-tranche cumulative-in / cumulative-dilution table. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Note Maturity** — principal, interest % p.a., term months, valuation cap, discount %, maturity pre-money → simple accrued interest = principal×rate×years, balance = principal+interest; three scenarios: (1) repay cash = balance, (2) convert at lower of cap or discounted price (pre×(1−disc)) → ≈ balance/(convVal+balance) stake, reporting binding basis, (3) extend +12 months → grown balance → 3 metric cards + 3 scenario rows. Simple (non-compounded) interest. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Dilution Over Rounds** — starting founder %, eventual exit value, 1-5 editable rounds (name, new investor %, new pool %) → each round scales existing holders by (1 − investor% − pool%) so founder ownership compounds down; final founder %, total dilution, founder value at exit = exit × final% → cards + per-round before/(investor+pool)/after table with bars. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Exit Proceeds Split** — exit value, 1-6 editable holders (name, ownership %, invested, pref ×, participating) → non-participating preferred converts if as-converted ≥ pref else takes pref; participating takes pref + pro-rata of remainder; preferences treated pari-passu (pro-rated if exit can't cover); per-holder preference paid, from-remainder, total payout, return multiple → table (with treatment label) + colored payout waterfall + ownership-total validity check. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **ESOP Value at Exit** — grant % (fully diluted at grant), strike price/share, implied price/share at grant, future dilution % before exit, exit valuation, vested at exit % → on notional 1,000,000-share table: diluted ownership = grant%×(1−futureDilution); gross = exit×ownership; strike cost = grantedShares×strike; net (full) = gross−strikeCost; net (vested) = net×vested%; implied gain multiple vs grant-date price; sensitivity over 0.25/0.5/1/2/4× exit → 4 cards + scenario table. Ignores taxes and liquidation prefs. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Discount vs Cap** — investment, discount %, valuation cap, next-round pre-money → discount effective pre = pre×(1−disc), cap effective pre = min(cap, pre); investor takes whichever gives the lower (better) price; binding term (cap / discount / tie), effective pre-money, investor ownership = inv/(effPre+inv); compares no-terms / discount-only / cap-only → 3 cards + table with winning row ✓. Pre-money cap method. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
## Investor Relations (`/investor`) — 14 tools

A role-gated investor portal that bundles AA-style portfolio monitoring, live deal flow, syndicates, and a suite of fundraise/IR planning tools — most metrics computed live from the firm's transactions, the deal/portfolio data illustrative. Stakeholders: owner, investor. Backend: `/api/capital/raises/*` (public list + commit) + AppContext capital store + `useFeatureState` KV. NOTE: this page is role-gated (`investor`/`super_admin`, else redirects to `/dashboard`) and leads with a portfolio-monitoring view.

Tabs (`TABS` array, 14 entries): `portfolio`, `dealflow`, `syndicates`, `update-composer`, `data-room`, `tearsheet`, `exit-waterfall`, `mrr-movement`, `burn-efficiency`, `cohort-retention`, `fundraise-pipeline`, `board-agenda`, `runway-timing`, `esop-pool`.

- **Portfolio Monitoring** (`PortfolioTab`) — fixed `MOCK_PORTFOLIO` of 4 companies (invested, equity %, runway days, monthly burn/revenue, burn/revenue trend, AA-verified flag, last alert) → derives totals (total deployed, company count, avg runway, active-alert count) and splits into "Early Warning / Needs Attention" (runway < 60d OR alert severity critical/high) vs "On Track"; each `CompanyCard` color-codes runway/severity and renders trend arrows; AA "sample preview" trust banner up top → at-risk + healthy company cards with metrics. The critical-alert count feeds the Portfolio tab badge. _Persist: none (in-memory mock constant)._ _Class: Simulated._

- **Deal Flow** (`DealFlowTab`) — live `GET /api/capital/raises/public` raises (name, raise_type/track, target/raised amount, status, closes_at) + search box, track filter (all/rev_share/reg_cf/reg_a_plus), sector/rev-band filter tokens (declared but rev/sector filters not wired to filtering) → filters by name+track, then `enriched` decorates each with deterministic mock metadata (sector, "verified" revenue, MoM growth %, DSO, employee count, founded year, use-of-funds via index math); per-card computes funded % and days-left, flags raises the user already invested in (`capitalInvestments` match on raiseId+email) → raise cards with sample MRR/growth/use-of-funds, funding progress bar, and "Express Interest →" button (opens commit modal); track-explainer footer (rev_share/reg_cf/reg_a+). _Persist: live API for raise list; commit writes via AppContext (see Express Interest modal)._ _Class: Backend (raise list + commit) with Simulated per-raise metrics._

- **Express Interest modal** (in `InvestorPage`, triggered from Deal Flow) — selected raise + investment amount input + "not binding / KYC required" agreement checkbox → on confirm `POST /api/capital/raises/:id/commit { amount }`, uses returned `equity_pct` (fallback amount÷target×100), calls `addCapitalInvestment` (status `confirmed`), toasts, reloads raises; live estimated-ownership % preview shown as amount is typed. _Persist: Backend POST + AppContext capital store (`addCapitalInvestment`)._ _Class: Backend._

- **Syndicates** (`SyndicatesTab`) — seeded `MOCK_SYNDICATES` (2) plus create-form (name + min check size) → "Create Syndicate" prepends a new local syndicate (random UUID, lead = user email prefix, target 5M, 45-day close); "Join Syndicate" adds id to a local `joined` Set and toasts a KYC-handoff message; each card shows committed-vs-target % bar, members, min check, days-to-close. _Persist: none (component `useState`; resets on unmount/reload)._ _Class: Simulated._

- **Investor Update** (`InvestorUpdateComposer`) — live transactions → derives current-month MRR (revenue txns), monthly burn (expense+payroll), cash (sum of all amounts), net burn (burn−MRR floored at 0), runway months (cash÷net burn, ∞ if none) + free-text Highlights and Asks inputs → assembles a formatted monthly investor-update email draft (subject, headline metrics, highlights, asks, sign-off from email prefix); Copy-to-clipboard and `mailto:` email buttons. _Persist: none (draft is derived/`useMemo`; highlights/asks are local `useState`)._ _Class: Backend-derived live metrics (Indicative draft)._

- **Data Room** (`DataRoomBuilder`) — 16-item `DATA_ROOM_TEMPLATE` across categories (Corporate, Cap Table, Financials, Tax, Legal, HR) → each item's status cycles missing → in_progress → ready on click; computes ready count and readiness % progress bar; grouped by category; Reset restores all to "missing". _Persist: `useFeatureState("investor-data-room")` (cross-device KV)._ _Class: KV._

- **KPI Tearsheet** (`KpiTearsheet`) — live transactions → computes current-month MRR, ARR (MRR×12), prior-month MRR for MoM growth %, monthly burn (expense+payroll), cash, net burn, runway months, gross margin ((MRR−burn)/MRR) → 8-cell one-pager grid (MRR, ARR, MoM growth, burn, net burn, cash, runway, gross margin) color-coded; dated header with firm name. _Persist: none (derived from live transactions)._ _Class: Backend-derived live metrics (Indicative)._

- **Exit Waterfall** (`ExitWaterfall`) — editable share-class table (name, type pref/common, invested, shares, pref multiple; default 4 rows) + exit-value input → models non-participating preferred payout: if exit ≤ total liquidation preference, prefs share pro-rata; else iteratively decides per pref class whether to take preference or convert to common (as-converted vs preference), then distributes residual to common+converting on a share basis; shows per-class treatment, payout, % of exit, and total distributed; add/remove rows. Disclaimer: simplified (single pref, no participation cap/dividends), indicative only. _Persist: `useFeatureState("investor-exit-waterfall")` (cross-device KV)._ _Class: KV + Indicative (cap-table waterfall model)._

- **MRR Movement** (`MrrMovement`, uses `useMonthlySeries(12)`) — 12-month revenue/expense series from live transactions → current vs prior month MRR → net-new MRR, decomposed into expansion (positive delta) vs contraction/churn (negative delta), ARR (MRR×12), MoM growth %, net revenue retention (MRR/prevMRR ×100) → metric cards + a 12-month recurring-revenue bar chart. Footnote: delta-based proxy until line-item subscription data exists. _Persist: none (derived from live transactions)._ _Class: Backend-derived live metrics (Indicative)._

- **Burn Efficiency** (`BurnEfficiency`, uses `useMonthlySeries(6)`) — last-two-months MRR + current burn → net-new ARR ((MRR−prevMRR)×12), net burn, burn multiple (net burn ÷ net-new MRR; null if no net burn), SaaS magic number (net-new ARR ÷ annualized spend), gross margin → headline burn-multiple tile with Bessemer-style rating band (Elite/Great/Good/Suspect/Concerning) plus supporting metric cards and a static benchmark legend. _Persist: none (derived from live transactions)._ _Class: Backend-derived live metrics (Indicative)._

- **Cohort Retention** (`CohortRetention`, uses `useMonthlySeries(12)`) — months with revenue → builds a retention triangle: each starting month is a cohort, each subsequent month indexed to 100 at the cohort's first month (revenue/base ×100); heatmap-colored cells by retention band (≥110 green … <60 red); empty state when no revenue. Footnote: proxy from monthly totals, not true per-customer cohorts. _Persist: none (derived from live transactions)._ _Class: Backend-derived live metrics (Indicative)._

- **Raise Pipeline** (`FundraisePipeline`) — investor CRM rows (name, firm, stage, check size, next step; 3 seeded) → add investor (defaults to "sourced"), per-row stage select across STAGE_ORDER (sourced→intro→pitched→diligence→term_sheet→closed→passed), advance-stage button (caps at "closed"), editable next-step field, remove; summary cards: closed/committed (sum of closed checks), in-play (sum excluding closed/passed), active-conversation count (excluding passed). _Persist: `useFeatureState("ir-fundraise-pipeline")` (cross-device KV)._ _Class: KV._

- **Board Agenda** (`BoardAgenda`) — meeting date + agenda items (topic, minutes, owner, done; 5 seeded) + free-text minutes + action items (task, owner, due, done; 1 seeded) → add/toggle/remove agenda topics (total-minutes sum shown), capture minutes, add/toggle/remove action items (open-action count shown). _Persist: `useFeatureState` keys `ir-board-meeting-date`, `ir-board-agenda`, `ir-board-minutes`, `ir-board-actions` (cross-device KV)._ _Class: KV._

- **Next-Raise Timing** (`RunwayTiming`) — cash + net monthly burn pre-filled from live transactions, plus editable burn-growth %/mo and raise lead-time months → simulates month-by-month cash drawdown with compounding burn growth (120-month cap) to get runway months and cash-zero date; start-raise = runway − lead time, flagged "urgent" if ≤ 1 month (red banner) → runway / start-raising-in / lead-time-buffer tiles with projected dates. _Persist: none (seeded from live transactions; assumptions in local `useState`)._ _Class: Backend-seeded live metrics + Simulated drawdown projection._

- **ESOP Pool** (`EsopPool`) — pool size + grant register (grantee, options, 48mo vest / 12mo cliff defaults, grant date; 3 seeded) → granted total, available (pool−granted), allocation % bar; per-grant vested-to-date via linear monthly vest after cliff (days-since-grant ÷ 30.44 vs cliff/vest); update pool (rejects below already-granted), add grant (rejects over available), remove grant. _Persist: `useFeatureState` keys `ir-esop-pool-size`, `ir-esop-grants` (cross-device KV)._ _Class: KV + Indicative (vesting schedule)._

## ━━━ PILLAR 5 · Operations & Supply Chain ━━━

## Operations & Inventory (`/operations`) — 37 tools

Operations Hub: capture and fulfil orders, run inventory/stock-control analytics, manage procurement, and watch spend anomalies for an SMB. Stakeholders: owner, ops, finance. Backend: `/api/operations` + KV-synced (core orders/inventory/procurement flow through `AppContext` store mutators → backend + localStorage; analytics-tab records persist via `useFeatureState` into the synced `store.featureData` "app" KV namespace — written to localStorage immediately and debounce-pushed to the backend and other devices).

The tab-selector array (`Tab` type, lines 22-26; rendered list lines 161-202) has **34 entries**: overview, orders, inventory, procurement, intelligence, prices, bom, leadtime, reorder, payables, stockledger, batchtrack, jobwork, production, warehouse, stocktake, dispatch, abc, eoq, turnover, skumargin, landed, grn, scrap, returns, valuation, safetystock, carrying, aging, stockout, cyclecount, minmax, whutil, oversell. The **Overview** tab is a dashboard (not itself a working tool), and the **Inventory** tab renders three distinct tools (the SKU register plus two auto-derived analytics panels). Net total: **37 tools**, enumerated below.

### Overview / core CRUD tabs

- **Operations Overview Dashboard** (`overview`, lines 206-277) — what it does: reads live `orders`, `inventory`, `transactions` from the store → computes four KPIs (Total Order Value = sum of non-cancelled `order.totalValue`; Pending Orders count; Inventory Value = Σ qty×unitCost; Low Stock Alerts = items where qty ≤ reorderLevel) → renders KPI cards, a static WhatsApp-capture explainer card, and a "Recent Orders" preview of the latest 5. _Persist: live store (derived, read-only)._ _Class: Backend._

- **Orders Manager** (`orders`, lines 280-374) — what it does: add-order form (buyer name/phone, source whatsapp/email/phone/manual, single line item name+qty+unitPrice) → `totalValue = qty × unitPrice`, auto `orderNumber` (`ORD-<base36 ts>`), status seeded `pending` → table with per-row status workflow buttons (pending→confirmed→dispatched→delivered) and native call / WhatsApp / SMS buttons to the buyer; confirming an order triggers a revenue transaction (per toast/handler). _Persist: live store (`addOrder`/`updateOrder`/`deleteOrder`)._ _Class: Backend._

- **Inventory / SKU Register** (`inventory`, lines 377-454) — what it does: add-product form (name, SKU, qty, unitCost, reorderLevel, category from general/fmcg/pharma/electronics/apparel/food/raw_material) → table listing each SKU with stock value (qty×unitCost) and status badge (Out of stock when qty=0, Low stock when qty ≤ reorderLevel, else OK). _Persist: live store (`addInventoryItem`/`updateInventoryItem`/`deleteInventoryItem`)._ _Class: Backend._

- **Slow / Dead Stock Panel** (renders under `inventory`, lines 457-525) — what it does: maps last fulfilled-sale date per product from confirmed/dispatched/delivered orders → days since last sale → classifies each SKU (dead >180d, slow 60-180d, never-sold "unsold", else active) and sums "tied-up" value of dead+unsold → table of slow/dead/unsold SKUs with stock, value, last-sold age. _Persist: live store (derived)._ _Class: Backend._

- **Reorder Point Calculator (inventory panel)** (renders under `inventory`, lines 528-593) — what it does: avg sales per product from fulfilled orders over a ~3-month proxy window → dailyDemand = totalSold/(months×30); fixed 7-day lead time → safetyStock = ⌈dailyDemand×leadTime⌉, reorderPoint = ⌈dailyDemand×leadTime + safetyStock⌉, daysUntilOut = ⌊qty/dailyDemand⌋ → table flags items needing reorder with a one-click "Create PO →" that jumps to Procurement pre-filled (qty = max(reorderLevel×2, 20)). _Persist: live store (derived)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Procurement / Purchase Orders** (`procurement`, lines 596-672) — what it does: low-stock "AI Procurement Suggestions" list (one-click PO pre-fill at qty = max(reorderLevel×2, 50)) + create-PO form (supplier, expected date, single item qty+unitCost) → `totalValue = qty×unitCost`, status seeded `draft` → PO cards with workflow buttons draft→approved→ordered→received. _Persist: live store (`addProcurement`/`updateProcurement`)._ _Class: Backend._

- **Anomaly Radar** (`intelligence`, lines 675-740) — what it does: runs `detectAnomalies(transactions)` (src/lib/anomalies.ts) over tenant transactions → flags duplicate payments, spend spikes, subscription creep, large new payees; buckets by severity (high/medium/low) → cards per anomaly with title, detail, date, txn count, amount, and a deep link to `/transactions`. _Persist: live store (derived from transactions)._ _Class: Backend._

### Analytics & inventory-control tabs

- **Price List** (`prices`, `PriceListTab`, lines 775-940) — what it does: auto-seeds from first 20 inventory SKUs (base price = unitCost, default GST 18%) plus manual add → for each item computes three tier prices (Retail 0%, Dealer −10%, Distributor −20% off base) and GST-inclusive variants (×(1+gstRate/100)); inline base-price edit; CSV export of all tiers ex/incl GST. _Persist: `price-overrides` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._
