- **BOM Costing** (`bom`, `BomCostingTab`, lines 1092-1267) — what it does: build a Bill of Materials (finished product, output qty/unit, overhead %, optional selling price) with component lines (material, qty, unit, unitCost) → materialCost = Σ(qty×unitCost), overhead = materialCost×overheadPct/100, totalCost, costPerUnit = totalCost/outputQty, gross margin % = (sell−costPerUnit)/sell. _Persist: `boms` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._

- **Vendor Lead-Time Scorecard** (`leadtime`, `LeadTimeScorecardTab`, lines 942-1090) — what it does: log deliveries (vendor, item, ordered/promised/actual dates) → per vendor: avg promised lead days, avg actual lead days, on-time % (actual ≤ promised), avg delay = avgActual−avgPromised, grade A ≥90% / B 70-89% / C <70%; ranked scorecard + full delivery log. _Persist: `lead-time-deliveries` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._

- **Reorder Alert Monitor** (`reorder`, `ReorderAlertTab`, lines 1269-1426) — what it does: auto-populates SKUs from inventory (reorderPoint default = reorderLevel or ⌈qty×0.3⌉, reorderQty = ⌈qty×0.5⌉, 7-day lead time) merged with manually added SKUs → flags below-ROP and Critical (stock ≤ ROP×0.5), order value = reorderQty×unitCost; inline-editable reorder qty; stock-level bar. _Persist: `reorder-items` (featureData; inventory rows are live-derived)._ _Class: KV._

- **Aged Payables** (`payables`, `AgedPayablesTab`, lines 1428-1583) — what it does: track vendor bills (vendor, bill no, amount, bill/due dates, MSME flag) → daysOverdue = today − dueDate; buckets Current/1-30/31-60/61-90/90+; KPIs Total Payable, Overdue, Due This Week (due within 7d), MSME At-Risk (MSME bills >45d overdue, per Sec 43B(h)/MSMED Act); paid/unpaid toggle; CSV export. _Persist: `aged-payables` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._

- **Stock Ledger (FIFO / WA valuation)** (`stockledger`, `StockLedgerTab`, lines 1586-1774) — what it does: record in/out moves (date, product, SKU, qty, rate, note) → chronological cost engine per product key: receipts add lots; issues consumed by **FIFO** (oldest lot first) or **Weighted Average** (running value/qty); outputs closing qty, closing value, avg rate, and COGS per SKU; method toggle; movement log with SKU filter. _Persist: `stock-ledger-moves` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._

- **Batch / Expiry / Serial Tracking** (`batchtrack`, `BatchExpiryTab`, lines 1777-1895) — what it does: record lots (product, batch/lot no, serial, qty, mfg/expiry dates, location) → status from days-to-expiry (expired <0, red ≤30d, amber ≤90d, green beyond), KPIs tracked batches / expiring ≤90d / expired units+lots; rows sorted earliest-expiry-first (**FEFO**) for pharma/food/FMCG shelf-life. _Persist: `batch-tracking` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._

- **Job-Work Tracker (Sec 143 / ITC-04)** (`jobwork`, `JobWorkTab`, lines 1897-2024) — what it does: record delivery challans of goods sent to job workers (auto challan no, worker name+GSTIN, goods, qty sent, sent/expected-return dates, process) → receive-back updates status sent/partial/received; age = today − sentDate; flags **Sec 143** overdue >365d (inputs must return within 1 year, capital goods 3 years) for ITC-04 filing. _Persist: `job-work-challans` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._

- **Production / BOM Costing Run** (`production`, `ProductionCostingTab`, lines 2026-2178) — what it does: create a manufacturing run (product, planned qty, date), add per-unit components (material, qty/unit, unitCost), enter produced qty + labour + overhead → matPerUnit = Σ(qty/unit×cost), totalMat = matPerUnit×plannedQty, batch total = totalMat+labour+overhead, cost/unit = total/(produced or planned), yield % = produced/planned. _Persist: `production-runs` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._

- **Warehouses & Multi-Location Stock** (`warehouse`, `WarehouseStockTab`, lines 2180-2343) — what it does: define locations; add stock per location (qty merges into existing product+location balance); inter-warehouse transfer with source-balance check (rejects if source qty < transfer qty), debiting source and crediting/creating destination balance; stock-by-location table + transfer history. _Persist: `warehouse-locations`, `warehouse-balances`, `warehouse-transfers` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._

- **Barcode / QR Stock-Take** (`stocktake`, `StockTakeTab`, lines 2345-2456) — what it does: scan/type a code (Enter submits; USB/BT scanner-ready) → matches inventory SKU or product name, seeds systemQty, each scan increments countedQty → variance = counted − system per line, net variance, mismatch count; inline counted-qty edit; CSV export of the variance sheet (positive = under-recorded, negative = shrinkage). _Persist: `stock-take-counts` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._

- **Dispatch / Route Planner** (`dispatch`, `DispatchPlannerTab`, lines 2458-2578) — what it does: add delivery stops (date, customer, address, area/zone, weight) → today's stops sorted by area then customer to minimise back-tracking, numbered route, total load (kg), delivered count; status chip cycles pending→loaded→delivered; separate other-day stops list. _Persist: `dispatch-stops` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._

- **ABC Inventory Analysis** (`abc`, `AbcAnalysisTab`, lines 2580-2664) — what it does: annualValue proxy = unitCost×qty per SKU, ranked descending → cumulative-value share → **Pareto** classes A (cum ≤80%), B (≤95%), C (rest); summary cards per class (count, value, % of value) + ranked table with share % and cumulative %. _Persist: live inventory (derived, no separate store)._ _Class: Backend._

- **Economic Order Quantity (EOQ)** (`eoq`, `EoqCalculatorTab`, lines 2666-2741) — what it does: inputs annual demand D, cost per order S, unit cost C, holding % → H = C×(holding%/100), **EOQ = √(2·D·S / H)**, orders/yr = D/EOQ, cycle days = 365/orders, annual ordering cost = (D/EOQ)·S, annual holding = (EOQ/2)·H, total annual cost; optional product picker auto-fills unit cost from inventory. _Persist: none (transient inputs)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Stock Turnover & Days of Inventory** (`turnover`, `StockTurnoverTab`, lines 2743-2825) — what it does: units sold per product from fulfilled orders → COGS proxy = sold×unitCost, avg inv value = qty×unitCost (closing as proxy), turns = COGS/invValue, DIO = periodDays/turns; configurable period (default 365d); overall turns/DIO/COGS KPIs + per-SKU table. _Persist: live store (derived; `periodDays` transient)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Gross Margin by SKU** (`skumargin`, `SkuMarginTab`, lines 2827-2901) — what it does: per SKU, sell price defaults to unitCost×1.4 (40% markup) or saved override → profit/unit = sell−cost, margin % = profit/sell, markup % = profit/cost, profit-in-stock = profit×qty; KPIs SKUs priced / low-margin (<15%) / total profit in stock; inline sell-price edit; sorted lowest-margin first. _Persist: `ops-sku-margin-prices` (featureData; per-SKU sell-price overrides)._ _Class: KV._

- **Landed-Cost Calculator** (`landed`, `LandedCostTab`, lines 2903-3024) — what it does: item lines (qty, unitCost, weight/unit) + shipment overheads (freight, duty, insurance) → allocate overhead by each line's share of total goods value OR total weight (toggle) → landed total = lineValue + allocatedOverhead, landed/unit, uplift % vs unit cost; grand totals row. _Persist: `ops-landed-lines`, `ops-landed-freight`, `ops-landed-duty`, `ops-landed-insurance` (featureData; basis toggle transient)._ _Class: KV._

- **GRN vs PO Discrepancy Log (3-way match)** (`grn`, `GrnDiscrepancyTab`, lines 3026-3142) — what it does: record GRNs (date, PO ref, vendor, item, ordered/received qty, PO rate, invoiced rate, note) → qtyDiff = received−ordered, rateDiff = invoiced−PO, price variance = rateDiff×receivedQty, matched only if qtyDiff=0 and rate equal; KPIs logged / with-discrepancy / net price variance — catches short deliveries and price overcharges before payment. _Persist: `ops-grn-log` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._

- **Scrap / Wastage Tracker** (`scrap`, `ScrapWastageTab`, lines 3144-3268) — what it does: log wastage (date, product, qty, unitCost, reason from Damaged/Expired/Production loss/Quality reject/Spillage/Theft/Other) → loss value = qty×unitCost; total loss, top reason, loss-by-reason bar chart; product picker auto-fills cost from inventory. _Persist: `ops-scrap-log` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._

- **Returns & RTV Register** (`returns`, `ReturnsRegisterTab`, lines 3270-3401) — what it does: log customer returns and return-to-vendor (party, product, qty, unit value, reason, disposition restock/quarantine/scrap, open/closed status) → value = qty×unitValue split into customer-return value and RTV value; KPIs total/open/customer-value/RTV-value; note flags possible GST ITC reversal on RTV defects. _Persist: `ops-returns-register` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._

- **Stock Valuation Report (FIFO vs Weighted-avg)** (`valuation`, `StockValuationTab`, lines 3403-3525) — what it does: per SKU, W-Avg value = qty×unitCost; FIFO value uses an optional per-row oldest-lot cost override (else unitCost); method toggle drives closing value; shows W-Avg total, FIFO total, and method spread (FIFO−WAvg); product/SKU filter; CSV export for year-end audit. _Persist: `ops-valuation-oldest-cost` (featureData; per-SKU oldest-lot cost; method/query transient)._ _Class: KV._

- **Safety-Stock & Min/Max Planner** (`safetystock`, `SafetyStockTab`, lines 3527-3631) — what it does: inputs lead time, lead-time variance, service level (Z-factor 90→1.28 / 95→1.65 / 97→1.88 / 99→2.33), review cycle → dailyDemand from ~90d fulfilled orders; **safety = ⌈z·dailyDemand·√(leadVar)⌉**, min/ROP = ⌈demand·leadTime + safety⌉, max = ⌈min + demand·reviewDays⌉, orderQty = max(0, max−onHand); flags SKUs below min + suggested buy value. _Persist: `ops-ss-lead-time`, `ops-ss-lead-variance`, `ops-ss-service-level`, `ops-ss-review-days` (featureData; SKU rows live-derived)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Carrying-Cost Calculator** (`carrying`, `CarryingCostTab`, lines 3633-3713) — what it does: editable annual % components (cost of capital 14, storage 4, obsolescence 3, insurance 1) → total carrying rate %; per SKU annual carry = stockValue×rate, monthly = annual/12, carry/unit; KPIs total stock value, annual + monthly carrying cost; sorted by annual carry. _Persist: `ops-carry-capital-pct`, `ops-carry-storage-pct`, `ops-carry-obsolete-pct`, `ops-carry-insurance-pct` (featureData; SKU rows live-derived)._ _Class: KV._

- **Stock-Aging Report** (`aging`, `StockAgingTab`, lines 3715-3809) — what it does: daysHeld = today − last fulfilled-sale date (fallback last stock-update) per SKU → buckets 0-30 / 31-60 / 61-90 / 91-180 / 180+; value = qty×unitCost; value-by-bucket bar chart + per-SKU table sorted oldest-first; 180+ flagged as write-off/clearance candidate. _Persist: live store (derived)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Stockout-Cost Estimator** (`stockout`, `StockoutCostTab`, lines 3811-3926) — what it does: inputs gross margin % and goodwill % (of lost margin) → dailyDemand from ~90d orders, sell price = avg order line price (fallback cost×(1+margin)), coverDays = qty/dailyDemand over 30-day horizon → unitsShort = demand×(30−cover), lostMargin = unitsShort×sell×margin, goodwillCost = lostMargin×goodwill%, totalRisk; flags SKUs out within 14d; sorted by total risk. _Persist: `ops-stockout-margin-pct`, `ops-stockout-goodwill-pct` (featureData; SKU rows live-derived)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Cycle-Count Scheduler** (`cyclecount`, `CycleCountScheduleTab`, lines 3928-4043) — what it does: editable count cadence per ABC class (A 30d / B 90d / C 180d) → ABC class derived live from each SKU's revenue contribution from fulfilled orders (A ≤80% cumulative, B ≤95%, C rest); due-now if days-since-last-count ≥ cadence; "Mark counted" stamps the SKU's lastCounted to roll the schedule. _Persist: `ops-cycle-freq-a/-b/-c` + `ops-cycle-last-counted` (featureData; ABC ranking live-derived)._ _Class: KV._

- **Min/Max Stock Planner** (`minmax`, `MinMaxPlannerTab`, lines 4045-4141) — what it does: inputs lead days, safety-buffer days, max-cover days → dailyDemand from ~90d orders; min = ⌈demand·(lead+safety)⌉, max = ⌈min + demand·maxCover⌉, orderUpTo = max(0, max−onHand); status below-min (Reorder) / above-max (Overstock) / ok (Healthy). _Persist: `ops-minmax-lead-days`, `ops-minmax-max-cover`, `ops-minmax-safety-days` (featureData; SKU rows live-derived)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Warehouse-Utilization Planner** (`whutil`, `WarehouseUtilizationTab`, lines 4143-4242) — what it does: add zones/racks (name, capacity, used) → per-zone utilization % = used/capacity (bar colour green<70 / amber 70-89 / orange 90-100 / red >100); KPIs overall utilization, total capacity, zones over capacity; over-capacity warning; inline used-update on blur. _Persist: `ops-wh-util-zones` (featureData)._ _Class: KV._

- **Oversell / Negative-Stock Guard** (`oversell`, `OversellGuardTab`, lines 4244-4327) — what it does: committed = qty on open (pending/confirmed/processing) orders per product → available = onHand − committed; status oversold (available<0) / tight (≤ reorderLevel) / ok; KPIs oversold SKUs, units short (Σ|negative available|), SKUs with commitments; red alert banner when overselling detected; sorted by lowest available. _Persist: live store (derived from orders+inventory)._ _Class: Backend._
## Vendors & Procurement (`/vendors`) — 26 tools

End-to-end vendor & procurement workbench: directory + AP/MSME compliance, PO/requisition/RFQ lifecycle, payment optimisation, vendor TDS, and spend/risk analytics. Stakeholders: ops, finance. Backend: derives a vendor master from live `store.transactions` (`amount < 0 && counterparty`) + `store.obligations` + `store.bankAccounts`; tool-built records persist via `useFeatureState` into `store.featureData` (synced "app" KV namespace → localStorage + backend KV). The "Schedule Payment" modal writes a real cash obligation via `addObligation`; no payout rail is invoked (gated).

Tab array (26): directory, aging, msme, po, three-way, vendor-tds, kyc-vault, early-pay, pay-run, spend-analysis, dup-vendor, requisition, vendor-score, rfq, advances, debit-notes, pay-forecast, blanket-po, concentration, stmt-recon, msme-interest, savings, form16a, rebate, watchlist, pay-mode.

- **Directory** — live expense txns (negative, with counterparty) → groups by counterparty; per vendor computes totalSpend, txnCount, avgPayment, last payment date/category, this-month vs last-month spend → trend (up if thisMonth > 1.05×lastMonth, down if < 0.95×); search + category filter + sortable table; summary cards (vendor count, total spend, this-month spend, recurring = txnCount ≥ 2) → vendor table with "Schedule" action. _Persist: live store (derived, read-only)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Schedule Payment (modal)** — vendor + amount (default avgPayment) + future date + optional note → validates amount > 0 → `addObligation({type:"other", dueDate, amount})` so it flows into cash forecast/runway. _Persist: live store (obligations)._ _Class: Backend._
- **AP Aging** — `store.obligations` of type other/payroll/tax → daysOverdue = today − dueDate → bucket (current ≤0, 1–30, 31–60, 60+); bucket summary cards (amount + count) + sorted ageing table with status chip. _Persist: live store (obligations).` _Class: Backend._
- **MSME 45-Day** — obligations of type other/loan → daysSinceDue → classifies breach (>45d), at-risk (31–45d), safe (1–30d), upcoming (≤0); per MSMED Act 2006: >45d flags "Breach — ITR disclosure", 30–45d shows "pay within 45−n days"; summary cards with amounts. _Persist: live store (obligations)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Purchase Orders** — vendor (datalist of known vendors) + dates + multi-line items (desc/qty/rate) + notes → auto PO number `PO-YYYY-NNNN`, poTotal = Σ qty×rate; lifecycle status select draft→sent→received→closed→cancelled; cards: open POs, open value (sent+received), total committed (≠cancelled). _Persist: KV `vendor-purchase-orders`._ _Class: KV._
- **3-Way Match** — per line: PO qty/rate, GRN qty received, invoice qty/rate, tolerance% → flags GRN≠PO qty, invoice≠GRN qty, price variance |invRate−poRate|/poRate×100 > tolerance; overBilled = invQty×invRate − grnQty×poRate; cards: lines, flagged, over-billed exposure (Σ positive overBilled). _Persist: KV `vendor-three-way-match`._ _Class: KV._
- **Vendor TDS Ledger** — vendor + section (194C 2%/194C-ind 1%/194J 10%/194J-tech 2%/194Q 0.1%/194I-rent 2%/194I-land 10%/194H 5%) + gross + date → TDS = round(gross×rate/100), net payable preview; deposited toggle; cards: total deducted, deposited, pending; feeds quarterly 26Q. _Persist: KV `vendor-tds-ledger`._ _Class: KV._
- **Onboarding / KYC** — vendor name + PAN/GSTIN/IFSC (regex-validated on save) + MSME Udyam + bank acc + email → kycComplete = PAN∧GSTIN∧IFSC valid ∧ bankAcc ≥6 chars; cards: vendors on file, KYC complete X/N, MSME registered; masks account to last 4. _Persist: KV `vendor-kyc-vault`._ _Class: KV._
- **Early-Pay Discount** — per offer: invoice amount, discount%, discount days, net days + global cost-of-capital → effAnnual = disc%/(100−disc%)×365/(netDays−discDays)×100; savings = amt×disc%; carryCost = amountPaidEarly×coc%×period/365; netBenefit = savings−carryCost; worthIt = effAnnual > coc; cards: offers worth taking, net benefit. _Persist: KV `vendor-early-pay-offers` (cost-of-capital is local)._ _Class: KV._
- **Pay-Run Scheduler** — obligations (other/payroll/tax/loan) due within horizon (7/15/30/60/90d) → checkbox select into a dated run; runTotal = Σ selected; available = Σ bank balances; shortfall = runTotal − available; cards: run total, items selected, overdue in run, after-run balance; "Schedule" toasts warning if shortfall (no disbursement — gated). _Persist: KV `ven-pay-run-selected` (selection); reads live obligations + bankAccounts._ _Class: KV._
- **Spend Analysis** — live txns → spend by vendor ranked; total; concentration threshold (default 25%) flags vendors ≥threshold of total; top-5 share; long-tail vendors <2% each + consolidation suggestion; cards + share bars + risk/consolidation alert banners. _Persist: none (threshold is local; derived from live txns)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Duplicate Detector** — normalises vendor names from live txns (lowercase, strips pvt/ltd/llp/inc/co/&/punctuation) → groups names sharing a normalised key (length ≥2, >1 member); ranks by combined spend; "Not a duplicate" dismiss; cards: duplicate sets, vendors involved, spend at risk of fragmentation. _Persist: KV `ven-dup-dismissed` (dismissals); derived from live txns._ _Class: Backend._
- **Requisition → PO** — requester + item + qty + est unit cost + need-by + justification → auto `PR-YYYY-NNN`, status pending→approved→converted / rejected; approvedVal = Σ estCost×qty of approved; cards: pending approval, approved value, total; convert toasts to raise PO in PO tab. _Persist: KV `ven-requisitions`._ _Class: KV._
- **Performance Review** — vendor (datalist) + 1–5 sliders on on-time/quality/price/support + notes → score = mean of four, period = current MMM yyyy; cards: vendors reviewed, avg score, under-performers (<3); highlights top performer; star display. _Persist: KV `ven-performance-reviews`._ _Class: KV._
- **RFQ Comparison** — item + qty + N quotes (vendor/unit price/lead days/payment term days) → weighted score: price 50% (minPrice/price), lead 30% (minLead/lead), terms 20% (term/maxTerm); ranks descending, recommends top; order total = unitPrice×qty. _Persist: KV `ven-rfq-item`, `ven-rfq-qty`, `ven-rfq-quotes`._ _Class: KV._
- **Advances Tracker** — vendor + amount + date + purpose → record advance; "Adjust vs bill" knocks down balance (adjusted = min(amount, adjusted+amt)); cards: total advanced, adjusted, outstanding (Σ amount−adjusted); progress bar per advance. _Persist: KV `ven-advances`._ _Class: KV._
- **Debit / Return Notes** — vendor + amount + reason (return/rate-diff/shortage/damage/discount) + date → auto `DN-YYYY-NNN`, status open↔adjusted; openCredit = Σ open; per-vendor open-credit netting hint; cards: open notes, credit owed to you, total raised. _Persist: KV `ven-debit-notes`._ _Class: KV._
- **Payables Forecast** — buckets 0–30/31–60/61–90d: scheduled = Σ obligations (other/payroll/tax) due in window; recurring run-rate = (90-day spend on vendors paid ≥2×)/3 per month, added to each bucket; total = scheduled+runRate; 90-day total in footer; CSV export. _Persist: none (derived from live obligations + txns)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Blanket PO Drawdown** — vendor + annual total value + valid-till → umbrella PO; "Draw" release (amount + note) validated against remaining = totalValue − Σreleases (blocked if exceeds); cards: blanket POs, total committed, drawn to date; consumed% bar + expiry flag + release log. _Persist: KV `ven-blanket-pos`._ _Class: KV._
- **Concentration Risk** — live txns → spend by vendor & by category; totalShare = spend/total, catShare = spend/categoryTotal; flags totalShare ≥ threshold (slider 10–60%, default 30) or catShare ≥60% as single-source; HHI = Σ totalShare² (≥2500 highly / ≥1500 moderately / else diversified); cards + ranked table (top 30). _Persist: KV `ven-concentration-threshold` (threshold only); derived from live txns._ _Class: Backend._
- **Statement Recon** — vendor (datalist) + pasted statement lines (parses last number per line as amount) + tolerance → greedy match each statement amount to an unused ledger entry (|diff| ≤ tol) for that vendor; outputs matched, on-statement-only (unbooked), in-ledger-only, balance difference = stmtTotal − ledgerTotal. _Persist: none (transient; reads live txns ledger)._ _Class: Backend._
- **MSME Interest 43B(h)** — per due: vendor + amount + acceptedOn + global RBI bank rate (default 6.5%) → penal annualRate = 3×bankRate; overdueDays = max(0, daysHeld−45); months = overdueDays/30; compound interest = amt×((1+annualRate/12)^months − 1) (Sec 16 MSMED, non-deductible Sec 23/43B(h)); cards: tracked principal, dues past 45d, est interest; total in footer. _Persist: KV `ven-msme-dues`, `ven-msme-bank-rate`._ _Class: KV._
- **Savings Tracker** — vendor + type (negotiation/early-pay/consolidation/avoided/rebate) + baseline + final (final ≤ baseline enforced) + note → saved = baseline−final; cards: total saved, saved this year (FY-year filter), avg savings rate = totalSaved/totalBaseline; per-entry %. _Persist: KV `ven-savings-entries`._ _Class: KV._
- **Form 16A Tracker** — vendor + PAN + quarter (Q1–Q4) + FY + TDS amount → status cycle pending→downloaded→issued (15-day issuance deadline, ₹100/day penalty); cards: certificates, awaiting issue (≠issued), total TDS covered. _Persist: KV `ven-f16a-certs`._ _Class: KV._
- **Rebate Tracker** — vendor + purchase threshold + rebate% → ytdPurchase seeded from live spend-by-vendor (refreshable); earned = ytdPurchase≥threshold ? ytdPurchase×rate/100 : 0; cards: active slabs, rebate accrued; progress bar to threshold. _Persist: KV `ven-rebate-deals` (slabs); ytd seeded/refreshed from live txns)._ _Class: KV._
- **Watchlist / Blacklist** — vendor + level (watch/hold/blacklist) + reason → flag record with date; cards: flagged vendors, blacklisted, on watch/hold; meant to warn before raising a PO. _Persist: KV `ven-watchlist-flags`._ _Class: KV._
- **Payment-Mode Mix** — live txns → classifyMode from description+counterparty keywords (upi/@ → UPI; neft/rtgs/imps → NEFT; chq/cheque → Cheque; cash/atm → Cash; card/pos → Card; else NEFT) → count/total/% per mode, sorted; warns if cash share >15% (Sec 40A(3) ₹10,000 per-payment disallowance); bars + grand total. _Persist: none (derived from live txns)._ _Class: Backend._
## Suppliers (`/suppliers`) — 21 tools

Supplier-side procurement cockpit: pay invoices early, score/rank vendors, plan reorders, manage rate contracts, verify MSME status, and reconcile GST/quality/cost across the supply base. Stakeholders: ops. Backend: `/api/suppliers` + KV.

> Persistence note: all "KV" tools use `useFeatureState(<key>)`, which writes to `store.featureData[<key>]` in the "app" KV namespace — localStorage immediately, then debounced to the backend and synced across the user's devices. Only Early-Pay calls live backend endpoints.

- **Early-Pay** (`EarlyPaySection`) — loads offers from `GET /api/suppliers/marketplace` (supplier_name, invoice_amount, early_pay_discount %, days_early, due_date, precomputed saving); "Pay Early" → `POST /api/suppliers/pay-early {offer_id}`, marks paid client-side, toasts saving → stat cards (open offers, total payable = Σ invoice_amount, savings available = Σ saving of unpaid) + per-offer pay-today amount (invoice − saving). _Persist: live backend (`/api/suppliers/marketplace`, `/api/suppliers/pay-early`); paid set is in-memory only._ _Class: Backend._
- **Scorecard** (`SupplierScorecard`) — inputs: name, quality % (0–100), OTIF % (0–100), price index (100=market, clamped ≥1), responsiveness 1–5 → composite = quality·0.35 + OTIF·0.35 + priceScore·0.20 + respScore·0.10, where priceScore = clamp(0–100, 200−priceIndex) and respScore = resp/5·100; grade A≥85 / B≥70 / C≥55 / D<55; rows ranked desc by composite → ranked table + avg composite + top supplier. _Persist: KV `supplier-scorecards`._ _Class: KV._
- **Reorder Point** (`ReorderPointTracker`) — inputs: item, avg daily use, lead-time days, safety stock, on-hand → ROP = avgDailyUse·leadTime + safetyStock; days-cover = onHand/avgDailyUse; suggested order qty = max(0, ROP + avgDailyUse·leadTime − onHand); flags rows where onHand ≤ ROP as "Reorder" → table + counts (tracked / below ROP / healthy). _Persist: KV `supplier-reorder-points`._ _Class: KV._
- **Rate Contracts** (`RateContractManager`) — inputs: supplier, item, rate, UoM (unit/kg/litre/metre/box/dozen/tonne), valid-from, valid-to → status engine: validTo<today=Expired, validFrom>today=Upcoming, daysLeft≤30=Expiring Nd, else Active → table + counts (contracts / expiring ≤30d / expired). _Persist: KV `supplier-rate-contracts`._ _Class: KV._
- **MSME Verify** (`MsmeVerificationBatch`) — inputs: supplier, Udyam no. (regex `UDYAM-XX-00-0000000`), category (micro/small/medium/not-msme/pending), outstanding ₹, invoice date; "Verify pending" sets pending→micro if Udyam format valid else not-msme → Sec 43B(h): days-outstanding from invoiceDate; micro/small with outstanding>0 and >45 days flagged "Disallowed"; stats: batch size, protected (micro+small) count, at-risk amount = Σ outstanding of breaching rows. _Persist: KV `supplier-msme-batch`._ _Class: KV. (Udyam is format-check only, not a live portal lookup — Indicative on the verification itself.)_
- **Terms Optimizer** (`PaymentTermsOptimizer`) — inputs: supplier, invoice amount, discount %, discount days, net days, + global cost-of-capital % → annualised yield = d/(100−d) · 365/(net−disc) · 100; saving = invoice·d/100; verdict "Pay early" when yield ≥ CoC; stats: terms tracked, worth-early count, discount captured = Σ saving of worth-taking. _Persist: KV `sup-terms-optimizer` (rows) + `sup-terms-coc` (CoC, default 14)._ _Class: KV._
- **Price Trend** (`PriceTrendTracker`) — inputs: supplier, item, quoted price, quote date → groups by supplier+item, sorts by date, change % = (last−first)/first·100, sorted desc → table (first/latest/change/quotes/last date) + counts (tracked lines / prices rising / quotes logged). _Persist: KV `sup-price-trend`._ _Class: KV._
- **Lead-Time Var** (`LeadTimeVarianceAnalyzer`) — inputs: supplier, PO ref, promised days, actual days → per-supplier: mean slip (actual−promised), σ (std dev of slips), on-time % (actual≤promised); rating Reliable (σ≤2 & on-time≥80%) / Erratic (σ>5 or on-time<50%) / Watch; sorted desc by σ → stats table. _Persist: KV `sup-leadtime-variance`._ _Class: KV._
- **Alt Suppliers** (`AltSupplierShortlist`) — inputs: item, supplier, price, lead days, min order qty → groups by item, flags cheapest ("Best ₹") and fastest ("Fastest") per item, single-source (<2 options) flagged as risk; sorted by price asc → per-item tables + counts (items with options / single-sourced / total options). _Persist: KV `sup-alt-shortlist`._ _Class: KV._
- **Concentration** (`ConcentrationRisk`) — inputs: supplier, annual spend → Pareto: share = spend/total, cumulative %, HHI = Σ(share²); concentrated when top-vendor share >30% or HHI >2500 (warning banner) → ranked table with share bar + cumulative; stats (total spend / top share / HHI). _Persist: KV `sup-concentration`._ _Class: KV._
- **GRN 3-Way** (`GrnThreeWayMatch`) — inputs: PO ref, supplier, PO/GRN/invoice qty, PO/invoice rate, rejected qty, + global rate-tolerance % → match: qtyOk = invQty ≤ grnQty, rateOk = |invRate−poRate|/poRate·100 ≤ tol, reject % = rejected/grnQty·100, clean = qtyOk & rateOk; over-billed = Σ(invRate−poRate)·invQty for rate-fail rows → Pass/Hold table + counts (receipts / clean matches / over-billed ₹). _Persist: KV `sup-grn-match` (rows) + `sup-grn-tolerance` (tol, default 2)._ _Class: KV._
- **Negotiation** (`NegotiationPrepSheet`) — inputs: supplier, annual spend, current/target/walk-away rate + editable checklist (5 seeded prep items, add/toggle/delete) → target discount = (cur−tgt)/cur·100; annual saving = spend·(cur−tgt)/cur when tgt<cur; ready when all checklist items done → stat cards + checklist. _Persist: KV `sup-nego-supplier`, `sup-nego-spend`, `sup-nego-current`, `sup-nego-target`, `sup-nego-walk`, `sup-nego-checklist`._ _Class: KV._
- **Pay Priority** (`PaymentPriorityPlanner`) — inputs: supplier, amount due, due date, early-pay discount %, MSME checkbox → priority score: overdue 60+min(40,−dtd); due≤7d 35−2·dtd; else max(0,20−dtd/5); +50 if MSME & dtd≤5 (43B(h) clock); +discount·6 if discount & not overdue; sorted desc → ranked table + stats (total due / overdue amount / MSME urgent ≤5d count). _Persist: KV `sup-pay-priority`._ _Class: KV._
- **GST-2B Match** (`Gst2bMatchStatus`) — inputs: supplier, GSTIN (regex-validated, optional), ITC in books, ITC in GSTR-2B, + global ₹ tolerance → diff = book−2B; |diff|≤tol=Matched, diff>tol="Not in 2B" (vendor unfiled, ITC blocked), 2B>book="Excess in 2B"; ITC at-risk = Σ(book−2B) of "Not in 2B" rows → table + stats (total book ITC / matched count / at-risk ₹). _Persist: KV `sup-gst2b-match` (rows) + `sup-gst2b-tolerance` (tol, default 1)._ _Class: KV. (User-entered 2B figures, not a live GST-portal pull — Indicative on the reconciliation source.)_
- **Quality PPM** (`QualityPpmTracker`) — inputs: supplier, units received, units rejected, period (month), + global target PPM → per-supplier aggregate: PPM = rejected/received·1,000,000, reject %; flags suppliers above target; sorted desc by PPM → table + stats (suppliers / above-target count / worst PPM). _Persist: KV `sup-quality-ppm` (rows) + `sup-quality-target-ppm` (target, default 5000)._ _Class: KV._
- **Credit Util** (`CreditPeriodUtilization`) — inputs: supplier, granted credit days, avg actual pay days, monthly purchase ₹ → utilization % = avgPay/granted·100; unused float = (monthlyPurchase/30)·max(0, granted−avgPay); verdict Over-terms (avgPay>granted) / Paying-early (util<70%) / Well-used; sorted desc by unused float → table + stats (suppliers / unused free credit Σ / over-running count). _Persist: KV `sup-credit-util`._ _Class: KV._
- **Landed Cost** (`LandedCostCompare`) — inputs: supplier, item, qty, unit price, freight total, duty %, insurance total → goods = qty·unitPrice; duty = goods·duty%; landed total = goods+duty+freight+insurance; landed/unit = total/qty; groups by item, flags cheapest landed ("Best landed"), flips counter = items where cheapest-sticker ≠ cheapest-landed → per-item tables + stats (items / quotes / flips). _Persist: KV `sup-landed-cost`._ _Class: KV._
- **Contract Calendar** (`ContractExpiryCalendar`) — inputs: supplier, contract/SOW title, annual value, notice-period days, expiry date → days-to-expiry; status Expired (<0) / Notice window (≤noticeDays) / Renew soon (≤90d) / Nd left; value-at-risk = Σ annualValue of contracts in notice window; sorted by expiry asc → table + stats (contracts / in-notice count / value at risk). _Persist: KV `sup-contract-calendar`._ _Class: KV._
- **Terms Benchmark** (`PaymentTermsBenchmark`) — inputs: supplier, category, credit days they give, annual spend → per-category avg credit days; gap = creditDays − catAvg; working-capital upside = Σ(|gap|·annualSpend/365) over below-peer suppliers; sorted asc by gap → table (their terms / cat avg / gap / verdict Push-for-more vs At/above-peers) + stats (suppliers / below-peer count / WC upside). _Persist: KV `sup-terms-benchmark`._ _Class: KV._
- **Risk Spread** (`RiskDiversification`) — inputs: supplier, region/country, annual spend, single-source checkbox → region HHI = Σ(regionShare²) (0–1), top-region share %, single-source count; concentrated when top-region ≥50% or HHI ≥0.5 (warning banner) → spend-by-region bars + supplier table + stats (top region share / region HHI / single-source count). _Persist: KV `sup-risk-diversification`._ _Class: KV._
- **EOQ Check** (`EoqCheck`) — inputs: item, annual demand, cost per order, unit cost, holding cost % p.a. (default 20), supplier MOQ → EOQ = √(2·D·S/H) with H = unitCost·holding%; order qty = max(EOQ, MOQ); orders/yr = demand/orderQty; annual cost = (D/q)·S + (q/2)·H; flags MOQ-bound (MOQ>EOQ) vs Optimal → table + stats (items / MOQ-above-EOQ count / total annual cost). _Persist: KV `sup-eoq-check`._ _Class: KV._
## Field & Offline (`/field`) — 28 tools

Counter-, van- and doorstep-grade finance tools that bill, collect and reconcile on weak/no signal, then sync when the network returns. Stakeholders: sales, ops, owner. Backend: KV-synced via `useFeatureState` (shared `field-*` keys), with a common offline action queue (`field-queue`) that several tools write into; "Sync now"/"Flush" mark items committed locally to stage them — real server sync runs through the app's sync engine on reconnect. NOTE: connectivity (`navigator.onLine` + online/offline events, Network Information API), geolocation, device camera (`capture="environment"`) and canvas are all feature-detected with graceful fallbacks. Tab count excludes the Overview hub (29 tabs total: Overview + 28 tools).

- **Overview** — no inputs → renders KPI cards (network online/offline, pending-sync count vs total captured, firm name) + an explainer + a clickable grid jumping to every other tab → orientation hub. _Persist: reads `field-queue`, live network state, `store.firm`._ _Class: Preview._

- **Connectivity Status** (`connectivity`) — no inputs; reads `navigator.onLine`, online/offline events and the Network Information API (`connection.change`) → shows live online/offline badge, time-of-last-change, and `navigator.onLine` / effective type / downlink (Mbps) / data-saver fields ("Not reported" where unsupported) → connectivity diagnostic. _Persist: none (live state)._ _Class: Backend (live browser APIs)._

- **Offline Queue** (`queue`) — description, amount (₹), type (sale/collection/visit/day-sheet/receipt) → "Capture" appends a pending `QueueItem`; "Sync now" flips pending→synced only when online; per-row delete; "Clear all" → staged offline ledger table with synced/pending status. _Persist: `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (sync simulated; honest "pending" marking)._

- **Kirana Quick-Bill** (`quickbill`) — item name, qty, price lines (Enter-to-add) + merchant UPI ID → computes line totals and grand total, builds a real `upi://pay?pa=…&pn=…&am=…&cu=INR&tn=…` deep link to collect, "Save bill to queue" pushes a `sale` QueueItem → counter bill. _Persist: bill lines local state; UPI id in `field-upi-id`; saved bill → `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (UPI link is a real backend-grade deep link; bill itself queued)._

- **Field Collection** (`collection`) — customer, amount (₹), mode (cash/UPI), optional GPS stamp via Geolocation API → records a `collection` QueueItem with timestamp + optional `GPS lat,lng` meta as proof against fake-collection disputes → queued collection. _Persist: `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (GPS via live browser Geolocation; queued)._

- **Van Day-Sheet** (`daysheet`) — opening stock, sales, returns, cash collected (all ₹) → derives closing stock (open − sold + returns), expected cash (sales − returns) and variance (cash − expected, color-coded reconciled/excess/short); "Settle" pushes a `daysheet` QueueItem with closing+variance meta → route reconciliation. _Persist: inputs local state; settlement → `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (computed; queued)._

- **Low-Data Mode** (`lowdata`) — toggle switch; reads device `connection.saveData` → flips a stored low-data preference and lists what it would do (defer photos/charts/avatars, disable polling, batch the queue) → data-saver switch. _Persist: `field-low-data`; reads live `saveData`._ _Class: Indicative (toggle stored; behaviour described, deferral wired where media is fetched)._

- **Visit Log** (`visits`) — customer (req), purpose, outcome, follow-up → logs a visit record AND mirrors a `visit` QueueItem (outcome/purpose meta); per-row delete → field-call history table. _Persist: `field-visits` + `field-queue`._ _Class: KV._

- **Day Summary** (`summary`) — no inputs; filters `field-queue` to today → KPI cards: field sales (sale+daysheet), cash/UPI collected (collection), visits logged, awaiting-sync count → owner end-of-day rollup. _Persist: reads `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (derived/read-only)._

- **Beat / Route Plan** (`beat`) — next-stop customer (Enter-to-add) → ordered stop list with reorder (↑/↓), per-stop done toggle, delete, and done/total counter → day's visit route. _Persist: `field-beat`._ _Class: KV._

- **Receipt Capture** (`receipt`) — photo via camera/file picker (`capture="environment"`) + note → saves a receipt (data-URL preview if ≤1.5 MB, else stored by filename) and mirrors a `receipt` QueueItem; thumbnail gallery with delete → bill/challan capture. _Persist: `field-receipts` + `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (real camera/FileReader; large images stored by name)._

- **Beat Check-In** (`attendance`) — beat/market place name; Check in / Check out buttons (gated so you can't double-punch) → records geo+time attendance entries (GPS lat,lng when granted, else time-only) → anti-ghost-visit attendance log (last 12 shown). _Persist: `field-attendance`._ _Class: KV (live Geolocation; graceful no-GPS fallback)._

- **Order Booking** (`order`) — customer/shop (req) + item/qty/rate lines → computes order value, "Book order" pushes a `sale` QueueItem with line-item meta → offline beat order. _Persist: order lines local state; booking → `field-queue`._ _Class: KV._

- **Beat Outstanding** (`outstanding`) — customer filter; reads live `store.invoices` (non-paid, oldest-due first) → table of dues with status (overdue/pending); "Collect" pushes a `collection` QueueItem against the invoice number → on-beat dues collection. _Persist: reads `store.invoices`; collections → `field-queue`._ _Class: Backend (live invoice book) + KV (queued collection)._

- **On-the-Go Expense** (`expense`) — category (Fuel/Toll/Food/Loading/Phone/Other), amount (₹), optional note → logs a field expense, mirrors a `receipt` QueueItem, shows today's running total; recent list (15) with delete → travel/field spend log. _Persist: `field-expenses` + `field-queue`._ _Class: KV._

- **Stock Request** (`stockreq`) — item, qty, urgent flag (per-line) → builds a replenishment list; "Send request" pushes a `visit` QueueItem summarising items (with urgent tags) → warehouse stock-out request. _Persist: `field-stock-req` + `field-queue`._ _Class: KV._

- **Signature Capture** (`signature`) — customer name + on-screen canvas signature (pointer drawing; canvas feature-gated with fallback message) → saves signature as PNG data-URL; gallery with delete → credit-sale/delivery acknowledgement proof. _Persist: `field-signatures`._ _Class: KV (real canvas `toDataURL`; degrades if canvas unsupported)._

- **Proof of Delivery** (`pod`) — customer/delivery point (req), delivery photo (camera, ≤1.5 MB inline else by reference), note, optional GPS → saves a POD record and mirrors a `visit` QueueItem (POD/GPS meta); thumbnail gallery → delivery dispute settlement. _Persist: `field-pod` + `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (live camera + Geolocation)._

- **Daily Target** (`target`) — sales-target (₹) and visits-target inputs → live progress bars read from today's `field-queue` (sales+collection+daysheet vs sales target; visit count vs visit target), with both-hit celebration → rep goal tracker. _Persist: `field-target-sales`, `field-target-visits`; reads `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (derived progress)._

- **KM Expense Claim** (`km`) — from/to legs, distance (km), rate (₹/km); Mark start / End→auto-fill via Geolocation (Haversine straight-line distance) → computes claim amount, pushes a `receipt` QueueItem (km×rate, GPS flag), shows today's km+amount and trip history (12) → travel reimbursement by distance. _Persist: `field-km-trips`, `field-km-rate`; claim → `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (real GPS Haversine; honest "straight-line floor" note)._

- **Market Intel** (`intel`) — competitor/brand (req), product (req), their price (req ₹), our price (optional), note → logs intel, computes our-vs-their price gap (dearer/cheaper/level), mirrors a `visit` QueueItem → competitor pricing capture (works fully offline). _Persist: `field-intel` + `field-queue`._ _Class: KV._

- **Asset / Meter Log** (`meter`) — asset/meter name, reading value, unit (units/litres/kWh/km/hrs/kg) → logs reading, computes consumption delta vs the last reading of the same asset (case-insensitive), mirrors a `visit` QueueItem; recent list (15) → on-site meter/odometer log. _Persist: `field-meter` + `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (computed delta)._

- **Field Issue Ticket** (`issue`) — title (req), priority (low/medium/high), note, photo (camera), optional GPS → raises a ticket (priority dot, GPS/time meta), mirrors a `visit` QueueItem; resolve/reopen toggle, delete, open-count; recent list (15) → on-site problem escalation. _Persist: `field-issues` + `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (live camera + Geolocation)._

- **On-Site Quotation** (`quote`) — customer (req) + item/qty/rate lines, discount % and GST % → computes subtotal, discount, taxable, GST, grand total; "Save quotation" pushes a `visit` QueueItem (item count, total incl. GST) → at-premises priced offer (queues as visit, not a posted invoice). _Persist: `field-quote-gst`, `field-quote-disc`; quote → `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (computed totals; queued)._

- **Route-Wise Sales** (`routesales`) — per-entry route/beat tag inputs; reads today's money entries (sale/daysheet/collection) from `field-queue` → groups by route into a sales/collections/count table sorted by total, plus active-routes/sales/collections KPIs → where today's money came from (fully offline). _Persist: `field-route-tags` (id→route map); reads `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (derived rollup)._

- **Cash Handover** (`handover`) — denomination counts (₹500/200/100/50/20/10), "Handed to" → sums counted cash, compares against today's queued collections (expected), shows variance; "Record handover" pushes a `daysheet` QueueItem (expected+variance meta) → end-of-beat deposit reconciliation. _Persist: counts/handedTo local state; handover → `field-queue`; reads `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (computed; coins ignored)._

- **Discount Approval** (`discount`) — field self-approval cap %, customer, bill amount (₹), discount asked % → computes discount value, flags within-cap vs over-cap (needs owner approval), pushes a `visit` QueueItem (auto-approved or pending-approval meta) → margin-control discount gate. _Persist: `field-discount-cap`; decision → `field-queue`._ _Class: KV (policy check; queued)._

- **Sync Health** (`synchealth`) — no inputs; derives from `field-queue` + `navigator.onLine` → status (healthy/ready/waiting), pending count, oldest-pending age (stale >60 min warning), network state; "Flush pending" marks staged entries committed when online → reconnect-readiness dashboard. _Persist: reads/writes `field-queue`; live network._ _Class: KV (derived; flush simulated, real sync on reconnect)._

## ━━━ PILLAR 6 · Intelligence & AI ━━━

## AI CFO Copilot (`/copilot`) — 25 tools

An assistive layer over the workspace's live financial numbers: daily briefs, ranked recommendations, plain-language answers and planning aids — all computed locally from the user's own data. Stakeholders: owner, finance. Backend: NONE in this file — despite the brief's mention of `/api/ai`, `CopilotPage.tsx` makes zero network calls; every "AI" answer is rule-based pattern-matching over the in-memory `store` snapshot (`computeFinancialSnapshot`). Persistence is browser-local via `useFeatureState` (KV). HONEST NOTE: the page is explicitly assistive/preview — it never moves money, sends messages, or files anything; every "action" is a `navigate()` deep-link or a clipboard copy. The page banner says so verbatim.

Source: `src/features/copilot/CopilotPage.tsx`. Tools = the 25 entries in the `TABS` array (lines 30-56), each a rendered sub-component. UI filter/sort tokens (severity sort orders, horizon `7/14/30d` pickers, search keywords) are excluded as tools.

- **Overview** — `signals` snapshot → 4 KPI cards (cash, runway, overdue receivables, health score) + static "what the copilot does" cards + module deep-link chips → navigation hub. No computation beyond reading `signals`; the in-page cards navigate to `/copilot` (no-op) and the chips `navigate()` to other modules. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (display) + Preview (links)._ Rule-based.

- **Daily CFO Brief** — `signals` → conditional plain-language lines (cash/runway, net-positive vs burn, overdue, due-today, 90-day obligations, customer concentration ≥30%) with tone dots → digest + "Copy brief" (clipboard) + deep-link to `/cfo-brief`. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._ Rule-based (hand-written `if` thresholds); NOT AI.

- **Recommended Actions** — `buildRecs(signals)`: heuristic rules (overdue>0 → chase; runway<120 & burn → extend; expense>0 & burn → cut 10%; obligations90>cash → stress-test; topCustomer≥30% → diversify; DSCR<1.25 → improve coverage; else "nothing urgent"), sorted by severity then `impact` → ranked cards with CTA `navigate()`. _Persist: `cop-actions-dismissed` (string[] of handled ids, KV)._ _Class: Indicative + Preview._ Rule-based.

- **Quick-Action Launcher** — free-text `q` scored against a 12-entry `COMMANDS` keyword table (keyword substring match + label match bonus) → ranked matches; clicking toasts + `navigate()` to the module. Only navigates; does not perform the task. _Persist: none._ _Class: Preview._ Rule-based keyword matcher (NOT NL/AI).

- **Ask the Copilot** — question text → `answerQuestion(q, signals)`: regex pattern-match on topic (runway / cash-down / overdue / health / debt-DSCR / concentration / due-today; else help fallback) returns a templated sentence filled with `signals`. Logged in component state (in-memory, lost on unmount). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._ RULE-BASED Q&A — NOT真 AI / NOT LLM-backed despite "Ask"/"Copilot" framing; grounded only in stored metrics.

- **Runway Goal Planner** — `targetMonths` slider (3-24) → `cashNeeded = target×burn`, `gap = needed − cash`, then heuristic split of gap across collect (≤ overdue), cut (≤10% expense × horizon), borrow (remainder) → suggested-plan rows with deep-link CTAs. Returns "net positive / no gap" states when burn≤0. _Persist: none (slider is local state)._ _Class: Simulated (heuristic plan) + Preview._ Rule-based.

- **Month-End Close** — `snap`+`store` → 5 auto-status checklist items computed live (uncategorised txns w/ blank counterparty, overdue receivables, draft POs, overdue obligations, GST net payable — last always "review") + 4 manual sign-off items (bank recon, depreciation, payroll, owner/CA) → progress %. Open links `navigate()`; nothing closes the books. _Persist: `cop-close-checklist` (string[] of ticked manual ids, KV)._ _Class: Indicative (auto) + KV (manual)._ Rule-based.

- **Explain a Number** — pick a metric (runway / gross margin / DSCR / cash-conversion-cycle / health) → shows value + plain-words formula + the input rows pulled from `snap` (e.g. health renders all 7 driver components) + "see detail" deep-link. Read-only; changes nothing. _Persist: none (selection is local state)._ _Class: Indicative._ Rule-based drill-down.

- **Payment Prioritizer** — obligations due ≤30d + approved/ordered POs (max 8), sorted overdue-first then by type urgency (tax<payroll<loan<other<supplier) then due-date; walks the list spending only `cash − reserve` → PAY NOW / PARTIAL / DEFER tags + shortfall → `/credit` link. Triage advice only; releases nothing. _Persist: `cop-pay-reserve` (number, KV)._ _Class: Simulated (greedy allocation) + Indicative._ Rule-based.

- **Due This Week** (ComplianceDigest) — horizon picker (7/14/30d) → obligations due ≤horizon + advance-tax installments in window, sorted by date, overdue flagged → list + total + "Copy digest" + `/compliance` link. Reminds only; filing happens elsewhere. _Persist: none (horizon is local state)._ _Class: Indicative._ Rule-based.

- **Top Risks** — `snap.health.components` scoring <60 (weighted by `(60−score)×weight`) plus acute signals (overdue, runway<90 & burn, obligations90>cash), deduped by title, top 3 → ranked cards with fix-page deep-link (uses each driver's `fixPath`/`fixLabel`). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative + Preview._ Rule-based.

- **Savings Finder** — last-90-day expense txns grouped by counterparty/description → monthly avg (total/3); keeps items that are recurring or ≥3 occurrences AND ≥₹5,000/mo, top 12; tick to tally earmarked monthly/annual saving. Cancels nothing. _Persist: `cop-savings-cut` (string[] of earmarked names, KV)._ _Class: Indicative + KV._ Rule-based grouping.

- **KPI Targets** — editable targets for 4 KPIs (runway months, gross margin %, DSO days, health score) compared to live actuals from `snap`/`signals` → met/gap badges + progress bars + reset. Actuals read-only. _Persist: `cop-kpi-targets` (KpiTargetState, KV)._ _Class: KV (targets) + Indicative (actuals)._ Rule-based.

- **End-of-Day Brief** — today's non-transfer txns → money in/out/net for the day + position (cash/runway/health) + open items (overdue, due-today, GST payable, runway<120 & burn) → copy-ready `<pre>` + clipboard. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._ Rule-based.

- **Cash Early-Warning** — 8-week forward projection: opening cash + weekly operating run-rate (`monthlyNet/30 × 7`) + open invoices due in week − obligations due in week; flags first week closing < ~2-week expense buffer (`monthlyExpense × 0.5`) → per-week table + first-breach banner + collections/forecast links. Read-only forecast. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated (projection) + Indicative._ Rule-based.

- **Collect-First Worklist** — open (non-paid) invoices scored `amount × (1 + daysOverdue/30)`, top 12 → ranked tickable list + "still to chase" total + `/collections` link. Contacts no one. _Persist: `cop-collect-first-done` (string[] of ticked invoice ids, KV)._ _Class: Indicative + KV._ Rule-based ranking.

- **Invoice-Now Candidates** — delivered/dispatched orders with value >0, matched against existing invoice customer names; orders for an unbilled buyer flagged "likely unbilled", sorted unbilled-first then value, top 12 → list + likely-unbilled total + `/receivables` link. Raises nothing. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._ Rule-based heuristic (name-match, not invoice-line match).

- **Pay Now vs Later** — window picker (3/7/14d) → obligations due ≤45d split into "pay now" (overdue, or due ≤window — note the rule collapses to `dueDate ≤ soon` for all types) vs "can wait"; totals vs cash; warns if pay-now exceeds cash → two columns + `/spend` link. Timing advice; releases nothing. _Persist: none (window is local state)._ _Class: Simulated (timing split) + Indicative._ Rule-based.

- **This Week** (ThisWeekFocus) — impact-ranked task list from signals: chase overdue, settle obligations due ≤7d, pay advance-tax due this week, review unbilled fulfilled orders, runway<120 & burn, DSCR<1.25; else "nothing pressing"; top 6 → tickable list + "Copy list" + per-task deep-links. To-do list, not an executor. _Persist: `cop-this-week-done` (string[] of ticked task ids, KV)._ _Class: Indicative + KV._ Rule-based.

- **Off-Track KPI** (KpiOffTrackExplainer) — reads saved `cop-kpi-targets`, compares 4 KPIs to live values, picks the one with the largest `gapPct` off-track → now/target/gap cards + canned "why" text + lever buttons deep-linking to fix pages. Diagnosis only. _Persist: reads `cop-kpi-targets` (KV, shared w/ KPI Targets); writes none._ _Class: Indicative + Preview._ Rule-based (pre-written explanations, not generated).

- **Attention Feed** — heuristic anomaly/risk detections (runway<90 & burn; overdue; due-today; expense spike = max outflow >3× median when ≥4 expenses; concentration ≥30%; DSCR<1.25), severity-sorted → cards with "Investigate" deep-link. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative + Preview._ Rule-based.

- **Guardrails & Limits** — editable policy: per-action limit, daily limit, approval-over threshold, allowlist-only toggle, quiet-hours toggle → saved config + summary cards + reset. EXPLICITLY non-enforcing: banner states nothing enforces them automatically; they only document boundaries for hypothetical future automation. _Persist: `cop-guardrails` (Guardrails, KV)._ _Class: KV (stored policy, no effect)._ Config only.

- **Autopilot Toggles** — 5 boolean toggles (daily brief, collections nudges, spend watch, compliance radar, forecast refresh) → stored on/off prefs. EXPLICITLY simulated: banner states none move money, send messages, or file anything; they only record which assists the user would want. _Persist: `cop-autopilot` (AutopilotState, KV)._ _Class: Simulated / Preview (toggles drive nothing)._ Config only.

- **Action Log** — manual free-text entries timestamped on add → user's own assistive audit trail; removable rows. No auto-capture — purely what the user types. _Persist: `cop-audit-log` (LogEntry[], KV)._ _Class: KV._ Manual log.

- **Weekly Review** — `signals` → auto-generated wins / watch-outs summary (net-positive, no-overdue, healthy DSCR, strong health vs overdue, burn, runway<120, concentration, 90-day obligations) → copy-ready `<pre>` + clipboard. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._ Rule-based.

### Honest classification summary
- **真 AI-backed (LLM):** NONE. The "Ask the Copilot" and "Quick-Action Launcher" tabs imply NL/AI but are regex/keyword rule engines. No `/api/ai` call exists in this file.
- **Rule-based (live data, deterministic):** all 25 tools compute from `store`/`snap`/`signals`.
- **Simulated/heuristic projections:** Runway Goal Planner, Payment Prioritizer, Cash Early-Warning, Pay Now vs Later (forward/allocation math from assumptions).
- **Stored-config only (no effect):** Guardrails (non-enforcing by design), Autopilot (simulated by design).
- **KV-persisted state:** actions-dismissed, close-checklist, pay-reserve, savings-cut, kpi-targets, collect-first-done, this-week-done, guardrails, autopilot, audit-log.
- **Preview/navigation:** every CTA is `navigate()` or clipboard — no money moves, no messages send, no filings happen. The page banner asserts this explicitly.
## Analytics & BI (`/analytics`) — 35 tools

Self-serve financial analytics and BI workbench that turns the live transaction/invoice/order/asset store into P&L, cash-flow, profitability, concentration, forecasting and predictive views. Stakeholders: owner, finance, sales. Backend: KV-synced (every tab reads the live `useApp()` store — transactions, invoices, inventory, fixedAssets, activeLoans, bankAccounts, orders, firm); tool-specific assumptions persist via `useFeatureState` (KV). Page-level PDF/Excel export covers Monthly P&L + top customers/vendors.

- **Overview** — live transactions → revenue (sum +amt)/expense (|−amt|)/net/margin per month over 3/6/12-mo range; Revenue-vs-Expense bar/area chart (toggle series), Net-margin line, expense category pie (transfers excluded), top-5 revenue customers & top-5 expense vendors by counterparty share → dashboard. _Persist: none (range/chartType are local UI state)._ _Class: KV._
- **Revenue** — +amt transactions → this-month vs prev-month revenue, avg monthly (÷6), revenue concentration (top customer %), monthly revenue bar trend, per-customer revenue table with share bars → revenue dashboard. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Expenses** — −amt transactions → expense-by-category horizontal bar (last 30 days), top vendors by spend table (with % and inferred category), top-3 category cards with % of total expense. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Benchmarks** — your ratios (Payroll/Rev, OpEx/Rev, Net margin, Tax/Rev) vs static reference figures (35/40/15/8%) + efficiency ratios (Rev/Employee placeholder, Gross margin, Burn multiple, Operating leverage) → comparison bars. Reference points are hardcoded, explicitly "not live peer data." _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative._
- **P&L Deep Dive** — category totals → income-statement bridge (Revenue → −OpEx → Gross Profit → −Payroll −Taxes → EBIT → +Depreciation(est. 1.5% of rev) → EBITDA → −Interest(est. 35% of loan pmts) → Net Income), unit-economics cards (annual run-rate, avg ticket, EBITDA/gross margin, rev-per-₹-expense, burn multiple), monthly income-statement table (direct costs est. 50% of expense), revenue-quality (recurring %, customer concentration, HHI). _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (depreciation/interest/direct-cost are estimates)._
- **Cash Flow** — all transactions bucketed into 12 months → inflows/outflows/net/cumulative position, signed net-flow bar + cumulative line, inflow-vs-outflow bars, monthly table, CSV export. Independent of range selector. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Concentration** — revenue-by-customer & spend-by-vendor by counterparty → top-1/top-3 share, HHI (Herfindahl, Σshare²×10000), risk banner (>40% high / >20% medium), ranked bars; lender-scrutiny threshold at 20%. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Sales vs Target** — owner-entered monthly targets (global apply or per-row) vs actual revenue → variance, achievement %, on-track/near-miss/below-target badges, totals. _Persist: `sales-targets` (KV)._ _Class: KV._
- **Cash Forecast** — trailing 6-mo avg revenue/expense × user sliders (forecast months 1-12, monthly growth %, cost change %, opening cash) → projected revenue/expense/net + cumulative cash, shortfall warning. _Persist: none (slider state local)._ _Class: Simulated (forward projection)._
- **Balance Sheet** — auto-derived assets (bank balances, unpaid invoices=AR, inventory qty×unitCost, fixed-asset net book value per Schedule II depreciation) + manual inputs (other current, AP, short borrowings, taxes payable, other long-term, share capital, fixed override) → retained earnings as balancing figure, current ratio, balanced check. _Persist: none (manual inputs local state)._ _Class: Indicative (management-use, not statutory Schedule III)._
- **Ratio Analysis** — annualised revenue/net-profit/EBIT from trailing-12mo tagged transactions + store-prefilled manual balance-sheet inputs → liquidity (current, quick), profitability (net margin, gross margin, ROE, ROA), leverage (D/E lowerBetter, interest coverage), efficiency (asset turnover, inventory turnover) with good/watch/poor grades vs indicative SME benchmarks. _Persist: none (manual inputs local)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Trial Balance** — tagged transactions → cash-basis ledger (Sales credit, OpEx/Salaries/Taxes debit, Loan dr/cr by sign, Transfers, Cash & Bank as contra balancing account so dr=cr) → debit/credit columns, balanced check, CSV export. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (simplified cash-basis, not full double-entry)._
- **Sales Commission** — tiered (marginal/slab) or flat-% structure on manually-added salespeople's sales → per-person commission, effective rate, total payout, avg rate; TDS 194H/GST note. _Persist: `commission-mode`, `commission-flat-rate`, `commission-tiers`, `commission-people` (KV)._ _Class: KV._
- **SKU Profitability** — items inferred from transaction descriptions (2-word title-case proxy) × assumed COGS% (default 60%, per-item overridable) → revenue, units, gross margin, margin%, winners (≥40%) vs losers (<20%), top-8 margin bar. _Persist: `sku-default-cogs`, `sku-cogs-overrides` (KV)._ _Class: Indicative (no SKU master; COGS assumed)._
- **Customer Cohorts** — revenue by counterparty → first/last seen, months active, monthly rev, LTV proxy (monthlyRev×24×0.5 contribution), active(≤90d)/churned status, acquisition cohorts by first-seen month with retention %, per-customer profitability table. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (LTV/churn heuristic)._
- **Region P&L** — segment transactions by real bank account OR derived "region" (deterministic hash of counterparty into N/S/E/W/Central — no geo field) → per-segment revenue/expense/net/margin, best/weakest, grouped bar. _Persist: `branch-segment-by` (KV)._ _Class: Indicative (region is a proxy bucket)._
- **Unit Economics** — assumptions (S&M % of expense=15, gross margin %=50, lifetime months=24) → CAC (S&M spend ÷ new customers), LTV (monthly ARPA×lifetime×GM), LTV:CAC ratio (target ≥3x), CAC payback months, net-LTV bridge. _Persist: `ue-sm-pct`, `ue-gm-pct`, `ue-lifetime` (KV)._ _Class: Indicative (assumption-driven)._
- **Sales Funnel** — owner-entered leads/qualified + invoices (issued=proposals, paid=won; fallback to revenue-txn count) → 4-stage funnel with stage conversion %, overall win rate (won÷leads), won value, open invoices. _Persist: `funnel-leads`, `funnel-qualified` (KV)._ _Class: Indicative (no CRM; proxied from invoices)._
- **Expense Variance** — 12-mo monthly expense → MoM or YoY* mode, this-month vs base variance (abs & %), per-category cost-driver variance table (current vs prev/~12mo-ago), 12-mo trend line. YoY proxied to earliest month if <12mo history. _Persist: `variance-mode` (KV)._ _Class: KV._
- **Pareto 80/20** — revenue by customer OR item (deriveItem) → ranked contribution with cumulative %, count driving 80% of revenue, top-entity share, HHI, Pareto composed chart (bars + cumulative line), highlighted 80% cut-off row. _Persist: `pareto-dim` (KV)._ _Class: KV (item dim is Indicative — inferred)._
- **Margin Bridge** — base revenue × what-if levers (price %, volume %, unit-cost %, base COGS %=60) → price/volume/cost profit effects, new gross profit & margin, profit-impact, waterfall bars. Price→profit, volume→contribution at margin, cost→COGS. _Persist: `bridge-price`, `bridge-volume`, `bridge-cost`, `bridge-cogs-pct` (KV)._ _Class: Simulated (what-if model)._
- **Churn Flags** — heuristic scoring: customer churn risk from revenue recency/frequency/cadence (>90d=+40, ≤1 txn=+25, overdue vs own cadence) + invoice late-payment risk (overdue status, days overdue, prior-overdue same customer) → High/Med/Low lists, revenue-at-risk, exposure-at-risk. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (heuristic, explicitly not ML)._
- **Margin Trends** — trailing-12mo revenue/expense × assumed COGS% (default 60%) → monthly gross margin % vs net margin %, avg gross/net, this-month net, net-margin trend (pts vs prev), dual line chart. _Persist: `anl-mt-cogs-pct` (KV)._ _Class: Indicative (gross uses assumed COGS)._
- **Expense Ratios** — trailing-12mo expense by category (Operating/Payroll/Taxes/Loan) as % of monthly revenue → 12-mo avg per category cards, stacked area chart, total tracked-cost ratio. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **AR Ageing** — open (unpaid) invoices bucketed by days overdue from due date (0-30/31-60/61-90/90+) → total receivables, overdue >30d, 90+ provisioning candidate, DSO (AR ÷ avg daily revenue over trailing 6mo), bucket bars + pie + open-invoice detail table. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Break-Even** — trailing-6mo revenue/cost → payroll + assumed fixed-share% (default 55) of non-payroll = fixed cost; contribution-margin ratio; break-even revenue (fixed÷CM ratio); margin of safety; actual-vs-break-even line chart. _Persist: `anl-be-fixed-pct` (KV)._ _Class: Indicative (fixed/variable split assumed)._
- **Working Capital** — per-month (12mo): DSO (open AR ÷ daily revenue run-rate), DPO (~30-day purchase proxy), assumed DIO (default 30), CCC = DSO+DIO−DPO → KPI cards + trend lines. _Persist: `anl-wc-inv-days` (KV)._ _Class: Indicative (DPO/DIO proxied)._
- **Seasonality** — revenue transactions → day-of-week heat grid (intensity vs busiest day), monthly seasonality index (100=avg active month), peak day/month, weekend share. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Refund Impact** — negative-revenue txns + outflows matching refund/return/credit-note/discount/rebate regex → 12-mo gross vs net revenue, refund total, leakage rate %, avg/month, stacked gross-net + leakage-% chart, largest-refunds table. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Per-Employee** — annualised (trailing-12mo) revenue/profit/payroll ÷ owner-entered headcount → revenue/employee, profit/employee, payroll/employee, revenue-per-₹-payroll multiple, vs owner-set benchmark (default ₹15L). _Persist: `anl-pe-headcount`, `anl-pe-benchmark` (KV)._ _Class: Indicative (no HR module; headcount/benchmark entered)._
- **YoY Growth** — revenue per month vs same calendar month one year prior (subYears) over 3/6/12-mo window → this-period vs prior-year revenue, absolute & % growth, this-year-vs-last-year bars, monthly growth decomposition table. Needs 13+ months history. _Persist: `anl-yoy-window` (KV)._ _Class: KV._
- **New vs Repeat** — first-ever revenue date per counterparty (full history) defines new vs repeat within lookback window → new/repeat revenue & customer counts, retention/acquisition mix %, pie split. _Persist: `anl-nvr-months` (KV)._ _Class: KV._
- **Weekday Pattern** — revenue transactions grouped by booking day-of-week → strongest/weakest day, weekend share, revenue-by-day bar (peak highlighted), per-day table (revenue, count, avg ticket, share). _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **AOV Trend** — revenue ÷ revenue-transaction count per month over 6/12-mo → overall AOV, latest-month AOV, peak month, MoM % change, composed AOV-line + transaction-count-bar chart. _Persist: `anl-aov-range` (KV)._ _Class: KV._
- **Channel Split** — `orders` store grouped by source (WhatsApp/Email/Excel/Manual/Phone), cancelled excluded → total order value, top channel, channel concentration %, pie mix + per-channel table (value, count, avg order, share). _Persist: live store (orders)._ _Class: KV._
## Benchmarks (`/benchmarks`) — 21 tools

Benchmarks your live financials against your own trailing-12-month quartiles and indicative sector reference bands. Stakeholders: owner, all. Backend: KV-synced (reads `transactions`, `bankAccounts`, `invoices`, `inventory`, `procurement`, `activeLoans`, `fixedAssets`, `firm`; the only writable input is headcount, persisted via `useFeatureState` → `store.featureData`). NOTE: sector reference bands are INDICATIVE — directional guides for typical Indian SMBs, not live peer data (every tool re-states this). Sector selector (10 sectors) drives bands; data-backed bands exist only for `default`, `Manufacturing (SMB)` and `IT Services`, all others fall back to `default`.

Tabs (tool selector array): Overview, Industry Ratios, Cost Structure, Growth Percentile, Working-Capital, Profitability, Expense Ratios, Per-Employee, Digital Score, Valuation Multiple, Stock Turn, Tax Burden, EBITDA Margin, Revenue Stability, Liquidity Buffer, Debt Leverage, Opex Efficiency, Net Margin Band, Cash Runway Band, Customer Retention. (20 tabs + the Overview default = 21 distinct tools.)

- **Overview (Peer Scorecard)** — current-month revenue, costs (ex-payroll), payroll, monthly burn, bank balance + selected sector → computes gross margin, cash runway (days), revenue growth (MoM), payroll/revenue, burn multiple; ranks each into a percentile (90/65/40/20) and, where ≥3 months of history exist, replaces gross-margin/payroll/growth bands with the tenant's OWN trailing-12-month P25/P50/P75 (via `percentiles`); renders radar (you vs median), a circular composite peer score, per-metric quartile labels, and a P25/median/P75 bar breakdown with a "gap-to-median" coaching tip per metric → overall quartile position + per-metric ranking. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative (self-quartile bands are Backend-derived; sector fallback is Indicative)._
- **Industry Ratio Benchmarking** — TTM revenue/cost/payroll, bank balance, open invoices, active-loan EMIs → current ratio ((balance+openAR)÷(3×monthly EMI)), net margin (after est. interest = 40% of EMI), opex/revenue, interest coverage (EBIT÷est. interest), revenue/cash-assets; each placed on a sector low/mid/high band via linear `bandPercentile` → composite percentile, TTM revenue, TTM net profit, count benchmarked, and a per-ratio table with position bar. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative (live-computed ratios vs indicative bands; interest/liability proxies disclosed)._
- **Peer Cost-Structure Benchmark** — TTM revenue, cost, payroll vs sector cost-mix reference (payroll/COGS/rent/marketing/admin %) → live payroll/revenue and operating-spend/revenue ratios (non-payroll collapsed into one line since txns aren't sub-categorised) compared to peer payroll % and summed peer non-payroll %, plus a peer reference cost-mix stacked bar with legend; flags lines above peer benchmark (pp gap) → your structure vs peers. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Growth-Rate Percentile** — trailing-12-month monthly revenue series → compound monthly growth rate (`cmgr`, needs ≥2 positive months) and latest MoM growth; ranks CMGR within a sector growth distribution (P10/P25/P50/P75/P90, piecewise-linear interpolation) → your CMGR, latest MoM, segment percentile + a distribution bar chart highlighting bars at/below your CMGR. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Working-Capital Benchmark (CCC)** — invoices, inventory, procurement, transactions → DSO (`dso`), DIO (`dio`), DPO (`dpo`); CCC = DIO+DSO−DPO vs sector norm CCC; computes gap-days × average daily spend run-rate (annual spend ÷ active months ÷ 30) for cash locked/freed; per-component bars with norm markers and improvement hints → your CCC, sector norm, gap, cash impact. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative (CCC live-computed via 90-day proxies; sector norms indicative)._
- **Profitability Percentile** — TTM revenue and direct cost → gross margin %; ranks within sector gross-margin distribution (P10–P90, piecewise-linear) and computes rupee upside of reaching the sector-median margin (gap% × TTM revenue) → gross margin, sector percentile, upside-to-median + margin distribution bar chart. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Expense-Ratio Benchmark** — TTM revenue + per-category transaction sums (operating spend, payroll, tax) + active-loan annual interest run-rate (outstanding × rate) → each line as % of TTM revenue vs sector low/mid/high bands (lower is better for all); counts lines above median and sums rupee spend above median lines → per-line `BandRow` comparison. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Productivity (Revenue / Employee)** — TTM revenue, cost, payroll + user-entered headcount → revenue/employee (₹ lakh p.a.), profit/employee (revenue−cost−payroll ÷ headcount), avg payroll/head; revenue-per-employee placed on sector RPE band → per-head output cards + `BandRow` vs sector. _Persist: KV — headcount saved via `useFeatureState("bmk-headcount")` → `store.featureData`._ _Class: Indicative (headcount input is KV-persisted; bands indicative)._
- **Digital-Maturity Scorecard** — counts of bank accounts, transactions, invoices, inventory SKUs, purchase orders, fixed assets, GST-registered flag, % spend categorised → weighted score (8 checks summing to 100; e.g. bank 18, txn 16, invoicing 16, GST 14, inventory 12, procurement 10, assets 8, categorised 6); maps to tier (Digital leader/Digitising well/Early adopter/Mostly manual) and surfaces best next step → circular score, capability checklist with per-check detail and points. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Backend (scored entirely from own usage signals, no peer data; weights indicative)._
- **Valuation-Multiple Benchmark** — TTM revenue, cost, payroll → EBITDA margin; quality factor (margin/25, clamped 0–1) nudges applied EV/Revenue multiple within sector low–high band; computes low/median/high/your-estimate EV (revenue × multiple) → TTM revenue, EBITDA margin, applied multiple, indicative EV + valuation-range bar chart. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative (revenue-multiple estimate only; multiples indicative)._
- **Stock-Turn Benchmark** — inventory value (qty×unitCost) + 90-day received-PO COGS annualised (×4) → inventory turns/year (annualCogs÷invValue) vs sector turn norms; flags slow/dead stock (SKUs at/below reorder level holding value) → turns, sector median, inventory value, dead-stock value/count + `BandRow`. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative (turns live-computed via 90-day proxy; norms indicative)._
- **Tax-Burden Benchmark** — TTM net GST (`gstSummary` summed across months at firm `gstRate`, default 18%, after input credit) + direct tax cash paid (tax-category outflows) → total tax burden as % of TTM revenue vs sector band; splits GST %-of-rev and direct %-of-rev → burden, net GST, direct tax, sector median + `BandRow`; warns on above-range burden (ITC leakage). _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative (GST is an estimate not a filed return; bands indicative)._
- **EBITDA-Margin Benchmark** — TTM revenue, cost, payroll → EBITDA (revenue−cost−payroll) and EBITDA margin % vs sector band; rupee upside to sector-median margin (gap% × revenue) → margin, EBITDA, sector median, upside + `BandRow`; warns when below band low. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative (pre-interest/tax/depreciation proxy; bands indicative)._
- **Revenue-Stability Benchmark** — trailing-12-month positive-revenue months → mean, std deviation, coefficient of variation (std÷mean ×100, needs ≥3 months); CV placed on sector CV band (lower is steadier) → volatility, avg monthly revenue, std deviation, sector median CV + monthly-revenue spread bar chart (bars vs mean) and `BandRow`. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Liquidity-Buffer Benchmark** — total bank balance ÷ monthly burn (`monthlyBurn`) → months of opex held in cash vs sector buffer band; computes shortfall cash to reach sector-median buffer (band.mid × burn − balance) → months of buffer, cash balance, monthly burn, to-median gap + `BandRow`; warns below band floor. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Debt-Leverage Benchmark** — active-loan outstanding principal and annual EMI vs TTM revenue → debt/revenue multiple (lower safer) placed on sector leverage band, plus debt-service % of revenue (annual EMI÷revenue) → debt/revenue, outstanding debt + loan count, debt-service ratio, sector median + `BandRow`; warns above band low; notes "unlevered" when no loans. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Opex-Efficiency Benchmark** — TTM revenue and operating cost (ex-payroll) → opex/revenue % vs sector opex band (lower is leaner); rupee saving from trimming to sector median (gap% × revenue) → opex ratio, operating spend, sector median, saving + `BandRow`; warns above band low. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Net-Margin Band** — TTM revenue, cost, payroll less estimated loan interest (~40% of annual EMI) → net profit and net margin % vs sector net-margin band; rupee upside to sector median → net margin, TTM net profit, sector median, upside + `BandRow`; warns below band low. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative (pre-tax operating net margin; interest estimated; bands indicative)._
- **Cash-Runway Band** — total bank balance ÷ monthly burn (`monthlyBurn`) → months of net burn covered vs sector runway band; cash needed to reach sector-median runway ((band.mid − months) × burn) → runway, cash balance, monthly burn, cash-to-median + `BandRow`; warns below band low. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Customer-Retention Band** — invoices split into prior (>6 months ago) vs recent (last 6 months) customer sets by case-insensitive name → repeat rate = retained (prior customers re-invoiced recently) ÷ prior customers, vs sector retention band → retention %, prior customers, retained, lapsed counts + `BandRow`; warns below band low with win-back nudge. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Indicative (retention live-computed from invoices; name-matching caveat; bands indicative)._
## Predict & Digital Twin (`/predict`) — 27 tools

A model-based digital twin that war-games decisions, stress-tests cash and surfaces risk from the firm's own ledger/invoices. Stakeholders: owner, finance. Backend: KV-synced (every tool reads the live `useApp().store` — transactions, invoices, bankAccounts, activeLoans, firm — via `useTwinMetrics`; nothing writes except Scenario Compare). All figures are framed as estimates, not guarantees.

The `TABS` array (27 entries) drives the tab bar; each renders one component. `useTwinMetrics` is the shared engine: it buckets `store.transactions` into a contiguous trailing 12-month series, derives avg monthly revenue/expense/net (over active months), total revenue/expense, net cash (rev−exp as runway proxy), runway months, revenue volatility (coefficient of variation = std-dev/mean), and outstanding A/R (unpaid invoices).

- **Overview** (`OverviewTab`) — live store → 4 KPI cards (avg monthly revenue, avg monthly net, est. cash runway, revenue volatility %), a 12-month revenue-vs-expense area chart (the "training window"), and a clickable launcher grid jumping to every other tab; empty-state if no history. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Digital Twin** (`DigitalTwin`) — live store → 8 consolidated tiles (avg monthly revenue/expense/net, net cash generated, runway, outstanding A/R, revenue volatility %, overdue-invoice count) + monthly net-cashflow bar chart (green/red per sign). Mirrors the present, no projection. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (pure read-out of live data)._
- **What-If Sliders** (`WhatIfSliders`) — revenue Δ%, cost Δ%, horizon(3–36mo) sliders → applies flat % shifts to monthly rev/cost, linear projection of cumulative profit (net×month) base vs scenario → 4 KPIs (new monthly rev/cost/net, cumulative lift) + dual-line cumulative-profit chart. _Persist: none (local useState)._ _Class: Simulated (what-if)._
- **Monte-Carlo Cash** (`MonteCarloCash`) — opening cash, runs(500–10k), horizon(3–18mo) → Box-Muller normal-shock simulation: each run compounds monthly net ± random normal (sd anchored to revenue volatility) over horizon, counts ruin (cash<0) → P10/P50/P90 ending cash, cash-out risk %, and a P10/P50/P90 fan area-chart. Uses `Math.random` inside the run handler only. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated (Monte-Carlo)._
- **Scenario Compare** (`ScenarioCompare`) — add/remove scenarios (name, rev Δ%, cost Δ%) seeded with Optimistic/Base/Recession → each applies % shifts to run-rate → table of projected monthly net & annual net (×12). _Persist: KV key `pred-scenarios` via `useFeatureState`._ _Class: KV (Simulated compute on stored inputs)._
- **Early Warning** (`EarlyWarningBoard`) — live store → rules-based traffic-light board of 6 signals (cash runway, monthly profitability, revenue volatility, overdue receivables, customer concentration = top customer % of invoiced value, data freshness = days since last txn) each classed critical/high/medium/ok by thresholds. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (rules-based early-warning)._
