- **Trend Projection** (`TrendProjection`) — project-ahead(1–12mo) slider + seasonal toggle → OLS linear regression of revenue on month index, optional per-month seasonal index (month/overall-mean) scaling → trend direction (rising/falling ₹/mo), projected next-3-months sum, actual-vs-projected line chart. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (linear+seasonal trend)._
- **Churn Risk** (`ChurnRisk`) — invoices grouped per customer → heuristic churn score = clamp(days-since-last-invoice/90 ×0.6 + overdue-ratio ×0.4)×100 → High/Medium/Low flag, ranked table (last invoice, count, billed, overdue, score bar). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (heuristic churn)._
- **Break-Even** (`BreakEvenSimulator`) — monthly fixed cost (pre-filled ~60% of avg expense), price/unit, variable cost/unit → contribution margin (P−V), break-even units (F÷contribution) & revenue, current units (run-rate rev÷price), covered? verdict. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated (classic break-even)._
- **Sensitivity** (`SensitivityTornado`) — driver-swing(5–50%) slider → flexes Revenue (+) and Expense (−) by ±swing% around run-rate, measures swing in monthly net → tornado bar chart (downside/upside) sorted by impact + per-driver ± sensitivity. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated (sensitivity/tornado)._
- **Goal Probability** (`GoalProbability`) — cash target, deadline(1–24mo), revenue-stress-drop% → applies revenue drop then runs 3,000 Box-Muller randomized cash paths from current cash → probability of hitting target, median projected cash, stressed monthly net. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated (stress-test + Monte-Carlo goal prob)._
- **Run-Rate** (`RunRateProjector`) — base-window(1–12mo) + assumed monthly growth% sliders → avg recent monthly revenue → flat ARR (×12) and compounded 12-month projection at growth rate → 4 KPIs + projected monthly-revenue bar chart. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated (run-rate annualisation)._
- **Expense Creep** (`ExpenseCreepDetector`) — expense txns by category → compares last-3-month avg vs prior-3-month avg per category → change %, flags >15% "Creeping" / <−15% "Falling" / else "Stable" + KPI summary + table. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (period-over-period spend drift)._
- **Customer LTV** (`CustomerLtv`) — expected-lifespan(6–120mo) + gross-margin% sliders → per customer: monthly billing (total÷tenure in months) × lifespan × margin → predicted LTV ranking + totals. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (LTV estimate)._
- **Pay-Delay** (`PaymentDelayPredictor`) — invoices per customer → predicted days-to-pay from invoice→due term (paid) plus overdue lateness (overdue), averaged → Slow/Moderate/Prompt habit + open overdue/amount table. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (payment-behaviour estimate; explicitly proxies lateness as it's "unknowable")._
- **Cohort Retention** (`CohortRetention`) — invoices → first-invoice month = acquisition cohort, active-if-billed-that-month → last 6 cohorts × 6 month-offsets retention % heatmap (color-graded M0–M5). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (heuristic cohort retention)._
- **Working Capital** (`WorkingCapitalForecast`) — DSO + DPO sliders → receivables tied up (dailyRev×DSO), payables float (dailyExp×DPO), net working capital (AR−AP), cash freeable from AR (openAR − arNeed) + open-vs-target bar. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated (working-capital model)._
- **Revenue Band** (`RevenueConfidenceBand`) — project-ahead(1–12mo) slider → OLS trend midpoint with band widening by sd×√(k+1)×1.28 (≈P10/P90) anchored to revenue volatility → P10/P50/P90 area+line forecast band + next-month KPIs. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (probabilistic revenue band)._
- **Seasonality** (`SeasonalityForecast`) — revenue series → seasonal index per month (month/12-mo-mean ×100, 100=average) → peak/trough/spread KPIs + index bar chart vs 100 line. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (seasonal decomposition)._
- **Concentration** (`ConcentrationRisk`) — invoices by customer → revenue share %, Herfindahl-Hirschman Index (Σpct²), top-1/top-3 share → risk verdict (High/Moderate/Diversified) + top-8 share bar chart. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (concentration risk / HHI)._
- **Recurring Spend** (`RecurringSpendForecast`) — expense txns by counterparty → flagged `isRecurring` OR repeated 3+ times treated as subscription → avg charge annualised (×12) → recurring-vendor count, projected annual/monthly cost, largest, table (Flagged/Inferred source). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (subscription-sprawl projection)._
- **Cash-Out Day** (`CashBalanceProjection`) — opening cash (pre-filled from `bankAccounts` balances or net cash) + project-ahead(3–36mo) → adds avg monthly net per month, names first month balance turns negative → opening/net/lowest-balance/cash-out-month KPIs + balance area chart with 0 reference line. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (linear cash-balance projection)._
- **Invoice Pay-Date** (`InvoicePayDatePredictor`) — open invoices → learns each customer's typical invoice→due term from history (portfolio median fallback), adds to invoice date → predicted settlement date, days-out, confidence (High ≥3 samples / Medium ≥1 / Low), expected-within-30d totals + sorted table. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (per-invoice pay-date prediction)._
- **GST Liability** (`GstLiabilityForecast`) — output-rate (pre-filled from `firm.gstRate`) + ITC% sliders → output tax (rev×rate) − input credit (×ITC%) = net GST/mo, ×3 quarter reserve, per-month net-GST bar; warns if `firm.gstRegistered` is false. _Persist: none (reads firm settings)._ _Class: Indicative (GST-liability forecast)._
- **Payroll Stress** (`PayrollStressPredictor`) — opening cash (from bank balances), monthly payroll (pre-filled from last-3-month `payroll`-category txns), min buffer → projects cash after each salary run over 12 mo, flags first month breaching buffer → payroll runs covered, first-breach month + after-payroll bar chart vs buffer line. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (payroll cash-stress)._
- **Debt Service** (`DebtServiceProjection`) — `activeLoans` + extra-principal% slider → aggregates outstanding/EMI/outstanding-weighted avg rate, amortises combined balance monthly (cap 60mo) with optional extra principal → payoff month, total interest, outstanding-balance area chart. _Persist: none (reads activeLoans)._ _Class: Indicative (loan amortisation projection)._
- **Profit Trajectory** (`ProfitTrajectory`) — project-ahead(3–36mo) + margin-drift(±3 pts/mo) sliders → base net margin drifted per month, applied to revenue run-rate → monthly net + cumulative profit, current/end margin, trajectory verdict (Expanding/Compressing/Flat) + dual-line chart. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated (margin-drift profit projection)._
# Dashboard (`/dashboard`) — 31 tools/widgets

One-sentence purpose: the home screen — a single-scroll stack of cash-flow, P&L, receivables, alerts and goal widgets, each computed live from the shared store. Stakeholders: all. Backend: KV-synced (reads live store via `useApp()`; durable picks/goals persist via `useFeatureState`; treasury banner is the only HTTP-backed widget).

> Layout note: this page is one vertical scroll of cards (not tabs). The four StatCards, the eight quick-action tiles and the breach banner are header chrome above the widget stack. Many widgets render `null` when their underlying data is empty.

Bullet per widget/tool:

- **Forecast breach warning banner** — top-of-page red banner: runs `runForecast(store).risk` (Monte-Carlo) memoised on the store; shows only when `probBreachByDay[min(44,len-1)] ≥ 0.5`; displays `probBreach%`, expected days-to-breach, P10 worst-case runway; click → `/forecast`. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend (compute) / derived from KV store._
- **Header actions (Add Account / Import CSV / Add Transaction)** — three buttons; Add Account always shown, Import + Add Transaction only when `bankAccounts.length > 0`; all disabled in `isReadOnly` (client view). Open the respective modals. _Persist: writes to live store on submit._ _Class: Backend._
- **Onboarding wizard** — 4-step checklist (add bank account, import 3+ transactions, generate forecast, run credit pre-qual) with progress bar; `done` computed from store counts; dismissible. Shows hint card at 3/4 done; hidden when all done, dismissed, or read-only. _Persist: `localStorage hr_onboarding_dismissed`._ _Class: KV (steps from store; dismissal local)._
- **Empty state** — dashed-border CTA shown when `bankAccounts.length === 0 && transactions.length === 0`; single "Add Bank Account" button. _Persist: none._ _Class: KV (store-derived gate)._
- **StatCards ×4 (Total Balance / Monthly Burn / Cash Runway / Unread Alerts)** — animated count-up cards: balance = Σ account balances; burn = `monthlyBurn(transactions)`; runway = `runwayDays(...)`; unread = `alerts.filter(!isRead)`. Balance/Burn show MoM delta (rev/burn % vs last month); each click navigates (transactions/forecast/alerts). Note: Total Balance card mislabels delta — it shows `revDelta` (revenue MoM), not a balance delta. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Quick-actions row (8 tiles)** — static deep-link grid: Fin Health, Working Capital, Analytics, CFO Brief, Budgets, Compliance, Receivables, Operations → navigate to respective routes. _Persist: none._ _Class: KV (nav only)._
- **Cash This Week** — 7-day forward bar pairs (expected inflow vs outflow): inflow = avg of same day-of-week over last 4 weeks; outflow = `monthlyBurn/30`, weekends ×0.3; net shown per day in ₹K; current balance in header. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (heuristic projection from patterns)._
- **Priority actions (SmartActionsPanel)** — ranks up to 3 actions by hard-coded urgency: cash-crunch (<30d runway), chase overdue invoices, unread alerts, high-burn-vs-balance, plus a default "review sector benchmarks"; each deep-links. Returns `null` if none. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (rule-based prioritization)._
- **Treasury auto-sweep banner** — `GET /api/treasury/analysis` → shows idle cash (₹L) and `annual_yield_at_65` if idle ≥ ₹50K; "Enable Auto-Sweep" → `POST /api/treasury/sweep-enable` (queues enrollment, toast); dismissible. Carries `<PreviewBadge capability="treasurySweep" />`. _Persist: `localStorage hr_treasury_dismissed`._ _Class: Preview (PreviewBadge; sweep is queued, not executed) + Backend (analysis API)._
- **Business Health Score** — collapsible donut (0–100) = avg of 4 sub-scores, each banded with thresholds: Cash Health (runway), Revenue Trend (this-vs-last-month rev MoM), Debt Coverage (balance vs 6×EMI / activeLoans), Compliance (unread-alert count). Overall labeled Healthy/Needs Attention/At Risk. _Persist: none (open state is local useState)._ _Class: Indicative (scoring heuristic over KV data)._
- **Morning Brief Card** — greeting + condensed digest list: yesterday's net close, cash-on-hand + runway, today's movements, recurring payments due today, statutory items due today (`getUpcomingTaxDates` filtered to `isToday`), high-priority vs unread alerts; "all clear" line when nothing pending; links to alerts + compliance calendar. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (digest synthesis)._
- **My KPI Board (KpiWidgetBuilder)** — owner pins metrics from an 8-item catalog (balance, burn, runway, revenueMtd, netMtd, alerts, topBank, accounts); "Customize" toggles chips; renders pinned values live. _Persist: `useFeatureState("dashboard-kpi-board")` (KV-synced), default `[balance,runway,revenueMtd,alerts]`._ _Class: KV._
- **Daily Cash Position (DailyCashSnapshot)** — 4 summary tiles (opening/current balance, money in/out today, net today) + per-account breakdown of today's movements (count, +in/−out, balance). _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Goal & Target Tracker (GoalTracker)** — user adds revenue/profit/balance goals with label + target; live progress bars compare actual (MTD rev, MTD net, or balance) vs target; achieved badge at ≥100%; add/remove. _Persist: `useFeatureState("dashboard-goals")` (KV-synced)._ _Class: KV._
- **Overdue Invoices** — `store.invoices` filtered `status≠paid && dueDate<today`, ranked by amount; shows count, total outstanding, top 5 (customer, invoice no., days late); "Chase all" + rows → `/receivables`. Returns `null` if none. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Due in the next 7 days (UpcomingDuesWidget)** — timeline merging open invoices due within 7d (inflow) + recurring outflows projected onto the next-7-day window by day-of-month (outflow); sorted by date, header shows total +in/−out; rows deep-link. Returns `null` if empty. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (recurring outflows are projected, not actual dated rows)._
- **Recent Activity** — latest 8 transactions across all accounts (newest first); in/out icon, description/counterparty, category + date, signed amount; "View all" → `/transactions`. Returns `null` if no transactions. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **This Month · Mini P&L** — current-month cash-basis income statement: revenue minus payroll/opex/tax/loan/other (by category), net profit/loss, net-margin %; "Details" → `/analytics`. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Where the Money Goes (SpendByPayeeDonut)** — all-time outflow donut: top 5 counterparties (fallback category/"Other") + aggregated "Other", center shows total out, legend with %. Returns `null` if no outflows. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Cash Trend · 30 days (CashTrendSparkline)** — back-cast daily closing balance: walks backward from current balance subtracting each day's net flow; area chart in ₹K + 30-day change %. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (reconstructed/back-cast estimate, not recorded daily balances)._
- **Net Cash Flow · 6 months (NetCashFlowTrend)** — signed bar chart of monthly net (inflow−outflow) over 6 months in ₹K; subtitle shows cash-positive months count + avg; "Details" → `/analytics`. Returns `null` if all zero. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Biggest Movers · spend (ExpenseMoversWidget)** — per-category outflow change this month vs last; top 5 by |delta|, shows was→now and ±% (or "new"); "Review" → `/spend`. Returns `null` if no movers. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Customer Payment Status (CustomerPaymentStatus)** — donut of invoices split paid / pending (due) / overdue with counts + amounts; center shows total invoice count; "Receivables" → `/receivables`. Returns `null` if no invoices. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Days of Cash (CashGaugeWidget)** — half-circle gauge of days-of-cash = `balance/burn*30` (capped 180 for sweep, ∞ if no burn), colored Critical/Watch/Comfortable; plus MTD savings-rate bar = `(in−out)/in`; "Forecast" → `/forecast`. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (gauge framing) / KV._
- **Inflow by Weekday (WeekdayInflowWidget)** — 7-bar (Mon-first) total inflow per weekday over all history; highlights strongest day, tooltip shows amount + payment count. Returns `null` if no inflow. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Top Vendors · this month (TopVendorsWidget)** — current-month largest outflow payees (counterparty/description/category fallback), top 5 horizontal bars; "All" → `/transactions`. Returns `null` if none. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Invoice Status (InvoiceStatusWidget)** — paid/due/overdue stacked bar + 3 stat tiles (count + amount each), total in bar; "Open" → `/receivables`. Returns `null` if no invoices. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Burn Trend · 6 months (BurnTrendWidget)** — area chart of total monthly outflow (₹K) over 6 months + MoM % vs prior month; "Review" → `/spend`. Returns `null` if all zero. _Persist: live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Receivables vs Payables (ReceivablesVsPayablesWidget)** — working-capital snapshot: receivable = Σ open invoices; payable = Σ outflows that are recurring-or-this-month in [payroll, loan, tax, expense]; two bars + net position. Returns `null` if both zero. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Indicative (payable definition is heuristic, mixes recurring + this-month)._
- **Credit rescue CTA** — red banner shown when `0 < runway < 45`; "See rescue options" → `/credit`. _Persist: live store gate._ _Class: KV._
- **N-Day Cash Forecast (CashForecastChart)** — area chart of `store.forecast` P10/P50/P90 (₹L), 30/60/90-day segmented toggle, toggleable series legend; shown only if `forecast.length > 0`, else a placeholder pointing to `/forecast`. _Persist: live store (forecast array)._ _Class: Backend (forecast engine output)._
- **Monthly burn by category + This-month-vs-last + Revenue concentration + Tax calendar + Bank Accounts + Recent Alerts (inline panels)** — six inline cards: burn-by-category bars (payroll/expense/loan/tax/transfer); inflow/outflow/net this-vs-last MoM table; revenue concentration (top 5 sources, >40% triggers orange risk note with runway-impact estimate; shown if ≥2 sources); Tax calendar (`getUpcomingTaxDates` GSTR-3B/TDS/Advance-Tax with days-left urgency + estimated GST from last-month revenue × `firm.gstRate`); Bank Accounts list with share bars + Add; Recent Alerts (top 5, click marks read via `markAlertRead`). _Persist: live store; alert read-state writes to store._ _Class: KV; tax calendar = Indicative (standard statutory dates + GST estimate)._

Modals (triggered, not always-on widgets): **AddAccountModal**, **AddTransactionModal**, **TransactionImportModal** — write new accounts/transactions to the live store on submit. _Class: Backend (KV writes)._
## CFO Brief (`/cfo-brief`) — 19 tools

A view-switched CFO command center that turns the live financial store (transactions, bank accounts, loans, invoices, obligations, alerts, firm) into AI-drafted narratives, board materials, scorecards, ratios, and risk/cash briefs. Stakeholders: owner, finance, viewer. Backend: `/api/ai/ask` (POST) for the AI brief/investor update; everything else is computed client-side from the live store, with three tools persisting to KV via `useFeatureState`. Top-level tab array (`CfoView`, 19 entries) drives which component renders.

Helpers shared across tools: `monthBounds(back)` (YYYY-MM-DD month window), `flowsIn(txns,start,end)` (inflow/outflow/net), `pctChange(now,prev)`, `parseBrief(raw)` (markdown → section cards), plus `monthlyBurn`/`runwayDays`/`formatCurrency`/`formatAmount` from `@/lib/utils`.

- **AI Brief** (`ai-brief` → `AiBriefView`) — inputs: live store (transactions, bankAccounts, firm, alerts, activeLoans), an audience toggle (CFO Brief / Investor Update). Computation: builds a deterministic `DATA_BLOCK` (cash balance, runway days, monthly burn, this-vs-last-month revenue + MoM %, expenses, active-loan count/outstanding/EMI, unread-alert count + titles, top-3 vendors by spend, industry), then `buildPrompt()` produces either a CFO-brief prompt (Key Metrics / Revenue Analysis / Cash & Risk Alerts / Top 3 Action Items / Strategic Observations) or an investor-update prompt (Headline / Performance / Cash & Runway / Risks & Mitigations / Asks). POSTs to `/api/ai/ask` with a role-specific system prompt; on success parses the markdown into typed section cards; on failure shows a toast and renders a deterministic fallback brief built from the same real numbers. Output: section cards + 4 always-on quick stats (cash, runway, MoM revenue, active alerts) + a TXT export (`cfo-brief.txt` / `investor-update.txt`). Switching audience clears stale output. _Persist: none (output is in-component state; download only)._ _Class: Backend (AI via `/api/ai/ask`, with Indicative deterministic fallback)._

- **Variance Commentary** (`variance` → `VarianceCommentary`) — inputs: transactions for current vs prior month. Computation: per-category net movement (current − prior), the biggest counterparty swing inside each category as the "why" driver, `pctChange`, and a favourable/unfavourable read (revenue treated inversely to costs); rows sorted by absolute delta. Generates a plain-English sentence per category. Output: variance cards (prev vs cur amounts, delta, %, driver) + a "Copy commentary" clipboard action. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (deterministic, computed from live store)._

- **Board-Deck Generator** (`board-deck` → `BoardDeckGenerator`) — inputs: transactions, bankAccounts, firm, activeLoans, alerts; per-slide include toggles. Computation: assembles 8 text slides from live data — Title, Executive Summary, P&L Snapshot, Cash & Runway, Top Revenue Concentration (top-3 customers w/ % of month), Debt & Obligations, Key Risks (unread alerts), Asks/Next Steps — using current-vs-prior flows, balance, burn, runway, revenue MoM %, total debt/EMI, open risks. Output: toggleable slide cards + "Copy" and "Export Deck" (`board-deck.txt`) actions; only enabled slides export. _Persist: KV key `cfo-deck-slides` (which slides are included) via `useFeatureState`._ _Class: KV (slide selection) + Indicative (slide content)._

- **Risk & Watchlist** (`watchlist` → `RiskWatchlistBrief`) — inputs: transactions, bankAccounts, activeLoans, alerts. Computation: scores up to 5 risk classes — (1) runway <30d critical / <90d high, (2) negative net cash flow (severity scaled vs burn), (3) revenue concentration where top customer ≥40% of month, (4) debt-service load where EMI ≥30% of revenue, (5) open critical/high alerts pulled from the live store — each with severity, heuristic score, and plain-English detail; sorted by score. Output: critical/high/medium count tiles + severity-styled risk cards (or an all-clear state) + "Copy brief". _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (heuristic scoring of live signals)._

- **KPI Scorecard** (`scorecard` → `KpiScorecard`) — inputs: transactions, bankAccounts, activeLoans. Computation: 6 KPIs with MoM deltas and a RAG (red/amber/green) status each — Revenue (MoM), Net cash flow, Cash balance (×burn), Runway, Monthly burn (outflow MoM), Debt-service ratio (EMI/revenue) — using current-vs-prior flows, balance, burn, runway, DSCR. Output: KPI cards with RAG dot, value, % delta and hint + "Copy scorecard". _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Cash Snapshot** (`cash-snapshot` → `CashFlowSnapshot`) — inputs: transactions, bankAccounts (current month). Computation: sources-and-uses — groups this month's positive amounts by category (sources) and negative by category (uses), each as a share of its side total; derives opening cash = closing balance − net flow. Output: opening/sources/uses/closing tiles + two share-bar columns (sources vs uses). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Margin Snapshot** (`margins` → `MarginSnapshot`) — inputs: transactions for current vs prior month. Computation: cash-basis margin — revenue, direct costs (expense + payroll), gross profit/margin, overheads (tax + interest/loan), net profit/margin — for both months with deltas (pp for percentages). Output: gross/net margin tiles + a 7-row line table (prev, cur, Δ with improvement coloring). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (cash-basis approximation, not statutory P&L)._

- **Financial Calendar** (`calendar` → `FinancialCalendar`) — inputs: activeLoans, invoices (current month). Computation: consolidates dated obligations falling in the month — loan EMIs (using nextPaymentDate/amount), unpaid invoices due (receivables), and a fixed GSTR-3B 20th-of-month compliance deadline — sorted by date, flags overdue (date < today). Output: expected-in / committed-out / event-count tiles + a dated event timeline. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (GST date is a fixed assumption)._

- **Top Actions** (`actions` → `TopActionsThisWeek`) — inputs: transactions, bankAccounts, invoices, activeLoans, alerts. Computation: ranks up to 5 highest-impact moves by weight — chase overdue invoices, shore up runway (<90d), reverse negative cash flow, park idle cash (>3-month buffer, with ~6%/yr yield impact estimate), fund EMIs due this month, resolve open critical/high alerts — each carrying a rupee-impact figure; top-5 by weight. Output: numbered check-off action list with impact and completion count. _Persist: KV key `cfo-actions-done` (per-action checked state) via `useFeatureState`._ _Class: KV (check-off state) + Indicative (ranking/impact)._

- **One-Page Summary** (`one-pager` → `OnePageSummary`) — inputs: transactions, bankAccounts, activeLoans, invoices, firm, alerts. Computation: distills the whole position into three blocks — Position (cash, runway, open/overdue AR), Performance MTD (revenue + MoM %, expenses, net flow, burn), Obligations (debt outstanding, EMI/mo, open risks) — plus a rule-based headline takeaway (healthy / caution if runway <45d / stable-but-watch). Output: 3 stat blocks + takeaway card + TXT export (`financial-summary.txt`). _Persist: none (download only)._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Financial Ratios** (`ratios` → `FinancialRatios`) — inputs: transactions, bankAccounts, activeLoans, invoices. Computation: 6 cash-basis ratios each benchmarked good/watch/poor against a healthy band with a plain-English read — Liquidity coverage ((cash+AR)/EMI), Cash-to-burn (months), Debt-to-revenue (vs annualised inflow), Debt-service coverage (revenue/EMI), Receivables intensity (AR/monthly revenue), Expense ratio (outflow/inflow). Output: ratio cards (status dot, value, band, read) + "Copy ratios". _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (directional, not statutory)._

- **Profitability Trend** (`trend` → `ProfitabilityTrend`) — inputs: transactions (6-month rolling window). Computation: per-month revenue/expense/net for the last 6 months; counts profitable months, 6-month net trend `pctChange`, and a rule-based verdict (consistently profitable / mixed / mostly loss-making). Output: profitable-months / latest-net / trend tiles + per-month dual mini-bars (revenue vs expense) + verdict card + "Copy trend". _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Expense Control** (`expense-control` → `ExpenseControlScorecard`) — inputs: transactions (current month vs trailing 3-month average). Computation: per expense category, compares this month's spend to the trailing-3-month average baseline, classifies controlled (≤5%) / watch (≤20%) / overrun (>20%); assigns a letter grade A–D by overrun count; sorted by current spend. Output: grade / categories-tracked / overruns tiles + status-styled category cards + "Copy scorecard". _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Loan & Covenant** (`covenant` → `CovenantBrief`) — inputs: activeLoans, transactions. Computation: portfolio DSCR (month revenue / total EMI) plus per-loan repayment progress ((principal−outstanding)/principal) and per-loan DSCR (revenue / loan EMI), flagging a "covenant watch" when DSCR < 1.25×; loans sorted by outstanding. Output: portfolio DSCR / total outstanding / total EMI tiles + per-loan cards (outstanding, EMI, DSCR, next payment, repayment bar, covenant badge) + "Copy brief". _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (per-loan DSCR allocates full revenue to each loan)._

- **What Changed** (`what-changed` → `WhatChangedThisWeek`) — inputs: transactions, bankAccounts, activeLoans, invoices; a user-captured baseline snapshot. Computation: builds a current snapshot (cash balance, runway, revenue MTD, net MTD, open AR, overdue AR, total debt) and diffs each metric against the stored baseline, coloring improvements by per-metric good-direction; only changed metrics shown. Output: empty-state / nothing-changed / diff cards (from → to, signed delta) + a "Capture snapshot" / "Re-baseline now" button. _Persist: KV key `cfo-whatchanged-baseline` (the baseline snapshot) via `useFeatureState`._ _Class: KV (baseline) + Indicative (diff)._

- **Liquidity Position** (`liquidity` → `LiquidityPositionBrief`) — inputs: bankAccounts, activeLoans, obligations. Computation: worst-case liquidity test — nets total bank balance against dated obligations + EMIs (×months) falling due in the next 30/60/90 days; per-bucket coverage ratio and ok/shortfall flag (inflows from receivables deliberately excluded). Output: 3 horizon-bucket cards (amount due, covered ×cash or shortfall) + "Copy brief". _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Growth vs Burn** (`growth-burn` → `GrowthVsBurnBrief`) — inputs: transactions for current vs prior month. Computation: burn multiple = cash burned this month ÷ net-new revenue (current − prior inflow); verdict mapped to self-funding / efficient (<1×) / acceptable (1–2×) / expensive (>2×) / burning-no-growth. Output: verdict card + net-new-revenue / net-cash-flow / cash-burned / burn-multiple tiles. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Top Accounts** (`top-accounts` → `TopAccountsBrief`) — inputs: transactions (current month, by counterparty). Computation: ranks top-5 customers (inflow) and top-5 vendors (outflow) by amount, each as a share of its side total; flags concentration risk (customers) / negotiating leverage (vendors). Output: two ranked share-bar columns + "Copy brief". _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Working Capital** (`working-capital` → `WorkingCapitalBrief`) — inputs: invoices, obligations, activeLoans, transactions. Computation: net working capital = open receivables (with overdue slice) − near-term payables (this-month obligations + one EMI cycle); AR days = open AR ÷ (prior-month revenue / 30). Output: open-receivables / near-term-payables / net-WC / AR-days tiles + a positive/negative headroom narrative card + "Copy brief". _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
## Financial Health (`/health`) — 18 tools

One composite financial-health score with 18 diagnostic tools that derive every ratio, distress signal and stress test live from the transaction snapshot. Stakeholders: owner, viewer, all (read-only roles see identical output; only the Fitness Trend writes state, gated for viewers). Backend: everything recomputes from `computeFinancialSnapshot(store)` (`@/lib/finance`) memoised on `store`; no health-specific server call — all assets/equity/EBIT are transaction proxies, the lone persisted artefact is the fitness-trend history in the synced `featureData` KV bag.

### Header / always-on panels (not in jump-nav, render above the 18 sections)

- **Composite Health Score + Lender-Ready badge** — `snap.health.score/grade` → `ScoreRing` (SVG ring, green ≥70 / yellow ≥45 / red); `lenderReady = score ≥ 65 && (dscr null || dscr ≥ 1.25)` toggles "Lender-ready / Below typical lender bar" pill. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **Score Profile radar** — `health.components` → Recharts `RadarChart` of each component's rounded score (axis = first word of label). _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **What's dragging the score** — `health.components` sorted ascending, slice top-3 weakest → cards with score, `detail`, and a `fixPath`/`fixLabel` navigation CTA per component. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **Component Breakdown** — every `health.component` (liquidity, profitability, collections, leverage, growth, concentration, compliance) → row with `weight`% , `detail`, score-coloured bar (`barColor`) and fix-link to its module. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._
- **Key Ratios vs Lender Benchmarks** — 12 ratios, each value vs target with ok/needs-attention and click-through path: Current Ratio (≥1.5x→/working-capital), Quick Ratio (≥1.0x), DSCR (≥1.25x→/debt), Interest Coverage (≥3x), Cash Conversion Cycle (≤45d), Net Margin 6mo (`grossMarginPct` ≥10%→/analytics), Runway (≥90d→/forecast), Net Working Capital (>₹0), Top-Customer Share (≤30%→/invoices), EBITDA Margin (computed `monthlyEbitda = monthlyNet + monthlyInterest + monthlyRevenue×1.5%`; ≥15%), Free Cash Flow Ratio (`opCashFlow = monthlyNet + monthlyDebtService`; ≥10%→/debt), Revenue Growth CMGR (≥3%/mo). _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend (EBITDA/FCF inputs are proxy add-backs → Indicative)._
- **Snapshot strip** — 4 cards: Cash on Hand (/transactions), Open Receivables (/receivables), Debt Outstanding (/debt), Working-Capital Gap (/working-capital). _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._

### Jump-nav sections (iterating the `sections` array; 18 entries → 18 rendered `<section>` tools)

- **Distress (Z') — Altman Z' Score** (`#health-altman-z`) — inputs: snapshot proxies (estimatedFA=`monthlyRevenue×6`, totalAssets=cash+AR+inventory+FA, totalLiab=debt+AP+obligationsDue90, retainedEarnings=`max(0,monthlyNet×12)`, ebitAnnual=`(monthlyNet+monthlyInterest)×12`, bookEquity=assets−liab) → **Altman Z' for private SMBs**: `Z' = 0.717·X1 + 0.847·X2 + 3.107·X3 + 0.420·X4 + 0.998·X5` where X1=NWC/assets, X2=retained/assets, X3=EBIT/assets, X4=equity/liab (=3 if no debt), X5=revenue/assets → score + zone (>2.9 safe · 1.23–2.9 grey · <1.23 distress), per-factor weighted-contribution table, asset/liab/equity card. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (real formula over transaction proxies)._
- **Stress Test — Liquidity Stress Test** (`#health-stress-test`) — inputs: 4 sliders (revDrop 0–80%, arDelay 0–100% receivables stuck, costRise 0–50%, bufferMonths 1–12) → stressedRevenue/expense/net, arShortfall=`AR×arDelay%`, monthlyInflowGap spread over 3-mo window, adjDailyBurn, stressedRunway=`floor(cash/adjDailyBurn)`, safetyFloor=`stressedExpense×bufferMonths`, breaches if cash<floor or runway<buffer×30 → survive/fail verdict, runway today vs under-shock, 6-row stressed P&L ledger. _Persist: none (transient `useState` sliders)._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Fitness Trend — Financial Fitness Trend** (`#health-fitness-trend`) — inputs: `snap.health.score`, top driver/drag (components sorted desc), `snap.months` revenue → records one `FitnessPoint {month,score,topDriver,topDrag}` per calendar month (capped 24), charts live recorded points over an estimated back-trend (today's score scaled by `month.revenue/latestRev`, clamped 0.4–1.2); delta over N months with trend icon; current-score / strongest-driver / biggest-drag cards + reverse-chron history table. _Persist: KV key `health-fitness-trend` (`useFeatureState`, synced featureData; viewer writes gated)._ _Class: Backend score + KV-persisted history (chart back-trend is Indicative)._
- **Liquidity — Liquidity Ladder** (`#health-liquidity-ladder`) — inputs: currentLiab=`max(1, AP+obligationsDue90+monthlyDebtService×12)`, currentAssets=cash+AR+inventory → 3 progressively stricter rungs: Current Ratio (assets/liab, target 1.5x), Quick Ratio acid-test ((cash+AR)/liab, 1.0x), Cash Ratio (cash/liab, 0.5x) → banded bars + currentAssets/currentLiab/NWC cards → /working-capital. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (liabilities estimated)._
- **Solvency — Solvency & Coverage** (`#health-solvency`) — inputs: totalAssets (incl FA proxy), totalLiab, equity → 6 rows vs lender bands: Debt-to-Equity (≤2.0x, ∞ if no equity), Debt-to-Assets (≤50%), Equity Ratio (≥40%), Interest Coverage (≥3x), DSCR (≥1.25x), Debt÷Annual-Profit (≤3x) → `passes`/6 with solvent verdict (≥4) + assets/liab/equity cards → /debt. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Efficiency — Efficiency & Turnover** (`#health-efficiency`) — inputs: annualRev/annualCogs, totalAssets → turns/yr + equivalent days: Asset Turnover (rev/assets, ≥1.0x, CCC days), Receivables Turnover (rev/AR, DSO≤45d), Inventory Turnover (cogs/inventory, DIO≤60d), Payables Turnover (cogs/AP, DPO 30–60d) → Good/Slow table → /working-capital. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **DuPont ROE — DuPont ROE Breakdown** (`#health-dupont`) — inputs: annualRev, annualNet, totalAssets, equity=`max(1,assets−liab)` → `ROE = NetMargin × AssetTurnover × EquityMultiplier`; three driver cards (margin = net/rev, turnover = rev/assets, multiplier = assets/equity) → combined ROE% (target ≥15%) + annual-net/equity/assets cards. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Runway Gauge — Cash-Runway Gauge** (`#health-runway-gauge`) — inputs: `snap.runwayDays`, monthlyNet → months=runwayDays/30 (∞ if ≥999 CF-positive), dailyBurn, semicircle SVG gauge (0–12mo, green ≥6 / yellow ≥3 / red) + band copy; cash / daily-burn / monthly-net / runway-days cards → /forecast. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend._
- **Early Warning — Distress Early-Warning Checklist** (`#health-early-warning`) — inputs: snapshot fields → 8 live red-flag tests: negative op cash flow, runway<90d, DSCR<1.25x, current ratio<1.0x, >40% receivables overdue (`overdueReceivable/AR`), customer concentration>40%, CCC>75d, negative NWC → triggered-count maps to All-clear (0) / Early-warning monitor (≤2) / red-flags intervene (>2); each card click-throughs to its module. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend (overdue/topCustomer proxies → Indicative)._
- **CF Coverage — Cash-Flow Coverage** (`#health-cf-coverage`) — inputs: opCash=`monthlyNet+monthlyDebtService`, taxAccrual=`max(0,estAnnualProfit)×25%/12` → 3 layered coverage tests: Interest coverage (opCash/interest, ≥3x), Debt-service coverage (opCash/EMI, ≥1.25x), Debt+tax coverage (opCash/(EMI+tax), ≥1x) → `passing`/3, banded bars, op-cash/fixed-claims/tax-accrual cards → /debt. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (tax accrual estimated)._
- **Margin Stability — Margin Stability Score** (`#health-margin-stability`) — inputs: per-month net margin from `snap.months` (rev>0) → mean, SD, **coefficient of variation** `cv = sd/|mean|` → `score = round(clamp(100 − cv×100))` (≥60 steady); best/worst month, range, mini bar chart of monthly margins; needs ≥2 months. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend._
- **Growth Quality — Cash vs Accrual** (`#health-growth-quality`) — inputs: revenueGrowthPct (CMGR), opCash, arMonths=`AR/monthlyRevenue`, cashConv=`opCash/monthlyRevenue` → 3 tests: growth positive, cashConv ≥5%, arMonths ≤1.5 → passes/3 maps to high/mixed/low-quality verdict; op-cash/cash-conversion/AR-months cards → /receivables. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend (opCash is proxy)._
- **Expense Discipline — Expense Discipline Trend** (`#health-expense-discipline`) — inputs: per-month expense/revenue ratio from `snap.months` → first vs last delta (improving if <−1, worsening if >1), best-month ratio, **cost leakage** = `max(0,(lastRatio−bestRatio)/100 × lastRevenue)`; coloured bar chart (red ≥100% / yellow ≥90% / green) + latest/best/trend/leakage cards → /analytics; needs ≥2 months. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend._
- **Resilience — Overall Resilience Index** (`#health-resilience`) — inputs: runwayDays, grossMarginPct, dscr, topCustomerPct → 4 pillars blended: cash buffer (runway/180d, 35%), profit cushion (margin×5, 25%), leverage headroom (dscr/1.25×60+25, 25%), diversification (100−(topCustomer−15)×2, 15%) → `score = round(weighted)` with ScoreRing + A+–D grade and resilient/moderate/fragile band; each pillar links to its module. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Quick Ratio — Quick-Ratio Gauge** (`#health-quick-ratio-gauge`) — inputs: `snap.quickRatio` → 0–2x dial with 1.0x safety marker, band (healthy ≥1 / tight ≥0.7 / strained); optional "Why it matters" toggle; quick-ratio/current-ratio/cash cards → /working-capital. _Persist: none (transient `showWhy` useState)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Debt Burden — Debt-Burden Index** (`#health-debt-burden`) — inputs: dsrPct=`monthlyDebtService/monthlyRevenue`, dscr, interestCoverage → 3 strains blended: debt-service-vs-revenue (100−dsr×4, 40%), DSCR headroom (dscr/1.25×60+25, 35%), interest coverage (ic/3×70+15, 25%) → `score = round(weighted)` (100 if debt-free) with ScoreRing/grade and light/moderate/heavy band → /debt. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend._
- **Cash Buffer — Cash-Buffer Months** (`#health-cash-buffer`) — inputs: cash, monthlyExpense, monthlyNet → months=`cash/monthlyExpense`, burnMonths=`cash/(−monthlyNet)` (null if CF-positive) → `score = clamp(months/6×100)`, bar with 3-month floor marker, comfortable ≥6 / adequate ≥3 / thin band; cash/expense/months-covered/at-current-burn cards → /forecast. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend._
- **Revenue Mix — Revenue-Diversification Index** (`#health-revenue-diversification`) — inputs: `snap.customerHhi`, topCustomerPct → **Herfindahl** inverted: `score = round(100 − (hhi−1000)/9000 × 100)`, effective customers = `10000/hhi` → ScoreRing/grade, well-spread ≥70 / some-concentration ≥45 / concentrated band; top-share (≤30%) / HHI / effective-customers / diversification cards → /invoices. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend._
# Scenario Planning (`/scenarios`) — 21 tools

A tabbed "what-if" workbench that models hiring, pricing, funding, FX, cost and demand shocks against the SMB's live ledger and a Monte-Carlo cash forecast. Stakeholders: owner, finance. Backend: KV-synced (reads the shared `store` — transactions/invoices — via `AppContext`; no scenario state is persisted, all inputs are component-local React state).

Tab array (`ScenarioTab`, 21 entries): planner, price-sim, headcount, dilution, breakeven, revenue-shock, cost-cut, product-launch, supplier-hike, payment-terms, marketing-roi, capex, debt-vs-equity, client-loss, salary-hike, inventory-buildup, fx-shock, automation-roi, churn-impact, capacity-expansion, cost-inflation. Each renders exactly one tool component below.

- **Cash Planner** (`planner`, default tab) — preset/custom events (revenue, expense, hire, loan, deal; signed monthly impact, start month, duration) + 4 quick presets (Hire 2, Land ₹20L deal, Take ₹10L loan, Lose top client) → events mapped to forecast-engine `Scenario[]` (custom recurring monthly amount, start date = today + startMonth×30d, duration = months×30d) → both base and scenario run through the REAL `runForecast` Monte-Carlo engine (`store`, horizon 180d, 600 sims, pinned seed 1337 so base vs scenario differ only by the scenario delta) → 6-month P50 base vs P50 scenario area chart with P10 downside line, runway P50 (base/scenario), 6-month ending cash, scenario difference, viability/cash-crunch verdict; copy-to-clipboard share summary + "see credit options" deep-link to `/credit`. _Persist: none (events are local state; forecast reads KV store)._ _Class: Backend (Monte-Carlo forecast engine over live ledger)._
- **Price-Change Profit Simulator** (`price-sim`) — price/unit, cost/unit, current units, price-change %, elasticity (|%vol/%price|) → new price = p×(1+chg); volume factor = max(0, 1−chg×e); contribution profit before vs after = (price−cost)×units; revenue, margin %, profit-change table → before/after comparison + verdict on whether the price move lifts or cuts contribution profit. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative (linear-elasticity assumption, fixed costs held constant)._
- **Headcount / Hiring Scenario** (`headcount`) — cash on hand (auto from ledger sum), # hires, avg salary/mo, employer load %, added revenue/mo → fully-loaded per hire = salary×(1+load); new monthly cost; base burn vs scenario burn = (cost+newCost)−(rev+addRev); runway = cash÷burn → added cost, burn before/after, runway after, before/after table + 6-month-safety-line verdict. _Persist: none (cash/rev/cost derived live from `store.transactions`, last-90d annualised)._ _Class: Backend (live ledger) + Indicative (straight-line burn)._
- **Funding-Round Dilution** (`dilution`) — pre-money (₹Cr), amount raised (₹Cr), post-round ESOP %, founder % before → post = pre+raise; investor % = raise÷post; ESOP top-up created pre-money (dilutes existing holders); founder after = (1−investorPct)×founderPre − esop; founder dilution in pts → post-money, investor stake, founder after, dilution + post-round cap-table bar (founders/investor/ESOP/other). _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative (single-round, single-class, % ownership; no liquidation prefs/waterfall — defers to Cap-Table tools)._
- **Break-even & Margin of Safety** (`breakeven`) — fixed cost/mo, price/unit, variable cost/unit, actual/expected units → contribution = P−V; BE units = ⌈F÷contribution⌉; BE revenue = F÷contribution%; margin of safety units/revenue/% vs actual; profit at actual = A×contribution−F → BE units, BE revenue, margin of safety %, profit at actual + CVP table and above/below-BE verdict. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative (single-product CVP)._
- **Revenue-Shock / Recession Stress Test** (`revenue-shock`) — cash, monthly revenue, monthly cost (all auto from ledger), revenue drop %, variable-cost share %, safe-runway months → shocked rev = rev×(1−drop); variable cost shrinks with revenue, fixed cost held; shock burn & runway; cost-cut needed = max(0, shockedCost − (shockedRev + cash÷safety)) → revenue after shock, burn, runway, cost-cut-to-survive + normal vs shocked table and survival verdict. _Persist: none (live `useLiveMonthly()` from `store.transactions`; inputs local)._ _Class: Backend (live ledger) + Indicative (first-order linear cost flex)._
- **Cost-Cut Savings Simulator** (`cost-cut`) — cash, monthly cost, monthly revenue (auto from ledger) + checklist of 6 SMB levers each sized as % of current cost with pain rank (SaaS 4%, travel 6%, marketing 12%, contractors 10%, rent 9%, payroll 18%) → toggled savings summed; new cost, new burn, new runway = cash÷burn; runway gain vs base → monthly/annual savings, new cost, runway after cuts + savings verdict. _Persist: none (live ledger; toggles + inputs local)._ _Class: Backend (live ledger) + Indicative (lever percentages are illustrative)._
- **New-Product-Launch Model** (`product-launch`) — upfront launch cost, peak monthly revenue, contribution margin %, months-to-ramp, cannibalization %, horizon (capped 36) → S-curve ramp frac = 1−e^(−2.2t/ramp); per-month net contribution = revenue×margin×(1−cann); cumulative cash from −upfront → payback month, cumulative cash at horizon, total contribution, peak monthly margin + cumulative-cash area chart (crosses zero at payback) and profitability verdict. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative (S-curve + cannibalization assumptions; fixed overheads not re-charged)._
- **Supplier Price-Rise Impact** (`supplier-hike`) — selling price/unit, affected input cost/unit, other variable cost/unit, supplier rise %, monthly units → new input = IC×(1+rise); margin hit/unit = IC×rise; annual profit hit = hit×Q×12; pass-through price = P + hit (restores rupee margin), pass-through % → new input cost, margin hit/unit, annual profit hit, pass-through price + before/after table and absorb-vs-pass verdict. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative (only affected input rises; demand held at higher price)._
- **Payment-Terms Cash Impact** (`payment-terms`) — monthly revenue, monthly cost (auto), DSO now/new, DPO now/new, working-capital interest % → daily rev/cost = annual÷365; cash from faster DSO = ΔDSO×dailyRev; cash from slower DPO = ΔDPO×dailyCost; cash freed = sum; interest saved = freed×rate; cash-conversion cycle = DSO−DPO → cash from DSO, cash from DPO, total cash freed, annual interest saved + CCC now-vs-after and direction verdict. _Persist: none (live ledger for rev/cost; days/rate local)._ _Class: Backend (live ledger) + Indicative (one-time balance-sheet shift)._
- **Marketing-Spend ROI Scenario** (`marketing-roi`) — spend, cost-per-lead, lead→customer %, AOV, contribution margin %, extra lifetime orders → leads = S÷CPL; customers = leads×conv; first revenue = customers×AOV; LTV revenue ×(1+repeat); CAC = S÷customers; first-order ROAS; LTV:CAC; net first/LTV profit = margin − spend → customers, CAC, first-order ROAS, LTV:CAC + first-order vs LTV table and three-state profitability verdict. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative (funnel assumptions; no discounting)._
- **Capex: Buy vs Loan vs Lease** (`capex`) — asset cost, life (years), salvage %, loan rate %, lease rent/mo, tax %, depreciation % (WDV) → depreciation tax shield over life (WDV loop); BUY net = A − salvage − deprShield; LOAN net = A + flat interest(A×r×yrs) − interestShield − salvage − deprShield; LEASE net = totalLease − leaseShield → three option cards (lowest-cost highlighted) + component breakdown table (gross cash out, tax shield, salvage, net cost) and recommendation. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative (flat loan interest, no time-value discounting)._
- **Debt-Raise vs Equity-Raise** (`debt-vs-equity`) — capital needed, loan rate %, tenor (years), tax %, pre-money (₹Cr), expected exit (₹Cr) → DEBT: reducing-balance EMI, total interest, interest tax shield, debt net cost = interest − shield; EQUITY: investor % = amount÷post-money, founder kept, equity cost at exit = exit×investorPct → loan EMI/mo, debt net cost, equity given up %, equity cost at exit + term-loan vs equity table and which-is-cheaper verdict (deep-references Funding-Dilution tool). _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative (EMI exact; equity "cost" contingent on assumed exit)._
- **Top-Client Loss Impact** (`client-loss`) — lost monthly revenue (auto-suggested = top customer's invoiced share × live monthly revenue), variable-cost share %, cash on hand (auto) → ranks customers by total invoiced (`store.invoices`); top share; margin lost = lostRev×(1−varShare); new rev/cost/burn/runway; replacement customers needed = ⌈top.total ÷ avg of other customers⌉; concentration flag at ≥25% → revenue concentration %, margin lost/mo, runway after loss, customers to replace + now vs after-loss table and concentration-risk verdict. _Persist: none (reads live `store.invoices` + `useLiveMonthly()`; overrides local)._ _Class: Backend (real AR list + live ledger) + Indicative (linear margin/runway)._
- **Salary-Hike Affordability** (`salary-hike`) — current payroll/mo (auto-suggested = 55% of monthly cost), headcount, average hike %, employer load %, cash on hand (auto), contribution margin % → added monthly = payroll×hike×(1+load); added annual; per-head; new burn & runway; revenue lift needed = addedMonthly÷margin and lift % → added cost/mo, added cost/yr, avg per head, runway after hike + before/after table and affordability (6-month line) verdict. _Persist: none (live ledger for cost/cash; hike inputs local)._ _Class: Backend (live ledger) + Indicative (across-the-board hike)._
- **Inventory Build-up Cash Impact** (`inventory-buildup`) — monthly COGS (auto-suggested = 60% of monthly cost), days-inventory now/target, carrying cost %/yr, bulk discount %, cash on hand (auto) → daily COGS = annual÷365; cash locked = ΔDIO×dailyCOGS; carrying cost = locked×carry; bulk saving = COGS×12×disc; net annual = bulkSaving − carrying; cash after; affords-it check (locked ≤ cash) → cash locked, carrying cost/yr, bulk saving/yr, net annual benefit + now vs after-build-up table and three-state verdict (can't afford / pays off / carry exceeds saving). _Persist: none (live ledger for COGS/cash; days/rates local)._ _Class: Backend (live ledger) + Indicative (one-time cash lock + annualised carry/saving)._
- **FX-Rate Shock (Exporters / Importers)** (`fx-shock`) — USD revenue/mo, USD cost/mo, INR per USD now, rate move % (− = ₹ strengthens), contribution margin % → new rate = rate×(1+shock); net USD exposure = rev−cost (natural hedge from USD costs); monthly ₹ swing = net×(newRate−rate); annual swing; margin impact = swing×margin; exposure at risk = |net|×rate → net USD exposure, new rate, monthly/annual ₹ swing + rate-now vs rate-new table and favourable/adverse hedge verdict. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative (single-rate sensitivity; no forward/option premium pricing). [Note: multi-currency money rails are a deferred/non-faked area per memory; this tool is a calculator only.]_
- **Automation-Investment ROI** (`automation-roi`) — upfront cost, recurring cost/mo, hours saved/mo, fully-loaded cost/hour → gross saving = hours×wage; net saving = gross − recurring; payback mo = upfront÷net; annual net; 3-year net = net×36 − upfront; 3-yr ROI % → labour value freed, net saving, payback period, 3-year ROI + 12/24/36-month cumulative cost/saving/net table and worth-it verdict. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative (assumes freed hours convert to real savings)._
- **Churn-Increase Impact** (`churn-impact`) — active customers, revenue/customer/mo (ARPU), current monthly churn %, churn increase (pts) → avg lifetime = 1÷churn; LTV = ARPU÷churn; extra lost/mo = n×Δchurn; MRR after 12mo = ARPU×n×(1−churn)^12; MRR gap = base12 − after12; severe if gap > 10% of MRR → avg lifetime after, LTV after, extra lost/mo, MRR gap by mo 12 + current-vs-higher-churn table and retention-leverage verdict. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative (geometric-decay; no acquisition/discounting)._
- **Capacity-Expansion Model** (`capacity-expansion`) — expansion capex, added units/mo (at full), expected utilisation %, price/unit, variable cost/unit, added fixed cost/mo → real units = units×util; contribution = P−V; added revenue = realUnits×P; added profit = realUnits×contrib − fixed; payback mo = capex÷addProfit; break-even units = fixed÷contrib and break-even utilisation % → added revenue, added profit, payback, break-even utilisation + 50/70/90/100% utilisation sensitivity table (current util highlighted) and worth-it (payback ≤36mo) verdict. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative (steady-state; no ramp-up/financing cost/demand risk)._
- **Cost-Inflation Passthrough** (`cost-inflation`) — price/unit, input cost/unit, cost inflation %, passthrough %, units/mo → new cost = C×(1+infl); cost rise; new price = P + costRise×pass; full passthrough price = P + costRise and full hike %; margins: old, new (partial pass), absorb-fully; profit before vs after = margin×q; protects-margin check (new ≥ old − 0.5% of P) → new price, price hike %, margin after, profit change + four-strategy table (before / absorb fully / pass X% / full passthrough) and margin-protection verdict (references Price-Change Profit tool for elasticity). _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative (holds volume constant; variable cost only)._

Count: 21 tools.
## Automation & Workflows (`/automation`) — 31 tools

No-code finance-ops fabric: define IF-THEN rules, schedulers, approval chains, validation checks, and watchers, all previewed against live transactions/invoices/obligations/bank balances. Stakeholders: owner, finance, ops. Backend: KV-synced via `useFeatureState` (per-tool keys on the synced account). NOTE: no live cron — every rule/schedule/trigger evaluates on-demand against current store data when the tab renders or a Preview/Scan button is clicked; nothing fires, sends, posts, or mutates server-side. Header copy states this explicitly. UI filter tokens (rule action picker, bulk-action picker, notification-event lists) are excluded below.

- **Overview** — reads counts from all KV stores (active rules, reminders, chains, tasks, webhooks, logged events) → six clickable stat cards + three "where to start" jump links. _Persist: reads `auto-rules`/`auto-reminders`/`auto-approval-chains`/`auto-tasks`/`auto-webhooks`/`auto-activity`._ _Class: KV (read-only dashboard)._
- **Rule Builder** — name + subject (transaction|invoice) + field + op (>,<,==,contains) + value + action (flag/notify/tag/escalate) → IF-THEN rule builder; Preview evaluates condition against live `store.transactions`/`store.invoices` and lists matching rows (count + first 25); enable/pause toggle → stored rule + match preview. _Persist: `auto-rules`._ _Class: Preview (action never executed)._
- **Reminder Scheduler** — anchor (invoice due / obligation due / manual date) + record/label + day offset + channel (whatsapp/email/in-app) → scheduler; computes fire date = baseDate + offset, sorts, shows status (past/today/in Nd) → reminder rows table. _Persist: `auto-reminders`._ _Class: Simulated (delivery is client-side preview, no send)._
- **Approval Chains** — chain name + spend threshold + ordered approver steps each with mode (any one / all) → approval-chain builder; preview counts current outflows (negative txns) ≥ threshold that would route through → stored chain + routed-count. _Persist: `auto-approval-chains`._ _Class: Preview (routing illustrative, no sign-off engine)._
- **Trigger Library** — pick from 6 hardcoded recipes (overdue nudge, large payout, big invoice, payroll, tax, unpaid invoices) → one-click install seeds a ready-made `RuleRow` into Rule Builder. _Persist: writes `auto-rules`._ _Class: KV (recipe templates → seeds a rule)._
- **Bulk Runner** — selection (overdue-invoices / pending-invoices / flagged-txns) + action (send-reminder / mark-followup / tag) → batch picker; lists live matching records, "Preview run" logs intended action across N records. _Persist: none (logs to `auto-activity`)._ _Class: Simulated (dry-run, mutates/sends nothing)._
- **Notification Rules** — event (from fixed list) + channel (whatsapp/email/in-app) + recipient + quiet-hours toggle (daily digest vs instant) → notification routing registry table. _Persist: `auto-notifications`._ _Class: Simulated (definitions only; delivery depends on unconnected channels)._
- **Recurring Tasks** — task title + cadence (daily/weekly/monthly/quarterly) + anchor date + owner → scheduler; computes next occurrence ≥ today (cadence step days) → tasks table with next-run. _Persist: `auto-tasks`._ _Class: Indicative (next-run computed; nothing fires without cron)._
- **Activity Log** — passive consumer of the shared event stream every tool appends to (create/run/delete, capped at 200) → chronological audit feed; Clear-log button. _Persist: `auto-activity` (live shared store)._ _Class: KV (local audit trail, not backend execution log)._
- **Webhook Registry** — name + endpoint URL (http/https validated) + event → outbound webhook registry table with active/disabled toggle. _Persist: `auto-webhooks`._ _Class: Simulated (config only; no dispatcher, no secrets/signing collected)._
- **Templates Gallery** — 6 end-to-end blueprints (dunning, month-end close, P2P gate, vendor onboarding, idle-cash watch, GST pipeline) with persona + numbered steps → "Use template" seeds a starter rule (if `rule` present) or treats as manual checklist. _Persist: writes `auto-rules` (logs to `auto-activity`)._ _Class: KV (template→rule seed / checklist; multi-step execution needs absent orchestrator)._
- **Numbering Rules** — prefix + pad width + start + reset-yearly toggle → invoice numbering-rule builder; sample = prefix + optional FY tag + (start + current invoice count) zero-padded → scheme rows with computed next number. _Persist: `auto-numbering`._ _Class: Preview (sample computed from invoice count; assignment happens elsewhere)._
- **Auto-Categorize** — keyword + target category (revenue/expense/payroll/loan/tax/transfer) → auto-categorize rule; previews live txns whose description/counterparty contains keyword and how many would re-class (category differs). _Persist: `auto-categorize`._ _Class: Preview (no data mutated)._
- **Escalation Matrix** — day-overdue threshold + assignee → escalation tiers (sorted); buckets live overdue invoices into the highest tier each exceeds, shows count + ₹ per tier. _Persist: `auto-escalation`._ _Class: Preview (assignment illustrative, no one paged)._
- **SLA Timers** — stage (from fixed list) + window in hours + on-breach action (escalate/remind/reassign) → SLA-policy table (hours formatted h/d). _Persist: `auto-sla`._ _Class: Indicative (policy stored; no live clock server-side)._
- **Recurring Journals** — template name + debit acct + credit acct + amount + cadence (monthly/quarterly) + first-post date → recurring-journal templates; computes next-post date → table with Dr→Cr entry. _Persist: `auto-journals`._ _Class: Indicative (next-post computed; nothing posted to ledger)._
- **Document Routing** — keyword + destination folder + (optional) ledger → routing rules; estimates match count from live txns whose description/counterparty contains keyword. _Persist: `auto-routing`._ _Class: Preview (match estimate; no files moved)._
- **Data Validation** — toggle any of 5 integrity checks (missing invoice number, future-dated invoice, large transfer-category outflow >₹50k, duplicate amount+customer, revenue posted as outflow); each runs live against store and lists findings; "Run enabled checks" tallies total issues → per-check finding lists. _Persist: `auto-validation` (enabled-check ids; logs runs to `auto-activity`)._ _Class: Preview (findings surfaced, nothing auto-corrected)._
- **Scheduled Reports** — report (from fixed list) + cadence (daily/weekly/monthly) + channel (email/whatsapp) + recipient + first-delivery date → report-schedule jobs; computes next delivery → table. _Persist: `auto-reports`._ _Class: Simulated (next-delivery computed; no compilation/send without backend)._
- **Discount Rules** — min invoice value + discount % + optional label → tiered discount rules (sorted); preview applies each invoice's best-matching tier across live AR, shows qualifying count + est. total discount. _Persist: `auto-discounts`._ _Class: Preview (illustrative figures, not applied to invoices)._
- **Reminder Cadences** — cadence name + multi-step builder (offset days from due + channel whatsapp/email/call + note) → dunning-cadence templates; illustrates each step's fire date against the nearest-due open invoice. _Persist: `auto-cadences`._ _Class: Simulated (preview against sample invoice; sending needs backend)._
- **Segment Builder** — name + subject (transaction|invoice) + match logic (ALL/ANY) + multi-condition builder (field + op + value) → saved reusable segment; Preview evaluates AND/OR conditions against live records (count + first 25). _Persist: `auto-segments`._ _Class: Preview (counts computed; meant to later feed bulk actions/policies)._
- **KPI Watch** — metric (cash-balance / overdue-AR / open-AR / obligations-due≤30d / month-outflow) + op (below/above) + threshold + notify-target → KPI watch rules; computes current metric value live and flags breach state now. _Persist: `auto-kpiwatch`._ _Class: Preview (breach flagged; no alert sent)._
- **Tier Reminder Policy** — tier (platinum/gold/standard) + min outstanding floor + first-nudge offset days + channel → one policy per tier (replaces same tier); classifies live customers by total open outstanding into matching tier with nudge/channel. _Persist: `auto-tier-policy`._ _Class: Preview (classification table; sending needs backend)._
- **Status-Update Rules** — days-past-due threshold → auto-flip rule (pending → overdue, only-from pending); lists exactly which pending invoices would flip; "Preview run" logs the dry-run. _Persist: `auto-status-rules` (logs runs to `auto-activity`)._ _Class: Simulated (dry-run; mutates nothing)._
- **Run History** — derived view filtering `auto-activity` to kind=="run" only; aggregates run count + last-run timestamp per tool → per-tool stat cards + run feed. _Persist: reads `auto-activity`._ _Class: KV (local execution history; no scheduled executor)._
- **Credit-Limit Rules** — scope (all customers / one customer) + customer + credit limit + on-breach (flag/block) → credit-limit rules; lists customers whose live unpaid outstanding already exceeds the limit (count + offenders). _Persist: `auto-credit-limit`._ _Class: Preview (risk surfaced; nothing auto-blocked)._
- **Payment Matching** — amount tolerance % + match window days → reconciliation rule; finds candidate matches between open invoices and revenue inflows within tolerance band and date window (gap ≥0, ≤window). _Persist: `auto-paymatch`._ _Class: Preview (likely matches listed; nothing marked paid)._
- **Low-Balance Trigger** — watch scope (combined total or a specific bank account) + floor ₹ → cash-floor trigger; evaluates live balances now, shows breach/healthy state. _Persist: `auto-lowbalance`._ _Class: Preview (breach state shown; cannot push alert without backend watcher)._
- **Recurring Invoices** — customer + amount + cadence (weekly/monthly/quarterly) + first-invoice date → billing-template rules; previews next six raise dates + annualised value. _Persist: `auto-recurringinv`._ _Class: Indicative (schedule previewed; no invoices created, no scheduler)._
- **Duplicate-Payment Check** — day window between payments → read-only scan grouping outflows to same counterparty with identical amount within window; shows payment dates + ₹ at risk; "Scan" logs the run. _Persist: `auto-dupcheck` (stores window int; logs runs to `auto-activity`)._ _Class: Preview (read-only scan; reverses/blocks nothing)._

## ━━━ PILLAR 7 · Risk, Protection & Global ━━━

## Security & Fraud (`/security`) — 30 tools

First line of defence against payment fraud (double-pays, BEC bank reroutes, ghost vendors, structuring) running self-checks on the live ledger and invoices. Stakeholders: owner, finance, CA. Backend: KV-synced (scans live transactions/invoices from AppContext store; manual registers persisted via `useFeatureState`/KV). NOTE: heuristics — flag "suspects, confirm" (every tab ends with the disclaimer "These are heuristic flags — suspects, not verdicts").

> The `overview` tab (#0) is a non-tool summary dashboard: σ-outlier count, duplicate count (same payee+amount ≤7d), new payees (first-seen ≤30d), total scanned, plus a static "what this module does" card. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

- **Anomaly Scanner** — outflows + sensitivity σ slider (1–4, default 2) → mean/σ of \|amounts\|, z-score per payment, flag \|z\|≥σ (anomaly scan, σ-outlier) → table of outlier payments sorted by \|z\| with deviation in σ. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Duplicate Payments** — outflows + window slider (1–30d, default 7) → group by payee+rounded amount, flag groups with any consecutive pair ≤window (duplicate detection); estimates recoverable exposure = amount×(count−1) → suspect groups with rows. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Round-Trip Detector** — outflows/inflows + window (3–90d, default 30) + amount tolerance (0–50%, default 20%) → match each out to a later in from same counterparty within window and within ±tol% (round-trip/circular flow) → out↔back pairs with % returned. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **New-Payee Watch** — outflows + lookback slider (7–120d, default 30) → first-seen date per payee, flag those first paid ≤lookback (new-payee watch) → table: first paid, payments since, total. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Vendor Bank Change** — manual vendor + account + IFSC (datalist of known payees) → on save, compare to stored record; if account/IFSC differs, mark `lastChangedAt` and warn (vendor bank-change / BEC detection) → register with Stable/Changed status. _Persist: KV `sec-vendor-banks`._ _Class: KV._
- **Monitoring Rules** — user-defined no-code rules (field amount/payee/description; op gt/lt/contains; value; enable toggle) → match each outflow against active rules → flagged txns with triggered rule tags. _Persist: KV `sec-monitoring-rules`._ _Class: KV._
- **Access Review Log** — manual entries (person, role, scope, status active/suspended, lastReviewed) → flag grants not reviewed >90d (segregation/stale-access); mark-reviewed/suspend toggles → register + stale banner. _Persist: KV `sec-access-log`._ _Class: KV._
- **Fraud Risk Score** — txns + invoices → weighted rollup of 4 signals: outlier payments >2σ (w8,max5), duplicate suspects same payee+amt ≤7d (w12,max4), round-number payments %=multiples of 10k (w0.4,max100), invoices missing numbers (w5,max6) → 0–100 score, band Low/Elevated/High (fraud score) + signal breakdown. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Suspicious Invoices** — store.invoices → flag missing invoice#, duplicate invoice#, round amount (≥10k & %10k==0), sequence gap (numeric suffix jump >1) → invoices to review with reason tags. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Security Checklist** — 10 fixed hygiene controls (2FA, maker-checker, vendor verify, access review, recon, unique pw, device lock, backups, least-priv, incident plan), user toggles → completion % + band color. _Persist: KV `sec-hygiene`._ _Class: KV._
- **Off-Hours Payments** — outflows → flag those dated Saturday/Sunday via `getDay()` (off-hours/weekend) → table + total, weekend-day labelled. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Benford's-Law Audit** — \|outflow amounts\|≥1 → leading-digit distribution vs Benford expected %, χ² statistic (8 d.o.f.); verdict insufficient(<50)/normal(≤15.51)/elevated(≤20.09)/high(>20.09) (Benford's-law χ²) → per-digit bars with deviation flags. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Payment Velocity** — outflows + window (1–14d, default 3) + min count (2–10, default 3) → per-payee densest run within window, flag runs ≥minCount (velocity burst/mule/salami) → bursts with count, span, total. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Under-Limit Splitting** — outflows + approval limit (₹, default 100000) + band % (1–25%, default 10) → payments in [limit×(1−band%), limit); detect per-payee multiple just-under payments ≤7d apart (structuring/under-limit splitting) → just-under table + split banner. _Persist: KV `sec-approval-limit` (limit only); band/list live._ _Class: KV (input) / Indicative (analysis)._
- **Dormant Reactivation** — all txns per counterparty + dormancy slider (60–540d, default 180) → consecutive gaps ≥threshold (dormant-account reactivation) → reactivation events with idle gap. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Expense-Policy Flags** — expense-category outflows vs editable policy (per-txn cap default 50000, flag-weekend toggle, restricted keywords) → flag over cap / weekend / keyword hit → expenses to review with violation tags. _Persist: KV `sec-expense-policy`._ _Class: KV._
- **Duties Separation** — manual payee+creator+approver entries (datalist of payees) → flag where creator==approver (segregation of duties) → register + conflict banner. _Persist: KV `sec-sod-map`._ _Class: KV._
- **Vendor Dedupe** — distinct outflow vendor names → normalize (strip pvt/ltd/llp etc + non-alnum), flag identical-after-normalise or Levenshtein distance 1–2 with maxLen≥4 (near-duplicate vendor) → duplicate-pair table with reason. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Refund Anomalies** — txns matching /refund\|reversal\|chargeback\|return/ or revenue-category negatives → flag ≥3 refunds to one party, round-number refund (≥10k & %10k==0), revenue reversal (refund-abuse heuristics) → refund entries with reason tags + total. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Recurring-Charge Watch** — outflows where `isRecurring` per payee → first vs latest amount change %, new if first ≤60d; flag price creep ≥15% (recurring-charge/subscription leakage) → run-rate, new count, creep count, per-payee table. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Fake-ITC Risk** — manual supplier+GSTIN register (datalist of payees) → screen each: malformed GSTIN (15-char regex), invalid state code (not 1–38), same GSTIN across multiple vendors (fake-ITC GSTIN) → risk-marker table + banner. _Persist: KV `sec-vendor-gstins`._ _Class: KV._
- **IP / Device Allowlist** — manual entries (label, kind ip/device, value with IPv4/CIDR validation, trusted/revoked toggle) → maintain allowlist register (attack-surface reduction; no automatic scan) → table. _Persist: KV `sec-ip-allowlist`._ _Class: KV._
- **Data-Export Audit** — manual export log (who, dataset dropdown, rows, reason, PII checkbox) → counts: total, last 30d, containing PII → append log (DPDP/exfiltration trail). _Persist: KV `sec-export-audit`._ _Class: KV._
- **Key Rotation** — manual credentials (name, lastRotated date, intervalDays default 90) → age vs interval → state overdue/soon(≤14d)/ok; mark-rotated action (credential-rotation reminder) → table + overdue banner. _Persist: KV `sec-key-rotation`._ _Class: KV._
- **Cash Spike Monitor** — txns matching /cash\|atm\|withdrawal\|deposit/ + large-cash threshold (₹, default 200000) → monthly cash totals, flag months >1.5× mean, list single cash txns ≥threshold (AML cash-spike) → monthly bars + big-single table. _Persist: KV `sec-cash-threshold` (threshold only); analysis live._ _Class: KV (input) / Indicative (analysis)._
- **Sensitive-Action Log** — manual privileged-action log (actor, action dropdown: limit change/bank edit/grant/revoke/rotate/export/delete/other, detail) → counts total + last 7d → append log (privilege-abuse trail). _Persist: KV `sec-action-log`._ _Class: KV._
- **Round-Amount Flags** — outflows + round step slider (1k–50k, default 10k) + min-amount slider (5k–500k, default 25k) → flag \|amount\| ≥min and %step==0 (round-amount heuristic) → table + total. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Payroll-vs-Vendor Bank** — manual employee name+salary account (dup-account guard) → normalize names, flag any vendor whose name equals/contains/is-contained-by an employee name (ghost-vendor / self-payment) → collision banner + staff table. _Persist: KV `sec-staff-banks`._ _Class: KV (register) / Indicative (name-match)._
- **New-Account Over Limit** — outflows + amount threshold (10k–500k, default 50000) + first-payment window (1–60d, default 14) → flag payments ≥threshold made ≤window days after a payee's first-ever payment (large-payment-to-new-account / invoice redirection) → high-risk table. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Control Self-Assessment** — 8 fixed control questions (dual approval, bank verify, recon, access review, segregation, backups, MFA, petty cash) answered yes/partial/no → maturity score (yes=1, partial=0.5), band Weak/Developing/Strong, gap list → live score + gaps. _Persist: KV `sec-control-csa`._ _Class: KV._
## Insurance & Protection (`/insurance`) — 33 tools

Track, size, price and protect every business and personal cover in one place — policy register, gap analysis, premium estimators and claims tracking. Stakeholders: owner, finance. Backend: KV-synced (per-feature `useFeatureState` keys; ephemeral tools hold state in React only). Premium figures INDICATIVE (no IRDAI quote feed) — all rates/loads are hardcoded illustrative constants.

(Overview is a dashboard/launcher only — 4 KPI cards from `ins-policies` + revenue and a 10-tool jump grid; not counted below.)

- **Policy Register & Renewals** — insurer, type (17 `POLICY_TYPES`), policy no., sum insured, premium (incl. GST), start/renewal dates → adds to register; sorts by days-to-renewal; computes lapsed (<0d) / urgent (≤30d) / active status badges per policy → table + add/remove. _Persist: `ins-policies`._ _Class: KV._
- **Coverage-Gap Analyzer** — register policy types held + auto store signals (revenue from positive txns, payroll category presence, open receivables = unpaid invoices) → runs 8 fixed risk checks (fire, burglary, public liability, group health if payroll, PA if payroll, trade-credit if receivables>0 with high/med severity at >25% revenue, BI if revenue>0, cyber) → lists gaps not in register vs covered chips. _Persist: `ins-policies` (read)._ _Class: KV._
- **Sum-Insured Calculator** — building + plant + stock + furniture, turnover (auto from revenue), gross-margin %, BI indemnity months → asset SI = sum of four; BI SI = turnover × margin% × months/12; recommended = assets + BI → itemized rows + total. _Persist: none (read store revenue)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Group-Health Estimator** — head counts per 4 age bands (factors 0.7/1.0/1.6/2.6), cover/head, family-floater toggle (×1.45), maternity toggle (×1.12) → base premium = Σ(n × cover/1L × ₹1100 × ageFactor), ×loads, +18% GST → net/GST/gross + per-life cost. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Business-Asset Cover Calculator** — per-line asset name, sum insured, peril rate (% p.a.) → total SI; net premium = Σ(value × rate%); +18% GST → KPIs + schedule table; add/remove lines. _Persist: `ins-asset-schedule`._ _Class: Indicative (+KV for schedule)._
- **Trade-Credit Estimator** — insured credit turnover (auto = open receivables), rate (‰), cover % per invoice, largest-buyer exposure → net premium = turnover × rate/1000; +18% GST; max indemnity = turnover × cover%; concentration % = topBuyer/turnover with >25% warning. _Persist: none (read invoices)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Claims Tracker** — insurer, claim type/peril, claim amount, date → logs claim (status starts "intimated"); editable settled amount; status dropdown across 6 stages (intimated→surveyor→documents→approved→settled→rejected, settled auto-fills settled=claimed); claims-to-premium ratio = totalSettled/registerPremium. _Persist: `ins-claims` (reads `ins-policies` for ratio)._ _Class: KV._
- **Premium vs Cover** — per-quote insurer, sum insured, premium, deductible → rate-on-line = premium/SI × 100; premium+deductible exposure; picks BEST = lowest ROL → ranked table + add/remove. _Persist: `ins-quotes`._ _Class: KV._
- **Key-Man Estimator** — key person name + method (profit: profit × contribution% × years | salary: salary × multiple | loan: loan outstanding) → recommended sum assured; indicative net premium = SA/₹1cr × ₹35000; +18% GST → SA + premium cards. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Risk-Exposure Scorecard** — register + claims + store signals → 0–100 protection score: core covers held (≤40), employee health (≤15), receivables protection (≤15), no-lapsed freshness (≤20, −10/lapse), renewal hygiene (≤10); band (≥75 well / ≥50 partial / <50 under-protected); breakdown bars + open-claims note. _Persist: `ins-policies` + `ins-claims` (read)._ _Class: KV._
- **Premium-Due Calendar** — register policies with renewal dates → buckets renewals into rolling 12 months; per-month count + premium total; next-12 total, avg/month, undated count → horizontal bar chart. _Persist: `ins-policies` (read)._ _Class: KV._
- **CSR Comparator** — per-insurer claims received, claims paid (validated ≤ received), avg settle days → CSR = paid/received × 100; sorts desc, flags TOP; color bands (≥95 green / ≥85 yellow / <85 red) → table; user enters published IRDAI figures. _Persist: `ins-csr`._ _Class: KV._
- **No-Claim-Bonus Tracker** — per-policy name, base premium, claim-free years → NCB% via motor slab [0,20,25,35,45,50] capped at index; saving = base × NCB%; shows next-year NCB; total saving across policies → table. _Persist: `ins-ncb`._ _Class: KV._
- **Deductible Optimizer** — base premium, base deductible, expected claims/year, premium fall % per doubling → for multipliers [0.5,1,2,4,8]: premium = base × (1−sens)^log2(m), expected retained = deductible × freq, total annual cost; picks OPTIMAL = min total cost → table. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Business-Interruption Estimator** — turnover (auto), gross-profit %, indemnity months, increased cost of working, premium rate % → BI SI = GP × months/12 + addl; net premium = SI × rate%; +18% GST → SI/net/GST/gross cards. _Persist: none (read revenue)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Marine / Transit Calculator** — mode single (consignment value + freight) or open/annual (dispatch turnover), cover % (default 110 = CIF+10%), rate (‰) → base × cover% = SI; net premium = SI × rate/1000; +18% GST → SI/net/GST/per-shipment-or-annual cards. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Professional-Indemnity Estimator** — profession (7-key rate map 0.6–1.4), indemnity limit, annual fees, retroactive years → net premium = limit × baseRate% × (1 + feeLoad capped 0.5 + retro×0.08); +18% GST → limit/net/GST/gross cards. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Cyber-Insurance Need Scorer** — 7 weighted yes/no factors (sum to 100), PII records count → score = Σ checked weights; suggested limit = max(₹25L, records×150) × (1 + score/100); band (≥60 high / ≥35 moderate / <35 lower). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Vehicle / Fleet Insurance Tracker** — per-vehicle reg no., type (6 `VEHICLE_TYPES`), IDV, premium, expiry → total IDV/premium; expiring-≤30d count; per-row lapsed/urgent/active status → KPIs + table; add/remove. _Persist: `ins-fleet`._ _Class: KV._
- **Workmen-Comp Estimator** — occupation risk class (5-band rate map 0.2–2.0%), annual wage bill (auto from payroll outflow), headcount, medical-extension toggle (+15%) → net premium = wages × rate% × medLoad; +18% GST; per-worker → 4 cards. _Persist: none (read payroll txns)._ _Class: Indicative._
