- **Premium-Financing EMI Calculator** — annual premium, down-payment %, interest rate % p.a., tenure months → down payment; principal; reducing-balance EMI; total paid; interest = total − premium → cards + interest-as-%-of-premium note. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **GST-on-Premium ITC Checker** — cover type (10-rule map: yes/conditional/no), premium excl. GST → GST = base × 18%; gross; ITC claimable = GST if eligible="yes" else 0; eligibility badge + s.17(5) note (group-health & personal-vehicle conditional/blocked). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Health Top-Up / Super-Top-Up Optimizer** — target total cover, base cover (= deductible), eldest age, super-top-up toggle → top-up cover = target − base; age-loaded per-1L rates (direct 1900, base 2100, top-up 320 super / 480 regular, all ×ageLoad incl. GST); direct vs stacked premium; saving + % → cards, saving banner, breakdown. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **OPD & Wellness Benefit Tracker** — annual OPD limit, benefit head (9 `OPD_HEADS`), amount, date → logs usage; used/remaining/utilisation% vs limit; by-head breakdown; utilisation progress bar → KPIs + table; add/remove. _Persist: `ins-opd-claims` + `ins-opd-limit`._ _Class: KV._
- **Coverage Adequacy by Life-Stage** — age, income, dependents, loans, savings, existing life & health cover → life need = income × age-multiple (20/18/14/10/6) × (1 + deps×0.15) + loans − savings; life gap vs held; health need by age band × (1.5 if deps); health gap; life-stage label → recommended + shortfall cards. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Riders Comparator** — per-rider name (8 suggested), annual premium, benefit/cover, keep toggle → cost-per-₹1L cover = premium/(benefit/1L); totals over kept riders (count, added premium, added cover) → table with toggle + add/remove. _Persist: `ins-riders`._ _Class: KV._
- **Insurance TCO** — premium, deductible, co-insurance %, typical claim size, claim probability %, horizon years → OOP if claim = deductible + (claim−deductible)×coins; insurer pays = claim − OOP; expected annual OOP = prob × OOP; annual TCO = premium + expected OOP; multi-year TCO → cards + cost-split breakdown. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Surrender-Value Estimator** — plan type (endowment/ULIP), annual premium, policy term, years paid, fund value (ULIP) → ULIP: 5-yr lock-in → discontinuance value (fund − min(₹6000, 6%)) if locked else fund; endowment: nil if <2yrs, else GSV = (premium × (paid−1)) × factor% (30/50/65/80/90 by elapsed ratio) → paid/surrender/gain-loss cards + lock-in warning. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Group vs Individual Cost Compare** — lives, average age, cover/head, employer-share % → age-loaded per-1L rates (group 900, individual 1450 ×ageLoad); group vs individual gross (×1.18); saving + %; per-head group; employer outlay = group × share% → cards + saving banner. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Multi-Policy Renewal Planner** — register policies with renewal date + premium → buckets premium into rolling 12 months; total, count, peak month → bar chart per month with counts; empty state if none. _Persist: `ins-policies` (read)._ _Class: KV._
- **Sum-Insured Inflation Indexer** — current SI, cost-inflation % p.a., project years (0–20), years since last revaluation → indexed projection SI × (1+infl)^y per year; true value now = SI × (1+infl)^elapsed; shortfall + under-insurance % if not revalued; target SI at horizon → warning banner + projection table. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Insurance Spend Budget** — annual revenue (auto), target spend % of revenue, extra premiums not in register → budget = revenue × target%; actual spend = register premium + extra; actual % of revenue; variance = budget − spend (headroom/over) → cards + within/over-budget banner. _Persist: `ins-policies` (read) + read revenue._ _Class: KV._
- **Claim Document Readiness** — toggle 8 fixed checklist items (intimation, FIR, policy copy, repair estimate, photos, purchase bills, surveyor cooperation, KYC/bank) → ready count + % progress bar; all-done banner. _Persist: `ins-claim-readiness`._ _Class: KV._
## Sustainability & ESG (`/esg`) — 31 tools

Turn live books into an ESG ledger: carbon footprint, Scope 1/2/3, BRSR-lite, green spend, net-zero pathway and India-aware compliance (BRSR, SEBI, CBAM, CPCB EPR, DPDP). Stakeholders: owner, ops. Backend: KV-synced via `useFeatureState` (durable) or transient local state. Emission factors INDICATIVE — grid 0.71 kg CO₂e/kWh (CEA), diesel 2.68, petrol 2.31, LPG 2.98; spend-based EEIO factors per ₹1,000.

The tab bar has 32 entries; **Overview** is a dashboard, not a tool, so 31 tools below.

- **Overview** (dashboard, not counted) — reads live `store.transactions` → revenue (Σ positive), expense (Σ |negative|), rough screening footprint (expense/1000 × 35 mixed factor), framework badges; jump-buttons to 10 tools. _Persist: none (live store)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Carbon Footprint Estimator** (`footprint`) — annual ₹ per 6 spend categories (fuel 70, electricity 55, travel 40, logistics 50, materials 30, services 8 kg/₹1,000) → kg = (₹/1000)×factor per row → total footprint, categorised spend, largest source, % breakdown bars; shows live annual expense as hint. _Persist: none (transient useState)._ _Class: Indicative (live-store hint)._
- **Scope 1/2/3 Calculator** (`scopes`) — Scope 1: diesel/petrol litres + LPG kg × fuel factors; Scope 2: kWh × 0.71; Scope 3: purchased/logistics/travel ₹ × spend proxies (30/50/40 per ₹1,000) → per-scope tCO₂e, total inventory, % split bar (GHG Protocol). _Persist: none (transient)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Energy & Water Tracker** (`energy`) — monthly log: month, kWh, water kL, bill ₹; add/remove rows → totals (kWh, Scope 2 = kWh×0.71, water kL, spend) + per-row CO₂e table sorted by month. _Persist: `esg-utility-log`._ _Class: KV._
- **ESG Scorecard** (`scorecard`) — 12 yes/no checkboxes across E/S/G (4 each) → per-pillar % (yes/total), overall = mean of 3 pillar %, band (Leader ≥75 / Progressing ≥50 / Developing ≥25 / Starting out). _Persist: `esg-scorecard`._ _Class: KV._
- **BRSR-Lite Readiness** (`brsr`) — 12 checklist items in 4 sections (General/Environment/Social/Governance) → completed count + % progress bar; 100% banner. _Persist: `esg-brsr-lite`._ _Class: KV._
- **Green Spend Tracker** (`greenspend`) — description + ₹ amount + type (7 green types) → total green spend, % of live annual expense, count + line-item table. _Persist: `esg-green-spend`._ _Class: KV (live-store ratio)._
- **Emission Intensity** (`intensity`) — total tCO₂e + revenue (₹, auto-fills from live revenue) + optional prior-year intensity → carbon intensity = tonnes ÷ (rev/1cr), per ₹lakh variant, YoY % delta with improving/worsening verdict. _Persist: none (transient)._ _Class: Indicative (live-store auto-fill)._
- **Supplier Sustainability Rating** (`supplier`) — name + 3 sliders (emissions/labour/governance 1–5) + ISO-14001 certified flag → score = min(100, (sum/15)×100×(certified?1.1:1)), tier (Low ≥75 / Watch ≥50 / High risk) table sorted by score. _Persist: `esg-suppliers`._ _Class: KV._
- **Carbon Offset Cost Estimator** (`offset`) — residual tCO₂e + base ₹/t (default 1200) + quality tier (basic ×0.5 / verified ×1 / removal ×3) → effective unit price, tonnes, total offset cost. _Persist: none (transient)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Sustainability Goal Tracker** (`goals`) — goal name, unit, baseline/current/target, target year → SBTi-style progress = (baseline−current)/(baseline−target) clamped 0–100, achieved banner. _Persist: `esg-goals`._ _Class: KV._
- **Employee Commute Emissions** (`commute`) — per-leg: mode (6 factors, pax-km), # employees, one-way km → annual kg = emp×km×2×22 days×12×factor (Scope 3 cat 7) → total footprint, employees covered, per-employee + leg table. _Persist: `esg-commute`._ _Class: KV._
- **Business Travel Emissions** (`travel`) — short-haul (0.158) + long-haul (0.195) pax-km, rail (0.041), road (0.171) km, hotel nights (12 kg/night) → per-mode tCO₂e (Scope 3 cat 6), total, stacked split bar. _Persist: none (transient)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Waste & Recycling Tracker** (`waste`) — per-stream (7 streams): generated kg, recycled kg (validated ≤ generated) → totals, diversion rate = recycled/generated, to-landfill, per-row diversion table (BRSR/EPR metric). _Persist: `esg-waste`._ _Class: KV._
- **Renewable Switch ROI (Rooftop Solar)** (`renewable`) — monthly grid kWh, tariff ₹/kWh (8), system kW, capex ₹/kW (55000) → capex, annual gen (kW×1460), usable gen capped at consumption, bill saving, simple payback, CO₂ avoided (×0.71), 25-yr net benefit; <6yr payback banner. _Persist: none (transient)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **EV-Fleet Transition Calculator** (`evfleet`) — vehicles, km/yr (15000), ICE mileage (15 km/L), fuel ₹/L (100), EV km/kWh (7), charge ₹/kWh (9) → ICE vs EV running cost & emissions (petrol 2.31 vs grid 0.71), cost & CO₂ savings, narrative. _Persist: none (transient)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Diversity & Social Metrics** (`diversity`) — 8 headcount inputs (total, women, mgmt total/women, PwD, contractual, training hrs, safety incidents) → women %, women-in-mgmt %, PwD %, contractual %, training hrs/emp, incidents (BRSR Section C, P3/5). _Persist: `esg-diversity`._ _Class: KV._
- **Governance Checklist** (`governance`) — 12 items in 4 groups (Board/Ethics/Risk/Data, Companies Act + DPDP 2023) → completed count + % bar. _Persist: `esg-governance`._ _Class: KV._
- **CBAM Export-Exposure Estimator** (`cbam`) — per-line: product (5 goods, embedded-emission intensities tCO₂e/t: steel 2.1, aluminium 8.6, cement 0.87, fertiliser 1.6, hydrogen 10) + tonnes to EU; CO₂ price €/t (75) + €→₹ (90) → embedded emissions, ₹/t price, annual CBAM liability + line table. _Persist: `esg-cbam`._ _Class: KV (Indicative factors)._
- **EPR Compliance Tracker** (`epr`) — per-category (5 CPCB categories): obligation kg, fulfilled kg, cert ₹/kg (15) → totals, compliance % = fulfilled/obligation, shortfall, cost-to-close = shortfall×cert price, met/short status table. _Persist: `esg-epr`._ _Class: KV._
- **Green-Loan Eligibility Score** (`greenloan`) — 8 weighted criteria checkboxes (15/15/10/15/20/10/10/5 = 100) + optional loan ₹ → score, band (Strong ≥75 / Eligible ≥50 / Not ready), indicative rate step-down (0.75%/0.25%/0%), est. annual interest saving = loan×stepDown. _Persist: `esg-greenloan`._ _Class: KV (Indicative rates)._
- **Net-Zero Pathway Planner** (`netzero`) — baseline tCO₂e, base year, net-zero year, residual % (10) → linear glide path: per-year cap = base−(base−residual)×frac, required annual cut, annual cut %, SBTi-aligned check (≥4.2%/yr), residual, years-to-net-zero, sampled cap-by-year bars. _Persist: none (transient)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Supplier ESG Questionnaire Tracker** (`questionnaire`) — add supplier; advance through 4-stage flow (not_sent→sent→responded→verified) → suppliers tracked, sent/awaiting, verified, response rate = (responded+verified)/total + status table with Advance action. _Persist: `esg-supplier-survey`._ _Class: KV._
- **ESG Report Builder** (`report`) — business name, year, select from 8 report sections → exports plain-text outline (.txt download via Blob) of chosen sections. _Persist: `esg-report-sections` (section selection)._ _Class: KV (export Simulated — .txt skeleton, not PDF)._
- **Climate-Risk Assessment** (`climaterisk`) — 8 risks (4 Physical + 4 Transition) rated 0–3 sliders → per-type % = sum/(items×3), overall = mean, band (High ≥60 / Moderate ≥30 / Low) — TCFD-style screen. _Persist: `esg-climate-risk`._ _Class: KV._
- **Sustainable Procurement Scorecard** (`procurement`) — per-category: total spend ₹, sustainable spend ₹ (validated ≤ total) → totals, green-procurement share = sustainable/total, per-row share table. _Persist: `esg-procurement`._ _Class: KV._
- **Community / CSR Impact Tracker** (`csr`) — per-project: project, theme (6), spend ₹, beneficiaries → total spend, people reached, cost/beneficiary, project count; shows indicative 2% of live profit (Sec 135) as hint (BRSR P8). _Persist: `esg-csr`._ _Class: KV (live-store profit hint)._
- **Energy Cost-Savings Tracker** (`energysavings`) — per-measure: measure, capex ₹, annual ₹ saving, CO₂e cut t/yr → total capex, annual saving, blended payback = capex/saving, total CO₂ cut; table ranked by payback (fastest first). _Persist: `esg-energy-savings`._ _Class: KV._
- **Emissions per Employee** (`empintensity`) — total tCO₂e, FTE headcount, optional prior-year per-head → per-employee tCO₂e (and kg), YoY % delta with improving/worsening verdict. _Persist: none (transient)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Renewable vs Grid Mix** (`renewmix`) — grid / on-site solar / REC kWh → renewable share = (solar+rec)/total, total consumption, grid emissions = grid×0.71, emissions avoided = renewable×0.71, energy-mix split bar (RE100-style). _Persist: none (transient)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Green Certification Checklist** (`certs`) — 8 certifications in 4 groups (Environment/Product/Social/Governance: ISO 14001/50001/45001/27001, IGBC/GRIHA, BIS Ecomark, EPR CPCB, SA8000); click to cycle Not started→In progress→Certified → held + in-progress counts. _Persist: `esg-certifications`._ _Class: KV._
- **ESG Rating Improvement Planner** (`ratingplan`) — 10 weighted actions across E/S/G (points 6–12, effort Low/Medium/High) checkboxes → projected rating = earned/max ×100, band (Strong ≥75 / Moderate ≥50 / Emerging), points earned, actions done/open, top-3 quick wins (low-effort, high-point first). _Persist: `esg-rating-plan`._ _Class: KV._
## Global & Cross-Border (`/global`) — 31 tools

FX, exports & FEMA toolkit for Indian SMB exporters/importers — multi-currency maths, LUT invoicing, FIRC/BRC tracking, customs landed cost, export-incentive estimators and FEMA compliance trackers. Stakeholders: owner, finance. Backend: KV-synced (`useFeatureState` → feature-data store; calculators are stateless). FX rates manual (no live feed) — `DEFAULT_RATES` editable in the converter and shared via the `glb-fx-rates` key.

Tab array carries 32 entries (Overview + 31 tools); the 31 tools below exclude the Overview landing page (which only summarises 4 KV-derived stat cards and links into other tools). UI filter tokens (receivable/payable, royalty/FTS/interest/dividend, RoDTEP/drawback, costplus/resaleminus, lut/igst, sell/buy, AA/EPCG, odi/fdi, rodtep purposes, etc.) are excluded per spec.

- **Currency Converter** (`fx-convert`) — amount + from/to currency (INR + 7: USD/EUR/GBP/AED/SGD/AUD/JPY), editable ₹-per-unit reference rates, swap button → INR-pivot conversion, cross rate (1 from = X to), INR equivalent. _Persist: `glb-fx-rates` (rates table; shared with consolidator, MC P&L)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **FX Gain / Loss** (`fx-gainloss`) — foreign amount, rate at invoice, rate at settlement, direction (receivable/payable) → invoice INR vs settle INR; realised forex gain/loss (AS 11 / Ind-AS 21), sign-flipped for payables → gain or loss to P&L. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Export Invoice (LUT)** (`export-invoice`) — buyer, country, currency, FX rate, IGST %, line items (desc/qty/unit), with-LUT toggle → foreign-currency total, INR equivalent, IGST (0 under LUT else inrTotal×gst%), total payable, FEMA/LUT clause text; warns if firm marked GST-unregistered. _Persist: none (reads `store.firm`)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **FIRC / BRC Tracker** (`firc-brc`) — invoice ref, currency, amount, export date, remit date → table with 9-month FEMA realise-by deadline, days left, FIRC + eBRC status toggles, breach highlighting. _Persist: `glb-firc-brc`._ _Class: KV._
- **Letter of Credit** (`lc-tracker`) — LC no., issuing bank, currency, amount, expiry → per-LC card with UCP 600 7-doc checklist (toggles), docs-done count, expiry countdown, all-ready confirmation. _Persist: `glb-lc`._ _Class: KV._
- **Customs Duty** (`customs`) — currency, FOB goods value, FX rate, freight, insurance, BCD %, IGST % → CIF assessable value, BCD, Social Welfare Surcharge (10% of BCD), IGST (on assessable+BCD+SWS), total duty, total landed cost. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Payment Fee Compare** (`payment-fees`) — currency, amount, mid-market rate, editable provider rows (name/flat fee ₹/FX markup %) → per-rail effective rate, FX cost, flat fee, all-in cost, you-receive; ranks cheapest, shows saving vs most expensive. _Persist: `glb-pay-rails` (provider list)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Transfer Pricing** (`transfer-pricing`) — method (cost-plus CPM / resale-minus RPM), base (cost or resale price), markup/margin % → arm's-length transfer price or AE cost, embedded profit; Rule 10B/10C + Form 3CEB note. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **GST Export Refund** (`gst-export`) — route (LUT/IGST); LUT: export turnover, total turnover, net ITC → Rule 89(4) unutilised-ITC refund (export/total × ITC); IGST: IGST paid → IGST refund claimable. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **RoDTEP / Drawback** (`rodtep`) — scheme (RoDTEP/Drawback), FOB value ₹, rate % per HSN, optional value cap → raw benefit (FOB×rate), capped benefit, scrip/drawback value. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **FX Forward Cover** (`fx-forward`) — currency, amount, spot rate, forward points, tenor months, direction (sell/buy) → forward rate (spot+points), value at spot, value locked at forward, annualised premium %, pick-up/cost vs spot. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **FCY Balances** (`bank-consolidate`) — bank/account, currency (INR+7), balance → Nostro-style table of each balance × reference rate, total consolidated INR, net position by currency. _Persist: `glb-fcy-balances` (rows); reads `glb-fx-rates`._ _Class: KV._
- **SWIFT vs UPI Intl** (`swift-upi`) — currency, amount, mid rate, SWIFT flat+spread, UPI/fintech flat+spread → per-rail FX cost, flat fee, all-in cost, you-receive; cheapest flag + saving line. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Packing Credit** (`packing-credit`) — drawdown ₹, rate % p.a., credit-period days, subvention % → gross interest (365-day simple), interest-equalisation saving, effective rate, net interest cost. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **EEFC Account** (`eefc-tracker`) — ref, currency, amount, credit date → held-by-currency chips, table with convert-by deadline (last day of next month), days left, converted toggle, breach highlight. _Persist: `glb-eefc`._ _Class: KV._
- **DTAA Withholding** (`dtaa-lookup`) — counterparty country (9: USA/UK/Singapore/UAE/Germany/Netherlands/Japan/Australia/France), income type (royalty/FTS/interest/dividend), payment ₹, gross-up toggle → hardcoded treaty rate, TDS to withhold (or grossed-up TDS deposited), net remittance; Form 15CA/CB, s.206AA notes. _Persist: none (`DTAA` const table)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **AA / EPCG** (`advance-auth`) — scheme (AA/EPCG), licence no., EO value, currency, EO-period-end date → per-licence card with fulfilled/obligation %, progress bar, days left, fulfilled-to-date input, met/defaulted/% status. _Persist: `glb-export-oblig`._ _Class: KV._
- **Country Sales** (`country-sales`) — reads live invoices; map each customer → country once → revenue grouped by destination market, invoice count, % share bars, total; untagged roll up as "Unmapped". _Persist: `glb-customer-country` (customer→country map); reads `store.invoices`._ _Class: Backend._
- **IEC / AD Code** (`iec-adcode`) — type (IEC/AD Code/Port reg./RCMC/LUT), number, issuer, renewal date → register table with renewal-status badges (expired / <30d to renew / valid). _Persist: `glb-iec-adcode`._ _Class: KV._
- **LRS / TCS** (`tcs-lrs`) — purpose (general/education-loan/education-other/medical), this remittance ₹, already-remitted-this-FY ₹ → cumulative LRS, ₹10L FY threshold, taxable portion above threshold, TCS rate (0.5/5/20%), TCS collected, total debited. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **SOFTEX Tracker** (`softex`) — invoice ref, client, currency, amount, invoice date → table with file-by deadline (invoice +30 days), filed/overdue/days-left status toggle, pending count. _Persist: `glb-softex`._ _Class: KV._
- **LUT Renewal** (`lut-renewal`) — LUT/ARN reference, FY filed for → derives current Indian FY (Apr–Mar), validity ends 31 Mar, days-to-expiry, covered/not-covered banner with RFD-11 reminder. _Persist: `glb-lut-no`, `glb-lut-fy`._ _Class: KV._
- **FEMA Calendar** (`fema-calendar`) — form (template picker: FLA/APR/FC-GPR/FC-TRS/Form ODI/ECB-2 with auto note), note, due date → date-sorted deadline list with done checkbox, overdue/days-left badges. _Persist: `glb-fema-cal`._ _Class: KV._
- **ODI / FDI** (`odi-fdi`) — kind (ODI out / FDI in), entity, currency, amount, event date → table with report-by deadline (event +30 days), reported/overdue/days-left status toggle. _Persist: `glb-odi-fdi`._ _Class: KV._
- **Country Risk** (`country-risk`) — country, then 1–5 sliders for payment / political / FX / legal → weighted composite (0.4/0.2/0.25/0.15), Low/Medium/High band per destination. _Persist: `glb-country-risk`._ _Class: KV._
- **GIFT City** (`gift-city`) — annual profit ₹, mainland tax %, holiday years, out-of block → annual mainland tax, ₹0 IFSC tax during holiday (Sec 80LA), holiday claimed, total headline tax saved over holiday. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Incoterms Split** (`incoterms`) — Incoterm 2020 (10: EXW…DDP), cost legs ₹ (origin / main freight / insurance / destination / import duty) → per-leg seller/buyer badge from `SELLER_BEARS` table, seller-bears total, buyer-bears total. _Persist: none (`SELLER_BEARS` const)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Multi-Currency P&L** (`mc-pnl`) — editable lines (label, revenue/cost, currency INR+7, amount) → each line × reference rate to INR, total revenue, total cost, net profit, margin %. _Persist: `glb-mc-pnl` (lines); reads `glb-fx-rates`._ _Class: KV._
- **WHT Recovery** (`wht-recovery`) — payer, country, currency, gross, WHT %, FX rate → per-row credit ₹ (gross×wht%×fx), total withheld abroad, FTC-yet-to-claim total, claimed toggle; Form 67 / FTC note. _Persist: `glb-wht-recovery`._ _Class: KV._
- **Realisation Aging** (`edpms-aging`) — shipping-bill ref, currency, amount, export date → ages open shipments into 0–90 / 91–180 / 181–270 / >270-day (FEMA breach) buckets with counts, table with age, realised toggle, breach highlight. _Persist: `glb-edpms-aging`._ _Class: KV._
- **Bank Charge Recon** (`bank-charge-recon`) — currency, invoiced amount, received amount, FX rate → charges deducted (foreign + %), INR value of charges, flagged if >3% with OUR-charges advice. _Persist: none (local state)._ _Class: Indicative._

## ━━━ PILLAR 8 · Trust, Data, Reach & Admin ━━━

# Privacy & Consent (`/privacy`) — 32 tools

A single control centre for DEPA/Account-Aggregator consents, DPDP Act 2023 compliance, data-rights tracking and breach response. Stakeholders: owner, CA, admin. Backend: `/consent` partial + KV (all 32 tabs persist via `useFeatureState` browser KV keyed `priv-*`; no server writes — Overview note points backend consent toggles / export / deletion to Settings → Privacy & Data).

- **Overview** — reads all `priv-*` KV stores (aa, dpdp, dsr, shares, breaches, hygiene) → derives 4 cards (active AA consents, DPDP consents logged + live, open DSRs, hygiene-weighted DPDP readiness %) and dynamic red/yellow alert banners (unnotified reportable breaches, AA consents expiring ≤30d, unsigned DPAs, open DSRs) with jump-to-tab links + static obligations panel → dashboard. _Persist: reads live KV stores (no own key)._ _Class: KV (read-only aggregation)._
- **AA Consent Register** — FIP, Account Aggregator, purpose, scope, validity months → records DEPA consent (status active, expiry = today + months×30); `useMemo` reconciles active→expired against live clock without mutating storage; revoke / re-grant / delete → register table. _Persist: `priv-aa-consents`._ _Class: KV._
- **DPDP Consent Log** — data subject, purpose, collection channel (select) → logs grant (granted=true) with collected-on date; mark-withdrawn sets withdrawnOn; counts total/granted/withdrawn → defensible consent register. _Persist: `priv-dpdp-log`._ _Class: KV._
- **Permissions Matrix** — party/app name → adds row with per-category access toggles (bank, gst, pan, invoices, payroll) for data-minimisation review → field-level access grid. _Persist: `priv-perms`._ _Class: KV._
- **Access / Erasure (Data-Rights Tracker)** — subject, request type (access/erasure/correction/portability), note → logs DSR with 30-day SLA clock; computes days-due vs raisedOn+30; status dropdown (open/in_progress/fulfilled/rejected); delete → request tracker with overdue flags. _Persist: `priv-dsr`._ _Class: KV._
- **Retention Policy** — category, retain years, legal/business basis → adds retention rule (seeded with 4 statutory defaults: books 8y, GST 6y, marketing 2y, logs 1y) → retention table ("Until purpose ends" if 0y). _Persist: `priv-retention` (default-seeded)._ _Class: KV._
- **Sharing Registry (Third-Party)** — recipient, data shared, purpose, DPA-signed checkbox → registers processor with shared-on date; toggle DPA signed/missing; counts + warns on unsigned DPAs → recipient registry. _Persist: `priv-shares`._ _Class: KV._
- **Consent Expiry (Calendar)** — reads AA register live → filters active/expired, computes days-to-expiry, sorts ascending → expiry timeline with past/urgent(≤7d)/soon(≤30d) colour pills. _Persist: reads `priv-aa-consents` (none own)._ _Class: KV (read-only)._
- **Privacy Hygiene** — 10 weighted DPDP-control checkboxes (high/medium/low) → toggles; computes done count + readiness % (feeds Overview) → checklist with progress bar. _Persist: `priv-hygiene`._ _Class: KV._
- **Data Inventory (Personal-Data Map)** — element, sensitivity (low/med/high), location, purpose → catalogues data asset (seeded 3 defaults); also surfaces live store counts (transactions/invoices/orders) + high-sensitivity count → inventory table. _Persist: `priv-inventory` (default-seeded); live counts from AppContext store._ _Class: KV + Backend (live store reads)._
- **Breach Log** — description, records affected, severity (low/med/high) → logs incident; toggle DPB-notified / subjects-notified; sums affected + indicative exposure (high-severity records × ₹5,000 hardcoded) + pending-DPB count → notification register. _Persist: `priv-breaches`._ _Class: KV (exposure = Indicative)._
- **Policy Generator** — firm name, email, officer + email, purposes, third-parties, retention statement → `buildPolicy()` assembles 8-section plain-English DPDP privacy notice; copy-to-clipboard → generated notice text. _Persist: `priv-policy-firm`._ _Class: KV._
- **Cookie Consent (Configurator)** — toggle analytics/marketing/preferences (necessary locked on), banner text, reject-all + granular checkboxes → builds banner config JSON; compliance check (rejectAll && granular); copy JSON → developer config + preview. _Persist: `priv-cookie-cfg`._ _Class: KV._
- **DPA Checklist** — vendor name + 11 DPA-clause checkboxes → toggles; computes coverage %; reset for next vendor → checklist with score. _Persist: `priv-dpa-checklist` (vendor name local-state only)._ _Class: KV._
- **Grievance Officer (Register)** — officer name/email/phone + per-grievance complainant/subject → logs grievance with 30-day SLA; status dropdown (open/in_progress/resolved); flags overdue → officer record + grievance register. _Persist: `priv-grievance-officer` + `priv-grievances`._ _Class: KV._
- **Consent Rate (Dashboard)** — reads DPDP log live → computes active-consent rate, totals, live/withdrawn, and per-channel rate breakdown → KPI cards + per-channel bars. _Persist: reads `priv-dpdp-log` (none own)._ _Class: KV (read-only)._
- **Classification (Matrix)** — element + tier (public/internal/confidential/sensitive) → classifies (auto-fills required-handling text from CLASS_HANDLING map; seeded 4 defaults); inline tier dropdown re-derives handling → classification table. _Persist: `priv-classification` (default-seeded)._ _Class: KV._
- **Staff Training (Log)** — employee, role, valid months → records training (expiry = trainedOn + months×30); renew resets date; counts trained/valid/needs-refresh → training register with valid/expired status. _Persist: `priv-training`._ _Class: KV._
- **Penalty Estimator** — toggle 5 DPDP penalty-schedule gaps (statutory caps ₹50cr–₹250cr) + likelihood % → sums headline max exposure and likelihood-weighted exposure → two exposure cards. _Persist: `priv-penalty-gaps` (likelihood local-state)._ _Class: KV (figures Indicative)._
- **Localization (Checklist)** — 8 data-localisation checkboxes → toggles; computes confirmed % → checklist with progress bar. _Persist: `priv-localization`._ _Class: KV._
- **Marketing Opt-in (Consent Register)** — contact, channel (Email/SMS/WhatsApp/Phone/Push), opted-in checkbox → records preference with updated-on; toggle opt-in/out (re-stamps date); counts in/out → marketing register. _Persist: `priv-marketing-consent`._ _Class: KV._
- **SAR SLA Timer** — reads DSR store live → filters open/in_progress, computes days vs raisedOn+30, sorts → KPI cards (open/due-in-7d/overdue) + per-request countdown bars with progress %. _Persist: reads `priv-dsr` (none own)._ _Class: KV (read-only)._
- **RoPA Register** — activity, data categories, subjects, lawful basis (select), recipients, retention → records processing activity; CSV export (RFC-escaped, downloaded as `ropa-<date>.csv`) → RoPA table. _Persist: `priv-ropa`._ _Class: KV._
- **Data-Flow Map** — source, target, leg type (internal/processor/external), data carried, encrypted checkbox → maps a data hop; warns on unencrypted external flows → flow list with kind/encryption pills. _Persist: `priv-dataflow`._ _Class: KV._
- **Consent Receipt (Generator)** — subject, purpose, data items, lawful basis (select) → generates timestamped consent receipt (ID `CR-<base36>`, fiduciary from live store firm name) listing DPDP rights; copy + download `.txt` → receipt preview. _Persist: none (in-memory `useState`; firm name from AppContext store)._ _Class: Backend (firm read) + Preview (no record kept)._
- **DPIA Wizard** — project name + 8 weighted screening questions → computes weighted score/14 and risk band (≥8 high / ≥4 medium / else low); save outcome to register; delete → screening tool + saved-outcomes register. _Persist: `priv-dpia` (answers + project local-state until saved)._ _Class: KV._
- **Cross-Border Log** — overseas recipient, country, data, safeguard (SCCs/intra-group/consent/none) → logs transfer with logged-on; warns on unsafeguarded ("None / under review") transfers → cross-border register. _Persist: `priv-xborder`._ _Class: KV._
- **Vendor Risk (Scorecard)** — vendor name + 6 per-vendor control checkboxes (DPA, localisation, ISO/SOC2, deletion/export, no recent breach, least-access) → scores each vendor x/6 → % and risk band (≥5 low / ≥3 medium / else high) → per-vendor scorecards. _Persist: `priv-vendor-risk`._ _Class: KV._
- **Withdrawal Handler** — reads DPDP log (live grants) + sharing registry → "Process withdrawal" flips the consent-log entry (granted=false, withdrawnOn) AND auto-generates one notify-task per registry processor; tick tasks done → withdrawal action + downstream propagation checklist. _Persist: writes `priv-dpdp-log`, reads `priv-shares`, writes `priv-withdraw-tasks`._ _Class: KV._
- **Breach Triage** — records-affected estimate + 4 factor toggles (sensitive, financial, identifiable, contained) → computes severity score/8 (record-volume tiers + factor weights, contained subtracts) → severity band (≥5 high / ≥3 medium / else low) + notification-duty advice. _Persist: none (all `useState`)._ _Class: Indicative (advisory; advises logging in Breach Log)._
- **Purge Scheduler** — category (datalist from retention policy), last-touched date → schedules purge (years pulled from matching retention rule); mark-purged; computes due-date (lastDataDate + years×365) and status (overdue/due-soon/within-retention/purged) → purge schedule table. _Persist: `priv-purge`, reads `priv-retention`._ _Class: KV._
- **Lawful Basis (Advisor)** — pick scenario (6 hardcoded: marketing/order/employment/legal/list-sharing/minor) → maps to recommended basis + tone (green/yellow/red) + note; copy basis note for RoPA (uses live firm name) → advisory card. _Persist: none (`useState`; firm name from AppContext store)._ _Class: Indicative (static decision aid)._
## Documents (`/documents`) — 23 tools

A central, tenant-scoped vault for every compliance document (GST certs, bank statements, legal, tax, payroll) plus 22 surrounding paperwork utilities — capture, sign, track expiry, validate, file and pack. Stakeholders: all (SMB owner + team), CA, ops. Backend: `/api/files` (multipart upload, list, auth-gated blob download, delete) + KV (`useFeatureState` per-tool stores).

The section selector (`docTab`) iterates a 23-entry tab array; each maps to one tool. Bullet per tool below.

- **Vault** — inputs: file upload (drag/drop or browse, `ACCEPT` whitelist, max 10 MB), name, category (gst/banking/legal/tax/payroll/other), comma tags, optional expiry date → uploads multipart to `POST /api/files`; lists via `GET /api/files` → `rowToDoc` (humanises size, derives status valid/expiring(≤30d)/expired/uploaded from `expires_at`); category-sidebar + name/tag search filter; KPIs (total docs, expiring-soon count, "encrypted in transit" badge); per-row preview (auth blob → `window.open`), download (auth blob → anchor), delete (`DELETE /api/files/:id`). → searchable document store with renewal warning banner. _Persist: backend (`/api/files` rows + stored bytes)._ _Class: **Backend** (real upload/list/download/delete, bearer-token auth)._

- **Receipt OCR Capture** (`#157`) — inputs: photo/PDF pick (`capture="environment"`), then confirmable vendor/amount/GST/date/category → `parseReceiptHints` regex over the **file name only** (largest rupee-number = total, GST/tax line, dd/mm/yyyy date, first wordy token = vendor); user verifies; `save` writes an expense record; KPIs sum count/total/GST → booked expense table. _Persist: KV `ocr-expenses`._ _Class: **Simulated** — NOT real OCR; field detection is a filename-hint heuristic, no image/PDF content read (disclaimer states "no real OCR backend", "best-effort hint only"). Flag: OCR is **preview/simulated**, not real._

- **e-Sign Workflow** (`#158`) — inputs: doc title, signer, email, method (Aadhaar e-Sign OTP / DSC / email click-to-sign) → `send` creates a doc at status `sent`; `advance` walks the manual `SIGN_FLOW` (draft→sent→viewed→signed), `decline` sets declined; KPIs (awaiting, signed, declined) → signature-tracking table. _Persist: KV `esign-docs`._ _Class: **Simulated** — statuses advanced manually by button, no ASP/eSign provider; disclaimer says "wire to NSDL/CDSL/eMudhra". Flag: e-sign is **preview/simulated**, not legally-timestamped._

- **Expiry / Renewal Vault** (`#159`) — inputs: name, type (License/Insurance/Contract/Certificate/Registration/Other), expiry date, owner/dept, alert-notice days (default 30) → computes days-to-expiry (`differenceInCalendarDays`), sorts ascending, tones each Expired/Due-soon(≤noticeDays)/Valid; banner counts expired + due-soon → renewal tracker table. _Persist: KV `expiry-items`._ _Class: **KV** (client compute; disclaimer notes alerts must be wired to Alerts page for actual WhatsApp/email)._

- **Bank Statement Parser** (`#160`) — input: pasted statement text → `parseStatement` line-by-line regex (detects date dd/mm/yyyy or ISO, narration, trailing decimal amount(s) with Cr/Dr tag or sign → debit/credit direction); KPIs (rows, total debits, total credits) → cleaned preview table. → parsed transaction rows for review (no import). _Persist: none (component `useState`, lost on tab change)._ _Class: **Preview/Indicative** — client-side regex parse only; disclaimer: "preview only, formats vary by bank"._

- **Audit Trail / Document Versioning** (`#161`) — inputs: document name, action (created/edited/viewed/shared/deleted/signed), actor, optional note → `log` records entry; version = max recorded version for that doc +1 (view/share keep version), append-prepend; filterable by document/actor → who-changed-what table with version + timestamp. _Persist: KV `audit-trail-entries`._ _Class: **KV** (append-only by convention; disclaimer: real tamper-evident logs need server-side hash chaining)._

- **Document Checklist by purpose** — inputs: pick pack (loan / new GST-reg / govt tender / vendor onboarding), tick each curated item done → progress %, 100% completion banner. _Persist: KV `doc-checklist-done` (keyed `packId::item`)._ _Class: **KV/Indicative** — curated static paperwork lists per Indian purpose; disclaimer notes lists are indicative._

- **Template Library** — inputs: pick boilerplate (vendor NOC / address-proof declaration / authorisation letter / payment-reminder), fill `{{placeholder}}` fields (date auto-inserted) → live string-replace preview, copy-to-clipboard. _Persist: none (field values in `useState`; selected template not persisted)._ _Class: **Indicative** — static boilerplate, disclaimer: "not a substitute for legal advice"._

- **Share-Link Tracker** — inputs: doc name, recipient/email, access (view/download), expiry days (default 7) → `create` generates a register row with a random `token` and `expiresAt`; `copyLink` copies an illustrative `share.headroom.app/d/<token>` URL; `revoke` flags revoked; state derived Revoked/Expired/Active; KPI of active links → share-register table. _Persist: KV `doc-share-links`._ _Class: **Simulated** — links are illustrative tokens with no server enforcement; disclaimer: "wire to a signed-URL service". Flag: share-link is **preview/simulated**._

- **KYC Collector** — inputs: counterparty name, kind (customer/vendor/employee) → per-party 7-doc checklist (PAN/Aadhaar/GST/bank-proof/address/photo/agreement) with received-toggle and progress count; "Complete" badge at full set. → onboarding KYC tracker. _Persist: KV `doc-kyc-parties`._ _Class: **KV** — collection tracker only; disclaimer: "not a verification service; validate PAN/GSTIN against registries, mask Aadhaar (DPDP)"._

- **Contract Key-Dates** — inputs: contract, kind (Renewal/Notice deadline/Payment milestone/Expiry/Review/Other), date, counterparty, note → days-to-event compute + sort, tones Passed/Soon(≤30d)/Upcoming, banner counts passed + within-30-days → urgency-sorted key-date table. _Persist: KV `doc-contract-keydates`._ _Class: **KV** (manual entry; disclaimer: verify notice-period clause wording)._

- **Bill Filing Tracker** — inputs: vendor, bill no., amount, bill date, where-filed location → `add` queues at `to-file`; `markFiled`/`unfile` toggle status with `filedAt`; To-file / Filed sub-tabs; KPIs (to-file count, pending value, filed count). → physical-filing register. _Persist: KV `doc-filing-items`._ _Class: **KV** (disclaimer: keep original tax invoice for ITC/assessment)._

- **Approval Flow** — inputs: document/request, approver, amount, raised-by, reason → `raise` creates pending request; `decide` approve/reject via `window.prompt` decision note + `decidedAt`; KPIs (pending, approved value, total) → maker-checker table. _Persist: KV `doc-approval-reqs`._ _Class: **KV** (lightweight internal trail; disclaimer: tie to authenticated user + DoA matrix for binding authorisation)._

- **GSTIN Validator / Register** — inputs: party label, 15-char GSTIN → `validateGstin`: regex layout check (2-digit state · 10-char PAN · entity digit · 'Z' · checksum) + `gstinCheckDigit` (weighted alternating 1/2, mod-36 over first 14 chars); derives state from `GST_STATE_CODES` and embedded PAN; live result banner; `save` adds pass/fail to register; KPI of valid count → checked-GSTIN register table. → offline structural + checksum validity (catches typos/fakes). _Persist: KV `doc-gstin-register`._ _Class: **Indicative** — real offline computation but **no registry call**; disclaimer: cannot confirm active status / legal-name match, verify on GST portal._

- **Document Request Tracker** — inputs: requested-from party, channel (Email/WhatsApp/Portal/In-person/Other), due-by date, multi-line/comma items list → `create` builds a request with per-item received-toggles; state Complete/Overdue(past due)/Awaiting; KPI of open requests → per-party request cards. _Persist: KV `doc-request-tracker`._ _Class: **KV** — tracker only, "doesn't send the request itself; use WhatsApp/Alerts to nudge"._

- **Naming Helper** — inputs: party/vendor, doc type (INV/RCPT/STMT/CONTRACT/GST/TDS/KYC/OTHER), doc date, amount; pattern choice (date-first / party-first / type-first) → `buildName` slugifies and joins tokens (date as YYYYMMDD, `rs<amount>`) → generated `<name>.pdf` filename, copy-to-clipboard. _Persist: KV `doc-naming-pattern` (chosen pattern only; field values are `useState`)._ _Class: **Indicative** — a convention generator, not a file renamer (disclaimer)._

- **Compliance Calendar** — inputs: filing name (e.g. GSTR-3B), category (GST/TDS/Income Tax/ROC-MCA/PF-ESI/Other), due date → per-task `docsReady` and `filed` toggles; days-to-due compute, sort filed-last then by urgency; tones Filed/Overdue/Due-soon(≤7d)/Upcoming; banner counts overdue + within-7-days → statutory-filing calendar table. _Persist: KV `doc-compliance-tasks`._ _Class: **KV** (manual due dates; disclaimer: confirm against CBIC/MCA circulars as deadlines shift)._

- **Document Bundles** — inputs: bundle title, reference/txn no.; then per-bundle add doc name + type (Invoice/Challan/Payment-proof/PO/GRN/Contract/Other) → groups linked records under one expandable bundle with per-doc remove; doc-count badge. → transaction document trail. _Persist: KV `doc-bundles`._ _Class: **KV** — links docs by name only; disclaimer: attach real vault files for a tamper-evident pack._

- **Storage Usage Summary** (`#175`) — inputs: category, file count, total size (MB) → merges into existing category line (sums count + MB); totals (storage GB/MB, files tracked); sorted bar breakdown with per-category % of total. → vault footprint breakdown. _Persist: KV `doc-storage-lines`._ _Class: **Indicative** — manually-entered figures, not live vault stats (disclaimer: "for live usage, read off the vault file list")._

- **Access Permission Matrix** (`#176`) — inputs: role/person name → `addRole` seeds a row across all 6 categories at `view`; clicking any cell cycles none→view→edit→manage; per-role remove. → intended-access policy grid. _Persist: KV `doc-access-rules`._ _Class: **KV/Indicative** — documents intent only; disclaimer: "enforce with role permissions in Settings"._

- **Watermark Note** (`#177`) — inputs: preset (CONFIDENTIAL / DRAFT / COPY / INTERNAL USE / ORIGINAL), optional doc name, optional recipient, include-issue-date toggle → composes a `·`-joined stamp string, live preview, copy-to-clipboard. → watermark/header text. _Persist: none (all `useState`)._ _Class: **Indicative/Preview** — generates text only, no real watermark applied (disclaimer: "apply as a real watermark in your PDF editor; does not encrypt")._

- **Statutory Pack** (`#178`) — inputs: pick pack (GST Registration / Annual ROC Filing / Business Loan KYC / Income Tax Return), tick collected docs → progress bar + done/total count + ready-to-submit banner at 100%. _Persist: KV `doc-statutory-collected` (keyed `packId::doc`)._ _Class: **KV/Indicative** — standard static checklists; disclaimer: requirements vary by state/entity/officer, confirm with CA._

Notes: Only **Vault** is truly backend-backed (`/api/files`). Receipt OCR, e-Sign and Share-Link tracker are explicitly **simulated/preview** (no real OCR engine, no eSign provider, no enforced signed URLs). Bank Statement Parser and Watermark Note hold no persistence (lost on tab switch). GSTIN Validator does real offline checksum math but makes no live registry call. All other tools persist to per-tool KV via `useFeatureState`.
# Data & Import (`/data`) — 20 tools

Bring SMB financials into Headroom fast (CSV/Tally/statement import), keep them clean (quality, dedupe, find-replace), and get them back out (backups, range/archive exports, format converters). Stakeholders: CA, finance, owner. Backend: client-only — live Zustand store (`useApp`) for transactions/invoices/accounts/loans, browser KV via `useFeatureState` for tool presets/logs, and Blob/`download*` for all exports. No server round-trips for any tool here.

Tabs (from the selector array, line 88): `overview`, `tally`, `mapper`, `consolidate`, `backup`, `quality`, `dedupe`, `replace`, `templates-store`, `filings`, `archive`, `profiler`, `csv-json`, `number-clean`, `gstin-check`, `pivot`, `statement-parse`, `range-export`, `paste-dupes`, `json-fmt`.

- **Overview** — renders the page header + 4 stat cards (transactions / invoices / bank accounts / active loans counts from live store) and 4 action cards: (1) **Bulk import transactions** opens `TransactionImportModal` (auto-detects date/amount/description, guesses categories, preview-then-commit) and prepends rows to live store, creating a `manual-import` account if none exists; (2) **Load sample data** runs `generateDemoData()` to replace firm/accounts/transactions/invoices/loans/obligations with FY23–FY28 demo set (confirm-gated); (3) **Clear all** wipes transactions/invoices/accounts/loans/obligations (confirm-gated); (4) **CSV templates** download static transactions/invoices template CSVs. All gated on `canEdit()`. _Persist: live store (import/demo/clear write transactions, invoices, bankAccounts, activeLoans, obligations); templates are static download._ _Class: Backend (live store mutation) + Preview (modal)._

- **Tally Bridge** (`TallyBridge`, #162) — Export: live `store.transactions` → generated Tally `<ENVELOPE>`/`IMPORTDATA` voucher XML (Receipt/Payment vouchers, party + "Bank Account" ledger double-entry, dates de-hyphenated, XML-escaped) downloaded as `.xml`. Import: pasted Tally voucher XML → `DOMParser` extracts DATE/NARRATION/VOUCHERTYPENAME + first non-bank/cash ledger AMOUNT, signs by voucher type (payment/purchase negative, receipt/sales positive, else raw sign), categorises revenue/expense → preview table (first 50) → commit prepends to live store. _Persist: live store on import; download on export._ _Class: Backend._ **Real bidirectional Tally import/export** (client-side XML generate + parse), not just a format template — though it warns to verify ledger mapping in Tally after import.

- **CSV Mapper** (`CsvMapper`, #163) — Paste arbitrary CSV → `splitCsvLine` parse → user maps each column to date/amount/description/counterparty/ignore via dropdowns; options for header row and "treat all amounts as expenses (negate)". Amount column required; builds Transaction rows (`toIsoDate` dates, numeric-cleaned amounts, revenue/expense by sign) → preview (first 50) → commit prepends to live store under `importAccountId`. _Persist: live store on commit._ _Class: Backend._ Real CSV mapper/importer.

- **Consolidation** (`MultiEntityConsolidation`, #164) — Maintain a list of group entities (name, ownership %, revenue/expense/cash); "Add this firm from live data" derives revenue/expense from live transaction signs and cash from bank balances. Computes gross totals, applies a single inter-company elimination figure to both revenue and expense, derives Group PBT, minority interest (Σ entity PBT × (1−ownership%)) and owners' profit. _Persist: KV `data-consolidation-entities` (entity list); elimination input is component state only._ _Class: KV + Indicative (AS-21-style; self-described as indicative, intra-group balances/unrealised profit not auto-detected, "verify with your CA")._

- **Backup & Export** (`ScheduledBackup`, #165) — One-click **Full backup (JSON)** = snapshot of firm + accounts + transactions + invoices + loans + obligations + featureData → download; **Transactions (CSV)** = escaped transactions CSV → download. Each run appends to a durable export log (capped 30). Cadence selector (off/daily/weekly/monthly) computes a due/overdue/up-to-date reminder from last log entry. _Persist: KV `data-backup-cadence` + `data-backup-log`; actual data export is download-only._ _Class: KV + Backend (reads live store) — but note the cadence is a reminder only, exports are manual (no real scheduling/server upload)._

- **Data Quality** (`DataQualityChecker`, #166) — Read-only scan of live transactions: counts missing description, missing counterparty, invalid/blank date, zero amount, future-dated, no bank account, and likely duplicates (date+amount+counterparty signature); derives a health score (100 − issues/total ×100) and clean-row count. Per-check table with OK/Review status. _Persist: none (memoised over `store.transactions`)._ _Class: Backend (reads live store, computed)._

- **Dedupe** (`TransactionDedupe`, #167) — Groups live transactions by date+amount+counterparty+description; reports duplicate group count and removable row count; "Remove" (confirm-gated) keeps first occurrence of each signature and rewrites `store.transactions`. Preview table of first 50 groups with copy counts. _Persist: live store on dedupe._ _Class: Backend._

- **Find & Replace** (`BulkFindReplace`, #168) — Pick field (counterparty/description), enter find/replace, optional whole-value match; case-insensitive substring (or whole) match against live transactions, live match count + preview (first 50), apply (confirm-gated) rewrites the field across matching rows via escaped regex `replace`. _Persist: live store on apply._ _Class: Backend._

- **Mapping Templates** (`MappingTemplateStore`, #169) — Save named column-order presets (name + comma-separated column string + timestamp); list/delete; "Export all (JSON)" downloads the preset list. Storage of reusable layouts (e.g. "HDFC current account"); note: presets are not auto-applied by the CSV Mapper — they're a manual reference store. _Persist: KV `data-mapping-templates`; export is download._ _Class: KV._

- **Filing Templates** (`FilingTemplates`, #170) — Four static, hardcoded CSV templates for Indian filings/bulk-loads: GSTR-1 B2B outward supplies, opening balances, fixed-asset register, vendor/counterparty master. Each downloads a correctly-headed sample CSV. _Persist: none (static download)._ _Class: Indicative (format templates only; self-described "confirm exact column requirements with the GST portal")._

- **Archive & Purge** (`ArchivePurge`, #171) — Choose a cut-off date; counts/values transactions older vs kept; "Download archive CSV" exports older rows (escaped CSV); "Purge" (confirm-gated, edit-only) removes pre-cutoff rows from `store.transactions`. _Persist: live store on purge; archive is download._ _Class: Backend._

- **Column Profiler** (`ColumnProfiler`, #172) — Paste any CSV → per-column fill rate, distinct count, type (numeric if ≥80% of filled cells parse as numbers, stripping ₹/commas), and for numeric columns count/sum/mean/median/min/max. Read-only, browser-only. _Persist: none (component state + memo)._ _Class: Indicative (pasted-data analysis, no store)._

- **CSV ↔ JSON** (`CsvJsonConverter`, #173) — Two-way converter: CSV → JSON array of objects (header-keyed), or JSON array of objects → flattened CSV (union of keys, nested values JSON-stringified). Error messages for malformed input; output textarea + download. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (pure in-browser format conversion of pasted data)._

- **Number Cleanup** (`NumberCleanup`, #174) — Paste a column of messy amounts; per-line parser handles ₹/$ symbols, thousands separators, bracket negatives, trailing CR/DR direction, and k/lakh/lac/crore suffix multipliers → signed numbers. Stat cards (parsed/unparseable/total/rows), per-row OK/Skipped table (first 100), download cleaned `original,cleaned` CSV. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (pasted-data transform)._

- **GSTIN Validator** (`GstinValidator`, #175) — Paste GSTINs (one per line); validates 15-char structure via regex, state code against an embedded `GST_STATES` map, embedded PAN segment, and the official mod-36 checksum digit (`gstinChecksum`). Valid/invalid/checked counts, per-row status+state+reason table (first 100), download report CSV. Offline — no portal call. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (structural + checksum only; self-described "does not confirm the GSTIN is active/registered")._

- **Pivot Builder** (`PivotBuilder`, #176) — Paste CSV with header → pick group-by column + numeric value column → grouped sum/count/average per group, sorted by |sum|, with grand total; downloadable pivot CSV. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (pasted-data analysis)._

- **Statement Parser** (`StatementParser`, #178) — Paste raw bank-statement text (one row per line, leading date required); heuristically sniffs per line: `toIsoDate` date, all money-looking numbers, Dr/Cr keyword direction, picks largest-magnitude amount (drops trailing running balance when ≥3 numbers), strips numbers/keywords for description/counterparty. Credit/debit totals + preview (first 50) → commit prepends Transactions to live store under `importAccountId`. _Persist: live store on commit._ _Class: Backend (import) — but a heuristic parser, self-flagged "verify amounts and Dr/Cr direction before importing."_

- **Date-Range Export** (`DateRangeExport`, #179) — From/to date window filters live transactions (inclusive, end-of-day); shows matched rows + net amount; exports the subset as escaped CSV. _Persist: none (reads live store; export is download)._ _Class: Backend (reads live store)._

- **Paste Dedupe** (`PasteDuplicateFinder`, #180) — Paste any list (one value/line, e.g. invoice numbers, GSTINs, emails); counts repeats (optional case-sensitivity), shows total/unique/duplicated stats + duplicates-with-counts table (first 100), exports de-duplicated unique list as CSV. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (pasted-data analysis)._

- **JSON Formatter** (`JsonFormatter`, #181) — Paste JSON → validate, pretty-print (2-space) or minify; error message on invalid JSON; copy to clipboard or download result. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative (pure in-browser format utility)._

## Notes
- Excluded UI filter/mode tokens (not standalone tools): `amount`/`daily`/`weekly`/`monthly` cadence selectors, `json2csv`/`csv2json` and `pretty`/`minify` mode toggles, map-field options, etc.
- Backend-class tools all mutate or read the **live Zustand store** (transactions etc.); there is no server/Django call from this page — "Backend" here means live app state, not a remote API.
- Tally Bridge and Statement Parser are the only two heuristic/lossy importers flagged for post-import verification; CSV Mapper is deterministic (user-driven column mapping).
## Connectors & Integrations (`/connectors`) — 24 tools

Connect bank, accounting, payment, and operational systems so Headroom can auto-sync transactions, settlements, and business data. Stakeholders: ops, owner. Backend: `/api/connectors` only mints a connector ID on add (best-effort POST, falls back to `generateId()`); everything else is local. NOTE: be honest — every connector here is simulated/preview, not a live integration. No real bank, gateway, AA, GSTN, ERP, POS, payroll, CRM, or courier API is ever called. The top-level connectors store persists via `AppContext` (localStorage + debounced backend save); all the `#166+` depth tools use `useFeatureState` (local `featureData` namespace only).

Page-level (top section, `PROVIDERS` catalogue + active list):

- **Provider Catalogue & Connect/Setup** — pick a provider (AA Network, Finbox, Tally ERP, Zoho Books, QuickBooks, Razorpay, Stripe, PhonePe Business); `handleConnect` → either adds a Tally `pending` connector directly (webhookNote path) or opens a setup modal collecting per-provider fields (account name, mobile, webhook secret) → `handleSave` calls `persistConnector` (POST `/api/connectors`, returns id) then `addConnector` with status `pending`. → A connector row appears in Active Connections. _Persist: backend ID + live store._ _Class: Backend (ID mint only; consent/data flow Simulated)._
- **Active Connections list (sync / disconnect)** — lists connectors from store; per-row `handleSync` (1.4s fake delay → sets `lastSync`=now, status `connected`, toast "Sync triggered") and `handleDisconnect` (confirm → `deleteConnector`) → updated/removed row. _Persist: live store._ _Class: Simulated (sync is a timeout, no data pulled)._
- **Connected summary cards** — counts of Connected / Pending / Total over the store connectors → 3 stat tiles. _Persist: derived from live store._ _Class: KV (derived)._
- **AA Network explainer** — static informational panel (consent-based / RBI-regulated / real-time) describing India's Account Aggregator framework → read-only marketing copy. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._

Depth tools (jump-nav `#166`–`#188` sections, each ends with an honest demo disclaimer):

- **Bank / UPI Feed Connector** (`#bank-upi-feed`) — inputs: Bank/FIP name (req), UPI handle → `startConsent` raises a `pending` AA consent; `approveConsent` flips to `active`; `syncNow` (1.2s delay) adds a random 8–47 simulated txns to a counter; `revoke`/`remove`. → feed list + Active/Total/Txns-pulled stats. _Persist: `connector-bank-upi-feeds`._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Payment-Gateway Reconciliation** (`#gateway-recon`) — inputs: pasted orders + gateway settlements (`reference, amount` per line) + ₹ amount tolerance → matches by reference (case-insensitive), classifies into matched / amount-mismatch / missing-settlement / orphan-payout, computes order vs settled totals and gap. → 4 stat tiles + mismatch table. _Persist: none (in-component `useState`, recompute via `useMemo`)._ _Class: Simulated (client-side math; no gateway call)._
- **E-commerce (Amazon / Flipkart) Sync** (`#ecom-sync`) — inputs: marketplace toggle (Amazon/Flipkart/Meesho) + pasted settlement CSV (`order_id, sku, qty, gross, fees`) → `parseEcomCsv` (optional header skip) computes net = gross − fees per row; `importBatch` saves a durable batch; remove batch. → gross/fees/net totals + batch list. _Persist: `connector-ecom-batches`._ _Class: Simulated (CSV import; no SP-API/Flipkart)._
- **Connector Health & Sync Monitor** (`#sync-monitor`) — operates over the live connectors store: derives health (healthy / stale >24h since sync / failing / pending); `simulateFailure` injects a random incident + sets status `error`; `retry` (1.1s) clears incident, sets `connected`+now. → 4 health-count tiles + per-connector rows. _Persist: `connector-sync-incidents` (incidents) + live store (status)._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Connector Catalog** (`#conn-catalog`) — browse static `CATALOG` (Shopify, WooCommerce, Cashfree, PayU, ICICI/HDFC Corporate API, Xero, SAP B1, Shiprocket, Google Sheets) with category filter; `connect` (0.9s) stores a connected-at flag; `disconnect` removes it. → catalogue grid with Connected badges. _Persist: `conn-catalog-connected`._ _Class: Simulated (flag only, no OAuth)._
- **Sync Schedule** (`#conn-schedule`) — per live connector: Auto-sync toggle + frequency (manual/hourly/daily/weekly) + hour-of-day; `nextRun` computes next fire time in-browser. → schedule rows with computed next-run. _Persist: `conn-schedules`._ _Class: Simulated (no background job runs)._
- **Field Mapping Studio** (`#conn-mapping`) — inputs: connector name + source field + target (one of 6 canonical Headroom fields: date/amount/description/counterparty/category/reference) → `addMapping`/`removeMapping`; flags unmapped canonical fields. → per-connector mapping list. _Persist: `conn-field-maps`._ _Class: Simulated (saved for reference; not applied to any import pipeline)._
- **Credential Vault** (`#conn-vault`) — inputs: connector + key name + secret → `add` stores masked credential; reveal/hide toggle, copy to clipboard, remove. → masked credential list. _Persist: `conn-credentials`._ _Class: Simulated (in-browser local state only; NOT encrypted/production-safe — explicit "never paste real live keys" warning)._
- **Webhook Registry** (`#conn-webhooks`) — inputs: label + URL (http(s) validated) + comma-separated events → `add` registers endpoint; `testPing` (1s) returns simulated 200 / 500 / timeout(0) at ~75% success; remove. → endpoint list with last status. _Persist: `conn-webhooks`._ _Class: Simulated (no real HTTP request)._
- **Sync History** (`#conn-history`) — input: connector select (live labels + Razorpay/Stripe/AA/Tally) → `record` appends a simulated run (success/partial/failed at ~60/20/20%, random record count), capped at 50; clear all. → timeline of runs + run/record totals. _Persist: `conn-sync-history`._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Tally / Busy ERP Agent** (`#conn-erp-agent`) — inputs: software (Tally Prime/ERP 9/Busy/Marg) + company + local port → `create` generates a `hr_agent_*` pairing token (copyable); `pair`/`heartbeat` (delay) set `paired`+lastBeat; push-vouchers / push-masters toggles; remove. → agent profile cards. _Persist: `conn-erp-agents`._ _Class: Simulated (token/heartbeat generated locally; no agent connects)._
- **GSTN Portal Connect** (`#conn-gstn`) — inputs: GSTIN (regex-validated 15-char) + legal name + data scopes (GSTR-1/2B/3B/e-Invoice/e-Way) → `sendOtp` (validation only) → `verify` (≥6-digit OTP) stores a session; disconnect. → connected session card with scope chips. _Persist: `conn-gstn-session`._ _Class: Simulated (GSTIN format validated; OTP + session faked, no GSP call)._
- **Integration Cost Estimator** (`#conn-cost`) — inputs: per-source enable toggles + monthly txn volumes over 5 illustrative sources (AA fetch, gateway sync, marketplace import, GSTN pull, Tally agent) → computes monthly = platform fee + (volume × per-txn) and annualised. → per-source cost rows + monthly/annual tiles. _Persist: `conn-cost-enabled`, `conn-cost-volumes`._ _Class: Indicative (placeholder pricing, not commercial rates)._
- **Data-Flow & Scope Audit** (`#conn-dataflow`) — static `DATA_FLOWS` map per connector (reads / writes-back / OAuth-style scopes); per-scope grant/revoke toggle (default granted); counts write-scopes + revoked. → reads/writes chips + togglable scope pills. _Persist: `conn-dataflow-grants`._ _Class: Indicative (reference map; toggles change no live permission)._
- **Sandbox vs Production** (`#conn-environment`) — per connector (live store, or 4 fallback defaults): toggle sandbox ↔ production; production requires a confirm dialog (warns about real money/data). → per-connector env switch + live/sandbox counts. _Persist: `conn-environments`._ _Class: Simulated (planning only; no endpoint actually switches)._
- **POS System Connector** (`#conn-pos`) — inputs: provider (Petpooja/PineLabs/Posist/Square/Zomato POS/Custom CSV) + outlet name → `connect`; `importNow` (1.1s) adds random ₹5k–65k simulated daily sales; pause/resume; remove. → outlet rows + total imported badge. _Persist: `conn-pos-links`._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Payroll Software Connector** (`#conn-payroll`) — inputs: provider (RazorpayX Payroll/Keka/GreytHR/Zoho Payroll/Quikchex/Manual) + API key (req, not stored, dedup per provider) → `connect`; `syncNow` (1.1s) generates random headcount (4–33) × random cost → monthly payroll cost; remove. → provider rows + total ₹/mo badge. _Persist: `conn-payroll-links`._ _Class: Simulated (key not stored; no payroll API)._
- **CRM Connector** (`#conn-crm`) — inputs: provider (Zoho/HubSpot/Salesforce/Freshsales/Pipedrive/LeadSquared) + workspace/domain → `connect`; `syncNow` (1.1s) generates random open-deal count (5–44) × value → pipeline value (feeds revenue forecast narrative); pause/resume; remove. → provider rows + total pipeline badge. _Persist: `conn-crm-links`._ _Class: Simulated (no CRM OAuth/API)._
- **Shipping / Logistics Connector** (`#conn-shipping`) — inputs: provider (Shiprocket/Delhivery/Blue Dart/DTDC/Ekart/XpressBees) + account/seller ID (dedup per provider) → `connect`; `syncNow` (1.1s) generates random in-transit shipments (0–119) and COD-pending amount; pause/resume; remove. → courier rows + total COD-owed badge. _Persist: `conn-shipping-links`._ _Class: Simulated (no logistics API)._
## B2B Network & Trade Graph (`/network`) — 32 tools

Link every buyer and supplier into one trade graph — confirm invoices both ways, reconcile ledgers, score counterparties, and discover GST-verified partners — all derived from data already in Headroom. Stakeholders: owner, ops, finance. Backend: KV-synced via `useFeatureState` (each tool persists to its own KV key); a live counterparty list is derived in-memory from `store.transactions` + `store.invoices` (inflow/outflow/txns/invoiced per name) and feeds the Overview and most tools' name pickers — never persisted.

The page is a 33-tab switcher (Overview + 32 numbered tools). Below, one bullet per rendered tool, in tab order.

- **Overview** — live counterparty list + KV reads (`net-directory`, `net-trade-refs`, `net-disputes`) → counts buyers (inflow/invoiced>0), suppliers (outflow>0), linked partners, confirmed refs, open disputes; renders 4 KPI cards, a 3-feature explainer, and a top-8 live-counterparty table (money in/out, invoiced, txns). _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: KV._
- **Buyer–Supplier Directory** — name, GSTIN (validated via 15-char `GSTIN_RE`), role (buyer/supplier/both), state; plus "import from books" chips that auto-assign role from inflow/outflow → editable directory with per-row link/unlink toggle (counterparty recon/relationship registry) and role filter → directory table. _Persist: `net-directory`._ _Class: KV._
- **Two-Sided Invoice Confirmation** — open (unpaid) invoices from store + per-invoice status (sent/confirmed/disputed) → counts open/confirmed/awaiting + confirmed value; per-row Confirm/Dispute/Reset (invoice confirmation, both ledgers agree) → status table + KPIs. _Persist: `net-invoice-confirm` (status map); invoices read-only._ _Class: KV._
- **Counterparty Reconciliation** — selected party (pulls their invoices+txns as "my ledger") + pasted statement text parsed `ref,amount` per line → exact-amount greedy matching, my/their totals + difference, flags "not in my books" / "not on their statement" (counterparty recon) → side-by-side match table. _Persist: none (in-memory; party + textarea)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Trade-Reference Book** — referring party, GSTIN, relationship months, avg days-to-pay, credit extended, note → request→confirm lifecycle, aggregates confirmed count, avg relationship, total vouched credit (credit-signal scorecard input) → reference table for loan/credit-limit applications. _Persist: `net-trade-refs`._ _Class: KV._
- **Network Credit-Signal Scorecard** — live graph + confirmed refs (`net-trade-refs`) + invoice overdue rate → diversity (inverted Herfindahl of buyer revenue share, 25%), counterparty depth (buyers×8+suppliers×6, 25%), confirmed-references (×25, 20%), on-time invoicing (1−overdue/total, 30%) → composite 0–100 + Strong/Moderate/Thin band with factor bars (credit-signal scorecard). _Persist: none (derived); reads live store + KV refs._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Supplier Discovery Shortlist** — supplier name, GSTIN (validated), category, state, ONDC flag, GST rating 0–5, min order, notes; search box → list sorted by GST rating desc, filtered by name/category/state (buyer-supplier directory / sourcing) → ranked prospect cards with star rating + ONDC badge. _Persist: `net-discovery`._ _Class: KV._
- **Dispute Log & Mediation Tracker** — party, invoice ref, disputed amount, reason (preset list), note → open→mediating→resolved lifecycle + per-dispute settlement proposal (shows concession vs original); aggregates open count, value at stake, resolved (counterparty recon / dispute) → dispute cards with age. _Persist: `net-disputes`._ _Class: KV._
- **Top-Counterparty Concentration** — live graph + buyers/suppliers toggle → per-counterparty value (inflow+invoiced or outflow), top-1 share, top-3 share, HHI; warns if top-1 > 30% (concentration) → ranked bars + warning banner. _Persist: none (derived from live store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Payment-Behaviour Rating** — store invoices grouped per customer → overdue rate + worst aging; score = 100 − overdueRate×60 − min(40, worstDays/3), graded A/B/C/D (payment-reliability scorecard) → private per-buyer grade table. _Persist: none (derived from invoices)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Shared-Balance Confirmation Generator** — selected party + as-on date + manual adjustment; firm details from store → open AR balance (unpaid invoices for party + adj), Dr/Cr direction → auto-drafted year-end balance-confirmation letter, copy-to-clipboard (counterparty recon / audit). _Persist: none (in-memory)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Partner Onboarding Checklist** — new party + GSTIN (validated) → runs each through a fixed 8-step KYB/verification/terms/ledger-linking checklist with per-step toggles and % complete bar (onboarding) → progress cards. _Persist: `net-onboarding`._ _Class: KV._
- **Trade-Terms Agreement Tracker** — party, credit days, credit limit, early-pay disc %, late penalty %/mo, effective-from, review-on, note → draft→agreed lifecycle, flags terms due for review within 14 days (trade-terms registry feeding aging/reminders) → terms table. _Persist: `net-trade-terms`._ _Class: KV._
- **Joint-Reconciliation Statement Export** — selected party + as-on date; pulls party invoices (debit) + txns (credit/debit by sign) from store → chronological statement of account with running balance, debit/credit/closing totals; export CSV + copy (counterparty recon) → statement table with running balance. _Persist: none (derived); party in-memory._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Counterparty Payment-History Timeline** — selected party → chronological feed of that party's invoices + payments (in/out/invoice) from store, with money-in/out, event count, relationship months since first seen (payment-history) → vertical timeline. _Persist: none (derived from store)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Network Referral Tracker** — referred-by, new party, expected value, reward type (discount/cash/credit-note/none), reward amount → pending→converted→rewarded lifecycle; aggregates pipeline value, conversion, rewards owed (referral/network growth) → referral table. _Persist: `net-referrals`._ _Class: KV._
- **Shared Price-List Manager** — SKU, item, unit, price, GST% → versioned catalog (version date bumps on add/remove); computes incl-GST price, catalog list value; export CSV with version header (price-list / dispute prevention) → item table + KPIs. _Persist: `net-price-list` (items) + `net-price-list-version`._ _Class: KV._
- **Partner SLA Scorecard** — partner, on-time %, quality %, responsiveness % (all clamped 0–100), orders → weighted score (delivery 40% + quality 40% + responsiveness 20%), graded A/B/C/D (supplier SLA scorecard) → per-partner scorecards with metric bars. _Persist: `net-sla`._ _Class: KV._
- **Group-Buy / Consortium Calculator** — solo price, group price, min-qty tier + pool members (name, qty) → checks if pooled qty clears tier, effective price, per-member solo vs group cost and savings, total pool saving (group-buy / volume tier) → member breakdown table. _Persist: none (in-memory; members seed with "My firm")._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Warm-Intro / Connection Requester** — connector (existing partner), target, reason/context, tone (formal/friendly); firm name from store → composes a ready-to-send intro-request message; copy-to-clipboard (intro/network growth) → message preview. _Persist: none (in-memory)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Partner Risk Watchlist** — party, risk level (low/medium/high), distress signal (preset list), action; exposure auto-pulled from party's open invoices → flags counterparties, inline risk re-grading; aggregates count, high-risk count, high-risk exposure (early-warning / counterparty risk) → watchlist cards. _Persist: `net-watchlist` (exposure snapshotted at add from live invoices)._ _Class: KV._
- **Networking Event / Meeting Log** — party, date, channel (call/video/in-person/event), summary, follow-up → CRM-style interaction log with follow-up done toggle; counts interactions + open follow-ups (relationship CRM) → meeting cards. _Persist: `net-meetings`._ _Class: KV._
- **Mutual-Credit Netting Calculator** — per partner: they-owe-me, I-owe-them → net position, gross vs net settlement, payments avoided, settlement reduction %, who-pays direction (mutual-credit netting) → netting table + KPIs. _Persist: `net-netting`._ _Class: KV._
- **Partner-Tier Scheme** — editable tier bands (Bronze/Silver/Gold/Platinum: min volume + perk, seeded with defaults) → assigns each live counterparty a tier by total trade value (inflow+outflow+invoiced), counts per tier (partner-tier loyalty scheme) → partners-by-tier table (top 20). _Persist: `net-tier-scheme` (band config); tiering derived from live store._ _Class: KV._
- **Payment-Terms Benchmark** — per partner: agreed days, actual days + a sector reference (days) → per-partner slippage (actual−agreed) and vs-sector gap; network avg agreed/actual/slippage, sorted worst-first (terms benchmark / renegotiation) → benchmark table. _Persist: `net-terms-bench` (entries); sector ref in-memory)._ _Class: KV._
- **Co-Marketing / Joint-Campaign Planner** — partner, title, channel (WhatsApp/Email/ONDC/etc.), my budget, their budget, date → planned→live→done cycle; totals my/their/combined committed spend (co-marketing / shared budgets) → campaign cards + KPIs. _Persist: `net-co-market`._ _Class: KV._
- **Introductions Ledger** — direction (given/received), connector, to-whom, note → outcome (pending/deal/dropped) per intro; computes given vs received reciprocity balance and deal count (intros given/received ledger) → ledger table. _Persist: `net-intros-ledger`._ _Class: KV._
- **Collaborative-Forecast Sharing** — supplier, month, forecast qty, last actual → % change vs last actual; totals forecasts shared, total forecast qty, suppliers covered (demand-forecast sharing / bullwhip) → forecast table. _Persist: `net-forecast-share`._ _Class: KV._
- **Partner NPS** — partner, score 0–10, comment → categorizes promoter (≥9)/passive(7–8)/detractor(≤6) and computes Net Promoter Score = (promoters−detractors)/n × 100 (partner satisfaction) → NPS KPIs + entry table. _Persist: `net-partner-nps`._ _Class: KV._
- **Partner Spend-Share Trend** — selected supplier; store outflow transactions grouped by month (last 12) → that supplier's share of monthly spend, latest share, month-on-month pp delta, latest spend (concentration / spend-share trend) → monthly share bars. _Persist: none (derived from store transactions)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Joint-Venture P&L Split** — JV partner, revenue, cost, my-share % (clamped 0–100), note → profit (revenue−cost), my P&L (profit×share), their P&L (remainder) per deal (JV P&L split / settlement) → split table. _Persist: `net-jv-split`._ _Class: KV._
- **Partner Payment-Reliability** — store invoices grouped per buyer → on-time % (paid / (paid+overdue)), avg days late, reliable flag (≥80% on-time and ≤7d avg late); sorted least-reliable first (payment-reliability) → reliability ranking table with bars. _Persist: none (derived from invoices)._ _Class: Indicative._
## Voice & Vernacular (`/voice`) — 27 tools

Run the books by speaking, hear summaries aloud, and tune the app for any of 22 scheduled Indian languages or for low-vision use — built entirely on the browser's own Web Speech / MediaRecorder engines, with a typed fallback everywhere. Stakeholders: owner, all. Backend: mixed — most tools are ephemeral `useState` previews; the persistent ones use `useFeatureState` (KV-synced to `store.featureData`, debounced to backend, propagated across devices). Several read the live `store` (transactions/invoices/firm) read-only.

NOTE — browser-support gating: speech **input** (`SpeechRecognition`/`webkitSpeechRecognition`) is feature-detected once into `SPEECH_IN`; speech **output** (`speechSynthesis`) into `SPEECH_OUT`; audio recording (`MediaRecorder`+`getUserMedia`) into per-tool `CAN_RECORD`. When the relevant API is missing the tool shows a `FallbackNote` banner, disables the mic/play button, and accepts typed input — parsing/formatting is identical without the browser API. Tools needing speech-in (dictation/parse): Voice Capture, Dictation Pad, Expense Logger, Spoken Invoice, Voice Search, Reminders, Voice-to-WhatsApp, Speak Total, Ask Balance, Spoken Date, Work Log. Tools needing speech-out (TTS): Read Aloud, Audio Statement, My Day, Amount in Words, Payment Reminder, Audio Glossary, Speak Total, Ask Balance, Spell It Out, plus the Language test/UI-preview/Greeter play buttons. Greeting Recorder needs `MediaRecorder`. Number-formatting/transliteration/cheatsheet tools need no browser API.

### Tab-selector array (27 buttons, in render order)

`VoicePage.tsx:120-153`. Tokens: `overview`, `capture`, `language`, `reader`, `cheatsheet`, `access`, `numbers`, `audiostmt`, `voiceauth`, `scratchpad`, `expense`, `invoice`, `txnsearch`, `uipreview`, `digest`, `reminder`, `words`, `translit`, `payreminder`, `whatsapp`, `glossary`, `calc`, `greeting`, `askbalance`, `spellout`, `spokendate`, `quicklog`. These are render-routing tokens (not UI filter tokens); all are documented below. `overview` is the default landing dashboard, counted as one tool → 27 total. Shared helpers used throughout: `speak()` (TTS), `formatIndianGrouping()`/`lakhCrore()` (vernacular number grouping), `LANGUAGES` (22-language BCP-47 table), `getRecognitionCtor()`.

### Tools

- **Overview** (`overview`) — inputs: none → behaviour: static dashboard of 6 capability cards (speech-in, TTS, 22 languages, accessibility, vernacular numbers, audio statements) each badged "Available"/"Fallback only" via live `SPEECH_IN`/`SPEECH_OUT` feature-detect; closing browser-support caveat. → output: read-only status grid. _Persist: none._ _Class: Preview._
- **Voice Capture** (`capture`) — inputs: spoken/typed one-line utterance e.g. "received 5000 from Sharma" (mic uses `en-IN`) → behaviour: `parseEntry()` extracts first number, infers direction (in/out via keyword regex), and party (after from/to/for/paid); reads live `store.transactions.length`. → output: non-posted draft txn card (direction/amount/party). _Persist: none (preview only — confirm in Transactions)._ _Class: Indicative._ Needs speech-in for mic.
- **Language** (`language`) — inputs: tap one of 22 languages; "Play sample" TTS button → behaviour: stores preferred language, passes matching BCP-47 to `speak()` for a test phrase. This key (`voice-language`) is read by ~14 other TTS tools to set locale. → output: selected language + locale label. _Persist: `voice-language` (KV)._ _Class: KV._ Sample play needs speech-out.
- **Read Aloud** (`reader`) — inputs: live `store.transactions`; speech-rate slider (0.5–1.5x) → behaviour: computes inflow/outflow/net, builds a spoken P&L script (lakh/crore worded), speaks it in the preferred-language locale; Stop cancels synth; script also shown for screen readers. → output: 3 summary cards + spoken/printed summary. _Persist: live store (read-only); rate is local `useState`._ _Class: Backend (reads synced txns)._ Needs speech-out.
- **Commands** (`cheatsheet`) — inputs: none → behaviour: static 3-group reference (capture / ask / navigate) of example phrasings the parsers understand. → output: read-only phrase cards. _Persist: none._ _Class: Preview._ No API needed.
