- **Accessibility** (`access`) — inputs: Large-text + High-contrast toggles → behaviour: each toggle drives an inline live-preview card style; notes app-wide rollout is "wired separately." → output: preference toggles + live preview. _Persist: `voice-a11y-large-text`, `voice-a11y-high-contrast` (KV)._ _Class: KV (preview only — not yet applied app-wide)._ No API needed.
- **Lakh / Crore** (`numbers`) — inputs: amount field + Indian/International toggle → behaviour: `formatIndianGrouping()` (12,34,567 vs 1,234,567) and `lakhCrore()`/million labels; static side-by-side sample table. → output: formatted number + grouping label + comparison table. _Persist: `voice-number-indian` (KV) for the system toggle._ _Class: KV._ No API needed.
- **Audio Statement** (`audiostmt`) — inputs: typed lines + 4 auto-suggested lines built from live `store.transactions` (count/revenue/expense/net in lakh-crore) → behaviour: build a multi-line narration list (add/delete), concatenate, speak whole script in preferred locale; Stop cancels. → output: editable line list + spoken/printed narration. _Persist: `voice-audio-statement` (KV); suggestions read live store._ _Class: KV._ Playback needs speech-out.
- **Voice Auth** (`voiceauth`) — inputs: typed/spoken passphrase (min 6 chars) → behaviour: `hashPhrase()` SHA-256 (subtle crypto, weak btoa fallback) one-way hash stored locally; explicitly a STUB — no real voiceprint biometrics, gates nothing; re-enroll/clear. → output: stored-hash preview. _Persist: `voice-auth-hash` (KV)._ _Class: Simulated (self-described stub; needs server-side voice-liveness model)._ No browser API.
- **Dictation Pad** (`scratchpad`) — inputs: continuous dictation (preferred-language locale, `continuous`+final results) or typed text → behaviour: appends final speech chunks to a free-text notes area; copy/clear; word count. → output: synced notes blob. _Persist: `voice-scratchpad` (KV)._ _Class: KV._ Dictation needs speech-in.
- **Expense Logger** (`expense`) — inputs: multi-item utterance e.g. "200 tea, 1500 diesel, 300 packing" (mic `en-IN`) → behaviour: `parseExpenses()` splits clauses on comma/and/semicolon, extracts amount + cleaned label per clause, auto-categorises via `EXPENSE_CATEGORIES` keyword map (Travel/Food/Utilities/Rent/Salaries/Supplies/Maintenance/Uncategorised); totals. → output: non-posted per-item draft list + total. _Persist: none (preview only — confirm in Transactions)._ _Class: Indicative._ Mic needs speech-in.
- **Spoken Invoice** (`invoice`) — inputs: utterance e.g. "invoice Sharma 5000 for consulting" (mic `en-IN`) → behaviour: `parseInvoice()` extracts customer (after invoice/bill), amount, description (after for); computes indicative 18% GST line. → output: non-posted invoice draft card (taxable/GST/total). _Persist: none (preview only — rate/HSN/place-of-supply are placeholders; finalise in Invoices)._ _Class: Indicative._ Mic needs speech-in.
- **Voice Search** (`txnsearch`) — inputs: spoken/typed query e.g. "cash sales above 5000" (mic `en-IN`); live `store.transactions` → behaviour: parses above/below amount filters, in/out direction keywords, and residual free-text terms matched against description/category; filters live txns (cap 50; recent 20 when empty). → output: matched transaction list. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Backend (reads synced txns)._ Mic needs speech-in.
- **UI Language** (`uipreview`) — inputs: tap one of 7 bundled languages (Hindi/Marathi/Tamil/Telugu/Bengali/Gujarati/Kannada) → behaviour: shows 6 core UI labels (`UI_STRINGS`) translated via hardcoded `UI_TRANSLATIONS` dict; per-label TTS button; notes other 22 languages "roll out as translation packs land"; defaults to the `voice-language` pref if translatable. → output: English→language label table. _Persist: reads `voice-language` (KV); preview lang is local `useState`._ _Class: Preview (bundled samples, not a live i18n switch)._ Label play needs speech-out.
- **My Day** (`digest`) — inputs: live `store.transactions`; system date → behaviour: filters today's txns by date, computes today in/out + overall balance, builds a time-of-day-greeted spoken digest (lakh/crore worded), speaks in preferred locale; Stop cancels; script printed for screen readers. → output: 3 today cards + spoken/printed digest. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Backend (reads synced txns)._ Needs speech-out.
- **Reminders** (`reminder`) — inputs: spoken/typed reminder text (mic `en-IN`) + date picker → behaviour: saves reminder objects (text/when/created), sorted by due date, delete; explicitly notes these are personal notes shown here only — they do NOT fire push notifications yet. → output: synced reminder list. _Persist: `voice-reminders` (KV)._ _Class: KV (Indicative on notification delivery — no push wired)._ Mic needs speech-in.
- **Amount in Words** (`words`) — inputs: amount field (decimal) → behaviour: `numberToIndianWords()` spells rupees+paise in the Indian crore/lakh/thousand system ("Rupees … only"), capitalised; copy + read-aloud in preferred locale. → output: cheque/invoice-style worded amount. _Persist: none (local `useState`); reads `voice-language` for TTS locale._ _Class: Indicative._ Read-aloud needs speech-out.
- **Transliterate** (`translit`) — inputs: Roman-letter note (e.g. "ramesh ko 5000 dena hai") → behaviour: `transliterate()` longest-first phonetic Roman→Devanagari map (`TRANSLIT_MAP` — leaves digits/whitespace; explicitly a rough phonetic approximation, not linguistically exact); 3 example chips; copy. (The single Devanagari characters अ इ क etc. are this transliteration map's targets, not separate keys.) → output: Devanagari preview. _Persist: none (local `useState`)._ _Class: Indicative._ No browser API.
- **Payment Reminder** (`payreminder`) — inputs: live `store.invoices` (overdue/pending chips auto-computing days overdue) + manual party/amount/days; tone selector (gentle/firm/festive `REMINDER_TEMPLATES`); reads `store.firm.name` → behaviour: builds a polite spoken collection script (amount in lakh/crore); read-aloud in preferred locale / Stop / copy; nothing sent automatically. → output: reminder script + audio/clipboard. _Persist: none (script local); reads live store._ _Class: Backend (reads synced invoices/firm)._ Read-aloud needs speech-out.
- **Voice to WhatsApp** (`whatsapp`) — inputs: optional recipient number + dictated/typed message (continuous dictation in preferred locale) → behaviour: appends final speech chunks; builds a `wa.me` deep link with URL-encoded text and opens it in a new tab — a hand-off draft, NO background/API send. → output: opens WhatsApp share link. _Persist: none (local `useState`)._ _Class: Indicative (deep-link hand-off, not an automated send)._ Dictation needs speech-in.
- **Audio Glossary** (`glossary`) — inputs: search box → behaviour: filters a hardcoded 10-term `GLOSSARY` (ITC/TDS/EBITDA/working capital/AR/AP/gross margin/cash flow/GSTR-2B/reconciliation) by term/plain text; expand-to-read; per-term TTS button using preferred locale (note: explanation text is English for now). → output: expandable plain-language definitions. _Persist: none (local `useState`); reads `voice-language` for TTS locale._ _Class: Preview._ Read-aloud needs speech-out.
- **Speak Total** (`calc`) — inputs: spoken/typed amount string e.g. "200 plus 1500 and 300" (mic `en-IN`) → behaviour: `parseAmounts()` extracts all numbers; sums them (or subtracts the rest if "minus/less/subtract/deduct" appears anywhere); speaks the total in lakh/crore in preferred locale. → output: amount chips + total + spoken total. _Persist: none (local `useState`)._ _Class: Indicative._ Mic needs speech-in; spoken total needs speech-out.
- **Greeting Recorder** (`greeting`) — inputs: tone template (welcome/thanks/diwali/newyear `GREETING_TEMPLATES`, interpolating `store.firm.name`) → behaviour: TTS "hear script" in preferred locale; `MediaRecorder` via `getUserMedia` records an on-device audio greeting (Blob URL), playable/downloadable (`greeting.webm`)/discardable; nothing uploads; cleans up streams/object-URLs on unmount. → output: recorded audio clip + download link. _Persist: none (in-memory Blob URL only); reads `store.firm`._ _Class: Indicative (local recording only — no send/upload)._ Recording needs `MediaRecorder` (`CAN_RECORD`); hear-script needs speech-out.
- **Ask Balance** (`askbalance`) — inputs: spoken/typed question e.g. "what is my balance" (mic `en-IN`, auto-speaks answer); live `store` → behaviour: `answerQuestion()` classifies query (balance/in/out/count/party) and computes from live transactions (party lookup matches counterparty/description); answers in lakh/crore and reads back aloud in preferred locale. → output: textual answer + spoken answer. _Persist: live store (read-only)._ _Class: Backend (reads synced txns)._ Mic needs speech-in; read-back needs speech-out.
- **Spell It Out** (`spellout`) — inputs: code/IFSC/GSTIN/account number (upper-cased) → behaviour: splits to chars, maps letters to `NATO` phonetics (digits read as-is via `phonetic()`); read aloud char-by-char at 0.85x in preferred locale; copy phonetic spelling. → output: per-char phonetic chips + audio/clipboard. _Persist: none (local `useState`)._ _Class: Indicative._ Read-aloud needs speech-out.
- **Spoken Date** (`spokendate`) — inputs: spoken/typed date phrase e.g. "yesterday", "3 days ago", "15 march", "15/03/2025" (mic `en-IN`) → behaviour: `parseSpokenDate()` resolves today/yesterday/tomorrow/N-days-ago/month-name/dd-mm[-yyyy] against the system date. → output: resolved long date + ISO + copy. _Persist: none (local `useState`)._ _Class: Indicative._ Mic needs speech-in.
- **Work Log** (`quicklog`) — inputs: spoken (single-shot, preferred locale) or typed activity entry → behaviour: auto-timestamps each entry (ISO `at`), newest-first list, per-row + clear-all delete. → output: synced timestamped diary. _Persist: `voice-work-log` (KV)._ _Class: KV._ Speak needs speech-in.
## Alerts (`/alerts`) — 23 tools
Centralises every cash, compliance, operational and risk warning into one configurable alerting hub. Stakeholders: all. Backend: `/api/alerts` + KV.

- **Active Alerts** — unread alerts from `store.alerts` → sorted by severity (critical→high→medium→low), grouped into Critical/High/Warning/Info sections; each card lets you log a free-text action note + Resolve (moves to history with the note) or Dismiss (mark read) → triaged live alert feed. _Persist: live store (`/api/alerts`)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Resolved (History)** — read alerts from `store.alerts` → lists dismissed/resolved items at reduced opacity, shows `actionTaken` note, per-item delete → audit trail of cleared alerts. _Persist: live store (`/api/alerts`)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Safety-Buffer Configure** — `safetyThresholdDays` (slider 7–60, save validates 1–180) → buffer = (monthlyBurn/30) × days, shows ₹ buffer + severity-channel legend (critical=in-app+email+WhatsApp, warning=in-app+email, info=in-app) → persisted threshold via `updateFirm`. _Persist: live store (firm)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Smart Threshold Alert Builder** — metric (balance/burn/runway/revenue30/expense30) + condition (below/above) + value → live metric computed from store (balance=Σ bankAccounts, burn=monthlyBurn, runway=balance÷daily-burn, revenue30/expense30 from 30-day category sums); each rule evaluated live, breach shows Breached/OK + a "rules breached now" banner → user-defined metric watch. _Persist: `alert-threshold-rules`._ _Class: KV._
- **Compliance Due-Date Alerts** — obligation name + next due date + recurrence (once/monthly/quarterly/annual), plus 8 statutory presets (GSTR-3B/-1, TDS pay, PF&ESI, TDS return, advance tax, ROC AOC-4/MGT-7, ITR) → days-to-due banded (Overdue/≤3d/this-week/Upcoming/Scheduled), "Filed → next" rolls recurring date forward by cycle, urgent counter for ≤7-day items → escalating statutory deadline reminders. _Persist: `compliance-due-items`._ _Class: KV._
- **Cash-Low / Overdraft Alert** — optional minimum-balance floor (₹) → from live balances/burn computes total balance, runway, overdrawn accounts, days-to-floor; emits critical/high/medium warnings (overdrawn acct, negative aggregate, runway ≤14/≤30, below floor, days-to-floor ≤30) + per-account balance table → proactive liquidity warning. _Persist: `liquidity-min-floor`._ _Class: Backend (live store data) + KV (floor input)._
- **Fraud / Anomaly Alerts** — outlier sensitivity σ slider (1.5–4) → heuristics over non-transfer txns: outsized payments (>mean+σ·sd), new payees (counterparty first-seen ≤30d), round-trips (party with both in+out flow), manually-flagged txns; lists all findings as a review queue → anomaly review feed. _Persist: none (σ is local `useState`)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Snooze / Mute Alerts** — alert type (from distinct live `store.alerts` types) + mute window (1h/4h/8h/1d/3d/1wk) → adds time-boxed mute rule, computes how many live unread alerts are suppressed, lists active mutes with remaining time + Unmute; auto-expires, never deletes the alert → category notification suppression. _Persist: `alr-mute-rules`._ _Class: KV._
- **Alert Digest Scheduler** — enable toggle + frequency (daily/weekly) + delivery hour (0–23) + channel (email/WhatsApp/both) → live "next digest" preview of unread alerts grouped by severity (first 6 + overflow count); Save shows scheduled/disabled toast → periodic roll-up config + preview. _Persist: `alr-digest-enabled`/`-freq`/`-hour`/`-channel`; preview from live store._ _Class: Preview (settings persist to KV; no actual scheduled send wired)._
- **Escalation Rules** — severity tier (critical/high/medium/low) + recipient (name/phone/email) + channel (WhatsApp/email/call) → per-tier recipient list with live "N active now" count per severity from `store.alerts`; tiers with no recipient fall back to in-app only → severity-based routing config. _Persist: `alr-escalation-rules`; live counts from store._ _Class: KV (routing not actually dispatched)._
- **Overdue Receivables Alerts** — optional overdue-AR ceiling (₹) → ages open `store.invoices` into buckets (current/1–30/31–60/61–90/90+), totals overdue, flags when total exceeds ceiling, lists overdue invoices sorted by days → AR aging + drift alert. _Persist: `alr-recv-threshold`; aging from live invoices._ _Class: Backend (live store) + KV (threshold)._
- **Payment-Due Alerts** — look-ahead window (days, default 14) → from `store.obligations` shows overdue + upcoming within horizon (loan/tax/payroll/other), totals due, overdue count, colour-bands each by days-to-due → upcoming-outflow pre-funding view. _Persist: `alr-payable-window`; obligations from live store._ _Class: Backend (live store) + KV (window)._
- **KPI Target Alerts** — KPI (revenue30/gross-margin%/expense-ratio%/recurring-revenue%) + goal (atLeast/atMost) + value → live KPI from rolling 30-day txns; each target shows Missing/On-track + an "off track" summary list → operating-KPI pass/miss monitor. _Persist: `alr-kpi-targets`; KPIs from live store._ _Class: Backend (live store) + KV (targets)._
- **Low-Stock Alerts** — safety multiplier slider (1–3×) on reorder level → over `store.inventory` computes trigger=reorderLevel×buffer, flags out-of-stock + low, estimates re-order cost (shortfall×unitCost); summary cards + flagged SKU table → inventory replenishment alert. _Persist: `alr-stock-buffer`; inventory from live store._ _Class: Backend (live store) + KV (multiplier)._
- **Large-Transaction Alerts** — amount threshold (₹, default 100000) + direction (out/in/either) → filters non-transfer txns ≥ threshold in direction, sorts by abs amount, caps at top 50; lists matches with +/− amounts → fixed-rupee large-movement flag. _Persist: `alr-largetxn-amount`/`-dir`; txns from live store._ _Class: Backend (live store) + KV (settings)._
- **Budget-Overrun Alerts** — warn-% slider (50–100, default 80) → compares 30-day outflow per category against `store.budgets` monthly limits, flags over-limit + approaching (≥warn%), per-budget progress bar + over-by amount → category budget overrun monitor. _Persist: `alr-budget-warn`; budgets/txns from live store._ _Class: Backend (live store) + KV (warn%)._
- **Customer Concentration Alerts** — concentration ceiling slider (10–80%, default 30) → over `store.invoices` computes each customer's share of open AR + all-time invoiced, flags any above ceiling, lists top 20 → receivables concentration-risk warning. _Persist: `alr-concentration-pct`; invoices from live store._ _Class: Backend (live store) + KV (ceiling)._
- **Priority Inbox** — severity filter (all/critical/high/medium/low) → triage queue of live unread alerts ordered by severity then recency, per-item dismiss, "Clear all <tier>" bulk mark-read → fast morning-sweep triage. _Persist: live store (`markAlertRead`)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Licence Expiry Alerts** — licence name + expiry date, plus 10 presets (trade licence, FSSAI, Shops & Est., Fire NOC, prof. tax, GST reg., ISO, insurance, domain/SSL, IEC) → days-to-expiry banded (Expired/≤15d/Renew-soon/Valid), urgent counter for ≤45-day items → fixed-expiry registration tracker. _Persist: `alr-licence-items`._ _Class: KV._
- **EMI Coverage Alerts** — coverage window (days, default 30) → sums loan-type `store.obligations` due in window, compares to live bank balance, computes balance-after-EMIs, flags shortfall; balance/EMIs-due/after cards + EMI list → instalment funding-shortfall check. _Persist: `alr-emicover-window`; balance/obligations from live store._ _Class: Backend (live store) + KV (window)._
- **Recurring-Spend Watch** — optional monthly ceiling (₹) → from recurring expense/payroll txns takes latest charge per counterparty, totals committed monthly + annualised, flags creep above ceiling; lists each recurring charge → subscription/commitment creep watch. _Persist: `alr-recurring-ceiling`; txns from live store._ _Class: Backend (live store) + KV (ceiling)._
- **Tax Set-Aside Alerts** — effective tax-rate slider (0–40%, default 25) → over last 90-day txns computes profit (revenue−expenses), provision=profit×rate, nets off tax-category paid, derives still-owed; warns if still-owed > live bank balance → tax-reserve provisioning aid. _Persist: `alr-taxsetaside-rate`; txns/balance from live store._ _Class: Indicative (rough provisioning estimate)._
## Settings (`/settings`) — 30 cards/tools

Owner/super-admin-only workspace configuration: plan, security, team roles, document/invoice/tax defaults, locale, integrations. Stakeholders: owner, finance, admin. Backend: `/api/users`, `/api/account/*`, `/api/whatsapp/*`, `/api/push/test`, firm fields on AppStore, plus the synced `store.featureData` KV bag (localStorage + debounced backend sync via `useFeatureState`). Gated to `super_admin`/`owner`; everyone else redirects to `/dashboard`.

- **Plan & Billing** (`BillingCard`) — shows current plan tier and lets you upgrade to Growth (₹999/$39) or Pro (₹2,999/$99); region currency switches INR/USD, downgrades show "included," warns if Razorpay keys unset → live Razorpay checkout (UPI/cards/netbanking) when configured. _Persist: Backend (`/api/billing`, plan on user)._ _Class: Backend (Razorpay-gated; Simulated if `RAZORPAY_*` env unset)._
- **App Lock** (`AppLockCard`) — set/clear a 4-digit launch+resume PIN, plus optional Face ID/fingerprint fast-unlock on supported devices → app requires PIN/biometric to open. _Persist: live store (`lib/appLock`, `lib/biometric`; device-local)._ _Class: Backend (device-local secure store)._
- **Push Notifications** (`NotificationsCard`) — "Send test" fires a test push to every registered device in the tenant → confirms FCM delivery; warns if no devices or `FCM_SERVER_KEY` unset. _Persist: none (action only; tokens registered on native launch)._ _Class: Backend (FCM-gated; mock response if key unset)._
- **Privacy & Data** (`PrivacyCard`) — DPDP consent ledger toggles (essential/marketing/lending_partners/analytics; essential locked), JSON data export (right-to-access), and password-confirmed account-deletion request (right-to-erasure); lists grievance officer email. _Persist: Backend (`/api/account/consent`, `/api/account/export`, `/api/account/deletion-request`)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Role & Permission Matrix** (#170) — tick what each team type (Finance/CA/Sales/Ops) may do across 6 perms (view cash, edit txn, approve pay, manage invoices, view reports, manage team) → a grant table, per-role granted count shown. _Persist: KV (`settings-permission-matrix`)._ _Class: Indicative (grant table not enforced in app logic)._
- **Approval Policy** (#171) — add maker-checker rules: payments above a ₹ threshold require approval from a chosen role, with optional note; rules sorted by threshold, deletable → defines sign-off requirements. _Persist: KV (`settings-approval-rules`)._ _Class: Indicative (rules stored, not enforced on payments)._
- **Financial Year & Books Lock** (#172) — pick FY start month (default March/April-March) and lock books up to a date (or unlock) → entries on/before lock date treated as filed/closed. _Persist: KV (`settings-books-lock`)._ _Class: Indicative (lock advisory; not enforced on edits)._
- **Audit Log & Login History** (#173) — read-only list of recent sign-ins and security-relevant changes (login/permission/lock/policy), prepends current session; falls back to session only when empty. _Persist: KV read-only (`settings-audit-log`); current session synthesized in-memory._ _Class: Indicative (no events written by app yet)._
- **Invoice Defaults** (#174) — set invoice prefix, next number, payment terms (0–90 days), and default footer note; live preview of next number → pre-fills new invoices. _Persist: KV (`set-invoice-defaults`)._ _Class: Indicative (defaults stored; consumption by invoice flow not wired)._
- **Currency & Locale** (#175) — choose number grouping (Indian lakh/crore vs international), decimal places (0/2), and ₹ symbol position; live sample. _Persist: KV (`set-currency-locale`)._ _Class: Preview (sample only; `formatCurrency` not driven by it)._
- **Document Branding** (#176) — logo URL (with live image preview + load-error guard), authorised signatory, and footer line → stamped on invoices/statements/PDF exports. _Persist: KV (`set-document-branding`)._ _Class: Indicative (stored; PDF stamping not wired)._
- **Payment Reminder Cadence** (#177) — toggle auto-reminders; set days-before-due, days-after-due, on-due flag, and channel (WhatsApp/email/both) → default schedule for chasing new receivables. _Persist: KV (`set-reminder-cadence`)._ _Class: Indicative (schedule stored; not driving sends)._
- **Number Format & Rounding** (#178) — rounding rule (none/nearest/up/down), round-to step (₹1/5/10), and display unit (full/thousands/lakhs); live example. _Persist: KV (`set-number-rounding`)._ _Class: Preview (example only; totals not rounded by it)._
- **Theme & Density** (#179) — appearance (system/dark/light), layout density (comfortable/compact), and reduce-motion toggle → visual prefs meant to follow user across devices. _Persist: KV (`set-theme-density`)._ _Class: Indicative (stored; not applied to live theme/density)._
- **E-Invoice & E-Way Bill Defaults** (#180) — toggle IRN/e-invoice for B2B, toggle e-way bill with ₹ threshold + transport mode (road/rail/air/ship), and auto-generate-on-save flag → behaviour for GST docs. _Persist: KV (`set-einvoice-defaults`)._ _Class: Indicative (no IRP/e-way integration)._
- **Bank Account Defaults** (#181) — add settlement accounts (nickname, bank, masked last-4, IFSC), set/remove primary → primary printed on invoices and pre-selected for payouts. _Persist: KV (`set-bank-accounts`, `set-bank-primary`)._ _Class: Indicative (stored; not consumed by invoice/payout flows)._
- **Email & SMS Sender Identity** (#182) — set from-name (defaults to firm name), reply-to email, 6-letter DLT SMS sender ID (sanitised), and cc-self toggle; live preview → applied to outgoing invoices/reminders/statements. _Persist: KV (`set-sender-identity`)._ _Class: Indicative (stored; no live mail/SMS sender wiring)._
- **Data Retention** (#183) — keep-records window (3/5/8/10 yrs; statutory 8-yr note), include-attachments-in-archive, warn-before-purge toggles; shows archival cutoff date. _Persist: KV (`set-data-retention`)._ _Class: Indicative (no auto-archival job)._
- **Dashboard Default & Export Format** (#184) — default landing view (overview/cashflow/receivables/alerts), default date range (30d/90d/FY/all), and export format (PDF/xlsx/CSV); live summary. _Persist: KV (`set-workspace-defaults`)._ _Class: Indicative (stored; not driving landing/export)._
- **Late-Fee & Overdue Interest** (#185) — toggle late fees; pick type (%/month, %/annum, flat), rate or flat amount, and grace days; example charge on ₹1,00,000. _Persist: KV (`set-late-fee-policy`)._ _Class: Indicative (policy stored; not applied to overdue invoices)._
- **Tax-Code Defaults (HSN/SAC & TDS)** (#186) — default HSN/SAC code (numeric, ≤8 digits) and item type (goods/services); toggle TDS with section (194C/J/H/Q) and rate → pre-fills line items / deductions. _Persist: KV (`set-tax-code-defaults`)._ _Class: Indicative (defaults stored; not consumed by invoice/payment flow)._
- **Locations & Branches** (#187) — add places of supply (name, city/state, optional GSTIN), set/remove primary → invoices and reports taggable per branch GSTIN. _Persist: KV (`set-locations`, `set-locations-primary`)._ _Class: Indicative (stored; tagging not wired)._
- **Customer-Statement Template** (#188) — send frequency (weekly/fortnightly/monthly), show-opening-balance and include-ageing toggles, intro line and sign-off; live summary → composes periodic customer statements. _Persist: KV (`set-statement-template`)._ _Class: Indicative (template stored; statement send not wired)._
- **Your Team** (`/api/users`) — list members with role badges; invite by email + assignable role (with live role-scope preview), change member role inline, remove member; emails a temp password on invite. _Persist: Backend (`/api/users` GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Stakeholder Views & Permissions** — "Preview as" any assignable role (navigates to that role's landing with an exit banner) and per-role page-access matrix (tick TAB_CATALOG pages by group, reset-to-default) → controls which tabs each role can open. _Persist: live store (`setPreviewRole`, `roleTabs`/`setRoleTabs`/`resetRole` via AppContext)._ _Class: Backend (role-tab config drives nav gating)._
- **Business Profile** — edit business name, industry (select), and runway safety-threshold slider (7–60 days) → used in credit underwriting and advisor reports. _Persist: Backend (`updateFirm`: name/industry/safetyThresholdDays on firm)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Your Tenant ID** — read-only tenant ID with copy-to-clipboard → shared with CA/CFO/banker to link account into their Advisor Portal "My Clients." _Persist: none (display of `tenant_id` from `/api/users`)._ _Class: Backend (read-only identifier)._
- **GST Settings** — toggle GST-registered; set GSTIN (≤15 chars) and primary output rate (0/5/12/18/28%) → estimates monthly GSTR-3B output tax (revenue × rate) in tax calendar/forecast. _Persist: Backend (`updateFirm`: gstRegistered/gstNumber/gstRate)._ _Class: Backend._
- **WhatsApp Alerts** — connect a phone (with country code) for a 7am cash digest and NL queries, or disconnect; shows connected number and command reference (cash/runway/alerts/invoices). _Persist: Backend (`/api/whatsapp/status`, `/register` POST/DELETE)._ _Class: Backend (Twilio WhatsApp API; needs `TWILIO_*` env)._
- **What each role can do** — read-only reference grid of every assignable role with badge, read-only flag, blurb and scope bullets → helps owner pick the right role. _Persist: none (static `ASSIGNABLE_ROLES` reference)._ _Class: Preview (informational)._
## Platform Admin (`/admin`) — 21 tools

Super-admin console giving platform-wide visibility and control across every tenant/company on the platform. Stakeholders: super_admin (guarded by `canAccess("admin")`, redirects to `/` otherwise). Backend: `/api/admin/*` (stats/companies/users-reset) + `/api/users` (CRUD/role) on `BASE`, plus `useFeatureState` KV (device-synced) for admin-config tools, and the live `useApp().store` for activity-derived views.

The console is a 21-tab array (`TABS`). Each tab below is one tool.

- **Platform Overview** (`overview`) — no inputs → renders 6 aggregate stat cards (Companies, Total Users, Aggregate Cash, Aggregate Revenue, Open Receivables, Transactions) and a "Users by role" distribution from `byRole`, sorted desc → platform-wide totals. _Persist: none (read-only)._ _Class: Backend._ Hits real `GET /api/admin/stats`.

- **Companies** (`companies`) — search box (company/owner/tenant) → loads company roster with per-tenant cash/revenue/receivables/txns/user_count; **Inspect** button calls `setSelectedClient(tenant_id, name)` and navigates to `/dashboard` to impersonate/edit that company → sortable table. _Persist: live (impersonation writes save to that company)._ _Class: Backend._ Hits real `GET /api/admin/companies`; Inspect mutates app context.

- **Users** (`users`) — search (email/tenant/role) + **Create user** form (email, role from `ROLE_META`, optional tenant_id); per-row inline role `<select>` (changeRole), **reset password** (KeyRound, reveals temp password once), **delete** (Trash2, confirm) → user roster table with status (Pending login / Active). _Persist: live store._ _Class: Backend._ Hits real `GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE /api/users`, `POST /api/admin/users/:id/reset`.

- **CA Workspace** (`ca-workspace`, #174) — invite form (name*, email*, firm, role: ca/advisor/bookkeeper) → adds advisor (status "invited"), per-advisor toggle client-tenant access chips (from real companies list), Mark active, Remove (confirm) → advisor cards + 4 stat cards (Advisors/Active/Pending/Client Mappings). _Persist: KV `admin-ca-advisors`._ _Class: KV._ Reads companies via `/api/admin/companies`; advisor records are KV-only, not real auth grants.

- **Usage Analytics** (`usage`, #175) → 4 stat cards (Active 7d/30d via `last_activity`, Dormant, Total Txns), "Most Engaged Companies" ranked bar chart (score = txns + users×5, top 8), "Platform Adoption Signals" bars → engagement view. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend (derived)._ Hits real `GET /api/admin/stats` and `/api/admin/companies`; rankings/scores computed client-side.

- **Data Retention** (`retention`, #176) — 4 numeric inputs (transaction years, invoice years, audit-log days, inactive-user days) + 4 toggles (auto-purge, encrypt-at-rest, consent tracking, data localisation) + DPO email; **Save & mark reviewed** stamps `lastReviewed`; emits IT-Act/GST/DPDP compliance warnings → policy form. _Persist: KV `admin-retention-policy`._ _Class: Indicative._ No backend; controls are advisory ("Confirm with your CA before enabling auto-purge").

- **Feature Flags** (`flags`, #177) — per-flag enable/disable toggle + rollout % slider (0–100) for 6 seeded flags (ai-copilot, whatsapp-bot, aa-underwriting, b2b-bnpl, tally-plugin, multi-currency) → 4 stat cards (Total/Enabled/Disabled/Avg Rollout) + flag list. _Persist: KV `adm-feature-flags`._ _Class: KV._ No backend; flags stored in KV only, not enforced server-side.

- **Announcements** (`announce`, #178) — compose form (title, message, severity: info/warning/critical) → publishes platform banner (active), per-item Unpublish/Republish + Delete → announcement cards. _Persist: KV `adm-announcements`._ _Class: KV._ "Published to all tenants" is KV-local; no broadcast backend.

- **Audit Log** (`audit-log`, #179) — filter (All/Transactions/Invoices) → reconstructs financial-event log from `store.transactions` + `store.invoices`, newest-first, max 200 → table (When/Type/Actor/Event). _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._ Sourced from synced store, not a true server audit trail.

- **Seats & Quotas** (`quotas`, #180) — editable numeric limits for seats / companies / transactions → 3 usage meters comparing live stat consumption vs editable caps (over-limit highlighted). _Persist: KV `adm-seat-limit`, `adm-company-limit`, `adm-txn-quota`._ _Class: KV (usage from Backend)._ Usage from `/api/admin/stats` (passed-in `stats`); limits KV-only, "enforcement happens server-side on the billing plan" (not actually wired).

- **System Health** (`health`, #181) — **Re-run checks** button → live-probes 3 APIs (`/api/admin/stats`, `/api/admin/companies`, `/api/users`) measuring OK/FAIL + latency ms, computes all-ok banner + avg ms; plus Data Footprint cards from `stats` → health board. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend._ Genuine live fetch probes against real endpoints.

- **Maintenance** (`maintenance`, #182) — Enable/Disable toggle (stamps `since`), editable tenant notice textarea, "allow super-admins through" checkbox, live banner preview → maintenance state. _Persist: KV `adm-maintenance`._ _Class: Simulated._ Toggle/notice are KV-only; tenants are not actually gated.

- **Role Permissions** (`permissions`, #183) — pick a role (from `ROLE_META`) → toggle 7 capabilities (view_finance, edit_txns, manage_invoices, file_compliance, manage_team, export_data, manage_capital) with per-role defaults; super_admin locked to all-allowed; Reset to defaults → capability matrix. _Persist: KV `adm-role-permissions`._ _Class: Indicative._ "Advisory in the client; server-side enforcement follows your plan."

- **Login History** (`login-history`, #184) — no inputs → synthesises one most-recent sign-in per user from the live roster, deterministic via a hash of id/email (device, city, ts, ~1-in-9 failed, never-logged-in for first_login) → 4 stat cards + table. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated._ Roster is real (`GET /api/users`) but device/location/timestamps/outcomes are fabricated; "Wire to your auth provider's session log for full history."

- **Import Jobs** (`import-jobs`, #185) — enqueue form (file name, source: csv/tally/bank/gst, rows) → queue job (status queued→running→completed via Advance, Fail, Retry, Remove) → 4 status stat cards + jobs table. _Persist: KV `adm-import-jobs`._ _Class: Simulated._ No real import pipeline; statuses are manually stepped, no rows processed.

- **Config Snapshot** (`config-snapshot`, #186) — no inputs → assembles read-only JSON of platform stats + KV admin settings (quotas, maintenance, retention, feature flags); **Copy** to clipboard + **Download JSON** file → JSON `<pre>` + 4 summary cards. _Persist: none (reads KV + stats)._ _Class: Backend + KV._ Platform block from real `stats`; rest read from KV stores.

- **Error Log** (`error-log`, #187) — filter (All/Errors/Warnings) → derives operational signals from `store` (bank-sync error/pending, overdue invoices, flagged transactions), newest-first max 200 → 4 stat cards (Errors/Warnings/Total/Health) + table. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._ Derived from synced store data, not a real error/exception log.

- **Notification Templates** (`notify-templates`, #197) — per-template Edit (subject/body with `{{tokens}}`) + Enable/Disable toggle for 3 seeded templates (welcome email, invoice-due WhatsApp, password-reset email) → template cards. _Persist: KV `adm-notify-templates`._ _Class: KV._ Templates stored in KV; not connected to a real send pipeline.

- **Plan Usage** (`plan-usage`, #198) — plan tier `<select>` (Starter/Growth/Scale) → 4 stat cards (Active Plan + MRR, Seats Used vs cap, Companies vs cap, Transactions) + consumption bars vs plan caps with near-limit warning. _Persist: KV `adm-active-plan`._ _Class: Backend + KV._ Usage from real `stats`/`companies`; tier selection & pricing are KV/static (no billing backend).

- **API Keys** (`api-keys`, #199) — create form (label, scope: read / read-write) → client-generates `sk_live_…` secret (revealed once), table of keys with prefix/scope/created/status; Revoke (confirm) + Delete → key list. _Persist: KV `adm-api-keys`._ _Class: Simulated._ Secrets are `Math.random`-generated client-side; no server-issued, functional API credentials.

- **Onboarding** (`onboarding`, #200) — 6-item go-live checklist (owner invited, company profile, bank connected, team added, data imported, production-ready); 4 items auto-tick ("auto" badge, disabled) from live `stats`/`store`, others manually toggled → progress bar + checklist. _Persist: KV `adm-onboarding-checklist`._ _Class: Backend (auto-derive) + KV._ Auto items derive from real `stats.users`/`store.bankAccounts`/`store.transactions`/`store.invoices`; manual ticks in KV.

### Classification summary
- **Backend (real `/api/admin` or `/api/users`):** Platform Overview, Companies, Users, System Health (live probes).
- **Backend-derived (real data, client-computed):** Usage Analytics, Plan Usage (usage side), Onboarding (auto items), Config Snapshot (platform block).
- **KV (device-synced settings, no backend enforcement):** CA Workspace, Feature Flags, Announcements, Seats & Quotas (limits), Notification Templates, Role Permissions, Data Retention.
- **Indicative (advisory / reconstructed-from-store):** Data Retention, Audit Log, Error Log, Role Permissions.
- **Simulated (fabricated or non-functional):** Maintenance, Login History, Import Jobs, API Keys.
## CA / Advisor Workspace (`/advisor`) — 18 tools

A multi-tenant CA practice console where an accountant manages a portfolio of client businesses — viewing each client's cash health, triaging cross-client alerts, batch-filing GST, tracking compliance deadlines and tasks, accepting matched leads, and invoicing — all white-labelled under the firm's branding. Stakeholders: accountant/CA, super_admin (gated by role; others redirect to `/dashboard`). Backend: `/api/advisor` (clients, alerts, gst-status, marketplace, report-preview) + browser `localStorage` (KV).

### Header / portfolio-wide
- **Firm Setup (branding modal)** — opens `FirmProfileModal`; inputs firm name (required), tagline, GSTIN → saved branding reused on white-label client reports; renders a one-time setup prompt banner when no firm name is set. _Persist: `localStorage["hr_ca_firm"]`._ _Class: KV._
- **Add Client (link tenant)** — form: client Tenant ID (required) + optional display name → `POST /api/advisor/clients` adds the business to the CA's portfolio, then reloads list. _Persist: Backend (live store)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Portfolio summary stats** — derived from loaded clients/alerts → four read-only cards: Total Clients, Need Attention (alerts>0 or runway<45d), Active Alerts (severity≠low), Pre-qualified count. _Persist: none._ _Class: Backend._

### Clients tab
- **Client list (At-Risk / Healthy buckets)** — `GET /api/advisor/clients` → renders `ClientCard`s split into "Needs Attention" (unread alerts or runway<45d) and "Healthy" sections, each showing balance, runway badge, alert count, UW credit score, pre-qualified badge, top-alert message. _Persist: Backend (live store)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Client → Forecast (multi-tenant switch)** — per-card "Forecast" button → `setSelectedClient(tenant_id, label)` then navigates to `/forecast`, impersonating that client's context. _Persist: AppContext (live store)._ _Class: Backend._
- **Client Report (white-label)** — per-card "Report" button opens `ReportModal`; `GET /api/advisor/clients/:tenantId/report-preview` → renders firm-letterhead monthly report (cash balance, revenue, expenses, net-position bar, active alerts). _Persist: none._ _Class: Preview._
- **Report Download PDF / Email Client** — modal buttons → toast only ("PDF will be emailed shortly" / "Report emailed to <client>"); no file/email actually generated. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Remove client (unlink)** — per-card "Remove" button → `window.confirm` then `DELETE /api/advisor/clients/:tid`, reloads list. _Persist: Backend (live store)._ _Class: Backend._

### Alert Feed tab
- **Cross-client alert feed** — `GET /api/advisor/alerts` → read-only severity-coloured list (severity · client label · date · message) across the whole portfolio; tab badge counts non-low alerts. _Persist: Backend (live store)._ _Class: Backend._

### Bulk GST tab (`BulkGstTab`)
- **GSTR-3B status board** — `GET /api/advisor/gst-status` → per-client rows: status pill (filed/pending), net liability, GSTN ARN; expandable to a CGST/SGST/IGST split (CGST=SGST=50% of liability, IGST=0, computed client-side); header banner shows month, days-to-20th due date, pending/filed counts, total liability; footer totals. _Persist: Backend (live store) for status; breakdown computed locally._ _Class: Backend (liability split = Indicative)._
- **Prepare All** — header button → 1.5s artificial delay then success toast ("GSTR-3B prepared for all clients"); no filing performed. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Prepare <client>'s GSTR-3B** — per-row expanded button → success toast directing to client's GST tab; no filing. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated._

### Practice tab (`PracticeTab`)
- **Compliance Calendar (deadline matrix)** — `complianceDeadlines(today)` generates next ~12 statutory deadlines (GSTR-3B 20th, TDS 7th, PF 15th, ROC Jun 30, Advance Tax Jun/Sep/Dec 15, ITR Jul 31) × every linked client, colour-coded by days-left. _Persist: none (computed from system date + client list)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Document Requests** — for first 3 clients, static doc list (bank statement, sales invoices, TDS cert) each with "Send link" → success toast only. _Persist: none._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Task Board (kanban)** — add task (client dropdown, type gst/tds/audit/advisory/itr/roc/other, title, deadline); advance todo→inprogress→done; delete; To Do / In Progress / Done columns with overdue/due-today/days-left badges. _Persist: `localStorage["hr_ca_tasks"]`._ _Class: KV._

### Marketplace tab (`MarketplaceTab`)
- **Lead feed** — `GET /api/advisor/marketplace` → leads enriched client-side with synthetic reason, revenue tier, match score (70–99%), and est. annual fee (₹60k/120k/240k); shows name, city, industry, join date. _Persist: Backend (raw leads); enrichment computed locally._ _Class: Backend (enrichment = Indicative)._
- **Accept / Pass lead** — Accept → success toast w/ potential fee + marks accepted (in-memory Set); Pass → marks declined and hides (in-memory Set); neither persisted nor sent to backend. _Persist: none (component state only, lost on reload)._ _Class: Simulated._

### Billing tab (`BillingTab`)
- **Invoice management** — new invoice (client dropdown, amount, description, due date) → draft; advance draft→sent→paid; "UPI link" copies a payment-link toast; KPI cards Total Invoiced / Outstanding / Collected; table of all bills. _Persist: `localStorage["hr_ca_bills"]` (UPI link itself = Simulated toast)._ _Class: KV._

## ━━━ PILLAR 9 · Frontier (preview) ━━━

## Tokens & Programmable Money (`/tokens`) — 11 tools

Design and simulate programmable-money instruments (CBDC / tokenization) for Indian e-rupee & GIFT-City rails before they go live. Stakeholders: owner, finance. Backend: NONE — design/simulate only (no live rails). Be honest: everything here is a PREVIEW/design tool. All splits, balances, specs and lifecycle transitions compute locally in the browser; there are no live blockchain or e-rupee network calls, no funds move, and real settlement only becomes possible once CBDC/tokenization rails exist (and that integration is unbuilt).

- **Overview** — no inputs → renders an explainer card plus 10 clickable cards (each `onPick` switches to the matching tab) describing every other tool → navigation grid. _Persist: none._ _Class: Preview._
- **e-Rupee (CBDC) Ledger** — opening balance (₹), then per-movement direction (credit in / debit out), amount, note, date → manually mirrors an e-rupee wallet: sorts entries by date+id, computes a running balance from the opening figure, and totals in/out; KPIs show current balance / opening / total in / total out; rows deletable → ledger table with running balance. _Persist: live store keys `tok-erupee-opening`, `tok-erupee-entries` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Programmable-Payment Rule Designer** — payee/vendor, amount (₹), trigger type (milestone / delivery / date / approval), condition ("WHEN"), optional purpose-bind → composes a plain-English "Release ₹X to vendor WHEN condition (trigger verb), usable only for purpose" sentence plus a copyable `programmable_payment_rule` JSON spec (currency `INR-eRupee`, status `draft`); saves rules to a deletable list → readable statement + JSON spec + saved-rules list. _Persist: live store key `tok-rules` (useFeatureState); JSON copied via clipboard._ _Class: KV / Indicative._
- **Smart-Contract Escrow Designer** — payer, payee, amount (₹), optional arbiter, release conditions → builds a `smart_contract_escrow` JSON draft spec (currency `INR-eRupee`); creates escrows and locally simulates the lifecycle draft → funded → released via an "advance" button (no funds move); status pill colour-coded; removable → JSON spec + escrow cards with simulated status transitions. _Persist: live store key `tok-escrows` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: Simulated / KV._
- **Tokenized-Invoice Simulator** — invoice face value (₹), number of tokens, early-funding discount (%), then per-investor name + token count → fractionalizes the invoice: computes tokens minted, per-token face value, discounted per-token price, total investor outlay; allocates tokens across investors (capped at remaining), showing each investor's share %, funds-in (at discount) and on-maturity (at face) amounts → KPI cards + investor split table. _Persist: none (component-local `useState`; investors not saved)._ _Class: Indicative._
- **Tokenized-Asset Registry** — asset name, kind (invoice / inventory / equity), face value (₹), token count → logs "tokenized" assets to a record-keeping ledger (explicitly not an on-chain mint); auto-stamps date; computes per-token value and aggregate KPIs (asset count, total face value, total tokens); rows removable → summary cards + asset table. _Persist: live store key `tok-assets` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Atomic-Settlement Simulator** — Leg A party/item/value (₹), Leg B party/item/value (₹), plus a "committed" checkbox per leg → models a two-leg pay-vs-deliver (DvP) swap: warns if leg values are unequal, and shows all-or-nothing outcome — settlement "would execute atomically" only when both legs are checked, otherwise pending with which leg is missing (nothing moves) → readiness checkboxes + settlement-status panel. _Persist: none (all component-local `useState`)._ _Class: Simulated._
- **Conditional-Disbursal Builder** — total payout (₹), then per-milestone name + % of total (capped at remaining %) → splits a payout across milestones, tracking allocated vs remaining % and computing each tranche's ₹ amount; flags when 100% is allocated; rows removable → disbursal schedule table. _Persist: live store key `tok-milestones` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV / Indicative._
- **Token Cap-Table** — total tokens issued, then per-holder name + token count (capped at unissued, issued can't drop below held) → tracks issued / held / unissued supply and computes each holder's ownership % against issued supply; holders removable → KPI cards + holder ownership table. _Persist: live store keys `tok-captable-issued`, `tok-captable-holders` (useFeatureState)._ _Class: KV._
- **Settlement-Readiness Checklist** — toggle 8 fixed readiness items (e-rupee wallet onboarded, KYC identity, participating-bank rail, counterparties on rail, legal templates, accounting treatment, GIFT-City/IFSC setup, audit-trail/reconciliation) → tracks completion count, % and a progress bar; shows a success banner at 100% → checklist with progress. _Persist: live store key `tok-readiness` (useFeatureState, array of done ids)._ _Class: KV._
- **Glossary** — no inputs → renders 10 static plain-English explainer cards (e-Rupee/CBDC, programmable money, purpose-bound payment, smart-contract escrow, atomic settlement/DvP, tokenization, fractionalization, GIFT-City/IFSC, on-chain provenance, settlement rail) → reference cards. _Persist: none._ _Class: Preview._
## Frontier Lab (`/frontier`) — 11 tools

Headroom's R&D sandbox previewing the next decade of SMB finance (real-time, ambient, autonomous, quantum-safe) — shipping the buildable bits as working tools and the rail-dependent bits as honest interactive mockups. Stakeholders: owner. Backend: NONE — experimental/preview; durable tools persist only via `useFeatureState` (per-account KV sync), preview/derived tools compute client-side. A persistent honest banner warns that `Preview`-badged tools are interactive mockups, not live money rails.

Tabs (`TABS` array, line 23–35) carry a `"today" | "preview"` flag; `Overview` cards (line 99–110) carry a `live` boolean. Both classifications below match the source.

- **Overview** — no inputs (reads `store` → `computeFinancialSnapshot`) → renders a "what this lab is for" blurb, 4 live KPI cards (cash on hand + account count, monthly net w/ cash-generative/burning sub, runway in days or ∞, debt outstanding + loan count) and a grid of 10 launcher cards (one per other tool, each tagged LiveBadge or PreviewBadge, click → `onOpen(tab)`) → navigation hub. _Persist: none._ _Class: Indicative._ **Interactive** (live snapshot + navigation; no own output).

- **Alert Triggers** (`AlertTriggers`, line 180) — inputs: rule name, event (`low_balance` / `large_expense` / `invoice_overdue` / `new_revenue` / `debt_due`), threshold (₹ for money events, else days — label switches via `isMoneyEvent`), channel (`in_app` / `whatsapp` / `email`) → CRUD over event→notification rules: add (validates name + numeric threshold), toggle Active/Paused, delete; renders rules table → durable trigger rules. Note: WhatsApp/email delivery activates "as those channels are connected"; rules "evaluated by the Ambient Rules engine." _Persist: `frnt-alert-rules` (useFeatureState KV)._ _Class: KV._ **Interactive.**

- **Ambient Rules** (`AmbientRules`, line 289) — inputs: metric (`cash` / `runway` / `monthly_net` / `debt_outstanding`), operator (`<` / `>`), threshold value, action text → arms background "if metric op value then action" rules and evaluates them **live** against the real `computeFinancialSnapshot`; shows 4 current-metric cards, a "N rule(s) firing right now" orange panel, and a rules table with per-row Firing/Quiet state + current value + delete → live-evaluated automation rules. _Persist: `frnt-ambient-rules` (useFeatureState KV)._ _Class: KV (rules) + Indicative (live evaluation against real data)._ **Interactive.**

- **Scenario Supercompute** (`ScenarioSupercompute`, line 412) — inputs: starting cash (prefilled from live `snap.cash`), monthly revenue, revenue volatility %, monthly cost, cost volatility %, horizon (1–36 months, clamped), runs slider (1k–100k, clamped) → real client-side Monte-Carlo: per run draws monthly revenue & cost from a Box-Muller normal around assumptions, compounds cash over the horizon, sorts endings → outputs P10/P50/P90 percentiles, % chance of negative cash, min/max range, and a colour-coded summary sentence. Spinner deferred via `setTimeout(…,30)` so the heavy loop doesn't block paint. Validates revenue > 0. _Persist: none (in-memory result)._ _Class: Simulated (genuine compute) / Indicative output._ **Interactive.**

- **Quantum-Safe Readiness** (`QuantumReadiness`, line 532) — inputs: toggle 8 fixed checklist items (`PQC_ITEMS`: crypto inventory, hybrid PQC TLS on connectors, PQC encryption at rest, lattice-signed docs, QRNG/PQC key custody, crypto-agility process, quantum-safe backups, vendor PQC attestation) → computes a live readiness score % with band (Exposed <40 / In migration 40–79 / Quantum-ready ≥80), progress bar, and N/8 steps-complete → durable post-quantum migration tracker. References NIST PQC (Kyber/Dilithium/SPHINCS+) and harvest-now-decrypt-later. _Persist: `frnt-pqc-done` (useFeatureState KV, array of done ids)._ _Class: KV (scored)._ **Interactive.**

- **Event Stream** (`EventStream`, line 588) — no inputs (derives from live `store`) → builds an activity feed from transactions (revenue inflow vs outflow, tone by sign/category), invoices (raised, tone by status), alerts (tone by severity) and obligations (scheduled, warn tone); skips invalid dates, sorts newest-first, slices top 40; renders tone-coloured event-bus rows with relative timestamps (`formatDistanceToNow`) → read-only "event-driven finance" preview feed. _Persist: none (derived)._ _Class: Indicative (derived from real store)._ **Concept-card / read-only** (interactive only as a live derived feed; no controls).

- **Tech-Readiness Radar** (`TechReadinessRadar`, line 663) — inputs: 0–5 slider per axis (`ai` / `realtime` / `tokenization` / `quantum`, each with a description) → computes overall frontier-readiness % (sum / max) and renders per-axis score/5 with segmented bars → durable self-assessment scorecard guiding roadmap (lowest axis = next investment). _Persist: `frnt-radar` (useFeatureState KV, defaults `{ai:2, realtime:2, tokenization:1, quantum:1}`)._ _Class: KV (scored)._ **Interactive.**

- **Request-to-Pay** (`RequestToPayDesigner`, line 706) — inputs: payer name/VPA, amount (₹), purpose, expiry hours → live-renders a structured `REQUEST_TO_PAY` JSON payload preview (type, payer, amount, currency INR, purpose, expiresInHours, approval: one-tap) plus a settlement-line caption when amount > 0 → preview payload only; explicitly "does not send a real payment request yet" (awaits maturing UPI Request-to-Pay rail). _Persist: none (component state only)._ _Class: Preview._ **Interactive (mockup)** — `PreviewBadge`.

- **Streaming Payroll** (`StreamingPayroll`, line 746) — inputs: monthly wage/head (₹), headcount, working hours/day → computes per-second accrual per head (wage / (22 × hours × 3600)), team accrual per working day, and team monthly outflow → illustrative continuous-payroll maths; "not wired to your payroll," needs streaming rails. _Persist: none._ _Class: Preview (Simulated maths)._ **Interactive (mockup)** — `PreviewBadge`.

- **Autonomous Treasury** (`AutonomousTreasury`, line 784) — inputs: minimum cash buffer (₹), max % of surplus to sweep, minimum yield to act (% p.a.), auto-sweep toggle, auto-draw toggle → renders a plain-language policy-preview sentence summarising the guardrails a self-driving treasury agent "would obey"; "no funds move until autonomous treasury is enabled" → durable intended-policy config. _Persist: `frnt-treasury-policy` (useFeatureState KV object)._ _Class: Preview + KV (policy persists; behaviour is concept)._ **Interactive (mockup, persisted)** — `PreviewBadge`.

- **Machine-to-Machine Pay** (`MachineToMachine`, line 821) — inputs: device name, price/unit (₹), units consumed, unit label (e.g. km/kWh) → computes total auto-settle cost (perUnit × units) and renders a programmable-money micropayment narration ("would auto-settle ₹X for N units… streaming micropayments per unit with no invoice"); "no device or CBDC rail is connected," ahead of e-rupee programmable-money support → conceptual economics calculator. _Persist: none (component state only)._ _Class: Preview._ **Interactive (mockup)** — `PreviewBadge`.

### Notes
- **Badges**: `LiveBadge` ("Live tool") marks the 6 today-tools (Alerts, Ambient, Supercompute, Quantum, Event Stream, Radar) + Overview KPIs; `PreviewBadge` marks the 4 preview tools (RTP, Streaming Payroll, Autonomous Treasury, M2M) and their shared `PreviewHeader`.
- **Genuinely buildable today** (no money rail needed): Alert Triggers, Ambient Rules (live-evaluated), Scenario Supercompute (real Monte-Carlo), Quantum-Safe Readiness, Event Stream (derived), Tech-Readiness Radar.
- **Honest previews** (rail-dependent, no live money): Request-to-Pay (UPI-RTP), Streaming Payroll (streaming-payout rails), Autonomous Treasury (autonomous agent), M2M (programmable CBDC / e-rupee).
- **Persistence** is entirely client/KV via `useFeatureState`; no dedicated backend, no real notification dispatch, no payment execution anywhere in this file.

*End of detailed inventory — 55 modules.*
